The instructor's bronze skin came from Copper Skin Gu, which significantly increased toughness and defense.

"A single moonblade consumes ten percent primeval essence, making repeated casts impractical for beginners. It serves only as a trump card whose intimidation exceeds its lethality. For a Rank one Gu Master, martial arts are far more practical, offering durable and reliable offense. It is a pity that they will not understand this without firsthand experience."

Fang Yuan glanced around at his peers, a faint sneer forming on his lips.

When class ended, the academy elder arrived and pointed toward the grass puppets before the bamboo wall. Getting straight to the point, he said, "Today we test your progress. Come up in groups of five and strike three times with your moonblades."

The first group stepped up. After three rounds of flying moonblades, only nine struck the grass puppets.

The academy elder shook his head in displeasure at the abysmal hit rate.

"Practice harder after this, especially you two," the elder reprimanded, waving his hand. "Next group."

The two students hung their heads and left in dismay. One was a girl, her eyes red with grief. With only C-grade talent, she could not bear spending primeval stones to practice, leaving her technique clumsy.

Watching this, Fang Yuan secretly shook his head. "How foolish to sacrifice progress over a few primeval stones. Primeval stones are meant to be spent; what is the point of being a Gu Master if you only hoard them?"

"It's finally my turn." Smiling with supreme confidence, Gu Yue Mo Bei strode onto the field, his stout build radiating a fierce, powerful aura. Taking his stance, he cast three moonblades—all three hit. Two struck the puppet's chest, while the third clipped its left arm, shaving away strands of grass.

This result drew immediate admiration from the students.

"Well done." The elder's brow slightly smoothed out.

The next group stepped forward, with the short, pockmarked Gu Yue Chi Cheng looking slightly nervous.

He cast three consecutive moonblades into the puppet's chest before its self-healing ability erased the cuts.

Tying Mo Bei's score, Chi Cheng received the elder's praise and glared defiantly at his rival.

Among the final groups, Gu Yue Fang Zheng stepped forward.

# Chapter 25: The light of spring is enchanting

Pressured by the crowd's gazes, Fang Zheng grew nervous, his fingers trembling.

He threw his first moonblade toward the puppet's chest, but his nervous tension caused him to miss, hitting the neck instead.

Relieved after the near miss, Fang Zheng steadied himself and struck the puppet's chest with his next two blades.

In the very last group, Fang Yuan finally stepped forward.

With many eyes and the academy elder fixed upon him, Fang Yuan remained emotionless, cold and detached.

Standing still, he poured primeval essence into the Moonlight Gu in his palm and slashed out his first moonblade.

The moonblade flew high over the puppet and bamboo wall, vanishing fifteen meters away.

The academy elder shook his head, dismissing Fang Yuan as a C-grade student who had merely gotten lucky before.

He had already decided: though Mo Bei, Chi Cheng, and Fang Zheng were tied, he would still name Fang Zheng first.

Fang Yuan stared coldly at the grass puppet ten meters away, a faint blue light glowing in his palm.

Having deliberately missed the first shot, Fang Yuan knew claiming first place required an outcome exceeding all expectations.

With a sharp tear, a watery blue moonblade flew from his palm toward the grass puppet.

Instantly, his palm flashed again, launching the third moonblade in seamless succession.

Flying in rapid succession, both moonblades struck the grass puppet's neck precisely.

The grass puppet's head tilted sideways, dropped from its neck, and bounced two to three meters across the ground.

Fang Yuan had beheaded the puppet!

"Luck or skill?" the academy elder wondered, frowning. The same question gripped the students as silence enveloped the training field.

Fang Yuan looked up at the birds dazzling in the sunlight, indifferent to having decapitated the puppet.

"The light of spring is truly enchanting..." he sighed inwardly.

# Chapter 26: The nature of all organizations

At dusk, the academy elder called out names from the stage to distribute allowances to the fifty students.

"Gu Yue Fang Yuan," the elder read aloud.

Fang Yuan took the stage, receiving three primeval stones for his allowance and ten as his reward.

Before dismissing them, the elder announced, "You have now spent enough time in the academy to become familiar with your vital Gu. In the coming days, I will teach you to nourish your apertures and advance your cultivation. The higher a Gu Master's realm, the purer their primeval essence becomes. Rank one possesses green copper primeval essence, Rank two red iron, and Rank three white silver. One portion of red iron primeval essence equals ten portions of green copper. Likewise, one portion of white silver equals ten of red iron!"

"Remember, Gu are merely tools; cultivation is our foundation. The higher your rank, the stronger the Gu you can wield. Whoever reaches Rank one middle stage first within the next three months will receive thirty primeval stones and first choice of a second Gu. After three months, top performers will be appointed class monitor and vice-monitors with increased allowances. Class dismissed."

"Mere monitor positions have them itching for a fight. How naive and hot-blooded," Fang Yuan sneered, leaning against the academy gate.

So-called glory was merely a tool used by superiors to motivate subordinates—an illusory, useless concept.

Five centuries of experience had long unveiled the world's underlying truths to Fang Yuan.

"Whether clans, sects, or demonic factions, on Earth or in this world, all organizations establish hierarchies and promotion paths so those at the bottom strive endlessly upward. Self-interest is human nature, and authority breeds a sense of superiority, creating the illusion of a more valuable life."

"Power is the carrot dangled before a donkey, stimulating human desire. People curry favor with authority for gain, only to find another level waiting above each rank they climb. While scrambling for personal advancement, their labor is squeezed out and their value exploited by the ruling class."

"Every chain of command exists to serve the upper ranks. These class monitor positions are merely the smallest carrots, luring youths into the clan's hierarchy. To prevent subordinates from seeing the truth, leaders promote values like glory and merit, enforce rigid hierarchies with unequal rewards, and sometimes use religion to control hearts and minds."

"Such is the truth, yet few understand, foolishly toiling their lives away for others. The fundamental essence of every organization is simply resource redistribution: the higher the rank, the more resources one enjoys."

In his previous life, Fang Yuan had founded the Bloodwing Demon Sect in the Middle Kingdom, commanding tens of thousands through ranks of demon soldiers, generals, and sages. Each rank offered distinct benefits, laying bare the underlying mechanics of power to him.

"Thus, any organization is merely a facade for resources. Without cultivation resources, one will inevitably become weak and be bullied to death."

"And for a Gu Master, primeval stones are the ultimate resource!" His eyes deep as an ancient pool, Fang Yuan's lips curled into a faint sneer.

Having stepped outside the academy, he waited at the gate as the first group of students approached.

Ignoring him, the youths started past, only for Fang Yuan to step forward and block their path. "This is a robbery. Hand over one primeval stone each before you leave."

# Chapter 27: Outright extortion

"Scram! A mere C-grade dares block my way? Move, or I'll send you flying with my—Urgh!"

Fang Yuan suddenly lashed out.

His right palm chopped forward, striking the side of the youth's neck.

Unprepared, the cursing teenager collapsed unconscious from the heavy blow.

Before the others could react, Fang Yuan strode forward, charging directly into the group.

Armed with five centuries of combat experience, how could these green novices who had barely begun cultivating ever hope to match him?

In the blink of an eye, Fang Yuan downed the entire group, leaving them either unconscious or writhing in pain across the ground.

"What's going on here?!" Mo Bei demanded upon arriving to find fallen students surrounding Fang Yuan.

"Hand over one primeval stone each and I'll let you leave. Otherwise, you'll share their fate," Fang Yuan said callously, stepping forward.

"A petty C-grade dares challenge me?" Enraged, Mo Bei swung his fists and charged.

Sidestepping the punch, Fang Yuan jabbed two fingers directly into the hollow beneath Mo Bei's throat.

Mo Bei instantly blacked out, thumping heavily onto the ground unconscious.

"Give me your stones or share his fate," Fang Yuan pressed, before the remaining youths swarmed him.

Cool as ice, Fang Yuan dodged and struck with effortless precision.

Within a few breaths, bodies collapsed to the ground again.

"They're not going to die, are they?"

A few girls who had stayed behind trembled in terror at the sight.

Fang Yuan swept his gaze over them. Pale-faced, they hastily waved their hands and backed away. "No, don't come over. We'll surrender the stones!"

After collecting their primeval stones, Fang Yuan let them go. They stumbled out the academy gate just as several more students arrived.

Lacking authority to punish students, the mortal guards had reported Fang Yuan's extortion to the academy elder.

Upon hearing the report, the elder had not ordered them to stop it, choosing instead to observe from atop a pavilion.

"Looks like this child has fighting talent." The longer the academy elder watched the more interested he felt.

Watching him dismantle the class with effortless grace, the elder's earlier doubts were completely dispelled.

"Ah, a natural battle Gu Master. What a pity his talent is only C grade; he falls short by just one step." The academy elder sighed.

"Sir, are you not going to stop this farce? Letting his nonsense continue might bring bad consequences." The guards beside him looked worried.

# Chapter 28: Capital free business

"Why stop it?" The academy elder chuckled, pointing at Fang Yuan. "This youth has full control and restrains his strikes. He strikes only the neck's sides to knock them out, carefully avoiding lethal points. Attacking the back of the head or neck could cause death, so he avoided it entirely."

"Look at the youths on the ground—are any heavily injured? None! Even if they were, so what? Are our academy's healing Gu Masters unable to cure such wounds?"

"What's wrong with fighting? As long as lives are not lost, it stirs their competitive spirit and tempers their will to fight. Stopping this would stifle their passion! Did past classes not fight as well? At that aggressive age, newly gained strength made them itch for battle. Why didn't you stop them back then?" the elder asked coldly.

Another dozen students now lay on the ground by the entrance. As Fang Yuan stood proudly, three young girls huddled back-to-back opposite him.

A blue radiance glowed in their palms as they prepared to mobilize their Moonlight Gu.

Though an unarmored mortal body could not withstand moonblades, Fang Yuan stepped forward unfazed. "Using Gu to fight inside the academy brings immediate expulsion. Go ahead if you dare."

Seizing their hesitation, Fang Yuan dashed in and chopped two down.

Collapsing in tears, the last girl pleaded, "Fang Yuan, please let me go."

Fang Yuan looked down at her with cold indifference. "One primeval stone."

Expressionless, Fang Yuan reached out and lightly pinched a primeval stone from her hand with his thumb and forefinger.

"I only take one stone," Fang Yuan said plainly. "You may leave."

The academy elder stroked his chin thoughtfully. Fang Yuan possessed both combat talent and propriety. Extorting one primeval stone stayed within bounds; demanding two would have forced the elder to intervene.

Soon, the last group arrived—only five students, among them Fang Yuan's twin younger brother.

"Brother, how could you beat our classmates and steal their primeval stones?!" Fang Zheng stared wide-eyed. "Quickly admit your mistake to the elder! You might actually get expelled!"

Fang Yuan laughed and said, "You're right."

Fang Zheng sighed in relief, glad his brother had not gone mad and could still be reasoned with.

Yet moments later, Fang Yuan spoke again: "Every one of you, a piece of primeval stone."

"What?" Fang Zheng opened his mouth wide in surprise. "Even I need to pay?"

"Dearest brother, you may refuse," Fang Yuan smiled gently, pointing at the groaning students. "And share their fate."

Learning from their peers' mistakes, the remaining youngsters obediently surrendered to Fang Yuan's extortion, their eyes full of defiance.

"Hold on," Fang Yuan called out just as they were about to leave.

Facing the fallen teenagers, Fang Yuan sighed softly. "Do you guys really think I would squat down and search through their bodies one by one?"

Fang Yuan's frosty gaze made the five youngsters' scalps tingle with dread.

Intimidated, the five collected one primeval stone from each fallen student and surrendered them to Fang Yuan.

With fifty-seven students in the class, extorting one stone from everyone else yielded Fang Yuan fifty-six primeval stones.

Factoring in his allowance, reward, and wine purchases, Fang Yuan's wealth now reached seventy-nine primeval stones.

"Plunder truly is the most profitable business." Tucking his bulging pouch away, Fang Yuan strutted off.

# Chapter 29: Unscrupulous

"Put all the wine jars under the bed," Fang Yuan directed the inn workers, having bought twenty jars at once.

Each jar cost two primeval stones; Fang Yuan spent forty stones for the sake of the Liquor worm.

His pouch deflated to thirty-nine stones, but the wine would sustain the Liquor worm for a long time.

After the servants left, Fang Yuan closed his dormitory door and sat cross-legged on the bed.

In the dark room, Fang Yuan calmed his mind, letting his focus slip into the Primeval Sea.

Waves rolled across the Primeval Sea, glowing with green copper light. Filling forty-four percent of the aperture, it marked the limit of Fang Yuan's C-grade talent.

Floating atop the Primeval Sea, the chubby white Liquor worm played to its heart's content, diving into the water and splashing droplets with lively flicks of its head and tail.

At Fang Yuan's command, the Liquor worm curled into a dumpling shape and rose to the center of the aperture above the sea.

Fang Yuan mobilized a tenth of his primeval essence, channeling it into the Liquor worm, which absorbed the flow without resistance.

Powered by essence, the curled Liquor worm radiated soft white light, generating wine fumes that condensed into pale mist.

With a thought, Fang Yuan sent another ten percent essence into the mist, dissolving it back into the green copper sea. That ten percent of green copper primeval essence shrank in volume, leaving only five percent.

Yet this five percent was far more condensed—a darker pale green primeval essence unique to Rank one middle stage.

The Liquor worm's use was precisely to condense primeval essence and elevate it by a small realm rank!

Though only a Rank one initial stage Gu Master, Fang Yuan now wielded five percent middle stage primeval essence!

"Condensing five percent of middle stage essence requires twenty percent of initial stage primeval essence. To convert my entire forty-four percent Primeval Sea into middle stage essence, I'll need around one hundred and eighty percent initial stage essence. To reach this quickly, I must rely on primeval stones."

Thinking of this, Fang Yuan opened his eyes and took an intact duck-egg-sized primeval stone from his bag. The translucent grey ellipsoid would steadily shrink as the natural essence inside was consumed.

Clenching the primeval stone tightly in his palm, he absorbed its natural essence to replenish his aperture, steadily raising the sunken level of his Primeval Sea.

"Without backing, I can only rely on extortion. Every seven days when the academy distributes allowances, I will block the gates."

Every action had a clear goal, employing whatever method reached it fastest—he was thoroughly unscrupulous.

In his study, Chi family elder Gu Yue Chi Lian asked his grandson, "I heard you were beaten up by Fang Yuan today?"

Sporting a black right eye, Chi Cheng fumed, "Yes, Grandpa! That lowly C-grade dared to act so arrogant! He blocked us at the entrance and robbed our primeval stones while the academy turned a blind eye! The guards only hurried over after Fang Yuan walked away. Grandpa, this time you must help me vent this resentment!"

Gu Yue Chi Lian shook his head. "This is a junior dispute without serious injury; I have no justification to step in. Do you understand why?"

"Remember, you do not just represent yourself, but the entire image of our Chi family branch. We have confronted the Mo family branch for years; your every move represents our future hope. Grandpa can help from the shadows, but you must project a strong, self-reliant image. Otherwise, the elders supporting our family will see no hope and abandon us."

Chi Lian sighed. "This is why I helped you fake B-grade talent—our Chi family needs a strong successor to retain supporters."

"Understanding is not enough; train diligently in basic martial arts to reclaim your dignity. Fang Yuan is minor trouble. Simultaneously, cultivate hard to reach middle stage and secure the class monitor position for our family."

# Chapter 30: Fang Yuan, You’re Robbing Again?

"Honorary father and mother, that was how things went." Fang Zheng stood straight, his tone respectful and cautious.

In the hall, Fang Yuan's uncle Gu Yue Dong Tu and aunt sat frowning in wide-backed chairs, his aunt gritting her teeth. Indignant yet secretly gloating, she spat, "That bastard Fang Yuan! Extorting others is one thing, but not even sparing his own brother? After committing such a huge crime, he'll surely be expelled from the academy soon!"

"Enough, speak less." Uncle sighed and told Fang Zheng, "Collect a replacement stone from the treasury and focus on your cultivation. With your A grade talent, you have every chance to reach middle stage first. Do not waste heaven's blessing; we expect you to become number one."

"Yes, father and mother. Your son takes his leave." Fang Zheng left with a heart full of trepidation.

Once Fang Zheng left the hall, Aunt exclaimed happily, "Husband, cutting off Fang Yuan's living expenses finally drove the little bastard to commit a huge blunder! Daring to brawl at the academy gates and openly extort his peers is provoking the academy elder. His expulsion must be very near!"

Uncle shook his head. "You think too simply. Fang Yuan will not be expelled; in fact, there may not be any punishment."

Uncle's face clouded over. "What else can we do? Did you expect me to personally snatch away his primeval stones? Still, we can exploit this situation. Fang Yuan extorting his own brother is the key to his fall. Fang Zheng has A grade talent and will inevitably surpass him. We can use this matter to sow discord in Fang Zheng. We'll turn Fang Zheng against Fang Yuan for our own use!"

And with that, three days passed.

The disturbance from Fang Yuan's extortion did not escalate; instead, it gradually died down.

No elders broke rules to trouble Fang Yuan, and the academy elder turned a blind eye.

Another four days passed.

The academy elder distributed allowances once more, and Fang Yuan's time to act came again.

"Fang Yuan, you want to rob our stones again?!" The students glared in shock and fury.

Standing with hands behind his back, Fang Yuan said coldly, "One primeval stone per person to spare yourselves physical pain."

"Fang Yuan, your bullying is excessive! I challenge you!" Gu Yue Mo Bei roared angrily, stepping forward first.

"Oh?" Fang Yuan raised his eyebrows slightly.

Mo Bei charged with raised fists. After a few rounds, he fainted onto the ground.

"Mo Bei, useless fool! Watch me!" Gu Yue Chi Cheng dashed forward, only to be knocked out seconds later.

With centuries of combat experience, Fang Yuan countered every attack effortlessly; fighting him one by one, the inexperienced students stood no chance.

Fifteen minutes later, Fang Yuan strolled away with a bulging money bag, leaving behind a courtyard of groaning, battered youths.

# Chapter 31: Fang Yuan! You’re in huge trouble!

Mo Yan pushed open the study door. Inside, Gu Yue Mo Chen was practicing calligraphy with his back to her.

"You're back?" Gu Yue Mo Chen asked without turning. "What is the situation with the wolves' den?"

She asked, "Grandfather, little brother was beaten up! He lied about it, so I had to ask you."

Gu Yue Mo Chen's face turned serious. "You haven't answered my question." He put down his brush and sat back.

Mo Yan reluctantly reported, "The wolf den is nearly full. A wolf tide will definitely hit our village next year."

"An outbreak every three years is expected," Mo Chen said. "How many Thunder Crown Wolves are there?"

"Around three."

"Grandfather, you still haven't told me about little brother!" Mo Yan pressed.

"He was knocked out cold before the academy gates twice—first seven days ago, and again today," Gu Yue Mo Chen chuckled.

"Who dared knock out my little brother?" Mo Yan stared wide-eyed.

"Mo Bei's classmate, Fang Yuan. He fights remarkably well..." Gu Yue Mo Chen sniggered.

Gu Yue Mo Chen looked deeply at his granddaughter. "Mo Yan, as a girl, you may not understand. Defeat and humiliation fuel growth. Without failure, one never becomes a true man."

"Mo Bei's defeat is his own failure. When he wakes, he'll seek combat instruction from the teachers. That growth comes from Fang Yuan beating sense into him. With our backing, he will eventually trample Fang Yuan into the dirt."

"Leave this rival to Mo Bei. A woman needs family and love, but a man needs a rival above all. Do not pick trouble with Fang Yuan, understand? This is a matter between youths. If you intervene, it will be seen as bullying, and breaking the rules will bring disgrace upon our Mo family."

Mo Yan gasped wordlessly, but under Gu Yue Mo Chen's gaze, she finally lowered her head. "Yes grandfather, your granddaughter understands."

She stumbled out of the study, an ominous glint in her eyes unnoticed by Gu Yue Mo Chen.

"Grandfather, that is your way of loving him, but I have my own methods." Mo Yan already had other plans.

Fang Yuan sat by the window, eating his meal while gazing outside.

Rank one Gu Masters wore blue belts with bronze plates, while Rank two Gu Masters wore red belts with steel plates.

From the window, Fang Yuan spotted six or seven mostly young Rank one Gu Masters, along with a middle-aged Rank two Gu Master.

"At Rank one initial stage, I lack the qualifications to roam the Southern Border. I need at least Rank three cultivation to wander the world," Fang Yuan sighed over dinner.

Qing Mao Mountain was too small to contain his ambition; he was determined to leave.

"Haha, Gu Yue Fang Yuan, I finally found you!" A middle-aged man laughed deviously as he approached.

"Fang Yuan, you're in deep trouble. You dared strike our Mo family's young master, and now our young miss is here to settle the score," the servant sneered.

# Chapter 32: Making Fun

Fang Yuan glanced at him for only a second before returning to his meal, treating him as invisible.

"You still have the appetite to eat?" Failing to intimidate Fang Yuan, the muscular servant frowned in doubt. "Do you think I'm lying? Someone already went to report to the young miss, and she'll be here shortly. Don't try running, lad—my job is to keep you right here. Plenty of suffering awaits you."

"Fang Yuan has been found?" A proud female voice rang from the entrance.

Mo Yan strode into the inn, trailed by numerous family servants.

She wore a dark blue uniform and a red belt bearing a square steel plate engraved with "2".

"Greetings, Young Miss!" The servant scurried forward and dropped to his knees.

Ignoring the groveling Gao Wan, Mo Yan fixed her gaze on Fang Yuan and stepped forward fiercely. "So you're Fang Yuan? Enjoying a hearty meal, I see. Heh, ever had a knuckle sandwich? Let me give you a taste—it might be even more delicious."

Despite her words, Mo Yan did not make a move.

Fang Yuan was unnervingly calm. Did he have secret backers protecting him?

Unnerved by his calm, she hesitated. Fang Yuan chuckled. "Who told you I was Gu Yue Fang Yuan?"

Mo Yan was momentarily stunned, then glanced at Gao Wan.

Gao Wan dropped to his knees in a cold sweat, stammering incoherently.

They had a drawing of Fang Yuan, but didn't know he and Fang Zheng were nearly identical twins.

"Fang Yuan cannot compare to Fang Zheng. The former is merely a C-rank loner without background. The latter has A-rank talent, recruited into the clan head's faction with a bright future ahead!" Gao Wan's failure to answer only deepened Mo Yan's hesitation.

Finished with his meal, Fang Yuan stood up and glanced at Mo Yan. "Looking for Fang Yuan? Come with me, I'll take you to the hostel to find him."

"Fine, I'll go with you to the hostel. After you!" Mo Yan stepped aside, gesturing for Fang Yuan to take the lead.

Fang Yuan chuckled nonchalantly and strode forth, Mo Yan and her servants trailing closely behind.

"Halt. The school hostel only allows our clan's Gu Masters to enter," the two guards said, stopping the group.

"Insolent! Don't you recognize me? How dare you stop me!" Mo Yan glared and shouted.

"Young Miss Mo Yan, clan rules are absolute. At most, you may bring one servant," a guard replied politely.

Mo Yan clicked her tongue in frustration, yet dared not violate clan rules.

"The rest stay behind. Gao Wan, follow me," Mo Yan ordered after a moment's thought.

"Lead on, junior," Mo Yan said, eyeing Fang Yuan inquisitively.

Unfazed, Fang Yuan led them in, unlocked his dormitory door, and pushed it open.

Mo Yan peered inside, her expression darkening. "Junior, explain yourself—the room is empty!"

Fang Yuan smiled faintly: "Aren't I someone?"

Mo Yan glared at Fang Yuan, sudden realization dawning. "I am looking for Gu—Yue—Fang—Yuan!"

Fang Yuan snickered, "I never said I wasn't Gu Yue Fang Yuan."

# Chapter 33: Go ahead and scold away

Mo Yan frowned, then flared with rage as she realized Fang Yuan had played her for a fool.

"You're remarkably brave to lie to me!" Reaching out, she lunged to grab Fang Yuan.

Standing his ground, Fang Yuan laughed. "Mo Yan, you'd better think this through!"

Mo Yan froze outside the doorway, hand pausing in midair as hesitation crossed her face.

Clan rules protected students within the dormitories from intrusion. Mo Yan only wanted to teach Fang Yuan a lesson, not violate clan law.

Fearing disgrace upon her grandfather and family, Mo Yan reluctantly withdrew her arm, glaring with fury.

"I brought you to Fang Yuan, and here he is," Fang Yuan smiled faintly, fearlessly meeting her furious gaze.

"Heh, you know the rules well," Mo Yan said coldly. "Unfortunately, relying on them only buys you time. You can't stay in the dorms forever. Let's see how long you can hide in there."

Fang Yuan laughed. "It's late—I have a bed to sleep in, but what about you? If I miss class tomorrow and the elders investigate, what do you think I'll say?"

"You!" Mo Yan pointed at him, barely containing her rage. "Do you think I don't dare drag you out?"

Fang Yuan threw the door wide open, grinning. "Then show me."

"Heh..." Seeing this, Mo Yan calmed down and squinted. "You think I'd fall for your taunts?"

Fang Yuan shrugged, having already seen through her temperament.

Had he shut the door, she might have barged in; leaving it wide open only heightened her suspicion.

Five hundred years of experience had made him thoroughly familiar with human nature and its flaws.

He turned around, leaving his back exposed, yet Mo Yan remained frozen outside as if blocked by an invisible wall.

"How pathetic," Fang Yuan mused. "What restrains people is rarely physical obstacles, but the invisible restrictions in their own minds."

Fang Yuan took a final look at Mo Yan, closed his eyes, and sank his consciousness into the Primeval Sea.

"Damn it! Fang Yuan is too sly!" Furious, Mo Yan began hurling insults to provoke him out of the room.

"Fang Yuan, you can't hide forever!" Stomping indignantly, she turned to leave, ordering, "Gao Wan, watch him! He'll have to leave eventually."

"Yes, Master!" Gao Wan replied bitterly, left to guard in the freezing night air.

Inside his aperture, green copper primeval essence crashed against the initial stage white light barrier.

"To raise cultivation, a Gu Master must expend primeval essence to nurture the aperture. Initial stage has light barriers, middle stage water barriers, and upper stage stone barriers. To reach middle stage, I must nurture this light barrier into a water barrier."

Opening his eyes, he saw that it was already late into the night.

"Scoundrel, you're finally awake! Come out and face Young Miss's punishment!"

Fang Yuan squinted—it seemed the Rank two female Gu Master had left.

Ignoring him completely, Fang Yuan took a primeval stone from his storage bag and closed his eyes once more.

Seeing him resume cultivating, Gao Wan snapped anxiously. "With mere C grade talent, the most you'll ever reach is Rank two! What's the point of cultivating? You can't face the Mo family alone! Kid, are you deaf? Did you hear a word I said?!"

# Chapter 34: Suppressive Beating!

Ignoring him, Fang Yuan multitasked, simultaneously absorbing natural essence from the primeval stone and observing his aperture.

After half an hour, his green copper Primeval Sea reached its forty-four percent limit.

To accelerate his cultivation, he needed middle stage essence refined by the Liquor worm.

With a thought, the Liquor worm hovered before him like a white dumpling.

He channeled initial stage primeval essence into the worm, which exhaled a dense liquor mist.

Channeling more essence into the mist halved its volume and condensed it into pale-green middle stage essence.

"Ordinary students cultivate with initial stage primeval essence, but using middle stage essence makes my efficiency at least double theirs. Likewise, activating the Moonlight Gu with middle stage primeval essence makes moonblades far stronger than with initial stage essence."

Only after converting all the essence in his Primeval Sea to middle stage did Fang Yuan open his eyes.

Opening his clenched fist, he scattered a handful of spent stone powder.

"A night of cultivation cost three primeval stones," Fang Yuan calculated.

With C grade talent, he used primeval stones for fast recovery, while the Liquor worm refined his essence to middle stage.

"Though I plundered more primeval stones yesterday, a night of cultivation costs three stones. In this case, although it might seem that I have a lot of resources, it is unable to sustain me for a long time with my current cultivation speed, but this is the price I have to pay for pursuing cultivation speed and efficiency."

Glancing outside, Fang Yuan saw Gao Wan curled up in a corner, seemingly asleep.

Once his body had warmed up, he left the hostel.

"You finally came out, lad. Surrender obediently and apologize to our young miss," Gao Wan said, standing up.

With a low shout, Fang Yuan leaped forward and aimed both fists at Gao Wan.

"Bastard, you're courting death!" Enraged, Gao Wan raised a brick-sized fist and punched toward Fang Yuan.

Sidestepping the fist, Fang Yuan slipped behind Gao Wan and struck a finger toward his waist.

Gao Wan blocked with his retracted arm, so Fang Yuan's strike hit his left forearm instead.

Fang Yuan's finger ached with numbness, as if hitting a steel plate.

"Gao Wan is at the peak of mortal martial arts; without Gu assistance, I cannot match him in pure strength." Fang Yuan's eyes flashed, and he immediately broke off his attack, retreating several steps to create distance.

Aside from the Moonlight Gu, Fang Yuan only had the body of a fifteen-year-old. In strength, agility, and endurance, he was no match for Gao Wan.

"Capturing this lad will be a great accomplishment—Young Miss is sure to reward me! An initial stage moonblade is only like a small dagger; as long as it misses my vitals, it's just a light wound."

His heart beating faster at the thought, Gao Wan reached out with shovel-like hands to grab Fang Yuan.

When extorting the students, Fang Yuan had used open palms to control the crowd, but against Gao Wan, he went all out.

He struck ruthlessly at vital points—eyes, throat, jaw, neck, and groin.

Every strike was devious and lethal, aimed entirely to kill.

As a mortal, Gao Wan could not protect his eyes and throat with essence, leaving his vitals vulnerable.

Moreover, Gao Wan did not dare unleash lethal moves against Fang Yuan.

With apprehension clashing against sheer killing intent, Fang Yuan thoroughly suppressed Gao Wan in combat.

# Chapter 35: Go Ahead and Scream!

Fang Yuan held the upper hand, but could not sustain it for long.

Exchanging blows, Fang Yuan was soon panting in exhaustion, while Gao Wan's steady breathing highlighted their vast difference in stamina.

"Lad, you can't beat me! Clan rules forbid using the Moonlight Gu in the hostel—you're doomed!" Gao Wan laughed maniacally.

Fang Yuan's mind remained ice-cold; tempered over five centuries, his resolve never wavered.

"Moonlight Gu!" he called mentally, activating primeval essence while leaping backward to create distance.

Seeing watery-blue light emit from Fang Yuan's palm, Gao Wan's face darkened. "Fighting with a Gu here breaks clan rules!"

"So what if I do?" Slicing his palm forward, a blue moonblade flew toward Gao Wan's face.

Gao Wan crossed his arms to shield his face and charged forward, intending to tank the strike.

The moonblade struck his arms. Blood burst out under the moonlight, and agonizing pain nearly made him faint.

Halting in horror, he found both arms slashed to the bone, muscle shredded and forearms fractured.

"Impossible! Only a Rank one middle stage moonblade could cut to the bone!" Gao Wan gasped in terror.

"This boy is bizarre!" Grievously injured and having lost his fighting spirit, Gao Wan decisively retreated.

"Can you escape?" Fang Yuan smiled coldly and gave chase, firing moonblades in rapid succession.

"Save me!" Gao Wan shrieked in horror as he fled, his cries carrying far beyond the hostel.

"What's going on? Someone's calling for help!" The shouts alerted nearby hostel guards.

"It's Lady Mo Yan's servant." The arriving guards halted upon seeing the pursuit.

"He's only a servant—no need for us to risk protecting him!"

Despairing at the sight, Gao Wan screamed, "We are all outsiders! You can't leave me to die!"

Fang Yuan caught up, declaring coldly, "Go ahead and scream. It won't save you."

With a flick of his hand, he fired two moonblades toward Gao Wan.

The next moment, Gao Wan's world spun—he saw his own feet, chest, back... and severed neck.

Decapitated, his head flew through the air as his body collapsed, blood soaking the grass.

The guards could not help but scream out. They had witnessed the entire process, and they felt a sense of extreme trepidation and terror rushing all over their body.

Staring coldly, Fang Yuan kicked the severed head away.

Fang Yuan frowned at the corpse. The bone-deep wounds exposed that he possessed middle stage primeval essence.

Once exposed, others would quickly deduce he had a Liquor worm, leading the clan straight to the Flower Wine Monk.

"Too many onlookers." With less than ten percent primeval essence left, he could not silence the dozen guards.

After brief consideration, Fang Yuan grabbed Gao Wan's ankle and dragged the corpse away.

"Young Master Fang Yuan, you can leave this to us," the guards said politely, mastering their fear.

"Give me the sabre," he said calmly, extending his hand.

The nearest guard instinctively handed over the sabre at his waist.

Taking the sabre, Fang Yuan walked on, leaving a dozen stunned guards staring after him.

The morning sun peeked over the mountain peak, illuminating the academy hostel.

In his left hand was a shiny sabre.

In his right hand, a headless corpse.

A trail of bright red blood dragged along the ground in his wake.

# Chapter 36: Gifting a disseminated corpse!

"Did you hear? Fang Yuan killed someone!" a student whispered to his classmate.

"Many guards saw it. Fang Yuan chased him down, ignored his pleas for mercy, and beheaded him on the spot!"

"Not only that—Fang Yuan dragged the headless corpse back to the hostel and minced it into paste!"

"Why would I lie? The Academy Elder already summoned Fang Yuan over it!"

"Who did Fang Yuan kill?"

"I heard it was a servant from the Mo branch."

"I saw it myself yesterday—Mo Yan brought a gang of servants to cause trouble for Fang Yuan."

"The Mo family? Mo Bei is in trouble now."

Several youths glanced toward Gu Yue Mo Bei.

Mo Bei sat pale-faced, having just learned that Fang Yuan had killed Gao Wan, a servant from his household.

Only the Academy Elder and Fang Yuan remained in the room.

The elder sat while Fang Yuan stood, their silence rendering the atmosphere suffocatingly tense.

The morning report of Fang Yuan's killing had left the elder both shocked and suspicious.

Guards had already reported that Mo Yan cornered Fang Yuan in the academy the previous night.

Yet Mo Yan had left empty-handed, and the servant she left behind not only failed to beat Fang Yuan, but was slain.

Yet Fang Yuan stood calm as ice, showing neither guilt nor panic, as serene as if he had never killed anyone.

The elder marveled at Fang Yuan's cold detachment while admiring his instincts as a natural-born battle fanatic.

"Fang Yuan, do you know why I summoned you?" the Academy Elder broke the silence, his voice deep and solemn.

"I do," Fang Yuan nodded. "I used the Moonlight Gu in the academy, breaking clan rules. As a first offense, the fine is thirty primeval stones."

The elder's face darkened. "Don't play dumb! How do you explain Gao Wan's death?"

Fang Yuan narrowed his eyes. "Gao Wan cornered me at my room last night and attempted murder. I acted purely in self-defense with the Moonlight Gu. Fortunately, I slew the traitor. I suspect he was a spy from another village, and implore the elders to investigate thoroughly!"

The Academy Elder frowned, momentarily at a loss for words.

With Gao Wan dead, Fang Yuan could claim whatever he liked. The death of a mortal outsider meant nothing to the elder, but the Mo family's reaction was another matter. Since Gao Wan was their servant and had died within the academy, the elder had to provide an explanation.

After a moment's thought, the elder stared at Fang Yuan. "Then how did you dispose of Gao Wan's corpse?"

Fang Yuan offered a cruel smile. "I diced the corpse into a wooden box and left it at the Mo family's back door at dawn."

"What?!" The elder gasped, nearly leaping from his seat in shock.

"Since it's done, dwelling on it is pointless. Leave—your punishment will be decided soon." Thoroughly vexed, the elder waved Fang Yuan away.

# Chapter 37: Both a compromise and a threat

"What did I instruct you? Look what you've done!" In the study, Gu Yue Mo Chen slammed the table in fury.

Mo Yan stood before him with head bowed, eyes filled with shock and rage upon learning Fang Yuan had killed Gao Wan.

"Grandpa, don't be angry. Gao Wan was merely an outsider servant. But Fang Yuan is too audacious—one must consider the master before beating the dog! Not only did he strike our dog, he beat it to death!" Mo Yan fumed.

Gu Yue Mo Chen scowled furiously. "You have the nerve to say that! Have your wings grown so stiff that you disregard my words entirely? You forgot everything I told you!"

Pointing out the window, he snapped, "Hmph, the servant's death is trivial, but your continued hostility toward Fang Yuan proves how shortsighted you are! Fights among juniors are their own business—elders must not interfere. Those are the rules! By making trouble for Fang Yuan, you broke them. I can't tell how many people are out there now, looking at this disgrace of our Mo family!"

"Grandpa, please don't harm your health with anger. It is my fault for burdening the family, and I will obey whatever you command. But I cannot swallow this—Fang Yuan is simply too despicable and shameless! He tricked me into letting him enter the academy, hid in the dormitory, and killed Gao Wan the moment I left. He is utterly shameless!" Mo Yan reported.

"Is that so?" Gu Yue Mo Chen frowned, eyes glinting as he heard this for the first time.

Suppressing his anger, he stroked his beard. "I knew Fang Yuan showed early brilliance with poetry, only to awaken with mere C-grade talent. It was difficult for him to have a good future and thus I gave up on recruiting him. But now it seems that it's slightly interesting."

After a pause, Gu Yue Mo Chen tapped the table and ordered, "Bring that box in."

"Grandpa, what is this?" Mo Yan asked, eyeing the wooden box doubtfully.

"Open it and see," Gu Yue Mo Chen said with a complex expression.

Mo Yan stepped forward, lifted the lid, and looked inside.

It was actually a pile of flesh and blood!

Only Gu Yue Mo Chen remained unfazed among the three present. Glancing calmly at the gory contents, he said slowly, "Fang Yuan left this at our back door this morning."

"It was really him?!" Mo Yan gasped in utter disbelief.

Shock quickly turned to rage. "How dare Fang Yuan do this! This is a blatant provocation against our Mo family! I will go ahead now and bring him here to question him for his crimes!" After she said this, she headed towards the exit.

Gu Yue Mo Chen stood up, pointing at her angrily. "All these years of training were in vain! You disappoint me greatly! Against a small Rank one initial stage Gu Master, not to mention you involving so many people, but even getting led by the nose by the other party. Now that you've let your rage get the better of you, at this point, do you still not understand the meaning behind Fang Yuan's actions?"

"What meaning?" Mo Yan was puzzled.

Mo Chen snorted, "If he wanted pure provocation, why leave it at our quiet back door instead of the crowded front entrance to blow the matter up?"

"He wants to reconcile? But if so, why not apologize in person instead of sending a box of minced flesh? It has to be a provocation!" Mo Yan protested.

Gu Yue Mo Chen shook his head, then nodded. "He offers reconciliation while simultaneously threatening us. Leaving the box at the back door shows intent to settle, but filling it with corpse parts is a clear threat."

"Look," the elder pointed. "This small box cannot hold an entire body—it contains only a portion. He wants to settle amicably. But if we pursue the matter, he will dump the rest of the corpse at our main entrance and blow the scandal wide open. The entire clan knows we broke the rules first. Worse, Mo Bei would look too weak to handle his own affairs without his family coddling him."

"A brilliant move," Mo Chen said admiringly. "With a single stroke, he balanced force and concession, securing both advance and retreat. If the Mo family's reputation is tarnished, what follows after will be the Chi family's attack as well as the assault from the clan leader's side."

Mo Yan was incredulous. "Grandpa, aren't you overestimating him? Can a fifteen-year-old really think this through?"

"Overestimating?" Mo Chen glared at her. "Years of easy living have blinded you with arrogance. Fang Yuan stayed unfazed in danger and tricked you to enter the academy. After you left, he killed Gao Wan immediately, this is his bravery and courage. Now he sent this box, clearly showing his wisdom and planning ability. Can you still say that I thought of him too highly?"

Mo Yan listened wide-eyed, stunned by his praise. Still indignant, she protested, "Grandpa, he's only a C-grade talent."

Gu Yue Mo Chen sighed heavily. "Indeed, only C-grade. To possess such cunning with mere C-grade talent is truly a pity. As long as his talent was higher, even if it was just a B grade, he would certainly become an influential member of our Gu Yue clan. What a pity, he is only a C grade."

"You caused this mess. How do you intend to resolve it?" Gu Yue Mo Chen asked, testing her.

After brief thought, Mo Yan replied coldly, "Gao Wan's death is trivial, and Fang Yuan's C-grade standing is negligible. What's important is maintaining my Mo family's reputation. To appease this matter, we might as well kill Gao Wan's entire family to show the entire clan our attitude to protect the rules and regulations."

Gu Yue Mo Chen said solemnly, "Since you caused this, you will be confined for seven days. Do not trouble Fang Yuan again. Gao Wan defied his superiors—a servant who dares to offend his master deserves death, so he should have been executed for his crimes! We will compensate Fang Yuan thirty primeval stones for failing to discipline our servant. Give Gao Wan's family fifty stones and expel them from the clan."

"Yes, Grandpa."

# Chapter 38: Demon walking in the light

"The sky's light rain is sleek and crisp; grass color seen from afar vanishes up close." Gazing out the window, Fang Yuan recited softly before turning his attention back to the inn.

At the nearest table sat an old mortal man and his grandson in modest clothing.

"Sigh, I can't even drink in peace." The grandfather sighed dotingly, patting the boy's head with a twig-like arm. "Then let me tell you how Ren Zu gave his heart to Hope Gu and escaped capture…"

Ren Zu escaped through hope, but eventually grew old. Without Strength and Wisdom, he could no longer hunt, and losing his teeth left him unable to chew wild fruits and vegetables.

Hope Gu told him, "Human, you must not die. If you die, your heart will be lost and I will lose my only home."

Hope Gu said, "There's always hope. As long as you catch a Longevity Gu, you can increase your lifespan."

Ren Zu had long heard of Longevity Gu, but waved helplessly. "Motionless, it cannot be detected, and in flight, it is faster than light. How can I catch it? It's too hard!"

Hope Gu shared a secret: "Human, never give up hope. In the northwest corner of this continent stands a huge mountain. In its cave lives a pair of round and square Gu. Subdue them, and you can catch any Gu in the world, even Longevity Gu!"

Braving countless dangers, Ren Zu found and climbed the mountain. At the summit cave, he used his last strength to crawl inside.

The cave was pitch-black. Ren Zu stumbled blindly through the dark, constantly bumping into unseen obstacles until he was bruised and bleeding all over.

Just as he was at a loss, two voices spoke from the darkness.

One said, "Human, you came to catch us? Turn back; even with Strength Gu, it would be impossible."

The other said, "Human, go back, we will spare your life. Even with Wisdom Gu, you might not find us."

Ren Zu lay panting on the ground. "Strength and Wisdom Gu left me long ago, and my lifespan is nearly spent. But as long as hope remains in my heart, I will not give up!"

After a moment, one Gu said, "I see, human. You gave your heart to Hope Gu, so you will never give up."

The other added, "Then we will give you a chance. Speak our names, and we will submit to you."

Ren Zu asked Hope Gu, but it did not know either.

With no alternative, Ren Zu guessed blindly. He called out countless names to no response, clearly guessing wrong.

Finally, on his last day of life, he uttered the word 'Regulation'.

A voice sighed in the darkness, "Human, I admire your perseverance. Having named me, I will obey your commands. But only alongside my brother can I help you catch every Gu in the world. Otherwise, my power alone cannot suffice. Give up; you are dying, so use this chance to view the world one last time."

Ren Zu shook his head with determination, using all his time to guess the other Gu's name.

But at this time, he unknowingly said the word 'Rule'.

Immediately, the darkness dissipated.

Two Gu appeared before him. Just as Hope Gu said, the cubic one was 'Regulations' and the spherical one was 'Rules'—together forming 'Rules and Regulations'.

Both Gu said, "We obey anyone who knows our names. Since you named us, we serve you. But remember never to let others learn our names. The more who know, the more masters we must serve. As the first to subdue us, state your wish."

Ren Zu was overjoyed. "Then I order you both, go and catch me a Longevity Gu."

Working together, Rules and Regulations Gu captured an eighty-year Longevity Gu.

Though Ren Zu was a hundred years old, consuming the Gu vanished his wrinkles and restored vigor and muscle to his frail limbs.

"Rules and Regulations…" Fang Yuan swirled his wine cup with a dark gaze, deeply moved.

Ren Zu's legend was renowned worldwide, and Fang Yuan had naturally heard it as well.

Yet legends were understood through the reader's wisdom. The grandfather and boy saw only a fable, but Fang Yuan grasped its deeper meaning.

Fang Yuan reflected, "Heaven and earth are a vast chessboard, and all lifeforms are pieces bound by rules. Water flows downward, hot air rises, and humans likewise follow their own rules and regulations."

"Everyone has their own motives and principles. In Gu Yue village, lowly servants and noble masters are part of these rules. Thus Shen Cui curries favor with the rich to escape servitude, while Gao Wan flattered his masters to borrow their authority."

"Every person, profession, and society has its rules. Only by understanding them can one see through the darkness, embrace the light, and navigate the world with ease."

Fang Yuan analyzed his situation with clarity. "To clan elder Gu Yue Mo Chen, the goal is protecting his branch's prosperity. Mo Yan broke the rules by provoking me, so to preserve family honor, Mo Chen will not harm me—he might even compensate me."

"If the influential Mo family risked their reputation to punish me, I could not resist. Yet Gu Yue Mo Chen is afraid. Not of breaking rules himself, but of others following suit. Elders interfering in junior disputes would escalate matters, ultimately threatening the entire village. He is only subconsciously protecting the rules."

"Gu Yue Mo Chen surely understood my veiled threat and compromise when I delivered the minced corpse. That was precisely my intent. The Mo family will not pursue Gao Wan's death. Of course, had my talent been B grade or higher, they would have felt threatened. Even at the cost of reputation, they would have suppressed me," Fang Yuan sneered inwardly.

Strength can be relied upon, but weakness can also be leveraged.

Though a pawn on the board, Fang Yuan understood the rules and held a player's mindset.

Five hundred years of past experience made Fang Yuan a master of rules and regulations.

The rule of darkness is darkness, and the rule of light is light.

Yet the reborn demon had stepped onto the path of light.

# Chapter 39: Toad Caravan Merchant

The Southern Border held a hundred thousand mountains, Qing Mao Mountain among them. Countless clan villages spanned these mountains, bound by bloodlines.

With a primitive economy, merchant caravans were vital. Only large groups of Gu Masters could muster the strength to traverse the perilous mountain routes.

Led by a Treasure Brass Toad, the caravan entered Gu Yue village. The orange-yellow toad stood two and a half meters tall, its back studded with thick warts like bronze gate nails.

A fat, pockmarked man sat cross-legged atop the toad, his eyes smiling slits as he cupped his fists to greet the villagers.

This was Jia Fu of the Jia clan, a Rank four Gu Master and caravan leader.

Jia Fu cupped his fists and sighed, "The road was rough. We encountered Secluded Blood Bats and lost several good men. Then mountain fog on Jue Bi Mountain halted our travel, causing considerable delay and keeping Brother Gu Yue waiting."

Arriving at Qing Mao Mountain at dawn, the caravan set up in Gu Yue village by afternoon. By dusk, temporary shops sprawled across the area. Colorful tents were pitched everywhere, packed with countless street stalls in between.

Fang Yuan moved along with the crowd, walking alone.

"Intimate Grass, high-quality Intimate Grass! Freshly picked, only two primeval stones per catty, very cheap…"

Hearing this, Fang Yuan paused and walked toward the voice.

Intimate Grass was supplementary Gu food, useful for combining with other foods to feed a Gu worm.

"The thirty-stone fine for killing Gao Wan was offset by the Mo family's thirty-stone compensation, so I lost nothing. Two recent extortions brought my total to 118 stones. But refining middle-stage essence and nurturing my aperture walls uses three stones daily. With Gu food, living expenses, and green bamboo wine, I have ninety-eight stones left."

Ever since he killed Gao Wan, his ruthless reputation intimidated the students. His extortions went smoothly, with scarcely anyone daring to resist.

Calculating his funds, Fang Yuan looked away and walked deeper into the temporary market.

It was not that Fang Yuan lacked money for Intimate Grass, but he lacked time.

"The Mudskin Toad should be in that shop. In my past life, a Gu Master struck it rich by gambling on the first night. I cannot lose big by chasing petty savings."

# Chapter 40: Toad Gu Slumbering Within the Purple Gold Rock

Observing his surroundings, Fang Yuan stopped before a grey tent.

Fang Yuan walked inside. Inside the tent, there were three rows of counters on a side. On the counter were pieces of amber or fossils. Some were as big as a palm, others were as big as a face.

In the Gu world, there were all types of Gu, coming with different shapes and sizes and all sorts of effects. Gu worms have their respective food to consume. Without food, they can only last a short amount of time before dying.

Without food, Gu worms could survive by entering self-sealed hibernation.

For instance, lacking Moon Orchid petals, Moonlight Gu could self-seal, falling into deep slumber to conserve strength. Its blue glow would fade, turning its crystal body into grey rock. Over time, the encrustation thickened into a boulder.

Since the rocks looked identical externally, only cutting them open revealed if a Gu lay inside.

"In a small gambling den like this, nine out of ten rocks are solid stone. Even with a Gu inside, most are dead. Yet striking a live Gu brings a massive windfall. If rare, it brings great success—or robbery and murder."

Fang Yuan was clear about this in his heart, being very familiar with the situation beyond these doors.

In his past life, he had worked as a caravan gambling clerk and later ran his own medium-sized rock gambling den. He had conned gamblers, but also misjudged rocks and let precious Gu slip away.

After surveying the entrance, Fang Yuan slowly walked to the left counter.

A nearby female Gu Master approached with a smile. "Young Master, what Gu do you need? Rocks on this counter cost ten primeval stones each. If you just want to test your luck, the right counter is only five stones each. For thrills, the middle counter sells premium rocks for twenty primeval stones each."

"Let me look around first." Nodding expressionlessly, Fang Yuan began examining the pile.

In his memory, the prize was at this very counter.

But it had been 500 years, it's been too long. Many things were vague to him already, especially when 500 years of memory was a huge capacity, so to be honest Fang Yuan could not remember distinctly.

He only vaguely recalled that tonight, a lucky person would spend ten primeval stones on a purple-gold fossil.

Opening it revealed a Mudskin Toad, which he sold on the spot for a small fortune in primeval stones.

Fang Yuan frowned after observing for a while.

Over twenty fossils here possessed a purple-gold luster. Which one hid the Mudskin Toad?

At ten stones each, Fang Yuan's ninety-eight primeval stones could buy at most nine rocks.

"I must save primeval stones to survive and feed my Gu worms." With basic reserves, he could buy at most seven fossils.

"This rock has starry purple gold specks, but it's flat as a pancake—definitely no mudskin toad inside."

"This piece has striking purple gold color, but it's only fist-sized. If a mudskin toad were inside, the rock should be at least thirty percent larger."

"This purple gold fossil is large, but completely smooth. A mudskin toad's skin is rough and uneven, so this isn't it..."

When Gu worms hibernated after self-sealing, they formed natural fossils undetectable by most methods. The few remaining methods were too crude, instantly killing the barely alive Gu worm within.

Thus, when choosing rocks, Gu Masters could only rely on guesswork, experience, luck, and occasionally instinct.

But Fang Yuan was different.

Firstly, knowing part of the answer beforehand narrowed his search to under thirty pieces.

Finding that single rock among twenty-odd pieces was still difficult, but backed by five hundred years of experience and knowledge, Fang Yuan observed for a while and selected the six best-fitting purple gold fossils.

He was eighty percent confident the mudskin toad hibernated within one of these six fossils!

# Chapter 41: Dissecting Rocks

"I want to purchase some rocks." Having picked his targets, Fang Yuan said to the female Gu Master.

The female Gu Master showed nothing on her face, smiling brightly as she asked softly, "Then which piece are you choosing?"

Fang Yuan pointed across the counter, "These six pieces."

Six rocks were placed in front of Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan handed sixty primeval stones to the female Gu Master.

"Young Master, do you want to open them on the spot? We provide free rock-opening service," the female Gu Master advised gently, casting seductive glances.

Waving off the female Gu Master, Fang Yuan replied, "Purple gold is my lucky color. I'll open them myself!"

Gripping a purple gold rock in his right palm, Fang Yuan closed his fingers and slowly rubbed its surface.

Watery blue light rippled as the purple gold rock shrank, rock shavings streaming from between Fang Yuan's fingers onto the tent carpet.

"This youngster isn't a good-for-nothing. What skill." Seeing this, the Gu Masters looked at Fang Yuan in a new light.

The blue light intensified as the fossil shrank to pearl size, finally crumbling into a small pile of powder on the carpet.

This was a solid rock, there was no Gu worm inside.

Calm and unaffected, Fang Yuan grabbed the second purple gold rock and ground it down, but it too was solid with no Gu worm inside.

The third piece was still the same.

He ground the fourth piece down to palm size before abruptly stopping.

"Oh? There's something!"

"The composition changed—it's not purple gold sediment, but ink-black."

"Young Master, be careful," the female Gu Master cautioned immediately. "Hibernating Gu worms are fragile—too much force will kill it."

Fang Yuan slowed his pace, rubbing intermittently and letting fine powder fall bit by bit, no longer as fluid as before.

Powder piled on the carpet until Fang Yuan had completely scraped away the black rock.

"Sigh, what a pity, it's a rock in a rock."

Fang Yuan smiled without replying and took out the fifth rock.

He ground carefully, and within ten breaths, the purple gold outer layer was rubbed away, revealing a rough yellow mud ball.

# Chapter 42: It really is a Gu?!

"It looks like one, but strangely, a mudball enclosed by purple gold rock should be compressed smooth. Why is it still uneven?" The surrounding Gu Masters were perplexed.

Looking at the mudball in his hands, Fang Yuan remained outwardly calm, but inwardly stirred.

He continued grinding. Under the watery blue light, sand and soil crumbs shed onto the pile of powder beside his leg.

As the yellow mudball shrank to palm size, someone barged into the tent: "Young lad, hold on! I, Jia Jin Sheng, will buy it!"

He appeared twenty to twenty-five years old, wearing a golden robe with a lace belt. On the belt hung a square jade piece inscribed with the character "One."

Yet his status was unique. The Gu Masters in the tent bowed, greeting, "Your subordinate greets Second Young Master."

"Then he owns this gambling den. But stepping in like this breaks the den's rules," the Gu Masters muttered.

"That's right, I run this shop. Little brother, gambling so young, aren't you afraid of being scolded by your family? I'll offer forty primeval stones for that mudball. What do you say? Forty primeval stones is plenty when there might be no Gu inside, but I'm in a good mood. Since it's your first time gambling, I'll return some of your capital so you don't lose everything."

"Forty primeval stones?" Fang Yuan raised an eyebrow and looked sideways at Jia Jin Sheng, sneering, "Are you trying to force a purchase on my fossil? That violates the gambling den's rules. Furthermore, on Qing Mao Mountain, do you dare bully a Gu Yue clansman before everyone?"

At Fang Yuan's words, clan pride stirred hostility in the Gu Masters, and their gazes toward Jia Jin Sheng turned distinctly unfriendly.

Seeing the Gu Masters ready to intervene, Jia Jin Sheng hurriedly waved his hands and changed his tune: "Little brother, you misunderstand! As the shopkeeper, how could I ruin my reputation by breaking my own rules? How could I do business in the future? Haha, I just found your mudball interesting. If you don't want to sell, that's fine, but don't blame me if nothing comes out of it."

Ignoring him, Fang Yuan turned and focused on grinding the mudball in his hands.

His movements were slow and meticulous, shedding only a trace of dry soil powder at a time. Beneath his fingers, a hibernating Gu worm gradually appeared in front of everyone's eyes.

"There really is a Gu!" A cold light flashed in Jia Jin Sheng's eyes amid bitter regret; he hated being taken advantage of above all else.

What he obtained, was a toad Gu.

Surrounded by admiration, envy, and exasperation, Fang Yuan calmly channeled primeval essence into the toad.

Gu worms newly extracted from fossils were extremely weak and lethargic, offering no resistance and allowing Gu Masters to refine them easily.

Awakened by Fang Yuan, the toad Gu slowly opened its eyes and let out a soft cry, its belly vibrating.

"It's the Mudskin Toad—damn it, it really is!" someone shouted in recognition.

"A Mudskin Toad! A rare Rank two Gu worth five hundred primeval stones. Damn it! Someone opened such a Gu in my shop—I've lost big time!" Pale-faced, Jia Jin Sheng glared at the toad with a burning urge to snatch it away.

"No, maybe this kid doesn't know its value. I can still suppress the price and buy it!" Hope flared again in Jia Jin Sheng's heart.

Fang Yuan calmly observed the Mudskin Toad, ignoring the crowd's shock and praise.

In a calm tone, he said to Jia Jin Sheng, "Mudskin Toad, Rank two Gu. It eats five hundred grams of fertile yellow soil each meal. It is rare and essential for refining the Treasure Brass Toad, with a market price of five hundred primeval stones. Jia Jin Sheng, do you want it?"

"You... actually know so much..." Jia Jin Sheng mumbled, struck speechless by shock.

Fang Yuan chuckled. "If you don't want it, that's fine. I'm sure others will be interested."

"Wait, I'll buy it! But can't you lower the price?" Jia Jin Sheng smiled bitterly.

Fang Yuan turned around and walked away.

Jia Jin Sheng hurried after him: "Don't go! I'll buy it!"

The deal was quickly sealed. Fang Yuan handed over the Mudskin Toad publicly and received five heavy pouches containing one hundred primeval stones each.

Having spent sixty of his ninety-eight primeval stones on fossils, Fang Yuan's wealth now surged to five hundred and thirty-eight stones.

Stowing the five bags into his robes, Fang Yuan took the final purple gold fossil and walked out.

"Young Master, aren't you opening that fossil?" the female Gu Master called out, blinking in surprise at his back.

Fang Yuan paid no heed, leaving the gambling den without looking back.

# Chapter 43: The final sixth purple gold rock

As Fang Yuan refined his primeval essence, he absorbed natural essence from primeval stones, quickly replenishing his dwindling aperture reserves.

Finally, his forty-five percent Primeval Sea was fully refined into middle stage primeval essence.

"Thanks to this middle stage primeval essence, I could open five rocks in the gambling den." Sitting cross-legged on his bed, Fang Yuan slowly opened his eyes.

It was currently late at night.

Leaving the gambling den, he bypassed the other shops and headed straight back to the academy.

Stowing the primeval stones, Fang Yuan took out the final purple gold fossil.

He had bought six fossils at the den, opening five on the spot and bringing the last one back.

His eyes gleaming, he activated the Moonlight Gu and slowly ground down the rock.

The purple gold fossil shrank under the blue ripples, finally grinding into nothingness and leaving only powder on the ground.

Although the final fossil held no Gu worm, Fang Yuan felt no disappointment; looking at the rock powder, a smile spread across his face.

Indeed, this pile of rock powder was his true motive for visiting the den—the Mudskin Toad had merely been a bonus.

He had opened the fossil privately, so no one else knew the outcome.

Now he could claim he obtained and subdued the Liquor worm from this purple gold fossil.

First, no one could prove what fossils contained—who was to say a Liquor worm could not hibernate in purple gold rock?

Second, he had eyewitnesses; opening the Mudskin Toad had left a deep impression on everyone present.

Third, even under relentless questioning, he could attribute everything to unfathomable luck. Even if people suspected the Flower Wine Monk's Liquor worm, they could not refute an excuse of pure luck.

Trying to conceal it forever was like wrapping fire in paper—eventually, it would be exposed.

To defuse the hidden threat of the Liquor worm, striking first was Fang Yuan's style.

Furthermore, he knew he would need to reveal the Liquor worm during his upcoming cultivation.

"A Rank one Gu like the Liquor worm is precious to Rank one Gu Masters, but no longer useful to Rank two Masters. If the Spring and Autumn Cicada were exposed, I would die horribly the very next moment."

"The Flower Wine Monk's legacy and the Mudskin Toad were the only two local opportunities in my memory. Now that I have both, I can focus on steady cultivation."

Despite five hundred primeval stones and jars of Green Bamboo Wine hidden under his bed, he still felt urgency and danger.

These five hundred-odd stones were his peak; Fang Yuan knew his expenses would only grow from here.

He could feel his classmates improving. In recent extortions, Gu Yue Mo Chen, Chi Cheng, and Fang Zheng had noticeably sharpened their martial skills—where one or two strikes once sufficed, he now needed five or six.

"In three or four more rounds, their skills will be fully honed. If they challenge me in turn, my stamina won't endure a gauntlet. Five hundred primeval stones seems like a lot, but spending four a day, it won't last long."

"Qing Mao Mountain holds no more opportunities, but nearby Bai Gu Mountain hides a righteous Rank four strength inheritance."

Sigh, it still boils down to the Flower Wine Monk's treasure being too little, only giving me a Liquor worm. Hmm… there is still that film image wall, maybe I can sell it to a certain merchant in the caravan…

Fang Yuan's eyelids grew heavy, and he soon fell asleep.

# Chapter 44: Monkey Wine, not yielding the opportunity of the Liquor worm

During the next afternoon's lunch break, Fang Yuan returned to the merchant district outside the village.

"Come taste fine wines from every village! We have over a hundred varieties: Lantern Grass Wine, lingering Nine Tune Wine, elegant Ancient Dragon Well, sweet-and-sour Flower Rock Tune, savory Hundred Spring Old Cellar, and rich, fragrant Intoxication of Three Autumns..." Beside a blue vat outside the tent, a shop assistant hawked enthusiastically.

A glint flashed in Fang Yuan's eyes; intrigued, he turned into the wine shop.

At the back of the tent stood a long counter manned by a Gu Master, with dozens of basket-sized crystal ladybugs clinging to the cloth walls behind him.

"Young Master, would you like to see the wine catalogue?" A shop assistant walked over.

Fang Yuan glanced at the catalogue and realized the wine here was pricier than green bamboo wine.

"I'll have a cup of monkey wine." Fang Yuan put down the catalogue.

Fang Yuan took a small sip; the fruit liquor was sweet, refreshing, and delicate on the palate.

Setting it aside, Fang Yuan summoned the Liquor worm with a thought.

The plump Liquor worm flashed through the air in an arc of white light and plopped into the cup.

The Liquor worm splashed joyfully as the monkey wine visibly vanished. Within a few breaths, the cup was drained dry.

"It's the Liquor worm!" the counter Gu Master shouted, eyes sparkling. A Rank one cultivator with D-grade talent, he worked in the caravan wine shop to sightsee and seek opportunities.

"Young Master, do you plan to sell this Liquor worm?" he asked eagerly, approaching with sincere eyes.

Fang Yuan firmly shook his head in refusal and stood to leave.

His motive was only to reveal the Liquor worm, never to sell it.

The sun gradually set, replaced imperceptibly by a crescent moon.

"The Liquor worm is only Rank one, but vital to a Rank one Gu Master. It is useless to Rank two or three masters who cannot refine their essence further with it. Thus it is normal that no one paid attention to this. However, taking the initiative to expose the matter of the Liquor worm cannot be rushed for a period of time. If I overdo it, it might end up letting the cat out of the bag."

Then Fang Yuan heard someone shout, "Come quickly! A dishonest merchant is selling fake Gu to our clansmen!"

Fang Yuan followed the crowd toward the commotion.

Outside the tent stood two people.

One was a young Rank two Gu Master, dressed in distinct Gu Yue clan attire.

The other was a familiar face—the gambling den owner, Jia Jin Sheng.

Holding up a black Gu worm, the young Gu Master shouted to the crowd, "Clansmen, this man sold me a fake Gu yesterday! Lying to me that it was a Black Boar Gu, and sold it to me for two hundred and fifty primeval stones. To think that when I got home to refine it, I realized that it was not a Black Boar Gu but simply an ordinary stinky fat worm!"

Jia Jin Sheng sneered. "Don't make false accusations. When did I ever claim it was a Black Boar Gu? What proof do you have?"

Enraged by the denial, the young Gu Master grabbed his wrist. "Cunning merchant, you dare deny it? Cheating a Gu Yue on Qing Mao Mountain itself—are you courting death?!"

"Let go!" Furious, Jia Jin Sheng slapped the hand away. "Find another target to extort! I'm not afraid of you—my brother is the Rank four Gu Master Jia Fu! What can you do to me?"

"You!" The young Gu Master glared wide-eyed, intimidated into inaction by the name of a Rank four Gu Master.

"Bah!" Jia Jin Sheng spat, looking up with a disdainful laugh. "You were the one trying to get a cheap deal. Did you think that you could buy one for just two hundred and fifty primeval stones? Dream on!"

"No wonder he's so arrogant on Qing Mao Mountain—he's Jia Fu's younger brother."

"I heard they're only half-brothers, but despite his Rank one cultivation, he acts unrestrained in the caravan thanks to that relationship."

"What exactly happened here?" a loud voice called out.

The chatter ceased as the crowd parted to form a narrow path.

A short, muscular, pot-bellied Gu Master in yellow robes walked in—caravan leader Jia Fu.

"Lord Jia Fu, my regards." Swallowing his fury, the young Gu Master paid his respects.

Jia Jin Sheng froze, his face paling as angry sparks flashed in his eyes at his brother's unexpected arrival.

Observing from afar, Fang Yuan caught this peculiar expression and pondered the situation.

# Chapter 45: Clear of the schemes, unknowingly trapped in the urn

"Young Gu Master, what seems to be the problem?" Jia Fu stepped into the crowd and asked amicably.

Flattered, the young Gu Master cupped his fists, gathered courage before his clansmen, and explained everything.

Hearing the full account, Jia Fu nodded, then turned to Jia Jin Sheng. "Little brother, is this true?"

Jia Jin Sheng turned his head away and snorted coldly, not looking at his brother.

Jia Fu pondered briefly, then cupped his fists to the youth. "I understand. My brother was wrong to sell a fake and cause you a loss. I sincerely apologize!"

"Lord Jia Fu!" The young Gu Master was stunned and protested modestly, "You are Rank four while I am merely Rank two—this is too great an honor!"

Jia Fu waved his hand. "Cultivation does not matter; I act impartially. A wrong is a wrong, and I apologize on behalf of the caravan. As for compensation, how about this, you lost two hundred and fifty primeval stones, so I will compensate double that amount to you on behalf of the Jia family."

He fulfilled his promise at once, an attendant handing five money bags to the youth before the crowd.

"A word of advice, however," Jia Fu added. "Though only Rank one, the Black Boar Gu permanently increases strength and is exceptionally rare. Every time one appears in the market, it would be bought immediately. The pricing is around six hundred primeval stones. Trying to get one with two hundred and fifty primeval stones is impractical."

"Not at all." Jia Fu smiled, cupping his fists to the crowd. "Our Jia family bases our business on honesty. My brother is young, foolish, and playful, but fundamentally kind. Please bear with him and take no offense."

"Hmph!" With an ugly expression, Jia Jin Sheng stomped into the tent and slipped out the back.

Fang Yuan looked at this silently, thinking in his heart, "It seems that the image wall at the Flower Wine Monk's place can be sold."

Jia Jin Sheng slammed the mushroom table as he howled.

Fortunately, the tavern and surrounding streets were packed with people, so Jia Jin Sheng's drunken antics drew little notice amidst the bustle.

Downing cup after cup to drown his sorrow, his back to the crowd, no one noticed tears streaming down his cheeks.

"Do you want to deal with your brother? I can help you," a voice said.

Startled, Jia Jin Sheng turned around to find someone had been sitting beside him all along.

"It's you!" He glared at Fang Yuan in irritation. "The lucky kid who got the Mudskin Toad! Are you here to mock me?"

Fang Yuan met his gaze with cool detachment. "I have a major deal. If you want better results and more assets, why not hear me out?"

Suspicious, Jia Jin Sheng sat up straight. "How do you know about the assets?"

"The Jia family's affairs are hardly top secret," Fang Yuan laughed coldly, recalling memories of his past life.

Discussing here was far safer than a secluded spot, avoiding the eavesdropping of Gu worms.

He beckoned to Jia Jin Sheng. "Lend me your ear."

Jia Jin Sheng snorted sullenly, but leaned his head in.

Listening to Fang Yuan, he frowned suspiciously. "This involves the three Qing Mao Mountain clans, and merchants detest local disputes. Hmph, did Jia Fu send you to frame me?"

Expecting his suspicion, Fang Yuan rose without explaining. "Then I'll just talk to your brother."

Jia Jin Sheng glared after him, losing patience as Fang Yuan left the tavern. Hurrying outside, he caught up. "Don't go, let's talk."

Hands behind his back, Fang Yuan looked at him sideways. "You're cornered unless you trust me. Believe in me and there's hope; otherwise you're doomed. Dare you take the gamble?"

Jia Jin Sheng grimaced. "Jia Fu is barely older; I have never acknowledged him as a brother! But you're right, I'll take the bet."

Fang Yuan said solemnly, "Two thousand primeval stones, no haggling."

Jia Jin Sheng laughed bitterly, "Too expensive, this trade involves high risk."

"Greater risk brings greater rewards," Fang Yuan said firmly. "Selling to those two clans will earn you far more."

Jia Jin Sheng nodded solemnly. "I believe that. The Bai clan has grown fast, and their A-grade genius Bai Ning Bing has a bright future. Qing Mao Mountain's situation is shifting. Your Gu Yue family's dominance is wavering, and if I sell this to the Bai family, I can at least earn twice as much!"

Hearing Jia Jin Sheng's understanding of the Qing Mao Mountain's situation, Fang Yuan could not help but evaluate him again, thinking: "This Jia Jin Sheng, he is still a merchant family member after all, not those useless second generations."

Jia Jin Sheng sighed. "Trap or not, I'll bite. Two thousand primeval stones it is, but I must see the goods first."

"Of course, follow me." Fang Yuan laughed, leading the way with the situation firmly in his grasp—Jia Jin Sheng was already trapped in the urn.

# Chapter 46: Don’t think too much when killing people

Guided by memory, Fang Yuan led Jia Jin Sheng to the mountain cavern.

Entering the narrowing stone fissure as darkness enveloped them, Jia Jin Sheng grew increasingly wary in the unfamiliar environment.

Finally, he could not resist asking, "Jia Fu has an honest, amicable reputation, while I cheat and coerce. Why deal with me instead of him?"

Fang Yuan's voice echoed through the fissure. "His cultivation is too high. After seeing the wall, he could simply bypass me and hand it to the Gu Yue clan head. I do not like giving the decision-making to others. Furthermore, I do not believe in integrity. The so-called prestigious reputation is just because the profits are small and are unable to incur his greed."

Jia Jin Sheng chuckled dryly, his suspicions largely dispelled. "That last point truly resonates with me."

The two finally got into the secret cave.

Spotting the image wall, Jia Jin Sheng laughed aloud, "Haha, I knew it, you didn't lie to me!"

Jia Jin Sheng watched the shifting images depicting the clash between the Flower Wine Monk and the fourth-generation leader.

Glancing away, he mocked Fang Yuan, "Your fourth-generation ancestor doesn't look all that strong, huh."

Fang Yuan replied, "The Gu Yue clan needed a hero, so he became one; soon the Bai family will need a villain, making him a degenerate. Hero or scum is merely public opinion."

"Well said!" Jia Jin Sheng laughed, surveying the cave.

Fixing his gaze on the Flower Wine Monk's corpse, he paused. "What a pity for a Rank five expert. You've gained plenty from him, haven't you?"

Fang Yuan shook his head. "After so long, most Gu died; I only got a Liquor worm."

Jia Jin Sheng didn't buy it. "Don't lie to me, brother. Once this deal concludes, we're partners; I won't leak anything. Tell me honestly, what did you gain?"

Jia Jin Sheng added, "At the very least, I know he had a Thousand Li Earthwolf spider—a massive Rank five steed Gu proficient at burrowing underground. The Flower Wine Monk was a demonic cultivator, and his ability to get about freely was mostly due to this Thousand Li Earthwolf spider, allowing him to escape from the righteous cultivators."

"Is that so?" Fang Yuan frowned, knowing little about the Flower Wine Monk.

Jia Jin Sheng said smugly, "I heard the legend when visiting your village last year, found it intriguing, and researched it back home.
