The Thousand Li Earthwolf spider and Flower Wine Monk were inseparable, and in my opinion, this cave should have been dug out by the spider. Otherwise, with the Qing Mao Mountain's rich and heavy soil, how can a cave like this form? Brother, you don't have to conceal it anymore. The Flower Wine Monk died here so there's definitely his Thousand Li Earthwolf spider here!"

Fang Yuan frowned deeply with growing unease. "Indeed, there are no other exits. That massive spider could never have squeezed through the fissure we used. However, there is a possibility that the Thousand Li Earthwolf was plotted against and killed by the 4th generation. Seeing that image wall, even when the Flower Wine Monk was fighting he did not summon the Thousand Li Earthwolf spider."

Just then, something unexpected occurred.

The endlessly playing image wall suddenly changed.

A gravely wounded, pale bald Gu Master appeared on the wall.

Slumped against the wall, his chest and limbs were deeply slashed, yet not a drop of blood flowed, as though he were drained completely dry.

"I am the Flower Wine Monk." The bald monk laughed madly. "Whoever you are, letting this play for nearly a hundred days proves your malice toward the Gu Yue clan. Very well, you shall be my successor! My entire inheritance is yours, but I have a condition. You must exterminate the Gu Yue clan for me. Murder the entire clan and leave no one alive!"

"Wait, is it inappropriate to inherit a demonic cultivator's power? No, strength has nothing to do with good or evil. The Flower Wine Monk wants his successor to destroy the Gu Yue clan, but do I really have to? He's already dead—I'll just take the inheritance and ignore the rest."

"This is a godsend. Even with my D grade talent, the Flower Wine Monk's inheritance might let me improve it. Those rare talent-raising Gu worms might be inside. If I inherit this fortune and reach Rank four or five, I could contest with Jia Fu!"

"Should I split the inheritance with him? No, kill him to protect the secret! I'll calm him down first, pretend we're splitting the treasure, lower his guard, then ambush and kill him here. This place is so hidden that nobody will ever know."

Having a plan, he squinted and revealed a fake smile.

He turned toward Fang Yuan, but before he could speak, two blue moonblades flew at him.

His pupils shrank to pinpricks; at this distance, he could not react in time!

The moonblade struck his neck, sending his head flying as fresh blood poured out like a fountain.

"Don't think so much when killing people." Fang Yuan glanced at the corpse and turned toward the image wall.

"To think there was such a twist. How interesting," he muttered, eyes glowing eerily.

# Chapter 47: Jia Jin Sheng, I actually did not want to kill you

The rain crashed down heavily.

Across Qing Mao Mountain, vast expanses of jade green spear bamboo stood tall against the wind and rain, their straight stalks pointing toward the sky.

In Gu Yue village, rows of tall-pillared houses endured the downpour. Outside, the caravan had already set out once again.

The heavy rain cleansed heaven and earth. The cobblestone roads around the village held up, but after five hundred meters, they turned into a narrow, muddy mountain path.

Fang Yuan stood alone on a hillside, watching the caravan snake like a colorful python along the muddy road into the dense forest.

"Ah, the heavens are sending their blessings…" Fang Yuan sighed lightly.

He held a butter yellow paper umbrella, quietly standing in the rain.

He had killed Jia Jin Sheng and cleaned the scene last night, but the sudden act left inevitable flaws. In the unventilated cave, the bloody stench could not easily disperse.

The rain cleansed the surroundings, reducing scent tracking risks and diluting the cave air with fresh vapor through the rock crack.

Watching the caravan disappear around a bend into the forest, Fang Yuan looked grim rather than relieved.

"Though Jia Jin Sheng was weak, his status was special. The caravan will soon notice his absence. When Jia Fu returns to investigate, the real challenge begins."

"The Jia family head placed Jia Jin Sheng and Jia Fu on the same caravan with deep intentions. In both cultivation and cunning, they are worlds apart. It also tests Jia Fu's nature: if he is too overbearing toward Jia Jin Sheng, how could the clan leadership be entrusted to him?"

"Jia Jin Sheng never understood his father's intentions. Having only scratched the surface of merchant cunning, he wasted his potential. What a pity for such a good pawn."

Seeing their dispute that night, Fang Yuan had grasped their dynamic and formed a plan.

Jia Jin Sheng was an ideal pawn: weak in cultivation yet high-ranking in the caravan, possessing some wits but little experience, making him easy to manipulate.

First, he could build a smuggling network through him, preparing to fence loot from future killings.

Second, he could secretly use the image wall to incite conflict among Qing Mao Mountain's three clans, causing civil war and reaping the rewards.

Third, Fang Yuan could infiltrate the Jia family, using their future family dispute and Gu fighting competition to seize massive rewards.

"My cultivation is still too low, restraining my actions. A pawn allows me to do things I cannot attempt myself, which is convenient and lowers my risk. If exposed, I could simply discard the pawn and stay safe myself."

"Clansmen are loyal and know the local situation, making them hard to manipulate. Only an outsider like Jia Jin Sheng could effectively serve my plans. Unfortunately, I didn't expect the Flower Wine Monk to leave behind an inheritance."

Having both would have been ideal, but faced with such treasure, Jia Jin Sheng could no longer be controlled and had to be discarded.

The Flower Wine Monk's inheritance disrupted his plans. On the image wall, blood writing had instructed him to destroy the wall to reveal the cave entrance.

Cleaning the murder scene took the entire night, leaving no time to break the wall.

"Killing Jia Jin Sheng in a rush will cause future problems; I am only temporarily safe. Though I got rid of the evidence, trouble is bound to come for me. In this case, I must change how I reveal the Liquor worm and avoid the cave behind the rock crack. I have to stay in the village to prepare for the coming investigation."

Umbrella in hand, Fang Yuan turned and walked through the rain toward the village.

"I can spend primeval stones to refine middle stage essence and nurture my aperture. Reaching middle stage will double my strength, letting me claim the inheritance with greater confidence."

A demonic cultivator's inheritance was not gentle like a righteous one; it often contained dangerous trials where failing meant paying with one's life.

Sighing inwardly, he thought, "Jia Jin Sheng, actually I… didn't want to kill you."

# Chapter 48: A little cute

It rained for four days before stopping.

Bang, bang, bang.

Punches and kicks echoed across the academy training grounds.

After ten-odd exchanges, Fang Yuan kicked Gu Yue Mo Bei in the abdomen, sending him stumbling back out of the arena circle.

Seeing this, the martial arts instructor immediately declared, "Gu Yue Mo Bei has exited the stage—Gu Yue Fang Yuan wins for the 33rd consecutive time!"

"Hmph, I lost to you again." Gu Yue Mo Bei gritted his teeth, glaring at Fang Yuan. "Don't be arrogant. One day I'll defeat you—I can feel that day nearing!"

Fang Yuan looked at him expressionlessly, eyelids drooping. "That kick caused internal bleeding. I advise you to treat it first."

"This small injury is nothing!" Gu Yue Mo Bei retorted, but suddenly gasped and vomited a mouthful of blood.

Pale-faced, this was the first time he had suffered such an injury; fear flickered in his eyes.

Two healing Gu Masters waiting outside rushed over immediately and carefully helped Gu Yue Mo Bei away.

"Next!" the instructor yelled, seeing no challengers step forward.

No one answered. The only ones brave enough to challenge Fang Yuan—Mo Bei, Chi Cheng, and Fang Zheng—were already beaten.

Silence fell as students stepped back. Seeing their fearful expressions, the instructor frowned.

He recalled the academy elder's words: "Fang Yuan is too dominant. Under his pressure, the students cannot raise their heads, and their courage will be extinguished. Our academy nurtures courageous tigers and wolves, not fearful sheep."

"What's wrong with you? Fang Yuan is only fifteen, your peer! He eats the same food and drinks the same water. He doesn't have three heads or six arms—he's no monster! Pluck up your courage and show the Gu Yue clan's pride!" the instructor yelled, trying to motivate them.

As a bystander, he saw clearly: after thirty-three consecutive fights without rest, Fang Yuan's stamina was depleted despite constantly regulating his breath.

Arms folded, Fang Yuan stared coldly; though standing in the center of the stage, he acted like a complete bystander.

Angry and helpless, the instructor chided, "Fang Yuan, you should be friendly with classmates—how can you strike with such vicious force? Be careful from now on. If you cause another classmate to vomit blood, I will declare your loss and evict you!"

"Instructor, you are wrong."

Fang Yuan snorted, meeting his gaze. "In practice fights, we must give our all to achieve the training's purpose. In real battle, will enemies be gentle and friendly?"

Fang Yuan sneered, "You set up this match with a twenty primeval stone prize. Without your encouragement, would these people have been hurt?"

"Bastard!" The instructor pointed at Fang Yuan. "Argue further, and even if you take first place, I will declare you the loser! You're uncooperative and dare argue with your teacher—you have no right to claim the twenty primeval stones!"

Fang Yuan laughed heartily. "A competition for a mere twenty primeval stones—do you think I give a damn?"

Saying so, he turned and walked out of the arena under the class's stunned gazes.

Fang Yuan still had several hundred primeval stones; stones were never his goal here.

"You!" Seeing Fang Yuan actually leave the stage, the instructor was struck speechless with shock and confusion.

The surrounding students stepped back, maintaining their distance. Fang Yuan stood alone on the grounds, a five-step radius around him clearing into a vacuum.

Anyone standing close enough would have heard Fang Yuan panting.

"My stamina is depleted," Fang Yuan sighed. Beneath his energetic facade, his body was shivering slightly.

Despite his rich past-life fighting experience, the other youngsters' combat skills had improved significantly, giving Fang Yuan a growing sense of pressure.

"Technique requires a solid physical foundation to show its value." Fang Yuan narrowed his eyes, seeing through the scheme.

After he killed Gao Wan, few dared challenge him. Oppressed by his dominance, most students offered no resistance and obediently handed over their primeval stones.

Fang Yuan's invincible image risked traumatizing the youngsters. The academy elder needed Fang Yuan to drive their improvement, not extinguish their fighting spirit.

For Fang Yuan, he needed this pressure to extort primeval stones with greater ease.

If defeated, the youngsters would attack together. Extortion was his main income; losing it would drain his reserves.

"What are you waiting for? Get up on stage! The first prize is twenty primeval stones—don't you want it?" the instructor yelled, recovering his senses.

Fang Yuan's lips curled up slightly: "Such boorish methods; this instructor is a little cute."

# Chapter 49: Not afraid of Fang Yuan breaking out of their grasp

Standing by the third-floor window, the academy elder watched everything that transpired in the arena.

"This lad is hard to catch. Proficient in academy rules and answering properly when questioned, he leaves no flaws to exploit. Finding a weakness to suppress his dominance is going to be difficult."

This lad called Fang Yuan had arrogance deep in his blood!

Fang Yuan showed no respect for teachers' authority, having publicly rebuked the martial arts instructor.

He had no fear in his heart, as if seeing through all the academy's tricks.

How could someone with no reverence for the clan ever be useful, even if nurtured?

"Such people must be suppressed! Otherwise, his apparent invincibility will cause others to lose reverence for their teachers, making the academy unmanageable."

"To suppress Fang Yuan's dominance, we can only wait until the other students reach Rank one middle stage."

From his rich experience, the elder calculated that Gu Yue Fang Zheng, Chi Cheng, and Mo Bei had the best chance to advance first.

Backed by elders with abundant primeval stones, an A grade and two B grades would easily advance first.

His experienced eye saw that though the students stood casually, they had already subtly split into three factions.

In one circle, peers of similar age crowded around Gu Yue Chi Cheng.

The second circle centered on Gu Yue Fang Zheng, supported by the clan leader faction's younger generation.

The third circle gathered around Gu Yue Mo Bei. Treated for his internal injury, he stood pale-faced while classmates asked after his condition.

"Once the three take the lead to reach middle stage, appointing them chairman and vice chairmen will solidify their authority and circles," the elder thought.

Fang Yuan stood alone in a corner, hands behind his back and eyelids lowered. Even amidst the heated competition, his expression remained completely unchanged.

"I needn't worry; Fang Yuan is young and can be shaped slowly," the academy elder thought deeply, his gaze shining.

"Next comes establishing the chairman and vice chairmen. A year later, groups will form with team leaders and assistants. Over time, interaction will bind him with kinship, friendship, and affection. I don't need to worry about him escaping clan control."

Small roles like chairman or vice chairman should not be underestimated; once assumed, they integrate students into the clan's administration.

Such a system exerts constant influence, offering privileges of power while making detachment difficult. With carrot and stick, who can break free?

# Chapter 50: Middle Stage!

In the courtyard, Gu Yue Mo Bei looked up, his eyes glistening under the moon.

"Little brother, I heard you got injured today." Behind him, his sister Gu Yue Mo Yan's voice resounded.

"You know what, sister?" In the moonlight, the fifteen-year-old's eyes shone brightly. "Although I failed, I heard Fang Yuan panting during the match. Back then, he easily beat me in two or three calm strikes. His gasping revealed his weakness. He is definitely not as strong as everyone thinks. One day, I will defeat him fair and square."

"I have a plan, sister. Over the next few days, I'm going to nurture my aperture walls. I'll break through from initial to middle stage, claim class chairman, and suppress Fang Yuan's dominance. I'll show him that talent is what truly matters in cultivation!"

In the Chi family, elder Gu Yue Chi Lian sat on a prayer mat facing his grandson, Gu Yue Chi Cheng.

Gu Yue Chi Lian reached out and pressed his palm against Chi Cheng's abdomen.

Anxious, Gu Yue Chi Cheng focused his mind into his aperture, suppressing the ripples in his Primeval Sea with all his concentration.

If Chi Cheng allowed his primeval essence to resist, the clash could severely damage his aperture.

After a while, Gu Yue Chi Lian ceased transmitting primeval essence and slowly withdrew his hand.

Gu Yue Chi Cheng exhaled in relief. "Thank you Grandfather, for transfusing primeval essence to nurture my aperture every three days."

Sweat dripping from his forehead, Chi Lian sighed. "With only C grade talent, relying on yourself would take too long. It would take far longer than a B or A grade, exposing your true talent. We must use this method."

"As long as you understand." The old man sighed. "This method has another side effect. My silver primeval essence is more effective, but it is still foreign to your aperture. Even when your light walls become water walls, they will remain tainted by my energy. The more foreign energy accumulates, the more impure your aperture becomes, stifling your talent and limiting your future growth."

Pleased, Gu Yue Chi Lian stroked his beard. "It is good you understand. Yet heaven always leaves a path of hope; all is not lost. If we find a Cleansing Water Gu, it can cleanse your aperture walls and flush out all foreign energy from your primeval sea, removing this side effect."

"I am also searching for a Liquor worm. It can refine primeval essence and raise it by a minor realm. That refined essence will be your own, not foreign. Nurturing your aperture this way carries no risk and yields far better results!"

"However, the Liquor worm is hard to find. Among Rank one Gu, the Liquor worm, Boar Gu, and Bookworm are extremely rare, snatched up immediately once they appear on the market. Of course, there are also Gu rumored to alter talent, but at my age I have only heard rumors of them," the old man explained.

Gu Yue Fang Zheng sat on his bed with eyes shut, holding a primeval stone in both hands.

With A grade talent, his primeval essence filled eighty percent of his aperture, and his recovery rate was twice Fang Yuan's!

With such godblessed advantage, he was already close to Rank one middle stage.

"Time flies when cultivating," Fang Zheng murmured, opening his hands as two piles of stone powder spilled onto the floor.

Opening it, he saw three primeval stones left inside.

Fang Zheng received three stones weekly from the academy as resources, but with Fang Yuan extorting one, he only had two left each week.

Determined to surpass Fang Yuan, Fang Zheng had repeatedly approached his uncle and aunt to beg for more primeval stones.

"Father and mother are already doing everything to support me. With only three stones left, using one a day will last three days."

"In three or four days, I'll definitely advance to middle stage! But how far along is my brother?" Fang Zheng glanced subconsciously toward the academy dormitories.

"I have A grade talent, while he only has C grade. You're no match for me this time, brother!"

In the darkness, he was not asleep, but sitting on his bed.

Cultivating earlier that day, he had already felt merely a step away from middle stage.

"I might as well not sleep tonight, I'll rush straight for middle stage!" His eyes shone with determination.

Closing his eyes, he directed his mind into his aperture.

His forty-four percent Primeval Sea had just been refined by the Liquor worm into pale green essence.

"Rise." With a thought, the peaceful green copper Primeval Sea began to stir.

Tides raced against each other, rushing toward the surrounding aperture walls.

A fraction of essence was consumed, turning into invisible energy that penetrated the white light wall.

As the waves battered relentlessly, the white wall suddenly shook, its mild glow flaring into eye-piercing brilliance.

Overjoyed at reaching the crucial moment, Fang Yuan mobilized all his primeval essence to surge against the walls.

The light wall vanished, replaced by a thicker spherical water wall with ripples of light flowing across it.

The Primeval Sea regained its peace, the aperture still having 20% primeval essence left.

"I advanced to middle stage!" Fang Yuan laughed heartily, opening his eyes.

Unknowingly, the night had passed, and it was already morning.

# Chapter 51: Let's see how you are going to explain this

"Fang Yuan didn't come to class today. Look, his seat is empty."

With a stiff expression, the academy elder explained the essentials of aperture nurturing while secretly eyeing Fang Yuan's vacant seat.

He sneered internally, "Yesterday I worried I couldn't find your weakness, yet today you skip class. You're just a boy after all."

"Men!" The academy elder knocked lightly on the table.

"Elder." The two guards standing outside the door entered.

The academy elder snorted, "Fang Yuan is skipping class under my nose. Go bring him from the hostel."

"Yes, elder." The guards left, and as they vanished past the door, the academy erupted into chatter.

"Brother, you are too arrogant to challenge the elder. You had this coming," Fang Zheng thought, sighing.

Bam, bam, bam!

The academy elder with a strict expression, slammed the desk thrice, "Silence, no talking in class!"

Before long, footsteps echoed at the door. Instantly, ears perked up and dozens of eyes looked toward the entrance.

Hearing the footsteps, the academy elder narrowed his eyes.

He had already decided how to deal with Fang Yuan: punish him to stand outside for three hours.

The footsteps drew near, and someone stopped outside the door.

Knock, knock, knock.

A student pulled the door open, exposing a gap.

Sunlight infiltrated from the gap. The student opening the door suddenly froze, shuddering suddenly.

"AHH!" The student shrieked and leaped backward, crashing into a desk and tumbling down.

"What's wrong?!" Astonished, everyone frowned deeply.

The door was slowly pushed opened by the person outside.

A left hand with dripping blood.

"It's Fang Yuan!" Someone screamed out loudly.

Before their eyes stood Fang Yuan, bathed in blood as if fresh from an intense battle.

His right hand dragged an unconscious person by the hair, whose severed left arm gushed blood from the shoulder.

"It's one of the guards sent to find Fang Yuan!" someone recognized.

The academy elder was stunned, having never anticipated such a scene!

Fang Yuan was far too bold. After killing Gao Wan, he had actually attacked the academy guards!

Enraged, the elder roared, "Fang Yuan! Explain this, or for attacking guards you will be jailed awaiting clan verdict!"

Only Fang Yuan remained calm, his dark eyes unreadable and emotionless as ever.

Fang Yuan let go, letting the guard's head fall into the puddle of blood with a plop.

Cupping his fists toward the academy elder, his calm voice broke the silence. "Academy Elder, I have something to report."

"Speak," the academy elder said with hands behind his back, looking down coldly at Fang Yuan.

Laughing coldly in his heart, he thought, "Fang Yuan, your offenses grow ever graver. Let's see how you explain yourself!"

# Chapter 52: You have no choice but to accept my explanation

Ignoring the onlookers, Fang Yuan looked sternly at the academy elder and pointed at the unconscious guard.

"Elder, these two guards had devious intentions! They barged into my room at the crucial moment of my breakthrough to middle stage. As everyone knows, a Gu Master cultivating must never be interrupted, especially during a breakthrough. A single distraction would fail the breakthrough and damage the aperture. Fortunately, when they barged in, I had already reached middle stage."

"However!" Before anyone could react, Fang Yuan continued, "These two showed no remorse. Instead, they threatened me, insulted our clan ancestors, and lied that disturbing my cultivation was the esteemed elder's order. Refusing to believe it, I resisted fiercely. Both possessed strong martial arts, and I had to fight for my life to defeat them."

"Because they were academy guards, I spared them, merely severing the arms of one and the legs of the other. Despite the blood loss, they are still alive. That is my report. Esteemed Elder, please uphold justice for me!" With that, he cupped his fists toward the academy elder.

"You say you advanced to Rank one middle stage?" The academy elder's voice was icy, his expression unforgiving. "Fang Yuan, this is no laughing matter. Admit your wrongdoing now, and I can consider this a first offense and deal with you leniently. But if you persist in lying to cover your tracks, know that lies are easily exposed."

Without explaining further, Fang Yuan laughed lightly. "Please inspect, Elder."

Even without being asked, the academy elder stepped forward.

Placing his hand on Fang Yuan's abdomen, he sent a trace of his spirit within, immediately viewing the aperture.

Within the aperture, there was no Gu.

The Spring Autumn Cicada had concealed itself. Far superior to a Rank three elder, a Rank six Gu could easily avoid detection.

As for the Liquor worm, Fang Yuan had left it behind in the dormitory's wine reserves.

With eyes shut, the academy elder saw a green copper Primeval Sea, calm as still water.

Every drop of primeval essence inside was the emerald green hue of middle stage.

The white aperture walls shimmered like liquid, with currents of water surging rapidly across the surface.

Water wall!

"He really advanced to middle stage—how is this possible?!" the academy elder shouted inwardly, straining to conceal his shock behind a calm expression.

After digesting this fact, he withdrew his hand and announced solemnly, "It is indeed middle stage."

The academy elder's verdict caused a huge commotion in the academy.

"This…!" Gu Yue Fang Zheng turned pale. His confidence from last night shattered, and unable to accept reality, he collapsed to the floor.

Gu Yue Mo Bei clenched his fists, and Gu Yue Chi Cheng hatefully gritted his teeth.

Utterly baffled, he dropped any thought of suppressing Fang Yuan and asked directly, "Fang Yuan, explain yourself—how did you manage to advance to middle stage?"

Fang Yuan smiled quietly, "Heaven rewards the diligent; through persistent training, this student advanced smoothly."

"Lies!"

"Is that so?" The academy elder replied neutrally, his gaze staring at Fang Yuan, emitting pressure.

Fang Yuan met the elder's gaze candidly, without a shred of fear.

"This Fang Yuan shows no fear or panic; he won't break under interrogation here. For a C grade to advance first, there must be a secret. Since he refuses to speak, I cannot force an interrogation as academy elder. I can only investigate privately."

Thinking this, the academy elder looked away, his cold expression softening.

Fang Yuan did not let the matter drop. "Your student was frightened, Esteemed Elder. How will you deal with these two guards? They have lost heavy blood and may die if not treated soon."

Observing the elder's shifting expressions, Fang Yuan laughed inwardly; the academy elder was in a difficult spot.

The two guards were mere outsiders whose lives were cheap as grass; normally, no one would care if they died.

Now, however, the academy elder had dispatched them. If they died, the elder would lose face!

Thus, the guards could not die—the academy elder had to save them.

What truly troubled the academy elder was deciding Fang Yuan's verdict.

"This Fang Yuan is far too cunning! Had he killed the guards, I could still penalize him. Despite mediocre talent, such calculating finesse is astonishing for a fifteen-year-old. Worst of all, I cannot retaliate. No wonder rumors claimed he possessed early-awakened wisdom!" The academy elder realized he had lost.

He had demanded an explanation, yet Fang Yuan's story was blatantly full of holes.

Handpicked by the elder himself, the guards would never be foolish enough to insult the Gu Yue ancestors.

Fang Yuan's accusation was deliberate—a brazen frame-up and a trap laid in plain sight!

An investigation would reveal the truth, but how could he handle it then?

If he spared Fang Yuan, the guards were wrongfully framed, undermining trust in the elder's impartiality.

If he punished Fang Yuan, he would appear jealous and oppressive toward clan talent! Punishing a promising junior over two outside servants would trigger outrage among clansmen.

Thus, the best resolution was to turn a blind eye, discard the two guards as having made a grave mistake, and praise Fang Yuan.

Finally, the academy elder reached out and patted Fang Yuan's shoulder.

"Good lad." His face dark as still water, the academy elder forced the words out.

"It is all thanks to the academy's nurturing," Fang Yuan replied plainly.

The corner of the academy elder's eye twitched.

# Chapter 53: Fang Yuan, you are appointed as class chairman

Everything was going according to Fang Yuan's calculations.

Thereafter, the elder ordered a healing Gu Master to treat the guards.

Though saved, the heavily injured guards were nearly crippled and subsequently dismissed from the academy.

Fang Yuan received no punishment, only thorough praise.

Days passed by.

Soon, the second youngster to breakthrough to middle stage emerged.

It was Gu Yue Mo Bei.

Just three hours later, Gu Yue Fang Zheng advanced as well.

He had been held back by a lack of primeval stones, as well as the lingering trauma from Fang Yuan.

The third was Gu Yue Chi Cheng.

Though Gu Yue Chi Lian infused him with primeval essence, the risky method worked only once every three days and had limited effectiveness. For a C grade talent, placing third was already an accomplishment.

On the fifth day, the academy elder distributed subsidies once again.

"Gu Yue Fang Yuan." Standing at the front, he called Fang Yuan's name first.

Fang Yuan stood up and walked forward with a calm expression.

"Gu Yue Fang Yuan, as the first in this class to reach Rank one middle stage, this is your reward." He handed over a blue-and-white money bag.

Fang Yuan took the money bag and peeked inside in full view.

"Rest assured, there are thirty primeval stones inside; the academy would not shortchange you," the academy elder smiled.

Fang Yuan ignored the elder. Trusting only himself, he counted carefully, and confirming all thirty primeval stones were present, slipped the bag into his bosom pocket.

"Just as I thought. A C grade rushing to middle stage without backing must have expended massive primeval stones and run tight on funds. As long as he needs primeval stones, he cannot escape the clan's control. Once inside the system, he won't be able to keep his secret—eventually, he will confess."

The academy elder continued, "Fang Yuan, as the first to reach Rank one middle stage, academy rules award you thirty primeval stones, priority in choosing your second Gu, and appointment as class chairman!"

"Damn it." Gu Yue Mo Bei gritted his teeth, feeling indignant.

The one most affected was Fang Yuan's brother, Gu Yue Fang Zheng.

Pale and troubled, gloom hung over him. "Students must bow to the chairman and vice chairmen. I will surely be a vice chairman, but that means bowing to my brother whenever we meet."

"Hold on."

But at this moment, Fang Yuan spoke.

Smiling faintly at the elder, he said slowly, "Esteemed elder, your student lacks talent and wisdom to serve as chairman. This role should go to someone more capable."

"What? You refuse? The chairman receives ten primeval stones each time—are you sure you want to reject it?" The academy elder frowned deeply. In decades of teaching, no one had ever turned down the position!

He had considered this thoroughly; appointing Fang Yuan class chairman had clear advantages.

As chairman, Fang Yuan would be bound to the clan system, required to fulfill duties alongside his perks.

At the very least, a class chairman could no longer extort his classmates.

Looking at the stunned elder, Fang Yuan asked in return, "Do academy rules mandate that first place must become chairman? Is the position compulsory?"

With a cold expression, the academy elder replied, "Of course there are no such rules."

Fang Yuan laughed, "Thank you elder for your understanding."

With that, he cupped his fists and returned to his seat.

Witnessing this, the students erupted into a commotion.

"Fang Yuan gave up the position—does that mean I'm chairman?!" Happiness came so suddenly that second-place Mo Bei could barely process it.

"Brother..." Fang Zheng stared wide-eyed at Fang Yuan in disappointment.

The academy elder's face turned extraordinarily dark.

… The giving out of subsidies ended.

Taking second place, Mo Bei became chairman, while Fang Zheng and Chi Cheng were appointed vice chairmen.

The class chairman was given ten primeval stones. The vice chairmen got five.

# Chapter 54: But I am the class chairman!

The forest around the academy glistened as if coated in oil. A gentle breeze blew, and the students emerged from the classroom with newly distributed primeval stones in their bosoms, relaxed and carefree.

"I can't fathom what Fang Yuan was thinking, giving up the role of class chairman!"

"Good day class chairman."

"Greetings class chairman."

Gu Yue Mo Bei swaggered along, every passing student bowing to greet him without exception.

Meanwhile, the other vice chairman, Gu Yue Fang Zheng, felt miserable, his expression thoroughly unsightly.

"Brother, you!" He stared wide-eyed in disbelief at the academy gate, where a lone figure stood.

"Old rules: one primeval stone per person." Fang Yuan stood with folded arms, his tone flat.

Fang Zheng stammered for a moment before managing, "Brother, but I'm the vice chairman now!"

"That's true." Fang Yuan nodded indifferently. "The vice chairman receives an allowance of five pieces each time, so you should hand over three instead."

Fang Zheng was flabbergasted, momentarily struck speechless.

A group of youths crowded around Gu Yue Mo Bei as they approached.

Seeing Fang Yuan blocking the gate, Gu Yue Mo Bei pointed furiously at him. "Fang Yuan! You still dare hold us back?! I am the chairman now—as a regular student, you should bow and greet me!"

What answered him next was Fang Yuan's fist.

Caught off guard, Gu Yue Mo Bei stumbled back several steps in disbelief. "You hit me? You actually dared to hit me?! I am the class chairman!"

What answered him again was still Fang Yuan's fists.

Bang, bang, bang.

After several exchanges, Fang Yuan knocked Gu Yue Mo Bei to the ground, unconscious.

After subduing Gu Yue Mo Bei, Fang Yuan dealt with Fang Zheng and Chi Cheng as well.

The other youths realized nothing had changed: Fang Yuan was the same as ever, and the extortion would continue as always.

"Hand over one primeval stone each—vice chairmen three pieces, class chairman eight pieces," Fang Yuan announced.

Sighing, the youths obediently produced their primeval stones.

Bang!

The academy elder slapped the table fiercely, feeling extremely furious.

"Fang Yuan is utterly absurd! What is he trying to do? Extorting eight pieces from the class chairman and three from the vice chairmen! How does that leave any difference between them and the normal students?!" The academy elder tried to suppress his voice, but it boiled with fury.

Rejecting the class chairmanship meant refusing integration into the clan system. Strictly speaking, it was a form of betrayal against the clan, infuriating the academy elder.

Extending his reach to extort the chairmen directly crossed the elder's bottom line. This shakedown would completely undermine the prestige of both positions.

If so, the clan head would be the first to punish him, followed closely by Gu Yue Chi Lian and Gu Yue Mo Chen.

The clan head pinned his hopes on Gu Yue Fang Zheng, the sole A grade talent in three years, needing a tenacious, independent genius rather than a coddled, delicate flower.

Likewise, Chi Lian and Mo Chen placed high hopes on their grandsons, expecting them to grow through setbacks and adversity.

If the elder intervened to punish Fang Yuan, rumors would spread that the Mo and Chi successors could not defeat Fang Yuan without their elders' help.

The elder did not fear Fang Yuan, but worried his intervention would invite pressure from the clan head and the Mo and Chi factions—who comprised the Gu Yue high authority. A mere academy elder could never endure that.

"This all traces back to Fang Yuan's secret. What did he rely on to reach middle stage?" Suppressing his fury, the elder looked down at the three investigation reports on his table.

The first report detailed Fang Yuan's family background.

Fang Yuan came from a good family with a clean, spotless background. Both parents had passed away, and his uncle and aunt had taken him in. They did not get along, and Fang Yuan had stayed in the academy dormitory ever since starting school.

The second report was about Fang Yuan's life records.

Precocious in childhood, clansmen had predicted him to be an A grade talent, only for the Awakening Ceremony to reveal a C grade, greatly disappointing the clan.

The third report was on Fang Yuan's recent trail.

His daily routine was simple and rigid: attending academy lessons by day and sleeping in the dormitory by night. He cultivated assiduously every night to nurture his aperture, occasionally visiting the village's only inn for better meals and wine.

He had a special fondness for green bamboo wine, keeping dozens of jars beneath his dormitory bed.

"Orphaned early and estranged from his uncle and aunt... No wonder this brat feels no belonging to the clan. His simple life and tireless cultivation show an unyielding drive to prove himself to the clan! That explains why he retaliated so fiercely against my suppression..."

Shaking the reports in hand, the elder frowned. "Though detailed, these reveal nothing about his advancement secret. Days have passed—these investigators are useless!"

Boom, boom, boom.

At this time, knocking sounds were heard on the door.

"Come inside," the academy elder said.

The door opened.

It was clan head Gu Yue Bo's personal guard. "The clan leader orders the esteemed elder to the main pavilion immediately to discuss an urgent matter."

"What is the matter?" The academy elder rose, sensing the severity of the issue from the guard's tone and expression.

"The Rank four Gu Master Lord Jia Fu has returned, and his brother Jia Jin Sheng is missing!" the guard answered.

"Hiss…" The academy elder instantly drew a mouthful of cold air.

# Chapter 55: Exactly the words I wanted to hear!

Stepping into the clan head pavilion's conference hall, the academy elder sensed the solemn, tense atmosphere.

Clan leader Gu Yue Bo occupied the main seat. The short, stout Jia Fu stood in the center with five or six followers, striking an accusatory stance.

Rubbing his temples in distress, gray-haired Gu Yue Bo said, "The academy is under your supervision, so I'll be direct: where is Gu Yue Fang Yuan?"

The clan leader sighed, "Brother Jia suspects his brother Jia Jin Sheng's disappearance is connected to Fang Yuan. Bring him here immediately."

"Reporting to clan leader, Fang Yuan is here." The academy elder promptly ushered Fang Yuan into the hall.

"Gu Yue Fang Yuan pays respects to the clan leader, Lord Jia Fu, and the clan elders," Fang Yuan said impassively.

"Is it him?" Jia Fu eyed Fang Yuan coldly, asking an accompanying female Gu Master.

She was the female Gu Master who had sold Fang Yuan the purple gold rock at the gambling den.

Pointing straight at Fang Yuan, the female Gu Master affirmed confidently, "It's him! No mistake."

Displeased, the Gu Yue clan leader thought, "Losing your brother was your own blunder. Coming here to accuse us—do you think the Gu Yue clan can be bullied so easily?"

Instead of questioning Fang Yuan, he sharply addressed the female Gu Master, "Did you look closely? Fang Yuan has an identical twin brother—are you certain it was him?"

Jia Fu stepped forward to shield her, cupping his fists. "Brother Gu Yue, I have always respected your clan, especially the first and fourth generation leaders—both Rank five powerhouses. Brother Gu Yue, as clan leader, I trust you will act impartially. Please interrogate Fang Yuan."

"Fang Yuan, don't be nervous," the Gu Yue clan leader smiled benevolently, asking gently, "Do you know anything about that Jia Jin Sheng?"

"Who is Jia Jin Sheng?" Fang Yuan looked up and said calmly.

"He's lying!" the gambling den's female Gu Master shrieked as Fang Yuan finished.

The female Gu Master pointed agitatedly at Fang Yuan, "It's him! At our gambling den, he bought six purple gold rocks, and the fifth held a mudskin toad. Young Master Jia offered five hundred primeval stones to buy it. This left a huge impression on me; I won't forget it even after years. The other Gu Masters at the gambling den saw it too."

Fang Yuan nodded with feigned nervousness and sudden realization. "Oh, him. If he's Jia Jin Sheng, then I know him. But after the gambling den, I never saw him again."

"He's lying again!" This time, a male Gu Master called out publicly.

"Esteemed sirs, I work at the wine shop." Cupping his fists, the man pointed at Fang Yuan. "That night, I clearly saw him sitting with Young Master Jia Jin Sheng, secretly conversing for a long time!"

"How can that be!" Fang Yuan looked frantic, hurriedly saying, "The wine shop was crowded, how could you recognize me so easily? You must be mistaken!"

Raising his eyebrows smugly, he asked, "Remember? You ordered monkey wine at our shop, drank only a mouthful, then summoned a Liquor worm and fed it the rest. Seeing the Liquor worm, I excitedly tried to buy it, but you refused and walked away immediately!"

"Good, exactly what I wanted to hear," Fang Yuan sneered internally, though feigning shock, he subconsciously stepped back.

"Liquor worm!" Behind Fang Yuan, the academy elder's eyes shone.

Quickly frowning again, he asked, "Fang Yuan, where did you get that Liquor worm?"

Fang Yuan clenched his fists, gritting his teeth, but refusing to answer.

"How could it be his? I obtained the Liquor worm myself! I opened it!" Fang Yuan raised his head, shouting indignantly as if unable to bear such slander.

# Chapter 56: Dispelling suspicion

"You opened it?" Gu Yue Bo frowned deeply.

"That's right, at this point, I shall not hide it any further!" Feigning desperate urgency, Fang Yuan said, "I bought six purple gold rocks at the gambling den, but lacking enough primeval essence, I only opened five on the spot. I opened the last one back at the hostel and found a Liquor worm inside. Overjoyed, knowing from prior research that a rare Liquor worm could supplement my poor talent, I refined it on the spot."

"So what?" Fang Yuan replied matter-of-factly, pointing at the female Gu Master. "She can be my witness!"

Pressured by everyone's gaze, the female Gu Master admitted, "It's true. Fang Yuan bought six rocks and found the mudskin toad in the fifth. He took the sixth away, so I don't know what he opened."

Fang Yuan continued, "After refining the Liquor worm, I visited the shops the next day and bought monkey wine in the afternoon. That night, I returned and saw Jia Jin Sheng scamming a clansman, selling a smelly fart fat worm as a Black Boar Gu before Sir Jia Fu intervened."

"When I went to the wine shop again, I saw Jia Jin Sheng drinking in a foul mood. Thrilled with my new Liquor worm, I asked him how many primeval stones it was worth. Upon hearing of it, Jia Jin Sheng tried to force a purchase. I refused, having only wanted to know its value, not sell it. Even if I did sell it, that would only be after reaching Rank two, so I left immediately."

Under the pressuring gazes, Jia Fu coughed, his eyes gleaming. "Then did my brother chase after you?"

Fang Yuan nodded, half-lying, "He chased me, offering fifty extra primeval stones. When I refused, he grew furious, insulted our Gu Yue clan, told me to watch out, and stormed off. I never saw him again."

"Speak, was Jia Jin Sheng killed by you!" Thinking of this, Jia Fu interrogated fiercely, attempting to pressurize Fang Yuan with his aura.

"Brother Jia, pardon my interruption," the clan leader said. "Jia Jin Sheng has been missing for days, so hope is bleak. The culprit must have left traces. Have you found anything?"

Jia Fu glared at Fang Yuan, then looked up with a sigh. "I understand! Had there been traces, I wouldn't have come searching here. The culprit is clearly experienced, vicious, and meticulous. Truthfully, all clues are dead ends, and heavy rain on the day we left likely washed away any blood traces."

Gu Yue Bo smiled faintly. "Brother Jia, I heard your family has a tracking Gu called the Underworld Path Butterfly. It marks Gu worms with a permanent, colorless, odorless soul fragrance. Your family members' Gu all carry this scent. By following it with the Underworld Path Butterfly, you could locate his Gu worms and find your brother."

Jia Fu solemnly said, "I used the Underworld Path Butterfly long ago, but there was no effect. I'm sure you've heard that once the Gu worm dies, the fragrance dissipates. Evidently the murderer killed all of the Gu worms in my brother's possession."

Gu Yue Bo mused, "How strange. The killer neither took the Gu worms nor extorted primeval stones. What motive could there be to kill a mere Rank one Gu Master?"

Following their gaze, Fang Yuan suddenly remarked to Jia Fu, "Maybe you killed him. Your family is dividing its assets; with one brother dead, wouldn't you get a larger inheritance?"

"The Jia family's internal strife!" an elder muttered with sudden inspiration.

The Jia clan leader planned to divide his assets and pass down leadership, sparking fierce competition among his children—especially Jia Fu and Jia Gui, both Rank four Gu Masters with their own factions.

If Jia Gui acted from the shadows, however, everything made sense.

The culprit's seamless actions eliminated all clues, demonstrating vast experience. How could a fifteen-year-old like Fang Yuan pull this off?

"I understand everything now." Standing behind Fang Yuan, the academy elder's eyes shone. "Fang Yuan was both lucky and unfortunate to meet Jia Jin Sheng at the very end. A novice student could never erase all evidence. If he were so scheming, he wouldn't look so stubbornly indignant. He only denied it earlier to conceal the Liquor worm."

"Clearing suspicion is only the first step; what comes next is crucial." In full control as everything went according to plan, Fang Yuan sighed inwardly and looked at Jia Fu.

# Chapter 57: A gentleman’s lie

"Even if Jia Gui is the mastermind, accusing him without evidence will only make Father suspect I'm framing him!" Jia Fu thought in rage.

"If Fang Yuan becomes the scapegoat, I can weather this crisis and repay Jia Gui twice over later," Jia Fu plotted.

Raising his voice, he interrogated, "Fang Yuan, how do you prove you didn't harm Jia Jin Sheng?"

"A witness? Of course I have one." Fang Yuan smiled inwardly, though he feigned speechless shock.

Looking indignant, Fang Yuan finally gritted his teeth and said helplessly, "No."

"Haha. Then you are —" Jia Fu was about to declare his verdict but at that point.

"Halt!" The academy elder stepped solemnly in front of Fang Yuan. "Of course he has a witness—me!"

Encouraged by Gu Yue Bo's gaze, he raised his head. "When Fang Yuan broke through first, I had men investigate him. His daily activities were all recorded; he had no time to harm Jia Jin Sheng."

"Just like that..." Fang Yuan hid behind the elder, a smirk curling unobserved on his lips.

Determined to prevail, Jia Fu gritted his teeth. "In that case, let me use the Footprint Gu. It will reveal his last thirty thousand footprints on the ground."

"Go ahead and test!" Fang Yuan sneered coldly, stepping forward with his head lowered.

Confident and prepared, he had deliberately stayed within the village these past days without visiting the secret cave.

Jia Fu activated the Gu, revealing glowing tracks along the floor. The tracks looped between the head pavilion, academy hostel, and academy, extending only as far as the village inn.

The evidence was clear: having inspected the tracks, everyone saw Fang Yuan was completely innocent.

Jia Fu sighed, taking out a small jade box.

Jia Fu shook his head. "I obtained this Bamboo Gentleman through rock gambling, but it was only half-extracted. As everyone knows, this Gu feeds on honesty and inherently detects lies. Only an honest gentleman who has never lied can refine and nourish it."

"Store the Bamboo Gentleman in your aperture to answer my questions. If it changes color, you are lying!"

"No problem." Without hesitation, Fang Yuan opened the jade and followed Jia Fu's instructions.

Inside his aperture, the Bamboo Gentleman cast a faint green glow across the Primeval Sea.

With a thought, Fang Yuan awakened the Spring Autumn Cicada, releasing a trace of its aura.

The Bamboo Gentleman dimmed, curling up and trembling in fear, stripped of any ability to detect lies.

Jia Fu began interrogating, his first question direct: "Fang Yuan, did you harm my brother Jia Jin Sheng?"

"No!" Fang Yuan affirmed.

Jia Fu asked again, "Did you say anything dishonest to us earlier?"

"Alright, take out the Bamboo Gentleman," Jia Fu instructed after finishing the three questions.

Fang Yuan retrieved the Bamboo Gentleman; it remained emerald green, completely unchanged.

Jia Fu said solemnly, "My investigation has yielded nothing. Upon returning, I shall hire divine investigator Tie Xue Leng! Farewell."

Cupping his fists to Gu Yue Bo, he departed with brisk elegance.

Waving the clan elders away, he added, "Academy elder, please stay behind."

Without breaking a sweat, Fang Yuan stepped out of the pavilion safe and sound.

# Chapter 58: The clan does not only have rules and regulations

Clan leader Gu Yue Bo began his story on Ren Zu.

After subduing Rules and Regulation Gu, Ren Zu first ordered them to capture a longevity Gu.

He gave his second command: "Then capture all other myriad Gu in the world for me."

Rules Gu expanded into a circle encompassing the universe, while Regulation Gu became a square covering the world.

When they shrank back to Ren Zu, every Gu in the world except the longevity Gu was captured.

Yet as soon as he opened the net, swarms of Gu escaped, undoing all their hard work.

When Ren Zu closed the net, only five Gu were left.

Rules and Regulation told him, "The world holds myriad Gu with diverse abilities. We only capture them; making them obey is up to you."

They added, "For your second command, we add another rule: from now on, we will only catch one Gu at a time."

He asked Wisdom Gu, "Wisdom Gu, you left me before; do you regret it? Submit now, and I will grant you freedom."

Wisdom Gu replied, "You cannot subdue me, for I only yield to those wiser than me. But we can strike a deal."

Anxious, Wisdom Gu reluctantly conceded, "Alright, you win. Tell me how you captured me, and I will admit defeat without taking anything, remaining at your disposal."

Overjoyed, Ren Zu blurted out before Rules and Regulation Gu could stop him, "I used Rules and Regulation Gu to capture you."

Wisdom Gu laughed. "So they are called Rules and Regulation! Now that I know your names, you can never capture me again!"

With that, it turned into a flash of light and vanished into thin air.

Only then did Ren Zu realize he was tricked, grieving the lost chance to capture Wisdom Gu.

Concluding his story, Gu Yue Bo looked meaningfully at the academy elder.

Understanding dawned, and the elder bowed respectfully: "Clan leader, are you comparing Fang Yuan to Wisdom Gu and the clan to Ren Zu? Though Ren Zu trapped Wisdom Gu with Rules and Regulation, it eventually escaped."

"Fang Yuan has shown flashes of brilliance. Let his extortions continue; use him to hone unpolished jades like Fang Zheng, Mo Bei, and Chi Cheng. This brings many benefits—at minimum, this class possesses the strongest combat skills of any recent year."

The elder worried, "Excessive hurdles might shatter the jade. Under Fang Yuan's suppression with the Liquor worm, no peer can overturn the situation."

"Then let them fail!" Gu Yue Bo snorted with callous authority. "What is a minor setback? Is it worse than death? If they lack drive despite clan elder support, they aren't true jade and aren't worth nurturing. New students enter every year; if this class fails, there is always the next. As for Gu Yue Fang Zheng, I will tutor him secretly starting tomorrow night."

"Fang Yuan is childish; concealing the Liquor worm proves he remains immature. Do not overestimate him. Comparing him to Wisdom Gu gives him too much credit. At most, he has petty cleverness but lacks true wisdom. Quietly concealing his breakthrough to middle stage or accepting the class chairman post without complaint—that would be true scheming."

"A clan relies not only on rules, but also blood and kinship. Ren Zu tried capturing wisdom through rules, only to lose it and expose his methods. Rules are rigid, but bonds are alive. Relying solely on regulations will only further alienate Fang Yuan from the clan. Fang Yuan has C-grade talent; at best, he might become a minor elder in several decades. Yet he remains Fang Zheng's twin brother. Do you understand?"

Gazing out at the moon, Gu Yue Bo said, "Greatness comes from tolerance. Remember this, and leave."

"Yes, Clan Leader. This subordinate takes his leave," the academy elder said, exiting the hall.

# Chapter 59: Regardless of Rank Three or Four, you’re all monkeys

Riding the Treasure Brass Toad, Jia Fu and his entourage departed Gu Yue Village, returning toward the merchant caravan.

After a while, an uneasy subordinate asked Jia Fu, "Master, what do we do now? Once you return, how will you answer to old master regarding Jia Jin Sheng's death? Should we find a scapegoat first…"

Jia Fu shook his head, asking instead, "Do you know the story of Ren Zu?"

Jia Fu continued, "Ren Zu possessed the Rules and Regulation Gu to capture all ten thousand Gu in the world, gaining strength but losing wisdom. At that point, his net still held three Gu. Opening it, he saw Attitude Gu, Belief Gu, and Suspicion Gu. Ren Zu refused to let them go, so the three Gu made a bet with him. Once Ren Zu opened the net, they fled in three directions, and whichever he caught would submit to him. Guess which one he captured in the end?"

The trusted aide thought, and answered, "It's the Attitude Gu!"

Jia Fu laughed, "Because attitude is everything. Whether Father believes or suspects, I have shown my attitude. When Jia Jin Sheng vanished, I investigated immediately and rushed here. "I interrogated them without rest and used the Rank four Bamboo Gentleman to verify Fang Yuan's words.

"Once back, I will spend heavily to hire divine investigator Tie Leng Xue. As his brother, I have done all I should! I've thought it through—we don't need a scapegoat. We will return honestly, as I am blameless! A scapegoat might just fall into Jia Gui's trap. If I find a scapegoat, he can find someone to overturn the case."

Fang Yuan stood on the hillside as he silently watched.

This incident was a chess match between Jia Fu and the clan, where Gu Yue Bo, the elder, and Jia Fu were all pawns.

To protect his own piece, he had to maneuver between the two opposing sides.

The Bamboo Gentleman was unexpected, but under the Spring Autumn Cicada's aura, it ironically became Fang Yuan's strongest evidence.

Ultimately, Fang Yuan not only perfectly explained the Liquor worm's origin but also pinned the blame onto Jia Gui, escaping the incident entirely unscathed.

"The academy elder was kept behind; Gu Yue Bo will likely intervene to stop the pressure on me. He has the magnanimity for it, but his true focus isn't me—it's Fang Zheng. Stirring this commotion was meant to draw the higher-ups' notice. Even if Gu Yue Bo didn't step in, Gu Yue Mo Chen and Chi Lian would have acted to defend their reputations."

"Jia Fu is convinced Jia Gui is the culprit. Burning for revenge—hehe, my interference has escalated their rivalry. Will their family competition be moved up?"

"Right, there is still divine investigator Tie Leng Xue..." Fang Yuan smiled faintly. "A prominent righteous figure, but bogged down by duties. It won't be easy to summon him. Jia Fu must invite him to show attitude, but with timing uncertain, it will take two to three years at least."

In two to three years, he would reach Rank two or three, entering a completely different realm.

"The bright moon shines among pines, the clear spring flows over stone," Fang Yuan recited, recalling a fable from Earth.

A troop of monkeys saw the moon reflected in a well and tried to fish it out.

"In this life, I shall be the true moon—rising above mountains and skies, playing with clouds and seas, treading the dark heavens of antiquity." Fang Yuan's eyes were clear, reflecting the verdant mountains.

# Chapter 60: Passage behind the broken wall, how can the legacy be easily obtained?

Fang Yuan had slipped away from the village into the secret rock-fissure cave.

Half-kneeling, he caressed the image wall, blue moonlight glowing from his palm.

The original scene was gone. Without Fang Yuan's memories, no one would suspect the Flower Wine Monk's inheritance lay hidden beneath it.

Killing Jia Jin Sheng had forced him to erase evidence, while the impending interrogation had confined him to the village.

Only after Jia Fu departed and ten days passed did scrutiny fade enough for Fang Yuan to return.

Primeval essence from his green copper sea flowed into the Moonlight Gu on his right palm.

The Moonlight Gu flickered gently, shaving away the rock wall bit by bit as piles of stone powder fell.

This was his sixth day using the Moonlight Gu to shave the wall.

Qing Mao Mountain's soil was normally green, yet here it was vivid red and emitted a dim glow.

To avoid suspicion, Fang Yuan avoided tools. Steel hammers and pickaxes were faster, but the noise would reverberate far and wide.

In his past life, carelessness had cost him dearly; five hundred years of experience had etched patient caution into his soul.

Taking a deep breath, Fang Yuan slowly halted his primeval essence.

He had shaved three inches off the half-man-high slab of image wall.

Fang Yuan rapped his knuckles against the rock wall.

Hearing the hollow sound, he felt pleased—the wall was thinning.

Gritting his teeth, Fang Yuan pressed his right palm against the rock wall once more.

After fifteen minutes of continuous moonlight glow, a small hole appeared in the wall.

He immediately stood up and kicked.

With a loud crack, the hole widened to the size of a bamboo basket.

Fang Yuan backed away cautiously as stale air seeped from the opening into the cave.

Once stale air thinned, Fang Yuan enlarged the opening until it was large enough to crawl through.

Looking from the hole, there was a diagonal path downwards.

The tunnel walls were made of strange crimson soil emitting a faint glow that illuminated the path.

Squinting, he paused at the entrance without stepping in immediately.

A strength inheritance was deliberately designed with trials to test successors.

The Flower Wine Monk created this inheritance to groom an avenger against the Gu Yue clan.

"Which type does the Flower Wine Monk's belong to?" Fang Yuan pondered.

"A pure trap is unlikely, or he wouldn't have made the image wall. But are there hidden mechanisms?"

Fang Yuan tossed a rock into the passage.

The rock rolled down out of sight, bouncing with echoing clatters.

Fang Yuan pondered; the rock test indicated it was safe.

Still, he refrained from entering, scattering rock powder across the opening and fissure.

He then squeezed out through the rock crack and left.

In a hidden bush near the river, he retrieved his bamboo wine, drinking deeply and spilling liquor on his clothes to smell of alcohol.

Wine in hand, he returned to the village, reaching the dormitory right at midnight.

# Chapter 61: Life hanging on a grass rope

In the academy, the elder addressed the class. "Tomorrow, you will choose your second Gu. Choose carefully. With the experience of cultivating these few days and understanding of your own body, consider it as a whole. Normally, it is best if you pair the second Gu well with your vital Gu."

While the other youngsters pondered the elder's words, Fang Yuan was fast asleep on his desk.

Soon it was night once more.

Fang Yuan entered the secret cave again.

A wild rabbit struggled in his grip, a bell tied around its neck.

The passage entrance remained open and silent. Half-kneeling, Fang Yuan inspected the inconspicuous layer of rock powder he had scattered yesterday.

"The powder at the tunnel mouth is undisturbed, and the footprint at the fissure is mine." Fang Yuan relaxed.

Standing, he pulled dead vines from the wall and sat down to weave a makeshift grass rope.

An hour later, the rabbit dashed into the tunnel with its bell jingling, disappearing from sight in moments.

Fang Yuan held the makeshift grass rope tied to the rabbit's hind leg as it unspooled into the tunnel.

Feeling the tug, the startled rabbit scrambled deeper into the passage.

Eventually, the rabbit reached the end of the tunnel, the line stretching taut without budging.

Easily overpowering the rabbit, Fang Yuan hauled it back out by force.

Holding the squirming rabbit, Fang Yuan inspected it for injuries and breathed a sigh of relief.

"For now, this stretch of the passage seems safe."

Having served its purpose, the rabbit was swiftly killed and tossed to the ground.

Taking a deep breath after thorough testing, he finally stepped cautiously into the tunnel.

Since some mechanisms only triggered under human weight, the rabbit's trial was no guarantee, so Fang Yuan remained vigilant.

The tunnel sloped downward in a straight line, growing wider and taller the deeper it went.

Though only three hundred meters long, Fang Yuan took two cautious hours to reach the end of the tunnel.

"Navigating without a detection Gu is truly troublesome." Fang Yuan wiped his sweat, calmed down, and surveyed the area.

A massive, smooth rock blocked the tunnel's end, bulging outward like Jia Fu's belly.

"Did a cave-in block the tunnel?" Fang Yuan pondered the possibilities.

The Flower Wine Monk had used the Thousand Li Earthwolf Spider to carve this path before dying.

After centuries without maintenance, parts of the tunnel might have collapsed from the erosion of time.

"If so, am I stuck here?" Touching the door-sized boulder, he could only guess at its immense thickness.

Shaving it down with Moonlight Gu would take a year or two.

"Excavation tools might expose me with noise and traces." Fang Yuan frowned, weighing the risks.

If the risk proved too great, he would rather abandon this inheritance.

# Chapter 62: Choosing Gu again in the Gu storage room

Lined up outside the academy Gu storage room, the students flushed with excitement.

Having reached middle stage first, Fang Yuan earned first pick and led the queue.

Behind him stood Gu Yue Mo Chen, Fang Zheng, and Chi Cheng.

The guards opened the storage room door, and Fang Yuan stepped inside first.

Though modest in size, the room was lined with alcoves of varying sizes holding diverse containers.

Glancing around, Fang Yuan saw that many of the containers were empty.

The room was restocked only annually; since most chose Moonlight Gu, none remained.

On his first visit, Fang Yuan had chosen the Moonlight Gu immediately, but now several targets crossed his mind.

Fang Yuan walked along the left wall, carefully browsing the options.

In a square alcove at shoulder height sat a bronze bowl containing a Gu.

This was the Bronze Skin Gu; upon activation, it cast a yellow glow that boosted defense.

"Bronze Skin, Stone Skin, Iron Skin... if there's a Jade Skin Gu, I might pick it," Fang Yuan muttered.

The Jade Skin Gu was the rarest in the series, its market value second only to the Liquor worm—and occasionally rivaling it.

Finding no Jade Skin Gu, Fang Yuan chuckled. "Gu Yue is only a mid-sized clan. Expecting one was unrealistic."

Having finished the left wall, Fang Yuan moved to inspect the next.

Like the Skin Gu series, the Longhorn Beetle Gu formed a major Rank one family.

First to catch his eye was a Brute Force Longhorn Beetle Gu.

The Brute Force Longhorn Beetle Gu granted the temporary strength of a bull for five breaths.

First, activation consumed heavy primeval essence: 10% for Rank one middle stage, and 20% for initial stage.

It also risked muscle tearing from overuse, making it unsuitable for a smaller Gu Master like Fang Yuan.

Knowing this, Fang Yuan walked past the Brute Force Longhorn Beetle Gu to continue searching.

"Oh? This is not bad." He halted his steps.

It was a Yellow Camel Longhorn Beetle Gu.

In contrast, the Yellow Camel Longhorn Beetle Gu greatly reduced stamina expenditure without repercussions.

Among Rank one Gu, its value was surpassed only by the Jade Skin Gu and Liquor worm.

"The Gu isn't bad, but it's incompatible with me." Fang Yuan shook his head and moved on.

Fang Yuan turned to the third wall and continued inspecting.

On this wall, the Boar Gu were dominant.

There's the Flower Boar Gu, Pink Boar Gu, Black Boar Gu and White Boar Gu.

"The Flower Boar Gu is the most common and abundant on the market. At half the price of the Brute Force Longhorn Beetle Gu, it's truly good value. Yet only the last two Boar Gu hold greater value, being even more expensive than the Jade Skin Gu and Liquor worm!" Fang Yuan's eyes turned pensive.

A Black or White Boar Gu could fetch six hundred primeval stones, selling almost immediately upon appearing.

The Black and White Boar Gu permanently altered a Gu Master's constitution to raise their strength.

The strength accumulated permanently, remaining even if the Gu died.

"A Black or White Boar Gu would be ideal," Fang Yuan mused, though this room would never contain one.

At last, he retrieved a Little Light Gu from a corner.

The Little Light Gu was a common support Gu that suited his cultivation with the Moonlight Gu.

Fang Yuan walked out leisurely to find the students waiting outside growing restless.

"My turn! I wonder what Gu Fang Yuan picked?" Seeing Fang Yuan emerge, Gu Yue Mo Chen strode eagerly into the storage room.

Finding the sole Yellow Camel Longhorn Beetle still there, Mo Chen was overjoyed and claimed it immediately.

Afterwards, Fang Zheng went in.

"I have Moonlight Gu for offense, but lack defense." Fang Zheng chose the Bronze Skin Gu.

Gu Yue Chi Cheng was the fourth to enter.

"I need evasion so I can strike without being hit." Chi Cheng chose the Dragonpill Cricket Gu.

# Chapter 63: Gifting the jade skin under the moon; white boar hidden in the ground flower

This was a concealed courtyard in Gu Yue Village.

Gu Yue Bo looked gently at Fang Zheng. "Fang Zheng, what second Gu did you choose today?"

"Reporting to Clan Leader, I chose the Bronze Skin Gu," Fang Zheng replied stiffly, looking at him with admiration.

Gu Yue Bo nodded thoughtfully. "Not bad, a good choice."

"You think I'm insincere? No." Gu Yue Bo smiled at Fang Zheng. "Choosing a Gu reveals a person's disposition."

"The Bronze Skin Gu paired with Moonlight Gu gives you offense and defense, reflecting your pure nature—the righteous king's path."

"Gu Yue Mo Bei chose the Yellow Camel Longhorn Beetle for stamina and endurance, reflecting his stubborn, unyielding nature."

"Gu Yue Chi Cheng chose the Dragonpill Cricket Gu for evasion. He avoids frontal clashes and favors tactical wit, revealing a flaw in his character."

"Clan Leader, what did my brother choose?" Fang Zheng asked, thinking of Fang Yuan.

Gu Yue Bo chuckled. "He chose the Little Light Gu to amplify his moonblade attacks, revealing an extreme, aggressive, and invasive nature."

"Indeed, brother seems to be like that." Fang Zheng muttered.

"I analyzed Mo Bei and Chi Cheng, yet he only asked about Fang Yuan. He carries deep trauma from his brother, but in this rebellious phase, his desire to surpass Fang Yuan is understandable. If only Fang Zheng had Fang Yuan's intelligence. Of all the youths I've seen, Fang Yuan's political wit is unmatched. What a pity he only has C-grade talent."

He took out a Gu worm from his pocket.

"This is... Jade Skin Gu?" Fang Zheng's eyes widened as he saw it.

Gu Yue Bo said, "The Jade Skin Gu far surpasses the Bronze Skin Gu, offering greater defense for less primeval essence. Fang Zheng, do you want it?"

"I can give it to you," Gu Yue Bo smiled amicably, "but as clan leader, I must remain impartial. I have one condition."

His smile vanished into a stern look. "You must be the first to break through from Rank one to Rank two. This Jade Skin Gu is your advancement reward."

Fired up, Fang Zheng declared passionately, "I promise! I will defeat everyone and be the first to reach Rank two!"

"Good." Gu Yue Bo smiled gently and placed the Jade Skin Gu into Fang Zheng's hand.

He thought, "Fang Zheng, your inferiority complex harms your growth. The only cure for it is success. With your A-grade talent, reaching Rank two first is your easiest victory. You must succeed; failing even this would be far too disappointing."

Meanwhile, Fang Yuan returned to the secret cave behind the rock crack, reaching the end of the tunnel.

"The boulder blocking the path is suspiciously round and smooth—definitely man-made. The Flower Wine Monk placed it deliberately, but why put a hurdle here?" Fang Yuan pondered.

The smooth ground lay beneath a curved ceiling, while the red soil walls emitted a faint glow.

"Eh?" Looking at the ground again, he spotted something suspicious.

Fang Yuan knelt and touched the damp ground.

Rubbing the earth between his fingers, Fang Yuan noticed this patch was unusually soft and loose rather than clumped like dry red soil.

Fang Yuan's eyes brightened. Experience told him the Flower Wine Monk's hidden 'key' was right here.

Fang Yuan easily dug into the loose soil.

"This rich, elegant fragrance... could it be..." Fang Yuan's heart stirred as a possibility struck him, his hands digging faster.

As he dug, a faint dark-gold light emanated from below.

"It really is!" Fang Yuan's eyes gleamed as he carefully widened the hole.

Soon, a buried dark-gold flower bud emerged before him.

Buried two inches deep and sized like a millstone, the bud's delicate dark-gold surface looked mysterious and elegant.

"It truly is the Earth Treasury Flower Gu!" Fang Yuan exhaled deeply.

Rather than rushing, he rested, wiped the dirt from his hands, and carefully pried open the dark-gold petals.

Within the paper-thin, translucent petals, a Gu worm slept submerged in golden nectar.

"The Gu preserved here was left for his inheritor." Fang Yuan gently peeled the final petals, opening the core.

As the golden nectar drained, the core softened. Fang Yuan parted the petals and retrieved the slumbering Gu.

It was a ladybug no larger than a thumbnail.

"White Boar Gu!" Fang Yuan's face lit up with joy.

# Chapter 64: Easy to do secretly, difficult to do openly

Fang Yuan entered the cafeteria, instantly drawing the shop assistants' attention.

"Bring a jar of wine, six hundred grams of beef, and some side dishes." Fang Yuan took his usual window seat.

Fang Yuan nodded. "Bring rice wine then, and tell the innkeeper I'm pre-ordering a hundred jars of green bamboo wine this year."

With the Liquor worm openly revealed without suspicion, Fang Yuan no longer needed to restrain his wine purchases.

Fang Yuan looked distracted as his attention turned to his aperture.

The Spring Autumn Cicada slept deeply, hiding its form.

The plump White Boar Gu spread its wings and circled in the air.

The Black and White Boar Gu were equally renowned, rare Rank one Gu worth even more than the Liquor worm.

Meanwhile, the White Boar Gu combined with Jade Skin Gu into the Rank two White Jade Gu, then into the Rank three Heavenly Mugwort.

Obtaining the White Boar Gu was pleasing, but the Flower Wine Monk's strength inheritance was the true prize.

"The White Boar Gu boosts strength. The boulder must be the first test—I need to refine this Gu to push it aside and advance."

"Such meticulous planning implies further trials ahead. More importantly, this confirms his strength inheritance is genuine, not a trap."

"This inheritance will help me reach Rank three faster to leave Qing Mao Mountain and seek greater opportunities!"

Why did the Mo family ignore him killing their servant, or families overlook him extorting classmates, or the clan leader tolerate his defiance?

Because he was weak—merely a C-grade.

The powerful disdained bullying the weak. Petty retaliation against him would appear ungracious and damage their reputations within the clan system.

For now, weakness served as Fang Yuan's protective umbrella.

Thus, the Flower Wine Monk's inheritance was perfectly timed!

This secret inheritance provided resources to bypass clan control, letting him cultivate independently and quietly amass strength.

"It is always easier to act in secret than openly. With this inheritance, I can grow stronger without drawing scrutiny. But I must continue the extortion—stopping abruptly would arouse suspicion, and I still need primeval stones," Fang Yuan planned.

He really needed primeval stones.

While his peers raised only two Gu, Fang Yuan now maintained four after refining the Little Light Gu and obtaining the White Boar Gu.

Feeding just the Moonlight Gu and Liquor worm had cost one primeval stone daily; now his daily upkeep exceeded two stones.

Factoring in cultivation and living expenses, he required roughly five primeval stones each day.

Additionally, feeding the White Boar Gu presented another issue.

The Black and White Boar Gu had voracious appetites, consuming an entire pig's worth of meat every five days.

"I must hunt for pork myself from now on." Fang Yuan's eyes gleamed.

Buying pork in the village would cost precious stones and draw unwanted attention—no single person could explain eating so much meat.

"Waiter, bill!" Fang Yuan paid without hesitation and departed.

With the academy on break for students to refine their second Gu, Fang Yuan used the free time to scout outside the village and hunt wild boars.

# Chapter 65: Quickly get lost

"Clan Gu Masters keep the village surroundings relatively safe, but wild beasts and Gu still roam freely—I must stay cautious."

"Qing Mao Mountain is vast, and without a scouting Gu or familiarity with the cleared terrain, finding wild boars here is difficult. I'll head to the foot of the mountain!"

Finding nothing on the mountain, Fang Yuan redirected toward the base.

Qing Mao Mountain hosted three clan villages: the Xiong clan on the front mountain, the Gu Yue clan on the mountain waist, and the Bai clan by the rear waterfall.

At the foot of the mountain lay dozens of smaller mortal hamlets.

Soon, a broad hill and a riverside village came into view.

"Oh?" Nearing the village, Fang Yuan's sharp eyes spotted ground tracks he recognized as a wild boar's.

"Chase!" Energized, Fang Yuan tracked the trail deeper into the mountains.

The boar's tracks grew clearer, and knowing he was close, Fang Yuan became more cautious—wild boars were dangerous beasts.

"Oh? What's this?" When Fang Yuan found the wild boar, it had fallen into a pit, pierced and bleeding from a sharpened bamboo stake at the bottom.

From the pool of blood, he could tell the boar had been trapped for at least fifteen minutes.

Yet the boar continued struggling, whimpering and howling, still full of life.

"A hunter's trap. To think I'm reaping the rewards." Fang Yuan smiled, though his expression remained grim.

"Traps could be buried anywhere on this mountain, but hunters inform one another of their locations. I must find an old hunter's home and learn their trap locations. Combined with intel on beast territories, I won't have to search blindly like looking for a needle in a haystack," Fang Yuan thought.

With a flick of Fang Yuan's wrist, a moonblade flew out, striking the wild boar squarely.

With a soft chime, the moonblade slashed the boar's neck, unleashing a fountain of blood.

Its life faded as warm blood filled the pit, a pungent stench permeating the air.

"Survival or death—this is the eternal theme of nature," he sighed inwardly.

"Second Brother Wang inherited the old hunter's skills. Any boar he hunts is as good as caught!"

Amidst the flattery, a stern voice scoffed, "Hmph! Hunting one boar hardly proves my skill. Today I'll kill three! Er Gou Dan, stop lusting after my sister, or I'll thrash you!"

The voice replied, "Hmph, I'm a real man, above such petty temptations! One day I'll leave Qing Mao Mountain to explore the world and see everything under heaven—that's a true man's ambition!"

Saying so, the four young hunters walked out of the forest.

Their tall leader carried a bow and arrows, his bulging muscles and spirited eyes radiating strength.

Seeing Fang Yuan, he scowled, "Which village are you from, kid? How dare you steal from Wang Er—get lost!"

# Chapter 66: A person’s death is akin to a pig’s death

"Get lost?" Fang Yuan gave a cold smirk and slashed his palm forward.

With a light sound, a blue coloured moonblade shot forward.

Simultaneously, he kicked off the ground and charged the four hunters.

"Gu Master?!" the hunters shouted in shock upon seeing the moonblade.

One hunter reacted too slowly; the moonblade sheared through his right forearm, splitting bone and flesh as his severed arm dropped to the ground.

"Spare me!"

Seeing their companion mutilated, the other two turned deathly pale, dropping to their knees to kowtow desperately before Fang Yuan.

"Hmph, a bunch of cowards, what are you afraid of? He's just a student!" Only Wang Er remained standing. Startled and enraged by the unprovoked attack, he swiftly reached behind his back and drew his bow and arrow.

"Oh?" Fang Yuan narrowed his eyes. Mortals usually cowered before Gu Masters, yet Wang Er remained composed—a bold and unruly man.

Another moonblade shot out towards Wang Er.

Wang Er sidestepped the moonblade and loosed an arrow that whistled straight toward Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan ducked his head to dodge the arrow, closing the distance to Wang Er in a flash.

Wang Er tossed his bow aside and charged Fang Yuan with clenched, bowl-sized fists.

Muscular and broad, he stood half a meter taller than Fang Yuan, his eyes glaring with wolfish killing intent.

Watching the massive fist loom before him, Fang Yuan's expression never wavered as he stepped aside.

Wang Er grinned maliciously, swinging his fist horizontally to strike Fang Yuan's back.

Just then, blinding sunlight flashed into his eyes.

Fang Yuan smirked, flipping backward to evade the fist while swinging his right arm.

The third moonblade flew along the sunlight trajectory.

Sensing unprecedented danger, Wang Er turned his head just as the eerie blue moonblade flew past.

A piece of his skull detached along with a spray of blood, flying into the air.

Like a snapped bow, all energy drained from his form; he collapsed silently to the ground like a withered leaf.

"Second Brother Wang...?" a hunter called in horror, Wang Er having always been their leader.

Paralyzed with terror, the two hunters knelt frozen like statues.

Fang Yuan got up from the ground.

Fang Yuan had lost his balance dodging Wang Er's punch, but taking that blow would have made him vomit blood.

Standing up, he had suffered no real injury beyond a sore shoulder.

"Wang Er was far stronger than the Mo servant Gao Wan. Any other student would have lost, and even a Rank one upper stage Gu Master could fall to him through carelessness," Fang Yuan mused, studying the corpse.

His swift, steady archery rivaled moonblades—even outranging them. His melee prowess was at the mortal peak: a sturdy build, tough bones, and ruthless aggression.

"Wang Er was skilled. I aimed to decapitate him, but his sharp reflexes let him dodge enough that the moonblade only took off half his head."

"Life and death is nature's law where all living beings are equal. Nobles or peasants, in the face of death a man's demise is no different from a pig's. Dead is dead."

# Chapter 67: Don’t worry, I’ll spare you

Meanwhile, a pretty young woman was drawing water from a well.

Hearing the bucket land, an old man stepped out from the wooden house.
