His hair was grizzled and deeply lined, but flashes of sharpness revealed an old tiger that had not lost its majesty.

Smiling brightly, the girl said, "Father, let Second Brother handle the hunting while you rest at home."

"Hehe," the old man chuckled, nodding. "With your second brother's skill, he roams this mountain easily. His archery even surpasses mine in my youth. Yet I still worry—his ambitions are too wild, thinking martial arts alone will let him soar. Young men always dream too big."

The bamboo fence gate was smashed open from outside.

"You are Old Man Wang?" Fang Yuan stepped forward with cold, eerie eyes, glowing moonlight gathering in his hand.

Shocked by the moonlight in Fang Yuan's hand, the old man fell to his knees. "This old man greets Lord Gu Master!"

"Old Man Wang, your son dared to offend me, so I executed him. Bring forward his corpse!" Fang Yuan commanded, looking down at him.

At his words, the two young hunters carried in Wang Er's corpse.

"Second Brother—!" the girl screamed in anguish, clutching Wang Er's corpse as tears streamed down her face.

"Old Man Wang, I hear you are the best hunter among these villages, still bringing back good game despite your age."

Fang Yuan continued expressionlessly, "Draw me a map of all mountain traps and beast territories you've observed. Draw it, and I'll spare your family. Refuse, and... hmph."

"Murderer! You murdered my brother! You treat human lives like livestock and dare confront us? I'll avenge him!" the girl shrieked in hatred, lunging toward Fang Yuan.

"Insolent wretch!" Old Man Wang screamed, striking her to the ground with a hard slap.

"Your brother is dead, do you want to die too? Do you want your old father left all alone?!" the old man wept, trembling violently.

Prostrating before Fang Yuan, the old man pleaded shakily, "Lord Gu Master, my son deserved death! I will draw the map at once; please spare us!"

Fang Yuan's expression softened slightly as he looked down at him. "Rest assured, draw it truthfully and I will spare you. But if you try to deceive me, your lives are forfeit!"

"This old man understands," Wang kowtowed repeatedly. "Please allow me to fetch brush and paper."

"No need." Fang Yuan waved his hand, ordering the two hunters, "Go inside and bring out paper and brush."

"Lord, the paper and brush are on the kitchen table," Old Man Wang called out.

Fang Yuan's eyes shone but he remained silent.

The two hunters rushed into the house and quickly brought out paper and brush.

Kneeling on the ground, the old man drew lines, curves, and markings with the brush.

He used over ten bamboo papers, before handing them all to Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan glanced through them and split the sheets between the two hunters. "Check for errors. For each mistake you find, I'll give you a primeval stone!"

# Chapter 68: There is no innocence in nature

The young hunters scrutinized the maps for nearly fifteen minutes, reluctantly handing them back only after Fang Yuan urged them.

"The trap areas in these bamboo papers are all accurate."

"Lord Gu Master, our lives are on the line—I would never lie to you!" the old man shouted, kowtowing repeatedly.

"Not bad." Fang Yuan waved the papers, then his tone turned cold. "However, I don't believe you."

Old Man Wang looked up in shock, only to see an eerie blue moonblade expanding in his vision.

His head flew into the air, fresh blood spewing everywhere.

"Father—!" the girl cried out, leaping toward his headless corpse, only for another moonblade to strike her face.

"Get up, go inside and search!" Fang Yuan ordered, "I know that a hunter's family always keeps a beast skin map. On top of it is the terrain, trap locations and the beast territories. Find it for me, and I'll let you keep your lives."

"Right away, Lord Gu Master!" The two scrambled up and dashed into the wooden house.

"They've searched it thoroughly, but every hunter inherits a beast-skin map refined over generations, detailing territories and traps. It's their livelihood—how could they not have one?"

Fang Yuan pondered, "When I ordered them to find paper and brush, Old Man Wang blurted out their location immediately, worried they might stumble upon the beast-skin map. It must be in this house!"

Scanning the room again, inspiration struck as Fang Yuan looked at the firepit.

Fang Yuan walked over to the firepit, squatted down, and used a shovel to dig through the charcoal.

He shoveled out the charcoal block and smashed it on the ground, shattering it to reveal a bamboo tube.

Opening the bamboo tube, Fang Yuan gave it a shake, and a map slid out.

Made of white beast skin, the heavy map was covered in intricate multicolored lines.

His gaze locked onto five markings denoting wild boar herds.

Two were small herds, two medium, and one large. In the center of the large herd was a giant red cross.

Seeing the cross, Fang Yuan sneered. The bamboo paper had contained no such marking!

The young hunters hadn't noticed because their experience was limited to nearby areas. This red cross lay far from the village, proving Old Man Wang's cunning.

To hunt wild boars, he needed a reliable beast-skin map. A map drawn under duress could never be trusted.

Fang Yuan never killed for the sake of killing—slaughter was merely a means to an end. If it solved the problem, why not?

The old man had to die; even if he endured his son's murder, Fang Yuan would not leave loose ends. Weeds must be pulled by the roots, lest they return with the spring breeze.

In any world, living beings are entangled in cause and effect—who is truly innocent? Humans slaughter pigs; aren't the pigs innocent?

In this world, anybody can live, anybody can die, but nobody is innocent!

# Chapter 69: Life is as cheap as grass

The beast-skin map was too thick to fold, so Fang Yuan rolled it into the bamboo tube, tied both ends with hemp rope, and slung it across his back.

"Is there anyone else in Old Man Wang's family?" Fang Yuan asked coldly, his gaze dark.

"No one else, the entire family is dead, Lord Gu Master!"

"Hunter Wang's wife was killed by invading wolves decades ago, after bearing two sons and a daughter. Three years ago, the eldest son Wang Da died hunting on the mountain. There is no one else in the Wang family."

"Is that so…" Fang Yuan narrowed his eyes at the kneeling men, knowing they spoke the truth.

"Enough flattery. I only killed them for this beast skin map. You two have behaved well; you may leave." Fang Yuan's tone softened, but the hand behind his back glowed with gloomy moonlight.

"Hold it," a voice suddenly called out.

With a swoosh, a Gu Master leaped down from the treetop.

"Neither of you may leave. Tell me everything that happened here." The Gu Master wore a deep blue uniform with a scarlet belt, its metal plate carved with the character 'Two'.

"This commoner greets Lord Jiang He!" The two youths instantly knelt and prostrated themselves in greeting.

Ignoring the kneeling hunters, Jiang He looked straight at Fang Yuan and asked coldly, "I am Gu Master Jiang He of the Gu Yue clan, stationed here. Who are you?"

Fang Yuan chuckled and extended his right palm, where the Moonlight Gu emitted a ball of moonlight.

Seeing the moonlight in Fang Yuan's hand, Jiang He's gaze softened considerably—the Moonlight Gu was the Gu Yue clan's unmistakable symbol.

"Old Man Wang's second son offended me, so I killed the whole family. Those two are my witnesses," Fang Yuan stated, pointing at the kneeling hunters.

Jiang He stared blankly before laughing aloud. "Well killed! Just a bunch of lowly peasants daring to offend their masters—they deserved to die!"

He quickly shifted his tone, saying with deep meaning, "However, junior, what you did has also brought me trouble. Though Old Man Wang lived out here isolated with his family, they were still villagers under my charge. In the year-end examination, the clan's assessment of me will drop."

Fang Yuan's gaze flashed, instantly seeing through Jiang He's pretext to extort money from him.

He chuckled. "That is simple. Senior merely needs to report the truth—that I killed the entire family, and it has nothing to do with you."

Jiang He's eyelids twitched with rising anger at Fang Yuan's refusal to play along. His tone turned solemn. "Then I must conduct business impartially. If you do not fear the clan's investigation, state your name, and I will record the facts faithfully on paper."

Seeing Jiang He's weakness, Fang Yuan replied at once, "Then report it as you must. Ah right, my surname is Gu Yue, and my name is Fang Yuan."

"So you're actually Fang Yuan!" Jiang He showed open astonishment. "My little brother mentions you constantly. You beat up all your classmates and openly extort sixty primeval stones each time. By the way, my brother is Jiang Ya; you two must have met."

"So it's him." Fang Yuan nodded. "I always buy my Moon orchid petals from Jiang Ya's shop."

"Hahaha, since that's the case, we're acquaintances! Forget about it, I will handle this matter!" Jiang He thumped his chest, putting on an appearance of loyalty and self-sacrifice.

Jiang He did not fear exposure; mortal peasants were worth as little as grass. Even if the clan learned of the cover-up, they would hardly care about such a trivial matter.

"However, junior, you haven't graduated yet and lack a Gu Master uniform. Had Wang Er seen this outfit, he would have known your status and never offended you. You're a smart person, aren't you, hehe?" Jiang He added.

Fang Yuan understood the veiled warning: do not cause trouble or kill in Jiang He's jurisdiction again.

"Thank you for the advice, senior." Fang Yuan cupped his fists to bid Jiang He farewell and promptly took his leave.

# Chapter 70: Using the White Boar Gu

Fang Yuan stepped onto the green grass, quietly approaching a mountain boar.

Suddenly, it stopped digging and raised its head alertly, spotting Fang Yuan with a warning grunt.

Fang Yuan was not surprised. Unlike domestic animals, wild beasts were extremely vigilant—especially wild boars, whose acute sense of smell could uncover even well-hidden bird nests.

Fang Yuan continued without stop, getting closer to the wild boar.

The wild boar growled lowly, the white fur on its neck bristling in agitation.

Pawing the ground, the wild boar lowered its head and charged.

Its upturned lower jaw bore two white tusks that gleamed in the moonlight as it lunged toward Fang Yuan.

Lacking a defensive Gu worm, Fang Yuan risked having his stomach pierced and intestines punctured, suffering severe injury or death if struck by the tusks.

"Moonlight Gu." Remaining calm, Fang Yuan channeled primeval essence into the Moonlight Gu in his palm, making it emit an eerie glow that mingled with the moonlight above.

As the wild boar charged, Fang Yuan slashed his right palm through the air.

With a swoosh, the moonblade flew out and struck the boar's face, splattering blood.

The wild boar squealed, enraged, accelerating its charge until it was mere steps from Fang Yuan in the blink of an eye.

Fang Yuan leaped nimbly aside, rolling to break his fall.

Springing up, Fang Yuan closed in on the wild boar while launching another moonblade from his right hand.

Several deep cuts opened on the boar's black fur, bright red blood oozing from the wounds.

Powered by upper stage primeval essence, Fang Yuan's moonblade could shatter human bone, yet it only inflicted superficial cuts on the boar without damaging bone.

Though ferocious, the wild boar could not turn easily, charging only in straight lines that were simple to dodge.

"Little light Gu."

This time, Fang Yuan channeled primeval essence into both the Moonlight Gu and the Little Light Gu.

As dark green primeval essence entered both Gu, the Moonlight Gu shone with eerie blue light while the Little Light Gu cast a milky-white glow.

The milky-white light merged into the moonlight, instantly doubling the size of the glowing mass.

"Go."

Fang Yuan swept his right hand, unleashing an enhanced, giant moonblade.

Augmented by the Little Light Gu, the moonblade's size and attack range doubled.

The moonblade struck the boar's neck, slicing through black-gray fur and flesh to emerge from the other side, flying three to four meters before dissipating.

The wild boar froze in place for a moment before blood fountained from its wound.

It collapsed abruptly, half its head severed and hanging by a mere strip of flesh amidst the pouring blood.

Without hesitation, Fang Yuan pointed at the carcass. The White Boar Gu in his aperture transformed into milky-white light and darted into the boar's body.

Fang Yuan remained vigilant; the scent of blood could easily attract predators.

Before long, the White Boar Gu flew back safe and sound.

The boar had shrunk by half, leaving only black-gray hide and entrails; the White Boar Gu had devoured most of the meat.

"After this meal, I'll return in five days to hunt." Stowing the White Boar Gu, Fang Yuan drew his knife and extracted the boar's two tusks before leaving.

Fang Yuan hid the teeth in the rock crack's secret cave before returning to the hostel.

Instead of sleeping immediately, he sat on his bed and began cultivating.

As a middle stage Gu Master, Fang Yuan possessed upper stage primeval essence thanks to the Liquor worm's refinement.

After killing the boar, only twenty-three percent of his primeval essence remained in his aperture.

"White Boar Gu."

Within his aperture, a surge of dark green primeval essence flowed upward into the White Boar Gu.

The White Boar Gu emitted a brilliant light that enveloped Fang Yuan, making his entire body glow with pure white radiance from the inside out.

He felt a numb itchiness. As the white light modified his musculature, permanent strength was gradually infused into his body, never to be lost.

After a moment, the numbness turned to agony, shifting into the sharp pain of razor blades.

Fang Yuan hurriedly stopped the White Boar Gu.

The White Boar Gu could only be used for about fifteen minutes a day; exceeding this threshold caused escalating, unbearable pain.

# Chapter 71: Keeping a low profile even if one’s strength can carry a boar

Time flew, and in the blink of an eye, it was mid-June.

Beside a riverbank where the current washed over smooth cliff rock, a battle was underway.

Covered in deep wounds, a mountain boar kicked its hooves and charged Fang Yuan once more, trailing a path of fresh blood.

Fang Yuan wrestled with it, showing no signs of panic.

With vast experience, he fought steadily while watching his surroundings.

As time passed, the wild boar's attacks gradually slowed down.

Fang Yuan's eyes gleamed. Striding forward, he channeled strength into his shoulders and arms, hoisting the wild boar aloft in a single breath.

Steadying his footing, Fang Yuan hurled the wild boar with all his strength.

With a boom, the wild boar slammed into a large riverbank rock, squealing pitifully as its bones shattered.

"My strength can now rival a boar! Tonight I'll test the giant rock in the tunnel." Fang Yuan panted, excitement flashing in his eyes.

"I can carry a wild boar now, but that doesn't mean I can clash head-on with one—just as a strong man who can carry another doesn't necessarily share his strength. My strength can continue to grow." After feeding the White Boar Gu, Fang Yuan took the tusks and entered the secret cave.

Even if someone discovered it, it mattered little; Fang Yuan had altered the boar's wounds so they bore no resemblance to moonblade cuts.

Returning to the red-lit cave, Fang Yuan tossed the two boar tusks into a corner.

Fang Yuan squeezed through the passageway, reaching the end of the tunnel once more.

Everything remained unchanged; the giant boulder still blocked the path, and the hole left from excavating the Earth Treasury Flower had already been filled.

Stepping before the giant rock, Fang Yuan raised both hands and pushed.

Though he strained with flushed cheeks and exerted all his strength, the giant rock did not budge.

"This boulder weighs as much as five or six boars. The Flower Wine Monk's strength inheritance is not so easily claimed!" Fang Yuan assessed.

Undeterred, he exited the tunnel and returned to the secret room above.

Retrieving a bamboo tube from the corner, Fang Yuan sat down and removed the beast skin map and sheets of bamboo paper.

Unrolling the beast skin map, he began memorizing it, tracing the routes with his finger to aid his recollection.

Without a storage Gu, Fang Yuan could not carry the map everywhere, and hauling a bamboo tube into battle was impractical. Thus, he dedicated himself to committing the entire beast skin map to memory.

"Youth certainly aids memory; I've already memorized over half the map. In old age, I would forget lines faster than I could learn them. Hehe… of course, if I had a Bookworm, I could simply feed the map to it and recall it flawlessly forever, so long as the Gu wasn't lost."

He was at the middle stage, just like his peers. Keeping pace with A and B-grade talents despite his C-grade aptitude was no small feat, owed entirely to the Liquor worm and his deep cultivation experience.

His extortions had forced classmates to focus on martial training, slowing their cultivation progression even more than in his previous life.

Early cultivation yielded quick results for Gu Masters. In particular, Gu Yue Fang Zheng, Mo Chen, and Chi Cheng were advancing rapidly, threatening to surpass Fang Yuan.

Naturally, this was also because Fang Yuan spent considerable time and effort hunting to boost his strength with the White Boar Gu.

"At this rate, someone will soon reach the upper stage and claim the thirty primeval stone reward. But I have no intention of competing for it," Fang Yuan had long resolved.

More importantly, keeping a low profile avoided undue attention, allowing him to inherit the Flower Wine Monk's strength inheritance without interference.

Fang Yuan collected his thoughts and studied the beast skin map again.

"The red cross marks a forbidden danger zone surrounded by wild boars—likely home to a boar king. Encountering one with my current strength would mean certain death. Hmph!"

This crossed-out danger zone was absent from the bamboo paper. Had Fang Yuan trusted it blindly, he would have walked straight into the boar king's fangs. Old Man Wang was truly cunning.

"What do these three spots marked with red circles mean?" Fang Yuan pondered.

The three circles marked isolated, distant locations with few beasts, considered relatively safe spots in the wilderness.

"The red cross represents danger, but what about the red circles?" Fang Yuan pondered. "Old Man Wang marking them means they are important. Too bad they are far, or I would inspect them myself."

# Chapter 72: Any organisation would also require loyalty

In the blink of an eye, it was already late June.

"Gu Yue Fang Zheng!" the academy elder called out.

Gu Yue Fang Zheng stood up and came before the academy elder.

Under his classmates' admiring gazes, Fang Zheng received a heavy bag of primeval stones.

"Fang Zheng, you are the first to advance to upper stage, this is your reward. Good job, continue working hard." Academy elder smiled pleasantly, patting Fang Zheng's shoulders.

"I finally did it. The first to advance to upper stage, do you see that brother, I finally beat you for once!"

His eyes shone brightly as he looked over at Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan was sprawled on the table, sound asleep.

He had cultivated all night, collapsing in exhaustion the moment he reached class.

"Hmph, even if you don't admit it, it's the truth. Brother, I have finally surpassed you! This is the first time, and there will be a second and a third!"

Fang Zheng clenched his fists. This success was deeply meaningful to him, breaking through the dark clouds in his heart and letting light shine in.

"Hmph, I actually lost to Fang Zheng." Gu Yue Mo Bei sat with crossed arms, indignant.

"This is the advantage of an A grade, damn it…" Gu Yue Chi Cheng muttered, frustrated that despite his grandfather Chi Lian's support, Fang Zheng had surpassed him.

As the academy elder called them one by one, students went up to collect their primeval stone allowance and returned to their seats.

"Everyone quiet down." After distributing the allowance, the academy elder slapped the table, instantly silencing the room.

"You have all mastered your second Gu worm, and some have reached the upper stage. Now it is time to train in the wild. True battles are not against scarecrows or wooden puppets that stand waiting for your attacks."

"The mid-year exam will be held in three days, and all students must attend! You will hunt wild boars, and those who collect the most wild boar teeth will receive better scores. At the end of the examination, each wild boar teeth can be exchanged for ten primeval stones. Furthermore, you are allowed to hunt in groups."

"Didn't you hear? The elder said we can hunt in groups! Those lacking talent or suitable Gu worms can cooperate and share whatever boar teeth we get!"

The students reacted with mixed emotions—some rejoicing, some fretting, some eager, and others calling on companions. Even Fang Yuan was slightly stirred.

"It changed! Last year's exam was collecting wild honey, yet now it's wild boar teeth. Is this the butterfly effect?"

"In my past life I knew nothing of the future; do I lack courage in this life? Hehe, even if the world is full of thorns, I will cut through them and pave a bloody path myself!"

"Ten primeval stones per tooth is high. But selling my stockpiled fangs publicly might arouse suspicion with the clan elders."

"Wait, primeval stones aren't important. Maybe I can use this change to reshape my image." At this thought, Fang Yuan's eyes gleamed.

He needed to quietly amass wealth while keeping a low profile. If the Flower Wine Monk's inheritance was exposed, he could lose his life.

But his current position was awkward.

Standing against all his classmates outside the system, the higher-ups viewed him as an untamable, resentful, intelligent, and cold C-grade youth.

This image portrayed him as disloyal. Yet a clan—and in fact any organization—requires loyalty.

As Fang Yuan's cultivation rose, the higher-ups would become increasingly wary, potentially resorting to heavy-handed methods that would leave him completely passive.

He needed to make the elders believe he was yielding to the clan system, but without acting too abruptly.

More importantly, joining the system was only a facade; Fang Yuan would never truly submit to their control and restrictions. He held too many secrets.

But now the mid-year exam was a chance.

"It seems I need to do some plotting, starting with the extortion." Fang Yuan's gaze turned resolute as his scheme took shape.

# Chapter 73: The mysterious red circle marking

"Fang Yuan defeated Fang Zheng, Chi Cheng, and Mo Bei, but spared their primeval stones?" The academy elder looked startled.

"Fang Yuan has hundreds of primeval stones on hand, so not caring about thirty is understandable. But what is his reason for sparing Mo Bei, Chi Cheng, and Fang Zheng?"

The three represented the clan's core factions. Sparing them indicated Fang Yuan was finally willing to bow his head to the clan.

Three days passed in a flash, and the mid-year exam arrived.

Beneath the tent were several tables and chairs. The academy elder sat at the head, flanked by other clan elders, with several Gu Masters standing behind them.

A Gu Master darted out from the forest like a shadow and knelt outside the tent.

"How is the situation?" Academy elder asked.

"Reporting to Lord Elder, there are currently no casualties among the students," the Gu Master quickly answered.

Looking at the kneeling Gu Master, he asked, "Who has the best results so far?"

"Chi Cheng has killed three boars, Fang Zheng and Mo Bei five, while Fang Yuan leads with eight."

"You may go." The academy elder waved his hand. "Instruct the others to focus on protection. Pay special attention to Fang Zheng, Chi Cheng, and Mo Bei to ensure their safety!"

As the last sunlight shone into the forest, another wild boar fell on the ground.

"Twenty-third," Fang Yuan counted in his heart, squatting down to skillfully carve out a boar tooth.

The bag on his back was already filled with wild boar teeth.

He had already retrieved his previous stockpile of boar teeth from the cave and buried them nearby.

Fang Yuan looked at the sky; it was time to retrieve the other bag.

But when he was about to move, he hesitated.

"I am only five to six hundred meters from the nearest red circle marking. With such a rare chance, should I go take a look?"

Pretending to continue hunting wild boars, he moved deeper into the forest.

Seven minutes later, he arrived at the red marking location.

A treehouse sat hidden among the branches of a giant tree, undetectable without close observation.

"Old Man Wang's temporary hunting shelter?" Fang Yuan frowned, growing confused.

He climbed into the treehouse.

Seeing the sight inside the treehouse, his expression changed!

# Chapter 74: Solving the mystery with knowledge and discovering killing intent

The treehouse was small and cluttered with various items, looking extremely messy at first glance.

Several patched, ragged pieces of adult clothing hung on a rope, still damp and not yet dried by the sun.

On the opposite wall, a dagger was stabbed into the middle of a sheet of bamboo paper.

Drawn on the bamboo paper was a young man's portrait—Fang Yuan himself!

This meant someone had recently stayed in this hidden treehouse, and their target was unmistakably Fang Yuan.

"What does this person want, and why target me?" Thoughts raced through Fang Yuan's mind.

"Why did Old Man Wang's map indicate this treehouse? This person is clearly after me!"

Old Man Wang's family moving out of the village, isolating themselves...

Eldest son Wang Da, died on the mountains three years ago while hunting...

Wang Da's wife wanting to remarry, forced into suicide by Old Man Wang to earn a chastity memorial arch...

Old Man Wang chasing away all the young hunters seeking guidance...

Old Man Wang hiding the beast skin map and omitting these circles from the bamboo paper...

Young Wang Er remaining calm around Gu Masters, staying unmarried, and yearning to transcend the mortal realm...

Most importantly, the circles marked hidden shelters with clear human activity, and this person harbored intense hatred toward Fang Yuan...

Suddenly, Fang Yuan's eyes lit up as he saw the truth across time and space!

"Wang Da, did not die."

"Not only did he survive, he even became a demonic Gu Master by chance!"

"By some lucky chance—likely looting a dead Gu Master's fortune—he became a Gu Master three years ago. To avoid suspicion, he faked his death, which is why no one ever saw his corpse. Learning of this, Old Man Wang's family moved out of the village to live alone despite beast threats, all to hide the truth."

"Complications arose when Wang Da's wife disagreed, perhaps wanting to report him to the Gu Yue clan. The Wang family had to kill her, spreading rumors to obscure the truth. Something about her remarrying, or forcing her to commit suicide to hide the truth under all these rumors."

"Wang Da returned occasionally, so Old Man Wang chased away visiting hunters. Interacting with his brother dispelled Wang Er's fear of Gu Masters, sparking his ambitions!"

"For fear of exposing Wang Da, Wang Er remained unmarried even at his age. Wang Da could not stay at home, so Old Man Wang marked three red circles on the map—hidden shelters just like this one. With three hideouts, Wang Da rotated between them, living on the fringes of the three clans' authority."

Red indicated danger, but to Old Man Wang, these were his eldest son's hideouts. Omitting them from the bamboo paper protected his son, while omitting red crosses was meant to lure Fang Yuan to his death to avenge his family.

"Wang Da likely returned periodically, only to find his entire family dead. After investigating, he traced it to me, pinning my portrait to the wall with a dagger to swear revenge!"

"To think a beast skin map would provoke a demonic Gu Master. This world truly works in wondrous ways," Fang Yuan snickered.

Fang Yuan felt not a shred of regret, but rather complete ease.

Had he shown mercy when acquiring the map, Old Man Wang, Wang Er, and Sister Wang would have caused endless trouble, refusing to hand over the authentic map to protect Wang Da's secret.

Even if he had obtained the authentic map, Old Man Wang would have alerted Wang Da. Operating from the shadows against an exposed Fang Yuan, Wang Da would have held all the advantages.

"Good thing I struck first! I must always keep control in my own hands. That way, even if I didn't get the real map, I lost nothing and could simply snatch one from another hunter. Killing Old Man Wang and the girl forced Wang Da to spend substantial time uncovering my identity. Needless to say, those two young hunters have already been silenced," Fang Yuan reasoned.

Few people knew what happened, and Wang Da would not attack Jiang He; killing a clan member would trigger an investigation. But two mortal hunters vanishing on the mountain was easy to brush aside.

# Chapter 75: Resolution of the Demonic way!

Drawing on his past life's knowledge, Fang Yuan deduced Wang Da's existence—but how strong was he?

"Courage is built on strength. Having gathered my information and still seeking revenge, his cultivation must be above Rank one!"

"For three years he rotated between the three red-circled areas on the fringes of clan territory, risking discovery daily. This proves he lacks the strength to roam the outside world, which requires Rank three cultivation."

"This means that, at first estimate, he is Rank two."

Fang Yuan's eyes glinted coldly. "Reaching Rank two after three years means he likely has B or C grade talent, with forty to seventy percent primeval essence in his aperture."

"These three years, he could survive and at the same time, live at home despite Jiang He's observation, so he has to have a stealth-type of Gu worm at work."

"I kill others, so naturally others can come to kill me. It's nothing." Fang Yuan laughed softly.

Fang Yuan had long possessed this resolve—the true resolution of the demonic path!

"If he wants to kill me, this mid-year exam is his best chance. Normally students stay in the village, where a Rank two sneaking in would be courting death."

"Qing Mao Mountain is vast; hiding his tracks while searching for me alone is difficult. Now that this chance has arisen, he will almost certainly strike."

"He is a Rank two demonic Gu Master. At my current strength, I am no match for him, but that does not mean I have no chance of survival."

Escape!

Fang Yuan instantly decided.

Gu Masters almost never experience battle breakthroughs; fighting across realms relies entirely on having exceptional Gu worms.

The Spring Autumn Cicada was hibernating, and his other Gu worms could not bridge the realm gap.

Fighting to the death against an unwinnable foe was foolish, hot-blooded stupidity. Even a victory would not earn him fate's favor.

His preferred way was to prey on the weak, seize resources, and grow stronger until he surpassed his foes—then crush them ruthlessly.

"Which direction has the lowest chance of encountering Wang Da?" A map appeared in Fang Yuan's mind.

"Wang Da knows I have the beast skin map and will search the boar distribution areas. I must move in reverse to survive." An insane route surfaced in his mind as sunset settled over the mountain forest.

A pair of bloodshot eyes hid deep in the shadows, burning with hatred and fury that no tsunami could douse.

"Fang Yuan, I've finally found you…" Wang Da gritted his teeth, growling.

Under his gaze, a thin, pale youngster was traversing the forest not far away.

With his enemy nearby, Wang Da did not act immediately, glancing toward several other positions.

From each of these positions, an invigilator Gu Master was hidden.

"To kill Fang Yuan, I must eliminate these three invigilators first, or the pursuing Gu Masters will kill me."

"With Rank two middle stage cultivation and 50% primeval essence, I must strike in rapid succession before they discover the bodies..."

"Shadow Follower Gu." Wang Da closed his eyes, calling inwardly.

Immediately, darkness enshrouded his body, allowing him to move through the tree shadows.

In a thick bush nearby, a Gu Yue Rank two Gu Master sprawled, yawning in boredom.

One hand covered the Gu Master's mouth while dark purple, blade-sharp nails stabbed into his back, piercing straight to the heart.

The Gu Master stiffened, losing all signs of life.

"Used 10% primeval essence, 40% left." Wang Da checked his aperture and melted into the shadows again.

Moments later, the second Gu Master behind a rock was assaulted, his pupils shrinking to pinpricks as he collapsed.

"30% left." Wang Da chanted silently, turning into shadows again.

"Who is it!" Perched on a high branch, the third Gu Master sensed danger at the crucial moment and spun around, grabbing Wang Da's two poisonous hands as he struck.

"Damn it!" Wang Da laughed sinisterly as his nails grew to five centimeters long, piercing the Gu Master's forearm.

"Love Life Separation?!" The third Gu Master was shocked to the core as purple poison already spread across his face.

Knowing he lacked poison-resistance Gu and was doomed, he showed grim resolve and shouted, "Then let's die together!"

A moonblade shot out, slashing Wang Da's right shoulder and piercing straight through his back.

Wang Da grunted, swaying slightly, but the Gu Master had already passed away.

"Correct, this is Love Life Separation." Wang Da stood up on the branch, smiling bitterly.

Love Life Separation, the number one Rank two poison Gu! Refining it required Rank one life breath grass, widow spider, red needle scorpion, and a lover's heart.

To refine it, Wang Da had killed his deeply loving wife and dug out her heart!

"All for survival, I could only choose strength… this is my demonic path's resolution!" Red-eyed, Wang Da stared fixedly at the nearby youngster.

"I gave up love and had only kinship left, yet you took them from me! Fang Yuan…" he growled, "I'll make you deeply regret everything!"

# Chapter 76: Do you regret it?

"Kill!" Wang Da growled, leaping ferociously from the branch.

As he neared his mortal enemy, three moonblades suddenly shot out and intercepted him.

"A fourth Gu Master?" Wang Da dodged two moonblades, but the third struck his left leg.

"Damn..." Wang Da gritted his teeth, thinking inwardly, "Shadow Follower Gu!"

Instantly turning into dark shadow, his speed surged as he retreated soundlessly.

A Gu worm flew out as an old, weary voice rang out—

"Flash Blink Gu, explode."

The Gu worm detonated on command into blinding white light.

"Ah!" Wang Da shrieked. Exposed by the bright light, his shadowed form was forced back into human shape.

Flash Blink Gu was the complete counter to Shadow Follower Gu, forcing it into a three-hour recovery.

"Surrender now and my clan might spare your life!" A white-haired Rank two Gu Master appeared before him.

"Spare me? I'll kill you first!" Wang Da leaped forward, knowing stalling would only draw more defenders.

Gu Yue Sou snorted as his body hair intertwined into spiky, snow-white battle armor.

"If I cannot deal with you, I'll kill Fang Yuan first!" Three years of survival had made Wang Da cunning and ruthless.

He moved swiftly, bypassing Gu Yue Sou to attack his family's killer.

"Dream on!" Gu Yue Sou's spiked armor shot two sharp pins spiraling toward Wang Da.

Before him, Fang Yuan's face was filled with shock and panic.

Just as he was about to succeed, another figure blocked his path.

"Love Life Separation?" The middle-aged man remained unmoved by Wang Da's ferocious assault.

The middle-aged man activated Rock Skin Gu, turning his bare arms into thick, grey-white rock.

"Trying to hit me with that speed?" Wang Da sneered mockingly.

"Really? Jade Wind Wheel!" the Gu Master shouted as jade cyclones swirled around his rock arms like armbands.

"How can that be… urgh!" Shock crossed Wang Da's face as the rock arm swept him flying.

"5% primeval essence left..." He laughed bitterly, glaring at Fang Yuan with mad resolve. "Even if I die, you're coming with me!"

He ignored his injuries and rushed out.

The elderly Gu Master's hair formed spiral needles that shot out, piercing Wang Da from behind.

As Wang Da was about to strike, bright jade light burst from the other side.

"Jade Skin Gu!" Under the pressure of life and death, Fang Zheng screamed.

Wang Da's poisonous fingers stabbed forward; the Rank one Jade Skin Gu could not block his nails.

The elderly Gu Master shouted frantically as more snow-white needles riddled Wang Da's body.

Intense dizziness struck Wang Da. He laughed pathetically, knowing death was near.

"Wan Er…" He subconsciously called his wife's name as his knife pierced her chest.

"If I could start over, Wan Er, I would still do the same…" Tears streamed from Wang Da's bloodshot eyes.

Fang Zheng stared at him, his body glowing jade.

An unfamiliar Gu Master had rushed madly to tear him apart, yet Fang Zheng didn't know him.

Wang Da's nails sank one centimeter into the jade skin, then stopped.

He died.

Though it hadn't fully breached the jade skin, Love Life Separation—the deadliest Rank two poison—had already invaded his body.

# Chapter 77: An odd coincidence

An elephant-sized wild boar lay in the mud pit.

Wild boar king!

Though several hundred meters away, Fang Yuan still walked carefully with the wind.

"Though I can hunt normal wild boars now, I can only flee from this wild boar king. Even an average Rank two Gu Master couldn't kill it and might fall prey without knowing its Gu worms."

However, Fang Yuan's goal was not to hunt the beast king, but to evade Wang Da's pursuit.

Knowing Wang Da tracked him via the beast skin map, Fang Yuan headed straight into the beast king's territory.

Circling wide around the danger zone, Fang Yuan reached the hill where elders conducted the mid-year assessment.

An hour later, a mud-stained youngster with grass-strewn hair and a torn shirt ran up the hill carrying a bag.

Near a tent on the hill, students waited as academy guards tallied boar tusks, supervised by the academy elder.

"Why is the atmosphere so strange?" Fang Yuan frowned, sensing something significant had happened.

"Did you hear? An assassin struck earlier and killed several Rank two Gu Masters."

"It's true. I arrived early and saw clan elders rushing away with Gu Yue Fang Zheng."

"Hehe, Fang Zheng was the target because of his A-grade talent!"

Fang Yuan paused, immediately deducing the truth in his heart.

"Wang Da sought revenge on me, but my detour toward the beast king led him to stumble upon Fang Zheng. Mistaking Fang Zheng for me due to our identical looks, he attacked, but was stopped by the guarding elders. Right now the question is—Did Wang Da die?"

After brief contemplation, Fang Yuan decided to follow his original plan.

The academy elder looked grim over the assassination, suspecting the rival Bai and Xiong clans rather than a vendetta.

"The assassin died on the spot, but how are Fang Zheng's injuries?" the academy elder wondered just as an attendant handed him a paper.

The academy elder announced, "The mid-year exam results are: Gu Yue Chi Cheng sixteen tusks, Gu Yue Mo Bei fourteen tusks…"

The academy elder announced, "As such, the first place student is…"

"Halt!" Fang Yuan stepped out at this moment.

"Fang Yuan, you're late! Four tusks will be deducted as punishment!" Chi Cheng shouted.

Ignoring him, Fang Yuan grabbed the bottom of his bag and dumped out the contents.

Dozens of wild boar tusks piled up at his feet.

"How can there be so many?!" The academy elder stared in disbelief. "Fang Yuan, did you hunt all these?"

Fang Yuan cupped his fists. "I only hunted a dozen myself, but I found a hidden hunter's bag filled with tusks. The exam required gathering tusks without specifying we kill them ourselves, so I brought them back."

After staring at Fang Yuan for a moment, the academy elder declared, "Fang Yuan takes first place."

… The family head room's atmosphere was heavy.

Gu Yue Bo sat at the head while a dozen furious elders flanked him in two rows.

"Gu Yue Yao Ji, as our clan's top healer, how is Fang Zheng?" Gu Yue Bo asked.

Coughing twice, she replied slowly, "Clan Head, he is stable and out of danger, though still unconscious. His talent remains intact."

"Good that his talent is unharmed." Relieved, Gu Yue Bo turned to the Punishment Hall Elder. "Have you identified the assassin?"

The Punishment Hall Elder bowed. "Male, thirty-five, identity unknown. He possessed Shadow Follower Gu and Love Life Separation."

Gu Yue Bo nodded. "An assassin wielding Love Life Separation, the top Rank two poison… no wonder he killed three of our Gu Masters."

"Clan Head, we must investigate! It's either the Bai or Xiong clan!" Gu Yue Chi Lian shouted furiously.

"Shadow Follower Gu and Love Life Separation aren't typical Bai or Xiong pawns. He was likely an outside demonic Gu Master seeking clan backing by proving his loyalty through assassination. Regardless, those two clans are definitely behind this," Gu Yue Mo Chen said coldly.

Having vanished for three years, Wang Da's identity remained unknown. Unconcerned with a lowly servant, the elders blamed their rival clans.

At this moment, the academy elder walked in.

"Are the other students safe? What are the exam results, and where did Fang Zheng place?" Gu Yue Bo asked.

The academy elder reported truthfully; hearing that Fang Yuan placed first by finding a bag of boar tusks, Gu Yue Bo's eyes gleamed.

The hall fell silent. Sensing something amiss, the elders observed each other, turning the tense mood subtly peculiar.

# Chapter 78: Great rewards all according to plan

"Fang Yuan taking first place is no simple matter. Claiming he found a hidden bag of tusks is far too convenient."

"Someone must have prepared that bag for him—Fang Yuan couldn't do it alone. Someone is secretly backing him."

"This exam differed from previous years; many clan elders knew the exam details in advance."

"Only the elders present or the Clan Leader himself could have prepared that bag in advance!"

Fang Yuan was Fang Zheng's brother; despite being only C-grade, their blood tie made him well worth investing in.

For Clan Leader Gu Yue Bo, secretly aiding Fang Yuan would bring him into his faction while binding Fang Zheng even closer to his side.

Fang Zheng was already in the Clan Leader's faction; supporting him would only strengthen the leader's power. However, if an elder claimed Fang Yuan, his blood tie with Fang Zheng would make him a potent pawn against the clan leader.

Thus, every elder in the hall had a motive to secretly aid Fang Yuan.

Gu Yue Chi Lian pondered, "I didn't recruit Fang Yuan, so who did? Old Mo Chen? Possible. Fang Yuan killed his servant, but a servant's life means nothing. The Clan Leader is more likely; taking Fang Yuan would tighten his control over Fang Zheng. Yet the norm was always to recruit during the year-end exam. Acting now during the mid-year exam breaks the unspoken rules."

"Not breaking rules, but certainly a curveball. Who values Fang Yuan even more than I do?" Gu Yue Mo Chen mused.

Fang Yuan's blatant excuse was taken as a faction's bold declaration: Fang Yuan is protected; do not touch him.

Knowing he hadn't recruited Fang Yuan himself, Gu Yue Bo suspected Chi Lian and Mo Chen most.

Remaining neutral and outside politics, the academy elder thought more simply: "So an elder has recruited Fang Yuan. No wonder he spared Fang Zheng, Mo Bei, and Chi Cheng while extorting—his backer must be the Clan Leader, Chi Lian, or Mo Chen. This is good news; he is finally accepting reality and integrating into the clan. As a clan member, once he fully integrates, he will dedicate his life to the clan!"

Breaking the silence, Gu Yue Bo spoke, "Since they targeted Fang Zheng, we will retaliate! Dark Hall Elder, formulate a plan."

"As an A-grade talent of monumental importance, I will teach Fang Zheng personally from now on," Gu Yue Bo said.

"As for Gu Yue Fang Yuan…" Gu Yue Bo dragged his words.

The clan elders paid full attention. Was the clan leader hinting that he secretly supported Fang Yuan?

"Taking first place with C-grade talent is commendable. In my personal name, I reward him thirty primeval stones."

"No matter who recruited him, these thirty primeval stones express my goodwill amid external threats," Gu Yue Bo thought.

Wang Da is dead.

Three days later, Fang Yuan got the news.

Jiang He reported that the two young hunters had vanished, and the injured one committed suicide from depression.

Jiang He gave Fang Yuan a meaningful look; having seen Wang Da's corpse, he recognized him.

Failing to detect a demonic cultivator for three years would ruin Jiang He's future if investigated.

"Fang Yuan, as acquaintances, you get a 50% discount at my cousin Jiang Ya's shop from now on!" Jiang He once offered.

With everyone else dead, only the two of them knew the truth, and exposure would hardly affect Fang Yuan.

Killing servants would at most cost Fang Yuan a minor fine of primeval stones.

Fang Yuan accepted Jiang He's bribe calmly. Despite the unexpected danger, the outcome was favorable.

Through this affair, the foundationless Fang Yuan gained half an ally and the illusion of factional backing.

This phantom backer shielded Fang Yuan as he revealed his strength and cultivated toward Rank two.

He could clearly sense the academy elder treating him with newfound amicability.

Over ten days passed in a flash.

Following Fang Zheng and Mo Bei, Fang Yuan and Chi Cheng advanced almost simultaneously to Rank one upper stage.

While continuing extortion, he spared Fang Zheng, Mo Bei, and Chi Cheng. Laying low, his strength grew several times faster than in his past life.

# Chapter 79: Breakthrough to the sixth Gu in the inheritance ground

Time flew like a fleeting white steed, and summer yielded to autumn.

In the walled passage, Fang Yuan stood before the giant boulder once again.

"Having attained one boar's strength with the White Boar Gu, I shall push this boulder again today!"

Under the pressure of both arms, the boulder slowly gained momentum and rolled forward.

Pushing a few more steps, Fang Yuan moved past the boulder into a secret room.

The room had glowing red soil walls and a crude stone door at the far end.

"Don't tell me…" A thought struck Fang Yuan; he knelt and dug into the soil.

The second Earth Treasury flower!

Laughing heartily, Fang Yuan parted the petals and retrieved the slumbering Gu.

Once he deployed his primeval essence, it was instantly refined.

It was a Jade Skin Gu, resembling a flat green bedbug emitting jade glow.

"The Flower Wine Monk prepared the Jade Skin Gu to refine the White Jade Gu with my White Boar Gu."

Though he possessed five Gu, none offered defense; the Jade Skin Gu remedied this vulnerability.

A top Rank one defensive Gu, it consumed two jade rocks every ten days.

"Must I mine for jade rocks now to feed it?" Fang Yuan laughed bitterly.

Storing the Jade Skin Gu in his aperture, Fang Yuan slowly opened the stone door.

Beyond lay neither tunnel nor chamber, but a vast underground stone forest.

Jagged stone pillars riddled with dark holes formed an inverted subterranean forest.

He suddenly understood why the Flower Wine Monk provided the Jade Skin Gu.

"If I am not wrong…" Fang Yuan flicked his right hand, releasing a moonblade.

The eerie blue moonblade arced through the air, striking a dark hole in the pillar.

With a swoosh, a grey monkey leapt from the hole toward Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan shot three moonblades.

The monkey deftly swung its tail to dodge two moonblades before the third killed it.

"Jade eye stone monkeys," Fang Yuan murmured, extracting two pearl-like jade eyes from the remains.

Killing jade eye stone monkeys would easily solve the Jade Skin Gu's feeding problem.

"I not only have to feed the Jade Skin Gu, I also have to continue the inheritance. Flower Wine Monk's next clue should be hidden in this stone forest."

He stepped forward carefully, watching the stone pillars closely.

On his seventh step, dozens of glowing jade eyes peeked out from holes in the nearest pillar.

Each pair represented a stone monkey; their agility forced Fang Yuan to expend four moonblades to kill just one.

Each pillar housed at least a hundred stone monkeys, with countless more across the entire forest.

Fortunately, the monkeys never emerged unless someone approached within ten meters or provoked them.

# Chapter 80: A surge in battle strength

"Hurrumph!" Bristling its mane, a black wild boar charged wildly forward.

Fang Yuan stood calmly, watching the approaching boar with cold calculation.

Striding forward, he planted his feet firmly to meet the boar head-on without dodging.

Sidestepping the tusks, Fang Yuan slammed his shoulder into the boar's skull.

Upon impact, a faint green light flashed across Fang Yuan's shoulder.

Fang Yuan was pushed back three steps, while the boar retreated one.

Roaring, Fang Yuan grabbed the boar's tusk and raised his right fist, coated in jade glow.

His fist slammed down, making the wild boar squeal and thrash violently.

Meanwhile, his right fist pounded relentlessly into the boar.

Dropping to one knee, Fang Yuan drove his elbow into the boar's skull.

"Life is as splendid as summer flowers; death as quiet as autumn leaves," Fang Yuan mused, appreciating the scene.

"On the demonic path, victory and loss mean life and death; defeat brings only doom."

Sitting beside the carcass, Fang Yuan released the White Boar Gu to feed while turning his awareness inward toward his aperture.

Within his aperture, dark green primeval sea surged and ebbed.

His aperture held forty-four percent primeval sea at full capacity. After repeatedly activating the Jade Skin Gu during combat, thirty-six percent remained.

Expending eight percent of his refined dark green essence was still a notable cost.

"A Gu Master's strength grows with cultivation, reflected in their primeval essence—higher stages produce darker, more potent, and more durable essence. My black green primeval essence was on the basis of upper stage primeval essence, refined by the Liquor worm. It is unlike Fang Zheng, who is already Rank one peak stage cultivation already." Thinking of this, Fang Yuan's gaze shone.

"I'm half a month from peak stage, but C-grade talent cannot match A or B grades."

Every few days, he had to hunt stone monkeys to feed the Jade Skin Gu and search the forest for the Flower Wine Monk's next clue.

"Exploring the stone forest confirmed the next inheritance clue must lie within the forest itself."

As Fang Yuan pondered, a shadow stepped over dry branches and approached.

Limping with mangy brown fur, it had only one alert, dangerous left eye remaining.

Fang Yuan rose swiftly, watching the old wolf in silence.

Previously, slaying a boar would exhaust his primeval essence and combat strength, forcing him to avoid other beasts.

Backed by the Jade Skin Gu, handling a crippled wolf was now effortless for him.

Satiated, the White Boar Gu returned satisfied to Fang Yuan's aperture.

The old wolf eyed the boar carcass, stripped of all meat by the White Boar Gu.

Its green eye shrank; backing away, the wolf darted into the bushes.

The next moment, the old wolf whined pitifully.

Twigs snapped within the bushes.

Heavy, fearless footsteps drew closer, making Fang Yuan's pupils contract.

"Killing that cunning wolf in a second..." His gaze turned icy.

---

# Chapter 81: Rank two Initial stage!

As the footsteps neared, the hillside foliage was shoved aside, and a burly man stepped into view.

With spiky short black hair and a bare torso, his thick, muscular frame glowed bright red.

Hanging from his waist were dead foxes, hares, pheasants, and the one-eyed wolf that had just fled.

Slightly surprised upon spotting Fang Yuan, he strode past without stopping.

"Gu Yue Chi Shan," Fang Yuan recalled, watching his retreating back.

A representative of the Chi faction at Rank two upper stage, his background mirrored Fang Yuan's.

"Clan youths awaken at fifteen to enter the academy, graduating at sixteen to form five-man squads, carry out clan missions, and inherit family fortunes. Only those enduring severe hardships reach Rank three, becoming clan elders among the ruling elite."

"With winter approaching, my year at the academy is nearly over, culminating in the year-end arena tournament. After the tournament, I must vacate the student dormitory and live on my own."

"I graduated at Rank one middle stage in my past life, but now I will reach peak stage. Reaching this far with C grade talent cost a fortune in primeval stones."

He raised six Gu worms by himself!

Refining essence with the Liquor worm and using the White Boar Gu drained immense essence, forcing him to constantly absorb primeval stones.

"Extorting classmates has been my primary income, but graduation will end both the academy allowance and extortion. But if I get first for the year end exam, I can get a hundred and fifty primeval stones as reward," Fang Yuan contemplated in his heart.

… Time flies, autumn leaves and winter comes.

Three tournament stages were erected in the academy arena.

The academy elder, clan leader, and several clan elders sat under a canopy.

Fifty-seven students stood erect in the arena, their noses red and breaths steaming in the frigid air.

The academy elder announced, "A year has passed. Tomorrow's year-end exam will test your achievements! The clan leader, clan elders, and family seniors will observe firsthand, scouting top performers for their squads."

"First place earns a hundred and fifty primeval stones and priority in selecting Gu! Now, begin your final cultivation assessment!"

"Gu Yue Chi Cheng." The middle-aged female Gu Master called.

The diminutive Gu Yue Chi Cheng puffed his chest and stepped forward.

Checking him, she announced, "Gu Yue Chi Cheng, Rank one peak stage!"

"Gu Yue Mo Bei." Soon, the examiner called.

The horse-faced Mo Bei quickly walked out.

"Gu Yue Mo Bei, Rank one peak stage." Declared peak stage, Mo Bei glared defiantly at Chi Cheng as he returned.

"Gu Yue Fang Yuan." The middle-aged female Gu Master called.

Fang Yuan walked up expressionlessly.

Opening her eyes in shock, she declared, "Gu Yue Fang Yuan, Rank one peak stage!"

"Gu Yue Fang Zheng." The female Gu Master finally said.

Closing then snapping open her eyes, she gasped in shock, "Gu Yue Fang Zheng, Rank two initial stage!"

"Even higher than my past life..." Fang Yuan chuckled softly with lowered lids. Having read Fang Zheng's expression earlier, he wasn't surprised.

Six months after Gu Yue Bo gifted him a Jade Skin Gu to reach Rank two first, Fang Zheng had succeeded.

"Clan leader, I succeeded! I will earn everyone's approval from now on. Brother, you are no longer a shadow over my heart—I have surpassed you and left the past behind!"

# Chapter 82: Start of the year end examination

Gu Masters streamed into the academy in the morning.

"No need to rush, Yao Hong. The clan leader already decided our new squad member long ago," the green-haired Gu Master chuckled.

"The A grade talent Fang Zheng?" Gu Yue Yao Hong clicked her tongue, grumbling, "The clan leader just wants us to babysit him!"

"Babysitting him won't be easy," the green-haired Gu Master sighed. "Come on, let's head in."

The sun gradually rose, as the students entered the fray.

"Oh? Two Gu Yue Fang Zhengs?" Yao Hong blurted upon spotting both brothers.

Gu Yue Qing Shu, the green-haired Gu Master, sighed helplessly, "Did you see yesterday's information? Fang Zheng has a twin brother who looks nearly identical, but only has C grade talent."

"Oh, so that's it. Wasn't Fang Yuan the one who wrote poetry as a child? Are we recruiting him?" Yao Hong patted her forehead, speaking whatever came to mind.

Qing Mao shook his head, "The clan leader specifically instructed not to recruit him; he seems to be observing something. Besides, their relationship is poor, so Fang Yuan probably wouldn't join anyway."

Clan leader Gu Yue Bo and authoritative clan elders Gu Yue Chi Lian and Gu Yue Mo Chen entered, taking seats under the tent.

Following the clan leader's speech, the year-end exam began.

The three stages held fights at the same time.

"Their physical fighting skills are high this year," Yao Hong soon noticed.

"Hehehe, all thanks to Fang Yuan." Gu Yue Qing Shu laughed.

Yao Hong exclaimed in shock, "Fang Yuan is truly daring, almost fearless. Bullying even his own brother—how interesting."

As the tournament progressed, the Gu Master then announced, "Next round: Gu Yue Fang Zheng versus Gu Yue Mo Bei!"

# Chapter 83: Sweeping strong foes, Fang Zheng displays his skills

Fang Zheng and Mo Chen both walked up the stage.

"Fang Zheng, don't think I'll lose just because you're Rank two! Today I'll defeat someone from a higher realm," Mo Bei declared, gritting his teeth.

"Come." Fang Zheng roared, rushing out ferociously.

Mo Bei's heart leaped; instead of exchanging moonblades from afar, Fang Zheng charged straight into melee.

Mo Bei stepped back to create distance, simultaneously flicking his wrist to fire a moonblade.

Unfazed, Fang Zheng rolled to dodge the moonblade and pressed his pursuit as lunar energy gathered in his palm.

Unsuited to this combat style, Mo Bei quickly fell at a disadvantage.

Gu Yue Qing Shu's gaze shone, "Fang Zheng narrowed the distance to six meters, yet dodges moonblades proficiently. Beyond the clan leader's guidance, he clearly put in immense effort and endured great hardship."

Once Fang Zheng closed within six meters of Mo Bei, he engaged in a rapid firefight with his Moonlight Gu.

Even if unable to evade in time, he could deploy his Jade Skin Gu's jade-green glow to block incoming moonblades.

Though Mo Bei resisted fiercely, after fifteen minutes he was covered in wounds, blood soaking his shirt red.

Seeing this, the presiding Gu Master announced, "Victory goes to Gu Yue Fang Zheng."

Gu Masters quickly healed Fang Zheng and restored his primeval essence.

This time, he was against Gu Yue Chi Cheng.

Chi Cheng chuckled dryly at Fang Zheng, "Great! You beat that rascal Mo Bei—defeating you now kills two birds with one stone."

Saying nothing, Fang Zheng kept his lips tight and charged toward him.

"Dragonpill cricket Gu!" With a thought, an orange-red glow enveloped Chi Cheng's legs, allowing him to leap ten meters back.

Chi Cheng scoffed, "Without a speed Gu, your two legs can't catch me—your tactic won't work on me!"

"Is that so?" Fang Zheng halted and stared intently at Chi Cheng.

Smiling with resolute eyes, he shouted, "Keep dodging, but every use of your Dragonpill cricket Gu consumes primeval essence. You only have green copper essence, while my Rank two red steel essence gives me thrice your endurance. You'll run out of essence first!"

"You!" Chi Cheng was shocked. Having overlooked his fatal weakness, he couldn't refute Fang Zheng, and his fighting spirit dampened.

Eventually, Chi Cheng exposed a fatal flaw and was knocked off the stage.

"Gu Yue Fang Zheng wins!" Gu Master announced.

"Last match, Gu Yue Fang Zheng vs Gu Yue Fang Yuan!" A while later, an announcer shouted.

# Chapter 84: Trample ruthlessly!

"Fang Zheng..." The clan leader watched closely. "Defeat your brother. With red steel essence and the Jade Skin Gu, you hold every advantage. Break through this obstacle, and you shall be reborn!"

The two brothers stood on the arena, staring at each other.

The younger brother's fighting spirit blazed, while the elder's gaze was deep as an abyss.

"Brother..." Clenching his fists, Fang Zheng stared firmly at Fang Yuan. "Surrender! I'm at Rank two with over 80% red steel essence. With merely 44% green copper essence, you stand no chance."

Fang Yuan glanced calmly at him. "If primeval essence alone decided victory, why bother fighting?"

"Since you're so determined, Brother, I must attack!" Before finishing his sentence, Fang Zheng charged forward.

Fang Yuan's expression remained like steel before the charging Fang Zheng. Standing his ground, blue moonlight gathered in his right palm.

Stomping the ground firmly, Fang Yuan advanced toward Fang Zheng instead of retreating.

"What!" Caught off guard by Fang Yuan's advance, Fang Zheng panicked and hurriedly fired a moonblade.

Fang Yuan rushed quickly, and twisted his body, avoiding the moonblade by a hair's breadth.

Fang Zheng instinctively stepped back as Fang Yuan breached his six-meter comfort zone.

Retreating, Fang Zheng flicked his wrist repeatedly, sending moonblades flying to force Fang Yuan back.

With fluid footwork, Fang Yuan effortlessly dodged each incoming moonblade.

How could Fang Zheng, trained for just a year by a Rank four Gu Master, match Fang Yuan's five hundred years of battle experience?

Every shift of Fang Zheng's shoulders and wrists telegraphed his actions, allowing Fang Yuan to easily anticipate every move.

Overwhelmed with panic by Fang Yuan's relentless pressure, Fang Zheng lost his tempo and forgot to deploy his Jade Skin Gu.

Striking from a blind angle, Fang Yuan smashed a fist into Fang Zheng's stomach.

Doubling over in pain, Fang Zheng reflexively guarded his head and retreated.

"Where is he?" Wide-eyed, Fang Zheng peered through the gap between his arms, searching for Fang Yuan.

"Behind!" Fang Zheng suddenly felt a heavy force slam into his waist.

Toughened by rigorous training, Fang Zheng flipped on the ground while firing a moonblade backward from his palm.

Fang Yuan sidestepped around him, letting the moonblade strike empty air.

Thinking he had created distance, Fang Zheng scrambled up to regroup, only to hear a sudden whoosh.

Immediately, Fang Yuan's fist struck him harshly on the ear.

Dizziness overwhelmed Fang Zheng, sending him collapsing to the floor.

"Damn it!" Fang Zheng was furious and tried to get up.

Under everyone's eyes, Fang Yuan lifted his right leg and stepped down firmly.

Fang Zheng's head slammed violently into the stage floor with a loud crash like a rock.

"Fuck!" Fang Zheng raged and tried to get up again.

Fang Yuan stared coldly, trampling down once again.

Fang Zheng's head struck the stage again, splitting his scalp and bleeding profusely.

"Damn! Damn it!" Gritting his teeth with blazing fury, Fang Zheng raised his head, trying once more to get up.

Fang Yuan's third stomp pinned Fang Zheng down, grinding his face into the floor.

"Right, I still have the Moonlight Gu!" In his desperation, inspiration struck Fang Zheng.

His right palm gathered moonlight again.

With a light sound, he shot out a moonblade onto Fang Zheng's right palm.

Argh!

Fang Zheng screamed as excruciating pain sent his body into lightning-like spasms.

His palm was pierced nearly to the bone, the Moonlight Gu upon it grievously injured.

# Chapter 85: Hot-blooded and cold-blooded

The crowd cheered fiercely, filling Fang Zheng's ears with encouragement.

Break the shadows, dispel the darkness! "ARGHHHHHHHHH!" Fang Zheng roared, his body radiating green jade light.

"What's that?" The crowd silenced."Jade Skin Gu!" Someone shouted.Bam!

"That's it, Fang Zheng! Stand up and be reborn!" The clan leader cheered silently. "Jade Skin Gu..." Fang Yuan's eyelids drooped. Pulling back his foot, Fang Yuan watched him slowly rise, bathed in the Jade Skin Gu's green glow.

Moved, the clan leader rose instinctively, seeing the bright future of the Gu Yue clan.

"I stood up, Brother! I will defeat you!" Fang Zheng's eyes blazed with resolve, bright green light cloaking him like jade armor.

Fang Yuan drew back his right arm like a bow. "Little brother, you're still far from defeating me." "Brother, you've lost!" Fang Zheng scoffed. With essence remaining, not even a moonblade could breach his jade barrier, let alone a bare fist.

Saying nothing, Fang Yuan delivered his answer straight with his fist.

The next instant, Fang Yuan's right fist struck Fang Zheng's cheek. Bam! Blood sprayed as the jade light shattered like broken mirror fragments and vanished. "What?!" Reeling from the shock and nearly breaking his neck, Fang Zheng hurriedly retreated. Fang Yuan closed in with a bow step, pulling back his left fist to strike upward! Bam!!

The strike hit the other side of Fang Zheng's face, throwing his head back amid flying blood and shattered jade light. He staggered back three steps, ears buzzing with severe dizziness. "How can this... be!"

Fang Zheng hit the ground, his defensive light fading as he immediately lost consciousness.

Total silence fell over the arena. Fang Yuan's fists were mangled and bloody, exposing cracked bone.

Fang Yuan stood motionless, surveying the stunned crowd with cold indifference as if his mangled hands belonged to someone else.

Dead silence reigned. Under the pavilion, the clan leader remained standing, utterly dumbfounded. "Sorry to disappoint you all," Fang Yuan remarked flatly.

# Chapter 86: Suspicion and probing

Being ruthless to others is easy; being ruthless to oneself is difficult.

Shattering the Jade Skin Gu barehanded was brutal to his brother, but harsher on himself.

"I'll go take a look." Unable to bear it any longer, the academy elder leaped onto the stage.

Examining Fang Zheng, he sighed in relief; the youth had merely fainted from head trauma.

"Unbelievable—he suffered such severe injuries through the Jade Skin Gu from bare hands alone." Shocked, the academy elder looked up at Fang Yuan with piercing eyes.

"Fang Yuan, I'll heal you." The academy elder stood up, strode over, and grasped his forearm.

Fang Yuan's flesh was mangled, exposing cracked bones from the impact.

Gentle moonlight emanated from the academy elder's hand, nourishing Fang Yuan's wounds.

The cracks in the bone began to mend as flesh and skin rapidly regenerated.

While healing him, the academy elder focused his senses to probe Fang Yuan's aperture.

Inside the aperture, dark green copper primeval essence surged in waves.

A plump, white Liquor worm was swimming playfully in the primeval sea.

The aperture's white crystal walls clearly reflected Fang Yuan's Rank one peak stage cultivation.

Without breaking focus, the academy elder continued probing Fang Yuan's body.

Ultimately, he detected only the Moonlight Gu and Little Light Gu on Fang Yuan's palm.

"No other Gu worms... did he break the Jade Skin Gu through raw strength alone?" Suspicion gleamed in the elder's eyes.

"Thank you for the treatment, Elder." Fang Yuan withdrew his arm and shook it lightly.

Fang Yuan felt no gratitude, well aware the elder's true purpose was inspecting his aperture.

Anticipating this scrutiny, Fang Yuan had hidden his extra Gu in the secret cave, while the concealed Spring Autumn Cicada was impossible to detect.

Finding nothing, the academy elder frowned. Though suspicious, he lacked any pretext to question Fang Yuan before the crowd.

"Well done, Fang Yuan. Keep working hard." Patting his shoulder, the elder announced loudly, "First place in this year's final exam goes to Fang Yuan!"

Fang Yuan stepped off the stage, smirking inwardly as he listened to the crowd's murmurs.

Clan leader Gu Yue Bo remained standing, frowning deeply.

"Under my guidance, Fang Zheng advanced first and built his confidence. Now that confidence is shattered—all my hard work up in smoke." Gu Yue Bo sighed deeply, feeling a growing sense of distaste and resentment toward Fang Yuan.

Not only the clan leader, but the other elders wore solemn, conflicted expressions.

Clan leader Gu Yue Bo smiled, "Fang Yuan, claiming first place with C grade talent is an unprecedented feat! You are rewarded with one hundred primeval stones and priority in selecting a Gu worm. Furthermore, I grant you the right to join any team of your choosing. Whichever group you want, you may join!"

"Though I decided on the spot, the clan elders surely agree with me," Gu Yue Bo smiled broadly at the elders beside him.

Led by Gu Yue Chi Lian and Gu Yue Mo Chen, the elders looked grim and some even frowned, yet none stopped him.

Fang Yuan's heart jumped.

Trouble had come.

# Chapter 87: Attitude is the mask of the heart

Fang Yuan saw through this action—it was a probing test from the clan leader.

His gaze swept the arena, noting the top three groups: Qing Shu's, Chi Shan's, and Mo Yan's.

"Joining any of the three means offending one of the clan's three major factions. That will not do! Sigh, I originally wanted to keep a low profile and join Jiang He's group, but Jiang He's group is backed by the punishment hall elder. Under everyone's watchful eye, if I join abruptly, that is inappropriate as well." Fang Yuan inevitably hesitated.

Gu Yue Bo smiled wider, continuing, "Fang Yuan, if you don't choose, I'll select for you."

"Hmph, forcing me?" Fang Yuan's eyes narrowed, guessing Gu Yue Bo would assign him to Chi Shan's or Mo Yan's group.

Gu Yue Bo knew he had not recruited Fang Yuan. Doing this cleared his own suspicion, weakened his rivals, and exposed the mysterious faction—killing three birds with one stone.

"I cannot let him dictate this. I must choose a mediocre group." Fang Yuan prepared to speak.

Suddenly, someone called out, "Why not join our team? We need an attacker."

The speaker was of average height with sickly, sallow skin and sharp, triangular eyes.

"It's the sickly snake Gu Yue Jiao San," someone identified him.

"Gu Yue Jiao San? Just a trash team with no background!" The leader and elders looked disappointed.

"Jiao San…" Fang Yuan's eyes shone with an undetectable dark shine.

"Then I'll join your group," Fang Yuan agreed immediately, cutting off Gu Yue Bo.

The clan leaders and elders' expression turned dim.

Three days later.

A group of five people ran in the snow.

Leading them was the average-height man with sickly yellow skin—Gu Yue Jiao San.

As he ran, he turned to the silent Fang Yuan with a kind smile. "Fang Yuan, don't be nervous. This is your first clan mission, but it's simple. Just follow us and learn."

"Yes," Fang Yuan replied calmly, looking straight ahead.

Jiao San's warm demeanor made Fang Yuan think of the legend of Ren Zu.

Legend says that when Ren Zu used Rules and Regulation Gu to gain strength, he lost wisdom, leaving behind three Gu: Suspicion, Trust, and Attitude.

Ren Zu grabbed the Attitude Gu.

Attitude Gu yielded to Ren Zu, saying, "From now on, I am at your command. Wear me and you can use my ability."

Attitude Gu resembled a mask. Ren Zu tried to wear it, but it fell off even when tied with rope.

"What's going on?" Ren Zu was troubled.

Attitude Gu laughed. "Human, you have no heart. Attitude is the mask of the heart; without one, how can you wear me?"

Ren Zu realized he had already given his heart to hope.

He was heartless.

The heartless cannot wear attitude; to someone with intentions, attitude is merely a mask.

"This warm attitude is merely Jiao San's mask. What is his true intention?" Fang Yuan pondered.

# Chapter 88: Initial display of authority, picking on, and suppression

Gu Yue Jiao San looked up and said, "It's getting late. Our mission is to collect permafrost decayed soil. It's easy but time-consuming. Speed up and stay close. Fang Yuan, tell us if you can't keep up. Don't worry, you're a newcomer, so we'll look after you."

Gu Yue Jiao San smiled very amicably.

Fang Yuan nodded silently.

The other three exchanged glances. The sky was still bright; Jiao San was simply establishing his authority over Fang Yuan.

"Let's go," Jiao San said lightly, striding out first.

Fang Yuan's eyes gleamed as he matched the others' speed, following closely behind.

Half an hour later, Jiao San halted at their destination.

He turned back, praising Fang Yuan with a smile, "Well done! Truly worthy of this year's champion, keeping pace the entire way."

The two waited until the other three members caught up.

Jiao San glared and barked, "Stand straight! Haven't you embarrassed yourselves enough? Look at Fang Yuan, then look at yourselves. Reflect on this after our mission!"

The three straightened up with lowered heads, thoroughly reprimanded and not daring to speak back.

Only their gaze towards Fang Yuan changed.

"Enough, buck up." Jiao San pointed ahead. "This valley has plenty of permafrost decayed soil. Split up to harvest it, then gather here in an hour. Kong Jing, distribute the tools."

A male Gu Master named Gu Yue Kong Jing stepped forward.

Activating his storage Gu, he produced their equipment. Soon, five steel spades and five strapped wooden boxes lay on the snow.

"Let's go," Jiao San waved, leading them into the valley.

Carrying his spade and wooden box, Fang Yuan picked another direction.

An hour later, everyone was stunned to see Fang Yuan return with a full box of permafrost decayed soil.

Even Jiao San's box was only half full.

"It's completely permafrost decayed soil!" a member inspected closely in shock.

"Fang Yuan, how did you gather so much frozen earth?" a female member asked curiously.

Fang Yuan raised an eyebrow, snowlight reflecting clearly in his eyes.

He chuckled, "The academy elder taught that permafrost decayed soil is fertilizer created when snow freezes a swamp. It is purplish-black and foul-smelling, though the frost traps the odor. The clan likely issued this mission to fertilize the moon orchid flowers in the underground cave."

His words left the four frozen on the spot.

Gu Yue Jiao San's eyes gleamed. "Good job, Fang Yuan."

His warm smile turned slightly forced as he praised him.

Jiao San turned to the others. "Our mission is complete. Pass your tools to Kong Jing, and let's return."

When they got back to the village, it was already afternoon.

Leaving the internal affairs hall, Jiao San distributed their six earned primeval stones—taking two for himself and giving one to each member.

Fang Yuan kept his primeval stone stealthily.

Fang Yuan thought, "When a newcomer joins a squad, the clan increases mission rewards to support them. This mission normally pays two primeval stones at most, but tripled because of me. Logically, I should have received more. The snow sprint established authority, gathering soil alone was hazing, and this stone distribution is outright suppression."

A primeval stone or two didn't matter to Fang Yuan, but he wondered why Gu Yue Jiao San was suppressing him when they had never interacted.

"Unless…" An inspiration struck Fang Yuan.

# Chapter 89: A sickly snake coiling around the legs

The five stood on the walkway.

Gu Yue Jiao San smiled warmly. "Fang Yuan, your performance was impressive. As a new graduate, let me explain how clan missions work."

"First, every Gu Master must complete at least one mission monthly. The more and better you complete, the higher your evaluation."

"Second, missions are issued by the internal or external affairs halls. A Gu Master can only take one at a time and must complete it."

"Third, abandoning a mission severely lowers your evaluation. Rank one Gu Masters cannot abandon missions; Rank two Gu Masters may only abandon one per year."

"Fourth, evaluations dictate your future. A higher evaluation means a brighter prospect in the clan."

Fang Yuan nodded. He already knew all this; Jiao San hadn't lied, but had omitted crucial details.

"Now that this mission is done, I accepted a wild deer hunt. It's safe and good practice for you," Jiao San told Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan laughed coldly inside, yet replied, "Thank you for your care, leader."

Gu Yue Kong Jing chimed in, "You should thank the leader, Fang Yuan. Simple missions offer little reward; he picked it just to look after you."

Jiao San waved his hand magnanimously. "Don't pressure him. Having just graduated, he must be short on primeval stones."

"How could he be? Placing first in the exam earned him a hundred primeval stones!" a female Gu Master said enviously. "Even to us, that's a huge sum."

Another female Gu Master leaned close to Fang Yuan. "A word of advice: you don't need to flatter us, but you must flatter the leader. The leader writes part of our evaluation. If he says you're outstanding, you are; if he says you performed poorly, you're done for."

Throughout it all, Gu Yue Jiao San watched with a smile.

He waved them off with a genial laugh. "Nonsense, I am always impartial and cannot be bribed. But don't worry, Fang Yuan, I'll take good care of your evaluation."

An ordinary youth would have been manipulated by them, unaware they were exploiting a newcomer's naivety.

But Fang Yuan's iris was calm as the sea.

Fang Yuan didn't give a damn about the evaluation.

His goal was to reach Rank three quickly and leave, never intending to spend a century slogging for the clan.

After a brief silence, Fang Yuan said, "I understand. I have other matters to attend to, so I'll take my leave."

Jiao San's mouth twitched.

Jiao San suppressed his temper, catching up to block him with a smile. "Don't rush off, Fang Yuan. You're looking to rent a place, right? Let us come—I have connections and know the rental market well."

"I do need a place since I can't stay at the hostel. Any suggestions?" Fang Yuan asked calmly.

"I know several cheap places in good locations," Jiao San smiled, leading the way.

Night fell without Fang Yuan finding a suitable house.

Fang Yuan shook his head. "I lack primeval stones; I must be frugal."

Seeing that Fang Yuan kept his silence, Jiao San said, "Actually the big hall's first floor is good too. Co-staying with other people causes the rent to reduce as well."

As Fang Yuan prepared to leave, Matron Shen appeared.

"Young Master Fang Yuan, I finally found you! Master and Madam prepared a banquet and sent me to invite you home. Living at home is far better than renting outside," she urged.

Fang Yuan laughed coldly inside. Matron Shen's timing was perfect—someone had clearly tipped her off!

Just as expected, his uncle and aunt were behind this.

And Jiao San was the chain they used to bind him.

Fang Yuan's silence looked like hesitation.

"Fang Yuan, why not stay with your aunt and uncle instead of making life difficult? Renting costs too many primeval stones," Jiao San chimed in with feigned concern, his eyes gleaming ominously.

# Chapter 90: It’s just a little wind frost

In front of them, Matron Shen was kneeling.

Uncle broke the silence. "To think Fang Yuan is dead set on opposing me. I intended to placate him with kind words, keep him home, and then find an excuse to expel him. Yet he didn't fall for it! He flatly refused without room for negotiation, not even setting foot inside!"

Aunt gritted her teeth, flustered. "The scoundrel is sixteen; we can't withhold the inheritance now that it's on record at the internal affairs hall. What do we do now?!"

"Leave us," Uncle dismissed Matron Shen, sneering. "Don't panic. I've prepared for this all year. First, reclaiming the assets requires Rank one middle stage cultivation. He reached peak stage and placed first in the exam—impressive indeed. Hehe..."

"However, claiming the family fortune isn't so simple! Rank one middle stage is only a prerequisite. To divide assets, Fang Yuan must apply and pass an internal affairs qualification mission—a rule meant to prevent reckless asset division and internal strife."

Aunt understood. "So he must complete the mission before receiving his parents' inheritance."
