"Exactly," Uncle sneered. "And missions are assigned to squads. To finish it, Fang Yuan must rely on his squad—a clan policy designed to foster squad unity and cohesion."

… Nightfall arrived and the snow stopped.

After turning Matron Shen away, Fang Yuan brushed off the group's advice and took his leave alone.

But Fang Yuan did not regret entering this group.

At the arena, he had been cornered; Jiao San's invitation provided an escape from that predicament.

Had he refused, his aunt and uncle would have devised unpredictable schemes against him. Inside the group, he could clearly see their ploys and counter them.

"I have ways to solve this. The simplest is eliminating Jiao San or assassinating Uncle and Aunt, leaving no one to contest the assets. But that is too risky; they are all Rank two Gu Masters while my cultivation is low. Even if I kill them, the aftermath would be troublesome. Unless I find a good opportunity to exploit... but such chances rely on luck."

"Inviting a greater problem to solve a small one is foolish. Oh? I'm here," Fang Yuan said, stopping before a worn-out bamboo building.

The dilapidated bamboo building stood like a dying old man panting for his last breath in the harsh winter.

Due to its run-down state, the rent was much cheaper and calculated daily rather than monthly.

"Other places might hold traps set by Uncle and Aunt, but my past life's memories confirm this place is safe." Fang Yuan knocked on the door.

After settling the rental terms, he was led to the second floor by the landlord.

Instead of sleeping, Fang Yuan sat on the bed and began cultivating.

Surges rippled across the green bronze primeval sea, crashing like frenzied beasts against the aperture walls.

Before long, a large portion of the forty-four percent primeval sea was rapidly depleted.

Black-green primeval essence continuously battered the crystal wall, spreading deep, prominent cracks across its surface.

Once his primeval essence was depleted and the assault stopped, the crystal wall began to heal, erasing the cracks.

Unsurprised, Fang Yuan gathered his spirits and opened his eyes.

"Advancing to Rank two is a safer way to break Jiao San's lock. Rank one Gu Masters cannot forfeit missions, but Rank two Gu Masters can once a year. If I advance to Rank two, I can give up on my mission and apply for the asset splitting."

"Yet breaking through to Rank two is no easy feat," Fang Yuan sighed, getting off the bed to pace the small room.

"Normally, reaching Rank two requires fifty-five percent black-green primeval essence. With only forty-four percent, my talent falls short—truly proving aptitude is crucial in cultivation!"

Thinking of this, Fang Yuan slowed in his steps.

Having wandered over to the window, he pushed it open.

Wind rattled the bamboo lattice, revealing a sprawling view of snow-covered mountains.

Under the moonlight, white jade snow spread across the landscape, transforming the pristine world into a crystal palace.

Moonlit snow illuminated Fang Yuan's calm, youthful face, his smooth brow, and eyes deep as an ancient moonlit spring.

Feeling the cold wind on his face, the youth chuckled, "Merely a bit of wind and frost."

# Chapter 91: Fang Yuan, we’re very worried about you

After a sleepless night, Fang Yuan stepped out at dawn and began buying supplies lavishly.

Gu Yue Jiao San and his Gu Masters waited anxiously at the village's northern gate.

Half an hour later, Fang Yuan had still not appeared.

Jiao San frowned darkly. "Whether he saw through us is secondary. We must find him—he might be trying to break through to Rank two on his own. Once he reaches Rank two, he can abandon this mission and apply to split the assets. Go find him! The village is small with few rentals. Find him before he has time to break through!"

"Yes!"

In the room, Fang Yuan sat on the bed.

"Not enough primeval stones," Fang Yuan sighed, his expression grim.

Like this, he had seven Gu worms on hand!

"At this rate, I can only hold out for two months. Reclaiming my inheritance is crucial, but doing so requires advancing to Rank two." Fang Yuan's gaze turned dark.

"Though my C-grade talent is only forty-four percent, reaching Rank two isn't impossible. The easiest way is finding a talent-raising Gu. Second, a supportive Gu like the Liquor worm could help break the barrier. Lastly, a senior Gu Master could assist, but foreign primeval essence causes severe backlash unless purged later with a Cleansing Gu."

"None of these work for me. I have no allies to assist, nor would I ever leave my aperture at someone else's mercy. Even if I obtained one, I couldn't keep or use it."

"That leaves one final, brute-force method: using primeval stones!" Fang Yuan's eyes flashed as he gripped a stone tightly.

"Though forty-four percent is insufficient, absorbing stones while attacking will sustain the assault, letting me smash the walls in four to five days!"

To keep his primeval essence from running dry and letting the cracks heal, Fang Yuan deliberately slowed his assault.

By the second afternoon, their search finally bore fruit.

A female Gu Master reported excitedly to Jiao San, "Found him! The landlord confirmed Fang Yuan rented the second floor of a run-down bamboo building."

"Hmph, he really is trying to break through in secret," Jiao San sneered. "Let's visit him and show some concern. He is a newcomer, after all."

The four immediately came to Fang Yuan's residence.

Jiao San tore down the door note stating Fang Yuan had entered seclusion to attempt Rank two, requesting no disturbances.

He laughed coldly, knocking on the door.

"Little brother Fang Yuan, are you in?" he yelled loudly. "How could you enter seclusion without telling us?"

Jiao San raised his voice, "Fang Yuan, why aren't you answering? Did something happen? Breaking through isn't easy; you should have asked us for guidance. Fang Yuan! Did you faint?"

Kong Jing chimed in on cue, "Leader, he hasn't answered—let's break in and save him!"

Jiao San's mouth curled, indicating to Kong Jing.

With a loud crash, the door flew inward, slamming onto the bed.

# Chapter 92: The future is in sight

The room was empty, Fang Yuan was not inside.

"Get the landlord!" Jiao San kicked the door aside.

"Looking for me? I was looking for you! Breaking my door and making a racket—how capable," the elderly landlord snapped firmly.

"Our mistake, we will compensate you," Jiao San chuckled dryly. Though furious inside, he handed over primeval stones with a generous extra.

The landlord's expression softened slightly. "If you're looking for that tenant, he hasn't returned since yesterday. He paid a month's rent, bought heaps of supplies, and asked where to get cheap coal. I told him about the northern valley mine. He could dig coal there for free. He thanked me, left, and hasn't returned."

"He must have been delayed mining coal. We'll return tomorrow," Jiao San said, walking out.

But on the third day, Fang Yuan still did not show up.

Jiao San gestured toward the bed and stove. "He paid a month's rent, stockpiled supplies, and posted that notice to cultivate here. Too bad he was unlucky. With the wolf packs swelling, beasts around the village have grown active. He likely ran into them."

Day four.

Black-green primeval essence relentlessly battered the aperture's crystal walls.

The sturdy crystal wall shattered into fragments that sank into the primeval sea, creating ripples before dissolving into white specks.

This was the Rank two initial stage red steel primeval essence!

"I've finally broken through to Rank two!" Fang Yuan snapped his eyes open, brightening the room.

"Four days and three nights of nonstop cultivation nearly collapsed my body," Fang Yuan smiled wryly, lying down. "No one disturbed me; my decoy at the rental house worked. The expense was well worth it. I'll rest tonight and head back to the village tomorrow."

Opening the door, he stepped outside for the first time in five days.

A slender man with narrow eyes stood outside—Jiang Ya's brother, Jiang He.

Seeing Fang Yuan, he sighed in relief. "Finally! A few more days and I would've broken in. I'd be held responsible if you died here."

Sensing Fang Yuan's aura, Jiang He gasped, "You actually reached Rank two in a single attempt!"

Fang Yuan smiled faintly. "Just good luck. I plan to return today, but I'd like a meal first."

After a hearty meal, Jiang He personally escorted Fang Yuan to the hamlet's entrance.

Insisting on leaving, Fang Yuan bid Jiang He farewell.

# Chapter 93: Small beast horde

On his way back, five-man squads rushed past Fang Yuan from time to time.

"Did something happen?" Within five hundred meters, Fang Yuan had already spotted thirteen groups.

Pondering as he walked, Fang Yuan soon arrived at the village's northern gate.

Seeing Fang Yuan, one person from a squad stopped. "Fang Yuan? Why are you still here?"

"What is it?" Fang Yuan looked up at his classmate, Gu Yue Chi Cheng.

Chi Cheng frowned. "You still don't know? A small beast tide has formed nearby. Left unchecked, it will become a major horde. The hamlets at the mountain foot will be wiped out, and losing those mortal slaves would inconvenience us."

With this reminder, Fang Yuan recalled that such an event had indeed occurred.

"The true wolf tide erupts next year; this small beast tide is merely a precursor. Migrating beast groups will naturally influence other beast groups."

"Wait! If that's the case... heheheh." Fang Yuan's eyes flashed, though he quickly looked down to hide his expression.

Seeing him silent, Chi Cheng added, "Mobilization orders were issued for an urgent mission—don't think of slacking off. Your group departed this morning; you must go too. But..."

Chi Cheng drew out his words smugly, "Beast tides are perilous with wild beasts everywhere, especially at night. A lowly Rank one like you better be careful, unlike a Rank two like me. Hehehe!"

"I only just learned of this," Fang Yuan smiled. "I'll come with your group and rejoin mine at the front lines."

Fang Yuan added coolly, "Clan rules require stranded Gu Masters to join the nearest squad. Don't you even know that, Chi Cheng?"

"Indeed, that is the rule," Chi Shan said, having watched quietly.

Nearly two meters tall, Chi Shan possessed metallic muscles and fiery red skin radiating faint heat.

Chi Shan looked at Fang Yuan expressionlessly. "Let us go. Keep up."

The six-man group departed through the gate, sprinting down the mountain path.

Fang Yuan kept up effortlessly the entire way, while Chi Cheng trailed far behind, panting incredulously.

"We're nearing the beast tide. Chi Cheng, Fang Yuan, move to the center," Chi Shan said, eyeing the dark forest.

Chi Shan placed Fang Yuan in the center to protect him, displaying true magnanimity despite their faction differences.

Chi She's tongue split into a bright red forked snake tongue. As they ventured deeper, wild beasts were seen thrashing about in the forest.

"Chi She is using the Snake Tongue Gu. This Rank two Gu detects thermal signatures, but suffers three flaws: short range, easy interference, and blindness to cold-blooded creatures. Yet no matter what, it is better than not having any detection. I have reached Rank two now, and in future to survive in the wild, I will also need a detection-type of Gu," Fang Yuan thought.

Chi She warned urgently, "Medium beast, likely a white tiger—it's spotted us and closing in!"

A fierce tiger leapt from the thicket, but Chi She's warning allowed them to engage it head-on.

The elderly Gu Master on the right puffed his cheeks and spat out a white, viscous substance.

The substance expanded in midair into a massive, sticky spider net, trapping the white tiger in the face.

As the white tiger struggled in the net, Chi Shan stepped forward and pounded his fist into its head.

Golden luster flashed across his fist; with a bang, the tiger's head shattered, killing it instantly.

"That took barely three seconds; alone, I would need five minutes," Fang Yuan noted.

They all used Rank two Gu worms that complemented one another, whereas Fang Yuan only had Rank one Gu.

Ahead of him, Chi She suddenly said, "I found Sickly Snake's group."

# Chapter 94: Suddenly withdrawing strength

Inspecting the tracks, Chi She flicked his tongue. "No mistake," he said. "Jiao San's red lava python is unique in the village, and they're fighting… a wild boar king!"

"Wild boar tusks are best for fusing my Gu," Fang Yuan thought, eager to kill one.

Chi Shan pondered, then said, "Go support them."

Despite ordinary faction grudges, clansmen still united against external beast threats.

At the center, the elephant-sized wild boar king fought a long, thin red python.

Noticing they were being watched, the support Gu Master Gu Yue Kong Jing broke away from the fight and ran over.

"This wild boar king is yours," Chi Shan told the old Gu Master.

The spider web expanded through the air, enveloping the wild boar king.

Meanwhile, Jiao San and the female healing Gu Master began treating the injured python.

Fang Yuan stepped out from behind Chi Shan.

Jiao San turned, overjoyed. "Fang Yuan, use your strength to hold the boar king while we heal the python!"

Without a defensive Gu and with only Rank one cultivation, approaching the wild boar king was perilous.

"Okay," Fang Yuan agreed, rushing to the wild boar king and seizing its massive tusks.

He pulled with all his might, suppressing the wild boar king and curbing its struggles.

"Good job!" Jiao San praised, his eyes flashing cold.

Jiao San never intended to kill Fang Yuan; losing a team member would lower his clan evaluation, harming his interests.

"I'll let Fang Yuan get injured so he has to recover in the village—let's see him reach Rank two then! I can't strike personally, but with so many beasts in the horde, I can borrow their strength."

"Good. Hua Xing, plant the corrosion blood grass Gu," Jiao San instructed while treating the python.

She stepped forward and raised both thumbs, purple vine insignia encircling her nails.

Tendrils snaked into the wild boar king's mouth, sliding past its teeth deep into its stomach.

"Hehehe…" In the darkness, Fang Yuan covered his mouth as it curved deviously.

His grip on the wild boar king's tusks suddenly went slack.

Growling furiously, the wild boar king swung its tusks and hurled Fang Yuan into the air.

The boar king's tusks swept across, striking the female Gu Master directly before she could react.

Her slender body snapped in two under the impact.

# Chapter 95: Garbage teammate

He twisted nimbly to his feet on the snowy ground; thanks to Jade Skin Gu, he was unscathed aside from back pain.

After a brief silence, Jiao San howled in fury, "Fang Yuan, didn't you say you could hold it!"

Fang Yuan feigned a limp, his gaze scanning the surroundings for other Gu Masters.

Free from the trap, the wild boar king was once again fighting the red lava python.

From the perimeter, Gu Yue Kong Jing shouted, "Fang Yuan, you scum, you got Hua Xin killed!"

"I… I didn't mean to! I really couldn't hold it!" Fang Yuan shouted.

"We'll talk later!" Jiao San roared under mounting pressure as the berserk boar pushed his wounded python to the brink.

"Kong Jing, forget Fang Yuan! Get the knife scale web out!" Jiao San shouted in panic.

"Yes!" Kong Jing summoned the big belly frog and channeled primeval essence, spitting out a steel net.

Fang Yuan shouted, "Let me help!"

Moonblades flew out and severed the steel threads, allowing the thrashing boar king to shred the net and break free.

"I… I messed up. I only wanted to help!" Fang Yuan said, sounding thoroughly innocent.

Dodging the charging boar, Jiao San screamed, "Fang Yuan—! Look what you did! You're utter garbage as a teammate!"

Fang Yuan snickered, but still obediently took tens of steps back.

"Get back!" Jiao San yelled, unleashing his trump card as two streams of poisonous yellow gas puffed from his nostrils.

Fang Yuan told the remaining female Gu Master, "Heal my leg, it's hurt!"

She raged, "My sister is dead and you only sprained your leg! Why don't you just die?!"

Slaying clansmen was an unforgivable crime carrying severe torture and execution; risking that for petty enemies was not worthwhile.

Five minutes later, the wild boar king collapsed.

As the yellow smoke cleared, a pale Jiao San panted heavily, his primeval essence completely exhausted.

Fang Yuan approached the wild boar king and quickly began dissecting the corpse.

Pairs of blue eyes glowed in the surrounding darkness.

"It's the wolves, the wolf pack!"

"Retreat! Forget the boar king, run!" Jiao San shouted as everyone's faces turned pale.

In the crisis, Jiao San abandoned the other three and fled for his life.

"Without speed Gu and out of essence, none of us can outrun lightning wolves," Fang Yuan calmly assessed.

He struck the dazed female Gu Master's neck, knocking her unconscious.

Fang Yuan squeezed into the bloody carcass, using the unconscious woman's body to block the opening and conceal himself.

The wolf pack fell upon them; most chased Jiao San and Kong Jing, while some tore into the boar king's carcass.

"Lightning wolves here are an anomaly; the clan will send reinforcements. As long as I hold out inside this massive carcass, I'll survive."

Calm amid crisis, Fang Yuan closed his eyes and gripped primeval stones to restore his essence.

# Chapter 96: For the sake of those who died

The Gu Yue elders were monitoring the beast horde, and responded swiftly to the unexpected lightning wolves.

Ten minutes later, sounds of battle and wolf cries echoed outside the boar's carcass.

He shoved the female Gu Master aside and crawled out, drenched in blood.

In the reinforcement team, the scout kept watch while their healer examined the female Gu Master's body.

"She's dead…" the healer sighed sorrowfully, evidently knowing her.

Electric currents from the wolves' bites had surged through her unconscious body, stopping her heart.

"She's dead because of you! Hiding behind a woman—you're a disgrace!" The healer glared at Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan shrugged calmly. "She fought me for the hiding spot and lost. I'm satisfied with the outcome."

"Scumbag!!" The healing Gu Master rushed up.

Fang Yuan raised his feet and sent him flying.

"Report me to the punishment hall if you think I committed a crime," Fang Yuan said coolly, walking away.

"A ship sinks, and a man grabs a wooden plank to save himself. The survivor was tried in court and acquitted—the famous wooden plank dilemma on Earth."

"Endangering another's life out of necessity to save oneself is no crime; the punishment hall won't penalize me."

"But..." If the fact that I knocked the female Gu Master unconscious was found out, I would be tried for 'harming a clansman'. Yet at that time, nobody should have seen it, Jiao San and Kong Jing were all looking at a different direction and rushing for their lives. Eh?""

He saw the corpses of Kong Jing and Jiao San.

Kong Jing had been mauled beyond recognition, but Jiao San's body remained intact.

"Fewer obstacles for me… Eh?" Fang Yuan paused; Jiao San's finger twitched.

"Not dead? What tenacity…" Fang Yuan sneered, killing intent surging.

Dozens of healing Gu Masters were approaching from ahead.

They were too close; killing Jiao San with a moonblade or strangulation would leave detectable traces.

"Eliminating Jiao San now clears a major obstacle. Miss this chance, and another won't come easily."

He knelt beside Jiao San, removed his shirt, and covered Jiao San's face and chest.

He pressed the sodden shirt over Jiao San's nose and mouth, smothering him.

A Gu Master approached. "Young man, I'm from the medicine hall, let me look…"

"Scram!" Fang Yuan growled angrily, pushing him away.

The Gu Master stumbled back, but consoled him gently, "My condolences! I know how it feels to watch companions die. You are drenched in blood—for the sake of your fallen comrades, you must live on. Let me treat you."

Fang Yuan stayed silent, shadows cloaking his face as he allowed the healer to examine him.

"I'm sorry, but the clan must retrieve his Gu worms," the Gu Master said apologetically, lifting the blood-soaked shirt from Jiao San.

The healer checked Jiao San's pupils and felt for a pulse, finding none.

The abdomen was still warm, but the aperture was already fading—empty of Gu.

The healer glanced at Fang Yuan pointedly. "A Gu Master's registered Gu belong to their heir; taking them violates clan rules."

Fang Yuan stared back without fear. "I know that. Are you suggesting I took the leader's Gu?"

Stealing them carried far too much risk in the clan for negligible gain.

The healer laughed hastily. "Not at all! The dead cannot return, so restrain your grief, little brother. Live on well for their sake!"

"You're right," Fang Yuan said with a resolute, meaningful gaze. "For the sake of the fallen, I must live even better than before."

# Chapter 97: Devils run amuck

Internal affairs hall, interrogation room.

Across the dim room sat three elders behind a long table.

The interrogation had already continued for an hour.

"Fang Yuan, are you certain your account is accurate?" an elder asked.

"Yes." Fang Yuan stared at his toes, sunlight illuminating his pale, sculpture-like face.

An hour-long debrief clearly meant someone was targeting him from the shadows.

"Recount the battle one more time," the elder pressed.

"I reached Rank two, met Chi Shan's squad, and rejoined Jiao San's group during the boar fight," Fang Yuan narrated calmly.

He disclosed his Rank two breakthrough voluntarily; without a concealing Gu, he couldn't hide his aura anyway.

With Sickly Snake's team dead, he fabricated key details while keeping the bulk of the story true.

This was Fang Yuan's fifth retelling, and the three elders listened with furrowed brows.

Her daughter was the fallen healing Gu Master; grieving and furious, the female elder glared with icy hatred.

"Fang Yuan, why did you slash the knife scale web with your moonblades?" the female elder demanded sternly.

"I wanted to contribute, but made a blunder," Fang Yuan replied.

"Then did you deliberately use your female teammate as a shield inside the boar's stomach?" she pressed.

"Terrified, I scrambled inside the boar, but she fought me for the spot and lost. I am deeply ashamed," Fang Yuan replied.

Fang Yuan's answer stated facts without admitting intent, leaving the furious elders unable to find fault.

She pressed him with several more questions, but finally yielded before his airtight responses.

"What are your plans now, Fang Yuan?" another elder asked meaningfully.

"I intend to claim my family assets and inherit my parents' legacy," Fang Yuan stated openly.

The elder shook his head. "You misunderstand. Your team is gone. My suggestion: join another group. Missions in the internal affairs hall are designed for groups; completing monthly duties or your inheritance mission alone is nearly impossible."

The elder added, "Alternatively, as a Rank two Gu Master, you're qualified to lead. Pass the exam mission, and you can form your own group. Which do you choose?"

Fang Yuan blinked. "I haven't decided; I need time to think."

"You may go. Decide quickly—it'll be hard to find an opening later," the elder advised.

He harbored too many secrets. Only by working alone could he stay secure without the constant nuisance of teammates.

Fang Yuan immediately applied for his inheritance mission, but was told to wait three days due to backlog.

"Today is the memorial assembly. Gu Yue Jin Zhu is dead, did you know?" Mo Bei called, walking with several youths.

"Brother, Jin Zhu was our classmate. Come with us," Fang Zheng urged.

"Death is routine in battle; once you see enough of it, you stop caring. Go ahead, I'm busy."

"Heh, you've all heard the rumor, right? Fang Yuan only survived by hiding in a boar's belly and shielding himself with his female teammate."

"He is a worthless disgrace, lacking even the manners of a man." Chi Cheng folded his arms and laughed coldly.

As Fang Yuan walked away, the mockeries behind him gradually faded.

"Why would insults matter to me?"

But, this was the exact situation Fang Yuan wanted.

Entering the clan system meant becoming a bound pawn, obstructing his essential need for solitude and resources.

So, he wanted to be a loner.

On Earth, social connections were essential to survive, and a loner could not stand alone.

In this world, individual cultivation could defeat thousands; rejection mattered nothing before absolute power.

So in this world —Devils run amuck.

# Chapter 98: Difficulty of refining Gu

"That lad reached Rank two and applied for the asset-splitting mission?!" Dong Tu's shocked voice echoed through the hall.

"Although I took your primeval stones, I could only stall him for three days. That is my limit," the Gu Master said.

"Don't rush, brother. I still need your assistance," Dong Tu said, hurriedly stuffing another money bag into his hand.

Tucking the bag of primeval stones into his chest pocket, the Gu Master's tone shifted with a loud laugh. "Brother Dong Tu, you are too polite. We've known each other for over ten years. Don't worry, I will assign Fang Yuan the toughest mission, but watch out in case he hires other Gu Masters to help."

"Rest assured. If he hires outsiders, it violates clan rules, and I am watching for his weakness."

"Collecting honey wine?" Fang Yuan's eyes glinted coldly upon receiving the asset mission.

The troublesome mission required collecting five liang of honey wine from fist-sized, sharp-stinging golden bees.

"This mission requires a defensive Gu to withstand stings; numbers alone mean nothing. My uncle used his connections to exploit my lack of a defensive Gu worm. Unfortunately for him..." Fang Yuan laughed coldly in his heart.

"However, the Rank one Jade Skin Gu is insufficient to collect the honey wine. If I advance it to the Rank two White Jade Gu, it will be easy."

Fang Yuan had seven Gu worms in his possession.

The vital Gu Spring Autumn Cicada, Moonlight Gu, Liquor worm, White Boar Gu, Jade Skin Gu and two Little Light Gu.

The Moonlight Gu could fuse with two Little Light Gu into Moonglow Gu, while the White Boar Gu and Jade Skin Gu could fuse into White Jade Gu.

Having spent most of his stones pushing to Rank two, Fang Yuan only had enough for a single attempt.

"Refining the White Jade Gu is the better choice. With it, I can collect the honey wine and advance further in the Flower Wine Monk's inheritance. But if the fusion fails, the results will be dire. With my finances near collapse, failing to get the White Jade Gu means losing my parents' assets, severely stalling my growth."

In the room, clan leader Gu Yue Bo meticulously instructed Fang Zheng.

"Refining, nurturing, and using Gu are each vast fields of knowledge that take lifetimes to master. As for refinement, you already know how to refine a wild Gu and make it yours."

"Through fusion, multiple Gu evolve into a higher rank. Fang Zheng, it is time you refine a Rank two Gu worm."

"Fusion requires a recipe discovered through trial and error. Our clan has researched the Moonlight Gu most deeply and holds two Rank five recipes."

"Following this recipe through multiple fusions, one can refine up to a Rank five Gu. Your Jade Skin Gu and Moonlight Gu meet the requirements for one such recipe. Following it will eventually yield the Rank five Moonlight Treasure King Gu!"

"Hehehe, it is too early for Rank five. Take out your Jade Skin and Moonlight Gu, and I will teach you to fuse them into the Rank two Moon Raiment!"

"Gu fusion requires parallel thinking. You must fuse the two consciousnesses carrying your will together."

Under Gu Yue Bo's guidance, Fang Zheng's red steel primeval essence enveloped the Jade Skin Gu and Moonlight Gu in midair.

After three hours of continuous attempts, Fang Zheng finally merged the two consciousnesses perfectly.

Instantly, the Jade Skin Gu and Moonlight Gu erupted in bright white light.

"Maintain this condition, and throw primeval stones into the light." Gu Yue Bo advised.

Fang Zheng retrieved a primeval stone and threw it into the light.

Upon entering, the primeval stone transformed into pure natural essence that merged into the light, leaving only rock powder scattered across the bed.

"Continue throwing until the Rank two Gu is complete." Gu Yue Bo said.

Fang Zheng was about to throw a second stone when the light vanished, and the two Gu violently repelled each other.

The fusion failed.

"Damn, I forgot to maintain the merged consciousness." Fang Zheng immediately realized his mistake.

"Failing at the start is normal, but repeated failures will destroy both Gu," Gu Yue Bo cautioned.

Fang Zheng recalled the Gu, finding cracks across the Moonlight Gu while the Jade Skin Gu appeared weakened.

# Chapter 99: Human, not God

The red steel primeval sea filled 44% of the aperture, beneath the hovering Spring Autumn Cicada.

"The Spring Autumn Cicada is still too weak to assist with fusion; its aura can only aid single refinement."

Three money bags and the wild boar king's snow-white tusk lay on the bed sheet.

Fang Yuan gazed at these items with a heavy expression. "I only have enough primeval stones for a single fusion attempt. Succeed or fail, my finances will be depleted. But if I don't fuse it now, my primeval stones will only last for twelve or so days and I will even lose the chance to fuse."

Nurturing seven Gu worms and relying on the Liquor worm strained his C-grade finances to the limit.

"Regardless, let us begin." Fang Yuan took a deep breath and focused his gaze.

"Fuse!" Fang Yuan commanded inwardly. The White Boar Gu and Jade Skin Gu flared brilliantly and collided directly into each other.

Fang Yuan used his consciousness to sustain the white ball of light while tossing primeval stones from his bag into it.

"Almost there." Fang Yuan narrowed his eyes; he decisively threw the wild boar king's snow-white tusk into the ball of light.

Any onlooker would be astonished. While the recipe fusing the White Boar Gu and Jade Skin Gu into the White Jade Gu had circulated for over a millennium, no one had heard of adding a wild boar king's tusk.

In place of the Jade Skin Gu and White Boar Gu, a brand-new Gu worm hovered calmly before Fang Yuan.

This was—Rank two White Jade Gu!

# Chapter 100: White Jade Gu

In the blink of an eye, ten more days passed.

Deep in the mountain, in the underground rock forest.

Tens of jade eye stone monkeys jumped in the air with blurred silhouettes.

Previously, Fang Yuan would have retreated without hesitation; now, he stood motionless as a rock with a cold expression.

The stone monkeys clawed and bit at Fang Yuan, but their attacks clinked harmlessly as though striking solid jade.

A bright white light coated his body, offering more than twice the defense of the Jade Skin Gu.

"The White Jade Gu's defense operates like the Jade Skin Gu, demanding continuous primeval essence that scales with the attack's intensity," Fang Yuan concluded inwardly.

Though the White Boar Gu was gone, the strength it granted remained in his body.

Ignoring the fleeing monkeys, Fang Yuan continued deeper into the stone forest.

Observing as he walked, Fang Yuan spotted a colossal pillar at the center of the forest, its circumference wider than dozens of men embracing it.

"Insufficient primeval essence." Fang Yuan inspected his primeval sea and sighed, forced to halt.

Previously, he would have replenished his essence with primeval stones, but refining the White Jade Gu had pushed his finances to the brink of collapse.

"It should be at the base of that central rock pillar, but I must forge a path first." With this growing conviction, Fang Yuan took one last look before returning to the second secret room.

Tying the pouch, Fang Yuan grabbed a bullskin water sack.

"With only two and a half primeval stones left, it is time to turn in the mission at the internal affairs hall."

Securing the water sack, Fang Yuan traversed the tunnel, squeezed through the rock crack, and returned outside.

Fang Yuan continued forward.His rented house was not far away.

On the bamboo house's ground floor stood his uncle Gu Yue Dong Tu, watching Fang Yuan approach from a distance and clearly waiting for him.

# Chapter 101: The person is as old as his heart has aged

He wore his rarely seen Gu Master uniform, complete with leg wrappings and a red belt. Pristine and tidy, it projected a faint air of authority.

"Reaching Rank two at sixteen with only C grade aptitude is astonishing; I don't know how he managed it. Yet his speed must stem from the Liquor worm, which is useless at Rank two."

Seeing Fang Yuan approach with unbothered calm, Gu Yue Dong Tu suppressed his anger as the youth stopped before him.

Sure enough, Gu Yue Dong Tu spoke, "Fang Yuan, I think we can talk."

"What do you want to talk about?" Fang Yuan frowned. Gu Yue Dong Tu laughed. "I was like you once—I became a Gu Master at fifteen."

"It was during a wolf tide with heavy casualties, and even students had to fight. With B grade aptitude, I reached Rank two initial stage at sixteen, upper stage at seventeen, and peak stage at eighteen. At nineteen, I began charging toward Rank three, confident I would achieve it by twenty."

"Hahaha, I was arrogant, believing I was omnipotent and capable of anything, ignorant of the immensity of heaven and earth. Then, at twenty, But after that event, my aptitude dropped down to C grade, I couldn't recover from this shock for a whole of eight years."

"On my twenty-ninth birthday, I reexamined myself and this world, realizing individual strength was limited. What if I reached Rank three? In society, connections matter far more than personal strength."

"I started anew at thirty and retired from the front lines at forty-five. Over a hundred Gu Masters petitioned dozens of times to make me an elder. Though capped at Rank two peak stage, I had already succeeded without needing that final step. Clansmen call me the 'hidden elder." Most of my peers have passed away, yet my life sailed smoothly. I still have influence on many Gu Masters.""

Concluding his speech, Gu Yue Dong Tu looked at Fang Yuan with a smirk. "Fang Yuan, you are young and inexperienced, just like I was—believing you can accomplish everything alone. Hehe."

Gu Yue Dong Tu shook his head. "With experience, you will realize humans are social creatures, not lone beasts. Sometimes, one must bow and step back. Arrogance and extremism only bring isolation and ruin. You must already feel it—no team accepts you, and everyone ostracizes you. Even with the inheritance mission, what can you achieve? As a person who has been left out by everyone, you definitely won't have the chance to finish the task. Give up."

His uncle's philosophy had actually guided Fang Yuan for a long period in his past life.

He had founded the Bloodwing Demon Sect, using systems and sentiment to command tens of thousands to seize resources and fight enemies.

Rank five Gu Masters were mortal, but Rank six were immortals. From that summit, he realized immense influence offered great power, but also created a massive burden.

In any world, the only thing one could truly rely upon was oneself.

He continued, "Fang Yuan, to be blunt, you are not Fang Zheng. Relinquish the family inheritance, and you will gain my friendship, access to all my connections, and a thousand primeval stones. I know you are strapped for cash and already two days late on rent, aren't you?"

Fang Yuan smiled faintly. "Uncle, you rarely wear those clothes, do you?"

"A true Gu Master's uniform is never so clean," Fang Yuan said. "It should be bloodstained and torn—that is the true mark of a Gu Master."

"You are old, Uncle. Your youthful ambitions are gone, eroded by years of comfort. You vie for family assets not for cultivation, but to sustain a life of luxury. With such a mindset, how could you stop me?"

Gu Yue Dong Tu's face turned ashen as fury surged within him.

"Fang Yuan!" Gu Yue Dong Tu shouted. "I tried to guide you as an elder, but you fail to appreciate goodwill. If you insist on opposing me, so be it! I already know your inheritance mission's contents. Young fools know nothing of the vastness of heaven and earth. Let's see how you complete this mission!"

Fang Yuan smiled mockingly. With reconciliation impossible, he saw no harm in enjoying Dong Tu's upcoming reaction. He took out the bullskin water sack and uncorked it, releasing the distinct scent of honey wine.

"What do you think is in here?" He asked.

Gu Yue Dong Tu paled in shock, his heart instantly sinking.

"How can this be?! Where did you get this honey wine?!" he roared in utter disbelief.

Ignoring him, Fang Yuan capped and stowed the water bag, then walked toward the internal affairs hall.

"If one thousand primeval stones won't do, how about two thousand—no, two thousand five hundred?" Gu Yue Dong Tu kept raising his offer.

Fang Yuan ignored him; his uncle's desperate bargaining only confirmed that the true family assets were substantial.

Sweating and seeing Fang Yuan unmoved, Gu Yue Dong Tu's face turned fierce. "Fang Yuan, think carefully! Offend me, and don't blame your uncle if you end up missing an arm or a leg later on!"

Fang Yuan shook his head and entered the internal affairs hall under his uncle's furious glare.

# Chapter 102: Spring comes after the winter winds

Arriving at the internal affairs hall, Fang Yuan submitted the golden honey wine.

The middle-aged Gu Master in charge was astonished. Pen in hand, he asked, "You've completed the asset mission already?"

The Gu Master frowned. He had chosen this mission specifically to make things difficult for Fang Yuan, yet the youth had finished it so quickly!

Fixing Fang Yuan with a threatening glare, he said solemnly, "Answer honestly, or it will affect your evaluation. Did you complete this mission alone? Speak the truth; we will investigate thoroughly."

Fang Yuan replied, "The situation was peculiar. I only went to scout the area, but found a bear raiding the hive and seized the chance to retrieve the honey wine."

Fang Yuan shrugged with a smile. "Did you think I could accomplish this alone? Truly, it's all thanks to you for assigning this mission."

Stunned, the middle-aged Gu Master gave a dry laugh with mixed feelings and resumed recording in the ledger.

With the mission completed and a solid cover story in place, the clerk could stall no longer.

Finishing his notes, the man said, "Your mission is complete. Since your uncle and aunt hold your inheritance, the hall will reclaim it for you. Return in three days."

Returning the report, Fang Yuan left the internal affairs hall.

In the guest hall.

"Brother Dong Tu, my hands are tied," the middle-aged Gu Master sighed before Gu Yue Dong Tu.

"Is there really no other way?" his aunt asked anxiously, fearful and indignant.

The middle-aged man shook his head, "The results are set in stone and have entered the internal affairs hall's procedure. Unless the two clan elders in power or the clan leader interferes. Brother Dong Tu, this inventory list details all the inheritance assets. Please return them and don't make my life difficult." With that, he produced the list.

Looking at the list, his aunt shrieked, "You can't do this! This is ours! Husband, say something! Without these assets, what do we have left? We'll lose half our servants—we can't afford this!"

Gu Yue Dong Tu stood up and slapped his wife, knocking her to the floor.

"Shut up!" Uncle raged. "Clan rules are absolute—do you think you can refuse?"

Veins bulging on his forehead, Dong Tu snarled, "Fang Yuan, I cannot stop the hall, but don't think you are safe just because you hold these assets!"

Three days later, Fang Yuan walked out of the internal affairs hall with deeds to houses and land, along with servant indenture contracts.

Over ten mu of farmland, eight servants, three bamboo buildings, and a tavern!

The three bamboo buildings alone produced enough monthly rent to sustain his cultivation.

Then there was the tavern—one of only four establishments selling wine in the entire village.

"With these assets, I can easily support my Gu worms. Yet the real treasure isn't any of that—it is this grass Gu!"

A grass Gu lay quietly in Fang Yuan's palm.

Dainty and delicate, its emerald roots resembled ginseng whiskers beneath jade-like translucent runners. Nine round, jade-green leaves overlapped in a circular plate.

This was the Nine Leaf Vitality Grass, a Rank two healing Gu.

Its true value lay in its foliage—every leaf plucked from it was a vitality leaf.

"Once I refine the Nine Leaf Vitality Grass, every leaf plucked becomes a vitality leaf, and infusing primeval essence will regrow them. This Gu is practically a walking goldmine. It is the most important asset of all. In this perilous world, every Gu Master suffers injuries on missions, creating a perpetual demand for vitality leaves."

"No wonder Gu Yue Dong Tu retained such influence long after retiring and was dubbed the 'hidden elder." It was all because of this Nine Leaf Vitality Grass.""

"Bound by clan rules, their options against me are limited. Whatever they try, I will handle it."

# Chapter 103: The larger your ambitions, the smaller the world

The tavern was modest in size but well-located on the village's east side near the eastern gate.

"Young lord, please take a seat." An old man lowered his head to Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan declined to sit, pacing the tavern to survey everything. *This is my wine tavern,* he thought.

Fang Yuan stopped. "Not bad, though the shop is a bit small."

Turning slightly, Fang Yuan glanced at the old man. "You are the shopkeeper?"

The old man bowed even lower. "I dare not presume, Young Lord. This tavern is yours—whoever you choose is the shopkeeper."

"Bring the account books and a pot of tea," Fang Yuan said, taking a seat.

Fang Yuan casually skimmed through several ledgers, occasionally asking questions.

Second only to the Nine Leaf Vitality Grass, the tavern was an asset Fang Yuan intended to firmly grasp.

Turning to the final page, however, Fang Yuan saw that Gu Yue Dong Tu had already taken this month's revenue.

"The previous master took it in person, Young Lord. We didn't dare resist," the shopkeeper explained, trembling and pale as he wiped his sweat.

Fang Yuan set the ledgers down in silence and glanced at the shopkeeper.

Fang Yuan said, "I won't dwell on the past. Your wages are low, so waiters will receive a twenty percent raise and the shopkeeper forty percent. Work diligently, and you will receive more benefits."

Fang Yuan stood up and walked to the entrance.

Leaving the tavern, Fang Yuan headed toward the three bamboo buildings.

Leased out by his uncle and aunt, the three bamboo buildings were practically full.

The buildings were well-managed and full, yielding rental profits only slightly below the tavern's.

Just two days ago, he had fewer than five primeval stones to his name. Now, he had suddenly joined the ranks of the clan's wealthiest.

"Yet that is not what I want." Standing on the balcony, Fang Yuan gripped the railing, gazing into the distance.

In the distance, undulating green mountains stretched endlessly, resembling a sleeping giant beneath a gray-blue sky.

"Since rebirth, I have drifted like rootless duckweed. Securing the family assets has finally given me an independent footing. Next, I will unearth the Flower Wine Monk's inheritance and strive for Rank three, then leave this village for the wider world!" Fire burned in Fang Yuan's dark eyes.

Qing Mao Mountain was just one of countless peaks in the Southern Border, which itself was merely a corner of the world.

"Brother, come down! I need to talk to you!" Gu Yue Fang Zheng called up from outside the bamboo building.

His thoughts interrupted, Fang Yuan looked down at Fang Zheng with indifference.

His brother's arrival was no surprise—Fang Zheng was simply their uncle and aunt's tool to contest the inheritance.

# Chapter 104: Want to buy the liquor worm?

Fang Yuan did not descend, and his brother did not come up.

"Brother, you've gone too far! To think you were such a person!" Fang Zheng yelled from below, frowning deeply.

Unruffled, Fang Yuan chuckled lightly. "Oh? What kind of person am I?"

"Brother!" Fang Zheng sighed deeply. "Uncle and Aunt took us in after our parents died. How can you repay their kindness with malice? Is your heart made of stone?"

"So strange, this inheritance is rightfully mine, what is there to be said about revenge and favour." Fang Yuan retorted unenthusiastically.

Fang Zheng gritted his teeth. "You can't take everything—leave enough for Uncle and Aunt to retire! Your actions are bitterly disappointing!"

Fang Yuan laughed coldly, knowing Uncle and Aunt had amassed a fortune managing the assets all these years.

Fang Yuan retorted, "What if I refuse? You were formally adopted, so you have no claim to this inheritance."

"I know!" Fang Zheng's eyes flashed. "That is why I challenge you to a formal duel. We'll fight in the arena—if I win, return a share of the assets to our parents!"

Clansmen used formal Gu battles to settle irreconcilable conflicts, with formats ranging from duels and team fights to contests of skill, strength, or life and death.

Seeing his resolve, Fang Yuan laughed. "Uncle and Aunt must have put you up to this. But having lost to me before, where does your confidence come from?"

"Things are different now!" Fang Zheng declared, eyes blazing. "Last time was a mistake. Now I've refined the Rank two Moon Raiment—you can't break my defense anymore!"

With that, a faint light-blue mist arose around his body.

"Moon Raiment indeed. How foolish to reveal your trump card so readily," Fang Yuan mused, eyes twinkling.

Against it, Fang Yuan could not break Fang Zheng's defense with bare fists; striking it was like hitting cotton, completely robbing his blows of force.

Without upgrading to Moonglow Gu or revealing his White Jade Gu, Fang Yuan could not easily pierce it in a duel.

He said to Fang Zheng, "You can challenge me, but do you have your team's approval? If your group gets a mission mid-battle, what will you choose?"

Fang Zheng paused; indeed, he did not think of this.

"Go consult your leader, Gu Yue Qing Shu, and explain the situation. I'll wait for you at the northern gate tavern," Fang Yuan said.

Fang Zheng hesitated, then gritted his teeth. "I'll go! But don't think stalling tactics will work!"

A servant escorted him into Gu Yue Qing Shu's residence.

Gu Yue Qing Shu was practising using his Gu.

"Green Vine Gu," Qing Shu murmured, expertly whipping a fifteen-meter vine before unleashing volleys of pine needles and curved moonblades.

*As expected of Senior Qing Shu, the number one Rank two Gu Master!* Staring agape, Fang Zheng forgot his purpose.

"Fang Zheng? Our mission just ended—you should rest and balance work with leisure," Gu Yue Qing Shu said, dismissing his combat aura with a gentle smile.

Fang Zheng explained his intentions and the entire situation.

Qing Shu raised an eyebrow. Having heard much about Fang Yuan, he was far more intrigued by the elder brother than Fang Zheng.

Gu Yue Qing Shu nodded. "I have matters to discuss with your brother as well. Let's go together."

At the tavern, an attentive waiter promptly escorted them inside.

By the window, a square table held several side dishes, two cups, and a pot of wine.

Seated across, Fang Yuan gestured with a smile. "Please sit."

Qing Shu nodded and sat, then told Fang Zheng, "Take a walk outside; I will speak with your brother."

Fang Zheng acknowledged and left the shop indignantly.

"I know you, Fang Yuan," Qing Shu smiled, smoothly uncapping the wine and pouring two cups.

"You are a very interesting, smart person," he said, raising his cup toward Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan laughed and raised his cup in return.

Pouring the wine, he said, "No need to beat around the bush with a smart person. I'll be blunt: I want to buy your Liquor worm. Name your price."

"A truly talented person. What a shame," Fang Yuan thought.

# Chapter 105: I will lose extremely terribly

Like Fang Yuan and Fang Zheng, Gu Yue Qing Shu was an orphan.

His parents had died in a wolf tide during his childhood.

He was personally raised by clan leader Gu Yue Bo.

With a top-tier B grade aptitude—virtually a 'false A grade'—he received Gu Yue Bo's personal guidance and was groomed as a clan leader candidate.

Warm, amicable, and deeply loyal, Gu Yue Qing Shu was well received by the clan. Though Fang Zheng's arrival dashed his hopes of becoming clan leader, he was instead happy and wholeheartedly cared for him.

A year later, when the wolf tide breached the northern gate, Qing Shu forcefully activated a Rank three Gu with Rank two cultivation to defend the village.

However, his aperture was destroyed because of this and he finally turned into a treeman and died.

Thus, Gu Yue Qing Shu had left a profound impression on Fang Yuan.

Seeing Fang Yuan sigh, Gu Yue Qing Shu naturally never imagined he was recalling his death, assuming he was merely vexed over the Liquor worm.

He smiled, "Fang Yuan, you should already know the limits of the Liquor worm. It is only Rank one and can only refine green copper essence. But you are already a Rank two Gu Master; the Liquor worm is useless for red steel primeval essence. Though you now own a tavern and can easily feed it, why vainly raise something that has no use to you?"

He added, "Yet it is invaluable to Rank one Gu Masters. Next spring's new students could benefit greatly from it. So, you might as well just sell the Liquor worm to the clan and contribute to clan's strength."

Fang Yuan didn't reply.

Qing Shu pondered, guessing his intent, "I understand. You are reluctant to part with the Liquor worm and want to fuse it. If I am not wrong, you plan to take the Rank two White Chrysalis, then Rank three Mist Perspiring Butterfly route, right?"

"This is the most widespread and practical recipe. The Mist Perspiring Butterfly is great, but the White Chrysalis has no abilities. This route holds little value for you. With your C grade aptitude and Rank two cultivation, a White Chrysalis has no abilities and will only squander food, offering you no help."

In fact, the Flower Wine Monk took this route to refine the Mist Perspiring Butterfly from his Liquor worm, using it to enchant women. After his death, the starving butterfly degenerated and reverted back into a Liquor worm.

Seeing Fang Yuan remain silent, Qing Shu said, "Our clan actually has a recipe to advance the Liquor worm into the Rank two Moon Harbinger Gu, then the Rank three Seven Fragrances Liquor worm, which refines primeval essence."

"Fang Yuan, if you don't want to sell the Liquor worm, we can make it a business transaction instead. Sell the Liquor worm to the clan, and if we refine it into the Seven Fragrances Liquor worm, you may use it for five years. If it fails, the clan will compensate you. What do you think?"

But Fang Yuan only sneered coldly in his heart.

With only 44% C grade aptitude, reaching Rank three was virtually impossible. In his previous life, Fang Yuan had stalled at Rank two for over a century before fortuitously obtaining a talent-raising Gu to advance.

Fang Yuan's memories held the optimal recipe for fusing the Liquor worm.

It advanced the Liquor worm into the Rank two Four Flavors Liquor worm, then the Rank three Seven Fragrances Liquor worm. Both possessed the ability to refine primeval essence.

However, refining the Four Flavors Liquor worm was no easy task.

First, its fusion required two Liquor worms, while Fang Yuan currently had only one. Second, it required four distinct wines representing sour, sweet, bitter, and spicy flavors.

Spicy, sour, and sweet wines were accessible, but bitter wine would require more thought.

Fang Yuan knew of a green bitter wine brewed from Ai grass, but Ai village was immeasurably far away—how could he obtain it?

Seeing Fang Yuan unmoved, Qing Shu played his trump card: "Sell the Liquor worm, and I will ensure Fang Zheng drops his Gu battle challenge over the inheritance. You know the clan rules; the challenge must be accepted. Even before the higher-ups approve the Gu battle request, you must accept it. Even if you are certain of defeat and forfeit without stepping into the arena, you must still accept the challenge."

"Fang Zheng's battle request is reasonable and fair; it will be approved. Regardless of the outcome, the elders will judge the result. Between you and Fang Zheng, who do you think they will side with?"

Qing Shu pressed on, "Even if you win, you merely keep a fraction more of the assets. But if you sell the Liquor worm to the clan, you will be contributing to the clan. The clan will remember you. I can also guarantee that Fang Zheng would never challenge you on the basis of family assets."

Fang Yuan, who had listened silently to Gu Yue Qing Shu until now, suddenly smiled and finally spoke.

"You think I will lose if we fight?" Fang Yuan asked Qing Shu.

Qing Shu smiled back, "Battles are unpredictable, but I must remind you: Fang Zheng has already refined the Rank two Moonveil Gu. You will have little advantage."

"Hahaha." Fang Yuan shook his head, his smile widening. "I will lose, definitely."

Qing Shu stared blankly.

"Not only will I lose, I will lose miserably. I'll surrender our parents' inheritance, then sleep on the streets and beg across the village."

"You..." Being sharp, Gu Yue Qing Shu grasped Fang Yuan's intent. His confident, graceful expression instantly turned grave.

As a future clan leader, if word spread that Fang Zheng had bullied his brother for the inheritance, his reputation would be ruined.

With anyone else, this might not work. But Qing Shu's extreme loyalty to the clan left him no choice but to take the threat seriously.

Quickly regaining his composure, Qing Shu glared at Fang Yuan and gritted his teeth: "You won't do this. The inheritance has been your goal all along—how will you cultivate if you give it up?"

Fang Yuan met his gaze with a slight smile. "Which is why you will drop the purchase and persuade Fang Zheng not to challenge me. Am I wrong?"

After a moment, Gu Yue Qing Shu lowered his eyes.

"Interesting, we will do just that." His tone carried a hint of melancholy.

# Chapter 106: Half his life-savings gone to dust, only his vitality remains for eternity

A day later.

On the futon, Fang Yuan sat cross-legged.

A large face-sized white light was an arms' distance from his face, silently floating.

Fang Yuan drew primeval stones from his money bag one by one, tossing them into the light ball.

The primeval stone vanished, and the light ball exploded!

With a bang, the three Gu flew apart, scattering across the room.

Refining the Moonglow Gu had failed!

Fang Yuan's heart sank. He quickly moved his finger, calling back his Gu worms.

The Moonlight Gu and one Little Light Gu drifted weakly into his palm, but the other Little Light Gu did not respond.

Such was the price of a failed fusion: depending on the recipe, Gu worms could be injured or even die.

"Fortunately, it was the Little Light Gu that died, not the Moonlight Gu. It is easy to replace from the shop, but replacing the Moonlight Gu would not be nearly as simple."

Next, he inspected the Moonlight Gu and the remaining Little Light Gu. The glow on both was dimmer than usual, a sign of injury from the failed fusion.

"Once injured, the fusion success rate drops drastically; I must wait for them to recover." Knowing haste was useless, Fang Yuan stored them.

He estimated it would take at least three days before he could attempt another fusion.

On his pale left palm rested a green grass insignia, etched like a dark green tattoo.

The insignia sprang to life: a grass tip emerged with nine rounded dark-green leaves and a translucent jade-green stem.

It was the Rank two grass Gu—Nine Leaf Vitality Grass.

Fang Yuan stretched out his right hand's finger and plucked the leaves.

After plucking all nine leaves, only the bare stem remained on his palm.

As the stem absorbed primeval essence, a new bud soon began growing from deep within.

"I used up 20% of my primeval essence." Fang Yuan inspected his aperture and concluded.

With only 44% primeval essence in his sea, he could produce only two vitality leaves at a time.

After growing another leaf, Fang Yuan absorbed a primeval stone to restore his essence.

Repeating the process for half a day, he regrew all nine leaves.

He placed the plucked leaves into a small bag to carry with him.

Each vitality leaf was a Rank one Gu worth fifty primeval stones, yielding four hundred and fifty primeval stones in total.

Even after accounting for production costs, he still netted around four hundred primeval stones in profit.

The Nine Leaf Vitality Grass was tantamount to a gold mine—and cost virtually nothing to raise, requiring only water and sunlight.

Fang Yuan could forfeit all other family assets, but the Nine Leaf Vitality Grass had to remain firmly in his grasp.

Three days later.

The light ball suddenly exploded, and a new Gu worm floated slowly in midair.

Yet it was not the Moonlight Gu, but the higher-ranking Rank two moonglow Gu.

Fusing a Moonlight Gu with two Little Light Gu created the Moonglow Gu, overcoming the non-additive limitation of separate Little Light Gu.

But the refined Rank two Moonglow Gu, its attack power reached three times of the Moonlight Gu!

Fang Yuan chose this path to maximize the moonblade's attack power, though its ten-meter range remained unchanged.

Higher-ranked Gu had lower fusion rates; in his previous life, refining the Spring Autumn Cicada had failed countless times with a sub-1% success rate.

Regardless of fusion success or failure, a vital Gu never dies—at most, it gets injured.

The vital Gu was a Gu Master's greatest asset, shaping their cultivation path and prompting constant efforts to advance its rank.

As a vital Gu, the Spring Autumn Cicada could never die during refinement—otherwise, his repeated past attempts would have destroyed it.

Though Fang Yuan could not yet utilize the Spring Autumn Cicada to its full potential, it remained his greatest treasure—making the Flower Wine Monk's inheritance pale by comparison.

# Chapter 107: Skillfully earning primeval stones, ill-intent customers come to wreck havoc

The cold snow came to an end, and spring wind took its place.

In the morning, the wine tavern was mostly empty with few drinkers.

Fang Yuan sat by the window in a private booth partitioned by wooden screens.

Jiang Ya sat opposite Fang Yuan, his face full of smiles.

"Here are the primeval stones for this batch, please verify." He placed four money bags on the table and pushed them toward Fang Yuan.

Rather than opening them one by one, Fang Yuan gauged their weight in his hand.

With over a century of trading experience, he could effortlessly discern the count in his hands, noticing immediately if even one stone was missing.

Finding the weight exact, Fang Yuan handed a small cloth bag to Jiang Ya.

Jiang Ya received it with both hands and opened the bag, checking the quantity carefully.

Though Fang Yuan produced nine vitality leaves daily, he had no interest in retailing them personally to build social ties.

Instead, he sold the Rank one healing Gu directly to shopkeeper Jiang Ya to resell.

As Jiang He's brother, Jiang Ya made a reliable proxy for clan sales.

"One, two, three... nine. Indeed, nine vitality leaves." Jiang Ya counted them thrice before tying the bag and tucking it securely against his chest.

Next, he raised his cup, toasting to Fang Yuan. "Lord Fang Yuan, happy doing business with you, let me toast you!"

By letting Jiang Ya profit from the price difference, Fang Yuan had become his cash cow, and Jiang Ya no longer dared offend this junior.

Setting down his cup, Jiang Ya said excitedly, "The clan charges fifty-five primeval stones each. At our price of fifty, demand is overwhelming! Lord, why not produce more daily?"

Fang Yuan shook his head and flatly refused. "No. Nine leaves is my limit—it already takes up too much of my cultivation time."

Even capped at nine leaves daily, Fang Yuan netted four hundred primeval stones each day.

"Indeed!" Jiang Ya exclaimed. "With such fortune, why stay in a shoddy rented flat when you could own a bamboo building? Marry a beautiful wife, hire seven or eight servants to attend to you—now that is the dream life, hehe."

Fang Yuan laughed lightly, not saying anything.

How could a farmer understand the ambitions of the conqueror!

Fang Yuan's gaze was slightly blank as he thought about his current predicament.

With Fang Zheng dealt with, the family assets were essentially in the bag.

With the White Jade Gu and Moonglow Gu covering offense and defense, only advancing the Liquor worm remained problematic. Refining the Four Flavors Liquor worm required a second Liquor worm alongside sweet, sour, bitter, and spicy wines—items he had no idea where to get.

"Without the Liquor worm, my cultivation speed slows by half. But to refine it, I must wait for the caravan and use that chance to reveal my White Jade Gu and showcase my strength."

Armed with the Moonglow Gu, White Jade Gu, and five hundred years of combat experience, Fang Yuan was stronger than most Rank two Gu Masters.

"If I can fuse the Four Flavors Liquor worm, my cultivation speed will be satisfactory. But I must also replenish my Gu worms. Defense I have White Jade Gu, offense I have Moonglow Gu, healing I have nine leaf vitality grass, but I need a movement-type and recon-type Gu, these two although are support types, they can cover up my weakness and at least triplefold my fighting strength!" Fang Yuan pondered.

Beside him, Jiang Ya asked, "Lord, I heard someone is causing trouble for you recently, targeting your bamboo building and wine tavern?"

Fang Yuan had investigated, this was his uncle Gu Yue Dong Tu's act.

Unable to use Fang Zheng after Qing Shu's warning, a resentful Dong Tu had begun sending Gu Masters to harass Fang Yuan's businesses.

"Owner, someone is causing trouble again," a fearful clerk reported, hurrying toward Fang Yuan.

"Oh?" Fang Yuan's brows raised, not expecting to encounter this immediately.

Before Fang Yuan could react, Jiang Ya stood up eagerly. "Wait a moment, lord, let me check."

He left the booth and returned almost immediately.

"It's Gu Yue Man Shi!" Jiang Ya whispered, pale with shock and worry.

Gu Yue Man Shi, leader of the Man Shi group, possessed great strength and specialized in defense, with a reputation slightly surpassing Jiao San's.

Bam!

A coarse, arrogant voice roared, "Pah! What kind of wine is this? Tastes like horseshit—you dare sell this to me?"

"Hmph!" Eyes glinting with cold light, Fang Yuan stood up.

# Chapter 108: That pair of eyes!

Jiang Ya hurriedly stopped him. "Lord Fang Yuan, Man Shi is formidable—one of the few who survived Bai Ning Bing. He cannot be underestimated."

"What is there to fear from someone who treats defeat as a badge of honor?" Fang Yuan chuckled lightly, patting Jiang Ya's shoulder. "Just stay here and drink."

Tongue-tied, Jiang Ya helplessly sat back down while Fang Yuan strode out of the booth toward the main hall.

In the center, a Rank two Gu Master had one foot planted on a stool and the other on a square table.

The elderly shopkeeper bowed low, apologizing in terror, "Lord, please be appeased! If the wine is unsatisfactory, we will serve another brew free of charge!"

"Your wine tastes like swill! You ruined my mood, so pay me five hundred primeval stones in compensation!" Man Shi roared.

Someone nodded. "That could be true. The clan policies encourage competition—the strong deserve more resources, don't they? If the weak cannot defend their assets, they must surrender them. It's all for the clan's prosperity!"

"Who dares leave? Stand where you are, no one leaves!" Man Shi roared from the table.

Clearly just looking for trouble. Watching this, Fang Yuan remained expressionless, but cold light glinted in his eyes.

"You must be Fang Yuan. Your tavern cheated me, but I'll give you a chance to apologize publicly so no one says I bully juniors. Hahaha!"

Man Shi laughed loudly. "As long as you bow and apologize, this matter is settled. I keep my word!"

Instead, Fang Yuan gave them a startling "surprise"—flicking his wrist, he instantly unleashed a moonblade.

Face-sized and glowing eerie blue, the crescent cleaved through a square desk like tofu.

Feeling the mortal threat, Man Shi panicked and screamed at the last second, "Monolith Gu!"

Deep grey light enveloped him instantly as his skin hardened into rock.

But before the armor fully formed, the moonblade struck his chest.

With a soft sound, the rock skin tore open, leaving a deep diagonal gash across his chest as blood poured out.

"Fang Yuan, what are you doing?!" Man Shi shrieked at the top of his lungs from the table, his expression savage.

His muscles bulged as the rock skin thickened over his body and covered the wound, though blood still poured out heavily.

Fang Yuan's face remained calm as he kept walking, having no intention of speaking.

Another moonblade!

"You!" With no time to speak, Man Shi raised his arms to protect his head and chest.

The moonblade struck his arms, carving a deep wound and sending rock fragments flying.

The moonblade's strength caused Man Shi's body to fly backwards.

Weighed down by stone armor, Man Shi crashed backward through the collapsing table.

Walking slowly, Fang Yuan spotted the opening with cold eyes and fired another moonblade.

Man Shi raised his arms, but the moonblade flew in at a cunning angle born of deep battle experience.

Man Shi's arms blocked only half of the attack; the rest struck his chest.

Gasping heavily, Man Shi tried to rise, but searing pain forced him back down.

"Fang Yuan, you can't kill me! If you do, the punishment hall will arrest you!" Man Shi scrambled backward against the floor.

Fang Yuan walked over and stomped heavily on his wounded chest.

Even more menacingly, an eerie blue moonlight hovered primed in Fang Yuan's right hand.

Recognizing a primed moonblade, Man Shi froze in terror.

"You... you can't kill me!" he growled with wide, terrified eyes.

"I won't kill you." Fang Yuan said the first sentence since he arrived.

"But I can break your limbs. Under clan rules, I'd only pay a fine and serve brief detention. But you? You would spend your life bedridden and ruined, unable to take missions. Is that acceptable?" Fang Yuan looked down at him.

"Damn it! If I hadn't been caught off guard and injured from the start, how could he... Urgh!"

Man Shi's voice cut off abruptly as his eyes met Fang Yuan's.

Fang Yuan's half-narrowed, pitch-black eyes looked down on him eerily.

Had they held killing intent, Man Shi would not have feared. Instead, they held only utter indifference.

An indifference born of supreme disdain for the world, disregarding all rules.

"These eyes... these eyes..." Man Shi's pupils shrank to pinpricks as his deepest memory resurfaced.

Two years ago, at night in the bamboo forest.

A white-robed youth had similarly trampled him underfoot.

"Damn it! If I had refined the Monolith Gu, how could you break my defense?" Facing imminent death, he had screamed in indignant fury.

"Oh? In that case, I won't kill you." The white-robed youth smiled with amusement. "Go cultivate harder, refine the Monolith Gu, and we'll fight again. Hehe, I hope you bring some excitement to my life in the future." The youth lifted his foot, sparing him.

That youth was Bai village's genius, Bai Ning Bing—who had slain Rank three elders even at Rank two.

Surviving and escaping Bai Ning Bing had skyrocketed Man Shi's reputation.

Over two years, Bai Ning Bing's face had blurred in his memory, but those eyes remained unforgettable.

To think that in his own village, he could see these eyes!

Overcome with terror, Man Shi's anger and indignance evaporated, leaving not a shred of fighting spirit.

Having achieved his objective, Fang Yuan lifted his foot. "You can scram now."

As if granted an imperial pardon, a pale Man Shi scrambled out of the tavern on all fours.

Fang Yuan stood on the spot, his gaze sweeping the area.

The surrounding Rank one and Rank two Gu Masters instinctively averted their eyes.

Jiang Ya finally understood how Fang Yuan achieved such success.

"Because we were never the same kind of person!"

# Chapter 109: Treehouse hides the Liquor worm

Gu Yue Man Shi was defeated by the newbie Fang Yuan!

After this, Gu Yue Dong Tu sensibly ceased his petty schemes.

Business at Fang Yuan's wine tavern happily returned to normal.

Amidst this backdrop, a merchant caravan made its way toward Qing Mao Mountain.

Leading the caravan was an orange-yellow Treasure Brass Toad, two and a half meters tall, bearing the Rank four Gu Master Jia Fu on its back.

Once the caravan settled in the village, Fang Yuan sighed inwardly. "It changed again. In my memories and by precedent, the caravan arrived in summer. Now it comes in spring, two to three months early, and on a far larger scale."

The root cause was undoubtedly his killing of Jia Jin Sheng.

Fang Yuan's deception had led Jia Fu to believe Jia Jin Sheng's death was a plot by his rival, Jia Gui.

Striving for commercial results, the competing Jia brothers had set out before the snow even melted.

The clan leader Gu Yue Bo received Jia Fu.

"Brother Gu Yue, I trust you've been well?" Jia Fu smiled warmly, though a long scar now marked his face.

"Hahaha, Brother Jia Fu, you are quite early this year." Seeing the scar, Gu Yue Bo's heart jolted, though he refrained from asking.

"The early bird gets the worm. I've brought many precious goods your noble clan will need."

"Good news indeed," Gu Yue Bo said, eyes flashing. "The day after tomorrow is our Awakening Ceremony; I cordially invite Brother Jia to attend."

"In fact, there is also another matter." Jia Fu hesitated.

"You have come from afar, so please speak freely. Our clan will do its utmost to assist," Gu Yue Bo said.

Jia Fu sighed. "It concerns Jia Jin Sheng. I brought clan investigation experts and hope you grant us leeway during our probe."

Gu Yue Bo immediately revealed a look of understanding.

Fang Yuan was strolling around tents and street stalls.

This year's caravan was far larger than before, boasting not only more tents but even a Gu house.

Infused with essence, the Rank three Three Star Cave Gu formed a three-layered treehouse of shops.

Following the crowd, Fang Yuan stepped inside the colossal tree.

Stepping onto the first floor, Fang Yuan immediately spotted a Liquor worm showcased on a central counter.

"Jia Fu isn't just targeting Gu Yue village; he's truly going all out. Returning to the clan must have provoked him deeply," Fang Yuan thought, intrigued.

# Chapter 110: Old woman, you’re too tender!

"Liquor worm..." Fang Yuan muttered, stepping up to the central counter.

Combining this Liquor worm with sweet, spicy, sour, and bitter wines would allow him to refine the Four Flavours Liquor worm, though fusion carried a risk of failure.

"I was just wondering where to find a second Liquor worm, and fate brings one right to me. I cannot let this chance slip—I must obtain it!" Fang Yuan's eyes gleamed with resolve.

He looked at the price on the counter.

"Liquor worm—Five hundred primeval stones."

A Liquor worm normally cost 580 primeval stones; this was eighty stones below market price.

"Grandma, this Liquor worm is only five hundred primeval stones!" A young girl walked in, screaming in shock.

Eyes glowing, the girl tugged her grandmother's arm. "Grandma, tomorrow is the Awakening Ceremony! You promised me a gift—why not buy me this Liquor worm?"

Gu Yue Yao Ji... Fang Yuan recognized her immediately as the medicine hall elder, leader of the clan's most lucrative department.

"Alright, alright. If my obedient granddaughter wants it, Grandma will buy it for you," the old woman sighed affectionately.

"Then Grandma, let's call the clerk and buy it right away!"

Gu Yue Yao Ji shook her head. "That's not how they sell Gu here. See that paper and pen?"

Gu Yue Yao Ji explained, "Write your bid on paper and drop it into the slot. The highest bidder gets the Liquor worm."

"How interesting!" The girl took a sheet of bamboo paper and picked up the brush, but hesitated.

Knitting her brows, she pouted, "Grandma, what price should I write? If it's too low we lose it, but if it's too high we overpay."

As the girl watched eagerly, Gu Yue Yao Ji wrote a price and signed her name.

Folding the paper, she motioned to the girl. "Go slip it in."

The girl obediently took the paper and dropped it into the counter's slot.

Returning to Yao Ji's side, she asked uncertainly, "Grandma, will this work?"

The old woman nodded. "It should. Someone might bid higher, but that would be an exorbitant price only a fool would pay. Don't worry, this Liquor worm is practically ours."

Seeing them leave, Fang Yuan's eyes shone with severity.

"Obtaining this Liquor worm won't be easy. This grandmother and granddaughter are just one competitor—who knows how many others have dropped bids into that slot?"

"Luckily, I reclaimed the family assets, sold Rank one vitality leaves, and rented out the tavern and bamboo buildings. With these savings, I can actually compete."

Though precious, the Liquor worm was only a Rank one Gu with a predictable price range.

The crux was: what price did Gu Yue Yao Ji bid?

She did not lack money, but how much was she willing to pay for her doted granddaughter?

As long as Fang Yuan's price, if even a single point, is higher, he wins.

"Yao Ji, did you say those words to scare off competitors? Against me, you are far too naive!" Fang Yuan smiled coldly.

Old woman, you're far too tender!

# Chapter 111: Primeval stones are but material goods

At the reception desk.

"What? Someone else bought the Liquor worm?" Gu Yue Yao Ji frowned, having never expected her high bid to fail.

"Hmph! Who is so wicked as to snatch my Liquor worm?" the young girl demanded angrily.

"Yao Le," Gu Yue Yao Ji reminded her granddaughter.

The young girl pouted and obediently shut her mouth.

The clerk, a Rank two female Gu Master, bowed. "I apologize, but business rules forbid disclosing customer information."

Gu Yue Yao Ji countered, "I won't ask for the buyer's name; I only wish to know the final price."

The clerk bowed again. "The price is confidential as well. Rest assured, the Jia family accepted the highest bid."

"Little girl, do you know who I am?" Yao Ji's face darkened.

"What is going on here?" A Rank three middle-aged Gu Master hurried over.

Turning to Gu Yue Yao Ji with a smile, the supervisor said, "Lord Yao Ji, please forgive us."

Seeing he was also a Rank three Gu Master, Yao Ji's expression softened, though she still pressed to know the winning price.

The supervisor pondered before replying, "How about this: since Lord Yao Ji wishes to buy a Liquor worm, I can privately arrange to transfer one. Truthfully, our warehouse holds three, and Lord Jia Fu personally decides their sale. You know how precious Liquor worms are. As for the price, we can simply use your previous bid."

Gu Yue Yao Ji shook her head slightly, tapping her cane on the floor.

"I am not seeking a discount," she said. "The price will match whatever the previous Liquor worm sold for."

"This..." The supervisor hesitated, clearly seeing through her intention.

Yao Ji pressured him, "What? Is the price so high you fear I cannot afford it?"

"Of course not." The supervisor sighed and quoted the winning price.

Hearing the price, the young girl sighed in relief, then felt indignant. "What? It was only twenty primeval stones more than ours?"

Gu Yue Yao Ji narrowed her eyes, but didn't speak.

Meanwhile, Fang Yuan had already left the treehouse for the wine tavern.

With the second Liquor worm acquired, he only needed the four wines—sour, sweet, bitter, and spicy.

"I already have sweet wine from the inheritance mission, and spicy and sour wines are common. My only concern is bitter wine," Fang Yuan muttered.

His worry proved true; scouring countless tents yielded spicy and sour wines, but no bitter wine.

"Not everything goes as wished." Helpless, Fang Yuan shelved the fusion plan.

In the afternoon, he went to the treehouse again.

The central counter, previously displaying the Liquor worm, now held a Cleansing Water Gu.

"As for the Liquor worm this morning, my quote was likely twenty primeval stones above Gu Yue Yao Ji's." Fang Yuan smiled coldly.

Fang Yuan did not bid on the Cleansing Water Gu, saving his remaining funds for better Gu worms in the coming days.

"I lack scouting and movement Gu. The wolf tide arrives next year, and the caravan won't return. Though Flower Wine Monk left an inheritance, he did so hastily after being injured. Who knows if it's complete, or what Gu comes next?"

On the third day, he discovered a Black Boar Gu!

The Black Boar Gu's strength increase would stack with his existing White Boar Gu.

Thus, at noon, he had another Gu worm in his possession.

It was the seventh day.

On the treehouse's second floor, Fang Yuan discovered a red steel Relic Gu.

It could instantly raise a Rank two Gu Master's primeval essence by a small realm!

Priced at three thousand primeval stones, it drew eager bids from numerous Rank two Gu Masters.

"With this red steel Relic Gu, reaching middle stage would let me use Moonglow Gu and White Jade Gu far more often."

"I've bought the Liquor worm, Black Boar Gu, and wine. This red steel Relic Gu's final price will definitely exceed five thousand primeval stones, perhaps reaching eight thousand. With the wolf tide looming, raising one's cultivation by a small realm is invaluable. My primeval stones aren't enough to secure this Relic Gu!"

# Chapter 112: Truly a great resolve

The Relic Gu was on sale for only one day; Fang Yuan's only option to raise funds quickly was selling his tavern or bamboo buildings.

With the clan underestimating the coming wolf tide, selling his buildings now would yield the highest price.

"Wealth is merely material; cultivation is the foundation. Yet selling to the clan is too cheap, and what individual has the means to buy them immediately? Haggling takes too long, and I only have one day. Wait, there is one person..."

Hesitating no longer, Fang Yuan left the treehouse for Gu Yue Dong Tu's estate.

His former servant, Shen Cui, opened the door.

"Home?" Stepping into the courtyard, Fang Yuan eyed the familiar surroundings with mockery, feeling neither belonging nor attachment.

"Fang Yuan, you dare return!" Seeing him, Aunt erupted with shrill fury. "You ungrateful scoundrel! Have you no conscience?"

Ignoring her fury, Fang Yuan sipped his tea and asked slowly, "I'm here to sell the tavern and bamboo buildings. Are Uncle and Aunt interested?"

"Bah, you ungrateful wolf—" Aunt froze as realization struck. "What? You want to sell the tavern and bamboo buildings?"

Setting down his cup, Fang Yuan leaned back. "Bring Uncle here to discuss it."

"Say another word of nonsense and I leave. Others would gladly buy these assets—don't regret it when I sell to them."

Aunt paused. "Do you truly want to sell these assets?"

"I will only wait five minutes," Fang Yuan added coldly.

Soon, Gu Yue Dong Tu appeared before Fang Yuan, unaccompanied by Aunt.

"Fang Yuan, I won't beat around the bush. Why sell the wine tavern and bamboo buildings? Keeping them guarantees steady income," Uncle asked incredulously.
