"Because I want to buy a red steel Relic Gu," Fang Yuan replied honestly.

"I see." Uncle's eyes gleamed. "Then are you selling the Nine Leaf Vitality Grass too?"

"Impossible." Fang Yuan shook his head unhesitatingly. "I am only selling the tavern, bamboo buildings, land, and eight servants."

Seeing Fang Yuan's resolve, Gu Yue Dong Tu felt deeply disappointed and helpless.

After discussing secretly for two hours, they signed a secure transfer agreement.

Gu Yue Dong Tu regained the assets, while Fang Yuan left with three servants carrying boxes of primeval stones.

Gu Yue Dong Tu's face was solemn throughout.

The deal restored Dong Tu's income, yet he was not happy at all.

To obtain a Relic Gu, Fang Yuan had unhesitatingly sold his assets, abandoning a life of future comfort.

Putting himself in Fang Yuan's shoes, Gu Yue Dong Tu wondered, *Could I do this?*

*I can't.*

Despite his hatred, he could not help but marvel inwardly, *To abandon future comforts so readily—what incredible resolve!*

# Chapter 113: Keeping quiet and earning a fortune

On the second floor, Gu Masters clustered around the central counter, discussing and sighing.

As night fell, competition peaked as many Rank two Gu Masters submitted final bids for the red steel Relic Gu.

Fang Yuan stood among the crowd, listening tranquilly to the chatter.

No one mentioned his name. To the crowd, Fang Yuan was merely a lucky youth who had inherited family assets, nowhere near the level of Chi Shan, Mo Yan, or Qing Shu.

"Good. Less attention means acquiring the red steel Relic Gu more smoothly. Still, repeated purchases should have drawn his notice."

The counter's branches and leaves suddenly surged, wrapping firmly around the Relic Gu. When they unfolded, the red steel Relic Gu was gone, replaced by a Revert Gu.

The crowd slowly dispersed, but Fang Yuan remained.

"Young Master Fang Yuan, please come to the third floor. Lord Jia Fu wishes to speak with you," a voice suddenly whispered into his ear.

Ascending the stairs, he entered a small study.

Hearing footsteps, he looked up, revealing his scarred face, and smiled warmly. "We meet again, little brother Fang Yuan."

"Greetings, Lord Jia Fu." Fang Yuan cupped his fists in salute.

Once Fang Yuan sat, Jia Fu pushed a bamboo paper toward him.

Glancing at it, Fang Yuan recognized his signed bids submitted over recent days.

Feigning doubt, Fang Yuan asked, "What does Lord Jia Fu wish to show me?"

Jia Fu's eyes gleamed. "These are your quotes. Frankly, I'm amazed by your business acumen; every bid was remarkably close to the final price."

"Though you only won three Gu, your other bids fell short by barely ten primeval stones. Brother, are you interested in working for me?"

Fang Yuan shook his head. "I am honored, but I cannot leave the clan."

"I see." Jia Fu's eyes flashed. "You may misunderstand me. A year ago I doubted you, but the Bamboo Gentleman cleared all suspicion. Jia Jin Sheng was my brother, so I hope you understand my feelings then. Also, I have invited divine investigator Tie Xue Leng, who has never failed to solve a case. He will surely uncover the truth and grant you justice."

"Tie Xue Leng..." Fang Yuan recognized the name; this was unwelcome news.

"I've heard of the divine investigator. When will he arrive at Qing Mao Mountain?" Fang Yuan asked with feigned anticipation.

"The divine investigator is busy; he might only arrive the year after next."

Fang Yuan was relieved; he had plenty of time to prepare.

"Take this token. Should you ever need my help, it will prove you are always welcome."

Fang Yuan offered polite apologies as he received the token and red steel Relic Gu.

Stowing the two items carefully, he made his way out.

Exiting the treehouse, Fang Yuan bumped into Chi Shan.

Chi Shan looked grim, while beside him, Chi Cheng inquired about the red steel Relic Gu.

Seeing Fang Yuan, Chi Cheng snorted and turned away, while Chi Shan nodded in silent greeting.

Fang Yuan nodded in return with a faint smile.

The red steel Relic Gu would instantly advance his cultivation by a realm.

A year ago, Fang Yuan had sought Jia Jin Sheng's friendship to enter the future Gu battle competition.

Unpredictably, circumstances had led him to kill Jia Jin Sheng with his own hands.

Relying on Jia Fu worked just as well; this token was his ticket into the future competition.

# Chapter 114: Monkey King in the cave

Three days after the caravan left the village.

A group of stone monkeys with round green eyes pursued Fang Yuan relentlessly.

"Moonglow Gu!" Fang Yuan willed, chopping his right arm toward the pursuing monkeys.

An eerie blue crescent moonblade sliced into the monkey pack.

The moonblade cleaved through the front ranks without slowing.

Amid frantic shrieks, five or six stone monkeys died on the spot.

Enraged, the surviving monkeys screeched and leaped at Fang Yuan with renewed fury.

Calm as ice, Fang Yuan fought while retreating, firing moonblades whenever monkeys drew close.

However, Moonglow Gu heavily drained his primeval essence.

Each moonblade consumed 10% red steel primeval essence, while his aperture held only 44%.

Three moonblades later, his aperture held only 14% essence.

Playing it safe, he stopped using moonblades and activated the White Jade Gu.

The monkeys surrounded him, one leaping to headbutt violently upward at his chin.

Fang Yuan snorted, intending to crush the brazen monkey with his fist.

On second thought, he checked his fist and took the blow with his chin instead.

Just before impact, his chin gleamed with cold white jade light.

The impact snapped his head back, while the monkey crashed down, screeching in agony.

Protected by White Jade Gu, he suffered only mild dizziness and no injury.

He had taken the blow intentionally to accustom his body to such impacts and the ensuing dizziness.

Fang Yuan was ruthless—not only toward enemies, but toward himself as well.

He came to hunt stone monkeys nearly every three days.

Beyond Flower Wine Monk's inheritance, his purpose was honing combat skills against the monkeys.

Fifteen minutes later, this battle ended.

Rock fragments littered the ground, interspersed with dozens of jade pearls.

Studying the monkeys' habits and adapting tactics, Fang Yuan had pushed deep into the rock forest's central area.

After many attempts, Fang Yuan finally exterminated the monkey group on this rock pillar.

Counting the caves on the final, massive pillar, Fang Yuan estimated at least five hundred stone monkeys.

Yet ordinary stone monkeys were merely minor trouble, requiring only a little extra effort.

What drew his grave attention was the cave at the very top.

Its entrance was at least twice as large as any surrounding cave.

"A monkey king must reside in that cave," Fang Yuan frowned.

A beast king posed several times more threat than ordinary beasts.

They harbored partner Gu worms that emerged to assist them in battle.

"This monkey king shouldn't be too strong, or it would already control surrounding groups," Fang Yuan judged.

Yet to defeat it, Fang Yuan could rely solely on his own power.

"Rank two initial stage essence is insufficient; it's time to use the red steel Relic Gu." Fang Yuan eyed the cave deeply, then left the second secret room and closed the rock door.

# Chapter 115: Advancing to middle stage

Inside his aperture, two Liquor worms drank primeval water while a Black Boar Gu circled the red steel Relic Gu.

"Time." At Fang Yuan's thought, primeval essence poured toward the red steel Relic Gu.

The red steel Relic Gu shook and flew up, radiating bursts of red light.

To advance normally, Fang Yuan would have to grind slowly, cleansing the light membrane wall with light red primeval essence.

Now, the red steel Relic Gu poured red light directly into the aperture wall with astonishing results.

The light condensed, transforming the light membrane into a shimmering water membrane.

Right now, Fang Yuan had advanced to middle stage!

Exhausting its energy, the red steel Relic Gu vanished into the red light.

A thread of scarlet primeval essence appeared in the Primeval Sea.

This middle stage essence, far more condensed, sank to the Primeval Sea's depths around the White Jade Gu.

Fang Yuan used primeval stones until his 44% sea was completely filled with scarlet primeval essence.

Half an hour later, he re-entered the stone forest, heading toward the central area.

As he entered the monkey groups' warning zones, furious jade eye stone monkeys emerged from the stone pillars.

Fang Yuan remained calm, his focus fixed on the highest cave.

Fang Yuan retreated cautiously, but the stone monkey king did not appear.

"Is there no monkey king? Or has it already emerged?" Fang Yuan wondered.

Just then, the Spring Autumn Cicada in his aperture trembled, releasing a startled cry.

The vital Gu's warning!

This only occurred when it sensed an intense threat to his life.

Fang Yuan immediately activated the White Jade Gu without hesitation.

A layer of bright white jade light enveloped his body.

The next instant, the stone monkey king—thrice the size of normal monkeys—appeared on Fang Yuan's left and struck fiercely at his shoulder with sharp claws.

The White Jade Gu's defense blocked the stone monkey king's attack.

The impact consumed five percent of his scarlet primeval essence.

"A wild concealing-type Gu worm!" Fang Yuan retreated hurriedly, severely disadvantaged without detection methods.

Cunningly, the monkey king vanished into concealment again after its attack failed.

Maintaining a full-body defense with the White Jade Gu constantly drained primeval essence, which he could not sustain indefinitely.

Over five hundred stone monkeys encircled Fang Yuan aggressively.

"Chirp!" Right then, the jade eye stone monkey king appeared and screeched commands.

Though over five hundred monkeys pursued him persistently, Fang Yuan smiled coldly without panic.

He had deliberately chosen a route surrounded by numerous rival monkey groups.

Storming under their king's lead, the horde infringed on neighboring territories, provoking fierce counterattacks.

The pursuers became entangled in territory fights, but the monkey king persisted in chasing Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan fought while retreating, repeatedly blocking sneak attacks; luckily, his newly advanced middle stage essence could withstand the heavy consumption.

The monkey king held the initiative with its concealment Gu, able to attack at will and slip away safely.

"Without a stealth-removal Gu, hitting this concealed monkey king with Moonglow Gu relies purely on luck."

Fang Yuan stopped abruptly about a hundred meters from the rock door.

"I only have 10% primeval essence left—not enough to reach the secret room and safely bar the door."

Fang Yuan stayed still, knowing the stone monkey king was hiding nearby, waiting to strike fatally.

"To take risks, one must risk life itself!" Fang Yuan's eyes glinted coldly as he stripped off his upper garment.

# Chapter 116: Killing the Monkey King and getting new Gu

"11% primeval essence means only two moonblades or enduring two sneak attacks. Relying solely on Moonglow Gu or White Jade Gu will not suffice. My only chance is to seize the moment the monkey king strikes, firing a moonblade to kill it instantly!" Fang Yuan calculated rapidly, devising the optimal strategy.

Stone monkeys lacked strong defense, and the monkey king's reliance on sneak attacks exposed defense as its weakness.

A single moonblade could kill five or six stone monkeys; even if it didn't kill the king, it would heavily injure it.

"This cunning monkey refuses to strike, waiting for my primeval essence to drain? No matter—I'll trust the Spring Autumn Cicada and gamble on this chance!" Fang Yuan resolved, eyes gleaming ruthlessly.

He stood with hands at his sides, holding his collar and narrowing his eyes, deactivating the White Jade Gu's defense.

Suddenly, the Spring Autumn Cicada vibrated in the aperture.

The next moment, a sudden crack sounded as the stone monkey king materialized on his left!

"White Jade Gu!" Fang Yuan's eyes flashed as white jade light enveloped his entire body.

The monkey king's heavy blow nearly toppled him, draining five percent of his primeval essence and leaving only half behind.

Failing to kill Fang Yuan, the stone monkey king tried to flee!

Fang Yuan lacked time to counterattack, but managed to flick his shirt outward.

To keep the shirt from tearing, Fang Yuan released both hands, watching the floating garment dart around at shocking speed.

"Now!" Fang Yuan's eyes gleamed coldly; this moonblade would decide the battle, his heart calm as ice.

A wild beast after all, the monkey king panicked when the shirt covered its face.

An eerie blue moonblade flew out, striking the stone monkey king.

The monkey king shrieked in pain, its camouflage broken.

A deep wound stretched from its chest to its left leg, pouring blood.

Fang Yuan's moonblade severed the shirt, but blood traces exposed the monkey king. Terrified, the heavily wounded beast fled.

Fang Yuan also retreated to the rock door; with his primeval essence nearly depleted by the moonblade, his battle strength plummeted.

"Monkeys, foxes, and bei are more intelligent and cunning than ordinary wild beasts, but they lack reckless courage and retreat when injured. Wild boars or bulls would go into a frenzy instead. This monkey king only has one Gu worm. It grants stealth but cannot hide blood traces—if I'm right, it must be the Rank one Stealth Rock Gu."

"The outcome is decided." Fang Yuan shut himself in the rock room to replenish his primeval essence.

Once his primeval essence returned to its peak, Fang Yuan opened the rock door and re-entered the forest.

Following the cleared path into the forest, Fang Yuan slaughtered every stone monkey in his way.

Fifteen minutes later, he reached the central pillar once again.

The stone monkey king lay on the ground, turned to stone and dead.

A jade eye stone monkey stood screeching atop the corpse.

"Saved some effort of mine." Fang Yuan slowly got closer.

A Gu worm rose from the monkey king's corpse, drifting toward the new king.

Fang Yuan drove away the new king with a timely moonblade, then stepped up and grabbed the Gu worm.

The Gu looked like an ordinary, uneven grey rock, neither square nor round. Thrown onto a road, no one would ever notice it.

As Fang Yuan predicted, it was the Stealth Rock Gu.

At Fang Yuan's mental call, the Spring Autumn Cicada leaked a trace of aura within his aperture.

The Stealth Rock Gu instantly froze, cowering like a mouse before a cat.

Fang Yuan's red primeval essence activated and instantly refined it.

Sent into his aperture, the Stealth Rock Gu sank to the Primeval Sea's depths alongside the White Jade Gu.

Driving the monkeys away, Fang Yuan quickly headed to the base of the rock pillar.

Drawing close, he discovered a cave beneath the pillar.

Inside the small cave, stone stairs descended into the darkness.

With the situation unclear and the forest restabilizing, Fang Yuan chose not to descend the stairs.

"Haste brings no success. Having found the inheritance clue, it is time to return." Controlling his curiosity, Fang Yuan retraced his route.

Enduring mounting pressure and the pursuit of hundreds of monkeys, he battered his way out of the rock forest.

TIme passed quickly, and summer replaced spring.

Fang Yuan cultivated diligently, using the red steel Relic Gu to instantly match Fang Zheng's cultivation speed.

Lacking a Gu to conceal his middle stage aura, his new cultivation level was exposed a day after obtaining the Stealth Rock Gu.

He nurtured his aperture toward upper stage while gathering ingredients to fuse his Gu, selling vitality leaves to sustain cultivation.

July, the start of autumn.

A wild Rank five Gu suddenly appeared at the mountain foot, shocking all of Gu Yue Village!

# Chapter 117: Bitter shell wine and River Swallowing Toad

A solemn and serious atmosphere filled the conference hall.

Clan head Gu Yue Bo sat in the main seat with unconcealed worry. "Three days ago, a River Swallowing Toad washed down the Huang Long River and settled at the mountain's foot near our village. It is currently sleeping and blocking a river channel. Left unchecked, it poses a constant threat to our village. Elders, do you have any ideas to drive it away?"

The River Swallowing Toad was a Rank five Gu of immense power that could vomit an entire river. If provoked, it could submerge half of Qing Mao Mountain and destroy the village.

After a long silence, Gu Yue Chi Lian spoke, "The situation is grave. If news leaks, someone might deliberately provoke the River Swallowing Toad to harm our clan."

He continued after a moment, "There is a graver issue: if the River Swallowing Toad floods Qing Mao Mountain, the submerged wolf dens will force wolf hordes uphill to survive. The wolf tide will erupt ahead of schedule, forcing us to battle countless beasts for space at the mountain peak."

Gu Yue Bo spoke heavily, "Do not forget our foundation. The first ancestor founded our village here for the underground spirit spring. If the mountain floods, that spirit spring could be destroyed."

"If disaster strikes, why not ask the Xiong and Bai villages for help? We are bound together; surely they won't refuse!"

"It is premature to ask for help," Gu Yue Bo shook his head, dismissing the idea. "We are not yet in dire straits. When the first ancestor established the village, he killed an attacking Rank five Blood River Python. Compared to that beast, the River Swallowing Toad is far more manageable."

"It has a mild temperament, alerting only to the aura of Gu and vomiting a river only when severely injured. The previous clan head mentioned a rumor..."

"We cannot rely on that," Gu Yue Bo shook his head. "Those are unverified rumors, and we cannot be careless. I suggest sending a group of Gu Masters to test it first."

Gu Yue Chi Lian spoke, "Then I shall send someone from my Chi faction. If he cannot succeed, no one in our clan can."

Clan head Gu Yue Bo smiled, "Very well, we shall assign it to Chi Shan's group."

At a window table in the tavern, Fang Yuan sat alone, calmly sipping wine.

"Shopkeeper, is there any progress on the bitter shell wine I asked about a few days ago?" Fang Yuan asked.

Fang Yuan needed a bitter wine for his Liquor worm fusion.

The old shopkeeper replied, "Young Master, someone in Bai Village has tasted that bitter shell wine. The bitter shell dissolves sand into bitter water, which was harvested to brew the fragrant bitter shell wine."

Fang Yuan raised a brow. "Does that mean Bai Village has this bitter shell wine?"

The shopkeeper bowed hastily. "I cannot guarantee it, having only heard rumors. In truth, Bai Village is famous for white grain liquid. Along with our green bamboo wine and Xiong Village's bear gall wine, it forms the three wines of Qing Mao. Even if Bai Village has bitter shell wine, there cannot be much."

"Even if there is little, I must find it," Fang Yuan thought.

Snapping out of his thoughts, Fang Yuan saw the shopkeeper still waiting and waved his hand. "Alright, you can leave; there is nothing more for you here."

Summoning his courage, the shopkeeper pleaded, "Young Master, please take back the tavern! The previous landlord slashed our wages, and we can barely support our families."

Fang Yuan shook his head expressionlessly. "I have already sold the tavern and cannot reclaim it under the contract. Furthermore, I have no interest in this business. Leave now."

"Young landlord, we cannot survive like this! Please have mercy on us!" The shopkeeper knelt and implored.

Laughing coldly, Fang Yuan grabbed a wine jar and smashed it over the shopkeeper's head.

The jar shattered, spraying wine as blood poured from the old man's head.

"Do you think I dare not kill you? Tactless fool, get lost." Cold light flashed in Fang Yuan's eyes.

Trembling from the murderous intent, the terrified shopkeeper scrambled away.

Nearby patrons began discussing the crisis. "River Swallowing Toad..." Fang Yuan smiled inwardly upon hearing this.

In truth, the River Swallowing Toad was gentle and not dreadful. Naturally inclined to sleep, it often drifted along rivers, floating belly-up on the water to sleep soundly.

Upon waking, it would drink its fill of river water and return to sleep. Uninterested in fighting, its first reaction when encountering enemies was to flee, only counterattacking fiercely if cornered without escape.

"This River Swallowing Toad probably fell asleep, drifted down the Huang Long River into a tributary, and reached the foot of Qing Mao Mountain," Fang Yuan deduced.

# Chapter 118: Legend of the River Swallowing Toad

Every River Swallowing Toad's stomach allegedly carried a river.

Though Fang Yuan never used a River Swallowing Toad in his past life, he remembered this Gu vividly because of one person.

Two centuries later in his past life, a unique Gu Master would appear—Jiang Fan.

His entire success was attributed to a River Swallowing Toad.

Jiang Fan was a fishery servant when a River Swallowing Toad drifted ashore, floating belly-up in deep sleep.

The "toad corpse" blocked the river's flow, causing countless problems for Jiang Fan at the fishery.

One day, the River Swallowing Toad awoke and stared at Jiang Fan with half-opened eyes.

Finally, the deadline arrived, and the village overseer came to collect the fish.

Facing death for failing his fish quota, Jiang Fan ran to the River Swallowing Toad to bid farewell.

He patted the toad's belly. "Old toad, to think I'll die first. I hope you live better in your remaining days."

Half-submerged in the water, it opened its mouth and began swallowing the river to sate its hunger.

Jiang Fan watched dumbfounded as the water level receded rapidly before his eyes.

A moment later, the river dropped drastically to expose bedrock, with water only knee-deep.

The River Swallowing Toad burped, its stomach contracting to vomit out a mountain of seafood.

Seeing the seafood pile into a mountain, Jiang Fan was overjoyed and shouted, "I'm saved! This is enough for three months. Old toad, oh old toad, it's all thanks to you!"

He packaged those seafood, and quickly handed them to the person-in-charge.

Shocked and suspicious at the massive catch, the overseer reported it, while village Gu Masters also discovered the drastically dropped water level.

Panicking, the villagers gathered in large groups to drive the River Swallowing Toad away.

Jiang Fan knelt and pleaded frantically, but the Gu Masters kicked him aside to execute him.

But at this moment, the River Swallowing Toad arrived.

Carrying Jiang Fan, it spewed a torrent that engulfed the whole village and flooded half the hill.

This battle startled the entire southern continent!

Thereafter, Jiang Fan's fame spread; having the River Swallowing Toad meant commanding a Rank five Gu.

Yet Jiang Fan lacked an awakened aperture, commanding a River Swallowing Toad as a mere mortal.

"I wonder if Jiang Fan will still appear given my influence," Fang Yuan chuckled after reminiscing.

"What? These lowly commoners dare surround the main gate? Outrageous! They grow bolder by the day—kill them all!" the punishment hall elder roared.

Gu Yue Chi Lian said, "That is not the priority. If even Chi Shan cannot wake the River Swallowing Toad, who else can? We may need reinforcements from the strength-specializing Xiong clan." He sighed. "For the clan's safety, we must hire them. Whatever the price, it will be worth it."

"Clan leader, elders, this junior has something to report," Gu Yue Chi Shan said, bowing from where he had been listening in the hall.

Gu Yue Bo nodded with appreciation. "Chi Shan, speak freely if you have an idea."

Chi Shan asked, "Elders, must waking the River Swallowing Toad rely solely on one person's strength?"

Gu Yue Bo replied, "The River Swallowing Toad is mild and will not rage when pushed awake, yet Chi Shan's attempt failed."

Chi Shan asked, "Could Clan Leader grant me a Brute Force Longhorn Beetle Gu? Combining one bull's strength with my natural might, I could push it awake."

"You cannot use a Gu worm's strength," an elder denied his request. "A Gu's aura will alert the River Swallowing Toad. If it feels threatened and rampages, who will bear the consequences?"

"Indeed," Gu Yue Bo agreed. "Using a Gu will never win its approval, even if awakened. It must be a single person waking it with their own strength."

"I understand," Chi Shan said, cupping his fists. "In that case, I recommend someone to the clan elders whose strength surpasses mine."

"That person is Gu Yue Fang Yuan," Chi Shan named him.

# Chapter 119: This lad is stubborn, he needs more polishing

"Gu Yue Fang Yuan?" The elders exchanged glances upon hearing the name.

"Ah, I remember him. Isn't he the kid who sold his inheritance to buy a red steel Relic Gu?" an elder realized.

"Regardless, the boy is strong. In the arena, he shattered Jade Skin Gu's defense to defeat Fang Zheng," an elder recalled.

This time, clan head Gu Yue Bo was the one left embarrassed.

"Even so, how could his strength compare to yours?" Chi Lian asked Chi Shan skeptically.

Chi Shan replied, "Fang Yuan also bought a Black Boar Gu, feeding it pork for months to raise his strength. I saw him testing his strength by moving a massive rock. His power is definitely not inferior to mine."

"I see. To think Fang Yuan has grown so much," Gu Yue Bo nodded. "Then let us dispatch Fang Yuan's group to try."

At this, the internal affairs elder rose awkwardly. "Lord Clan Head, Fang Yuan is a loner and belongs to no group."

The internal affairs elder sighed. "I urged him repeatedly, but he refuses to join a group, constantly slacking off after receiving that inheritance."

His face darkened. "He takes the compulsory mission every month only to fail. I have never seen a worse record; nearly every assignment ends in failure. I have spoken with him multiple times, yet he stubbornly refuses to repent. Because he hasn't broken clan rules, I can only give light punishments to this crafty kid!"

Gu Yue Bo decreed, "Issue a compulsory order for Fang Yuan to try. This stubborn lad needs polishing; if he fails, we will punish him."

Fang Yuan listened briefly before losing interest in the stale news. He stood to leave just as someone entered the tavern.

"Here you are." Under countless gazes, the man approached Fang Yuan. "Let's go, the clan has issued a compulsory order. We head to the mountain foot; I'll explain on the way."

Fang Yuan's eyes flickered. Unable to refuse a compulsory order, and knowing the River Swallowing Toad posed little danger, he agreed.

"Shopkeeper, heaven has eyes—what swift retribution! That is a Rank five Gu even lord Gu Masters cannot handle. Sending someone so young is practically sending him to his death!"

Just then, a nearby Gu Master chimed in, "I heard that."

"Well said! That bastard Fang Yuan deserves no pity even if he dies! Speak more, and I'll reward you generously!" The Gu Master slammed a primeval stone onto the table with a bang.

Had Fang Yuan seen him, he would have recognized the healing Gu Master whose beloved he had used as a human shield during the beast tide.

Fang Yuan hurried down the mountainside with Chi Shan's group to the base, where he saw the Rank five River Swallowing Toad.

Massive as a hill, it lay belly-up, blocking the channel until upstream waters swelled and downstream virtually dried up.

Sensing its aura, the two Liquor worms in Fang Yuan's aperture curled into balls. The Black Boar Gu lost its liveliness and dove downward, while the Moonglow Gu in his palm extinguished its light.

Only the Spring Autumn Cicada was still sleeping peacefully.

"Fang Yuan, you're up next," Chi Shan said from the side.

Standing by the river, Fang Yuan gave a test push, but the toad's slippery skin seemed to nullify his strength.

"Can you do it?" Chi Cheng sneered from the side.

Ignoring him, Fang Yuan told Chi Shan, "My strength is only slightly above yours. Pushing it is possible, but I will need your help."

"What help?" Chi Shan immediately asked.

Fang Yuan explained his plan. Chi Shan grew skeptical: "Wouldn't that count as teamwork? If the River Swallowing Toad wakes, will it still acknowledge you and leave willingly?"

Fang Yuan smiled. "Don't worry. As long as you stay out of its notice, it will work. It's just a Gu, after all—don't overestimate its intelligence."

# Chapter 120: Literally effortless

Calmly, Fang Yuan thought, "Enhanced by both Boar Gu, I possess the strength of two boars. Combined with my base strength, I match five or six adults. Pushing this toad requires two bulls of force, but harnessing the tides will suffice... here it comes."

Fang Yuan turned around and saw crashing waves surging forward, stirring the calm river waters.

The swelling tides crashed continuously against the River Swallowing Toad, throwing spray high into the air.

Aided by the relentless waves, Fang Yuan pushed persistently until the River Swallowing Toad finally shifted from its spot.

The riverbed widened downstream, and the flowing water made Fang Yuan's pushing much easier.

After three hundred meters, the River Swallowing Toad opened its drowsy eyes.

Its sleepy, deep green eyes gradually narrowed, fixing upon Fang Yuan beside it.

"Jiangaaaang!" The River Swallowing Toad raised its head, opening its maw in a strange croak.

Lowering its head, the River Swallowing Toad began guzzling the incoming river water.

The river rushed in at tenfold speed, swallowed into its belly as the water level plummeted.

Noticing the river's change, Chi Shan's trio hurried over, staring stunned as the River Swallowing Toad gulped down the water.

"You succeeded?" Chi Shan looked at Fang Yuan.

"Seems so," Fang Yuan nodded lightly.

Once the flow halted and the water level dropped, the toad raised its head and heaved, vomiting out countless fish, prawns, and turtles.

Fang Yuan glanced over casually, but suddenly caught the aroma of wine.

"Strange, why does it smell like wine?" Chi Cheng sniffed in surprise.

"It must be a Hundred year bitter shell," the female Gu Master said, pointing to a broken, plate-sized black shell.

"Indeed. Bitter shells turn sand into bitter water, which ferments into wine over a hundred years," Chi Shan added.

Fang Yuan did not need details; he had already grabbed the shell and begun collecting the bitter shell wine.

With the water swallowed by the toad, several Hundred year bitter shells buried in the riverbed were exposed.

Fang Yuan quickly collected six small Hundred year bitter shells: two were broken, but four remained intact.

"I've finally obtained the bitter wine! Now I can refine the Four Flavor Liquor worm!" Fang Yuan rejoiced inwardly.

After vomiting the seafood, the River Swallowing Toad croaked once more, gave Fang Yuan a parting look, and swam downstream.

"He actually succeeded!" Chi Shan murmured in relief, watching the River Swallowing Toad until it vanished from sight.

"What, it was that easy? Had I known, we could have done it ourselves instead of letting Fang Yuan become a hero!" Chi Cheng grumbled enviously.

"Fang Yuan, you've done a great service this time—you are the Gu Yue clan's hero!" Chi Shan looked at him with mixed feelings.

"Oh," Fang Yuan replied half-heartedly, his eyes gleaming as he kept rummaging through the seafood for Hundred year bitter shells.

News of the River Swallowing Toad's expulsion quickly reached the village.

The internal affairs elder wore a complicated expression, feeling slapped in the face after having just criticized Fang Yuan.

"For his merit in driving away the River Swallowing Toad, Gu Yue Fang Yuan is exceptionally promoted to team leader and rewarded five hundred primeval stones," Gu Yue Bo decreed thoughtfully.

"Fang Yuan became the clan's savior? This..." The male Gu Master bearing a grudge against Fang Yuan was left at a loss.

His group leader suddenly pointed at the tavern clerks and shouted, "Insolent mortals, you dare slander our clan's hero? You deserve death!"

Even before finishing his words, he shot a moonblade.

Never expecting such lethal danger, the elderly shopkeeper was struck in the neck by the moonblade and instantly beheaded.

"Lord, spare us!" Terrified by the sight, the clerks dropped to their knees, begging loudly for mercy.

"Group leader, what are you doing?" The male Gu Master rose.

"Times have changed, Ah Hai," his group leader sighed grimly. "Fang Yuan is now a hero. If word gets out that we slandered him, what will happen to us? There are investigative Gu Masters everywhere. If someone hostile reports our words to the elders, our futures are finished!"

The male Gu Master broke into a cold sweat.

Indeed, clan relationships ruled above all; slandering a hero who had defended the clan showed ungrateful disloyalty.

"We must show our stance immediately. These mortals have cheap lives—sacrificing them will protect us. Strike now and kill one each! Afterwards, praise Fang Yuan and declare our stance!" the leader shouted.

"Dammit!" the male Gu Master cursed, choosing his future over hatred without hesitation.

With a moonblade, a clerk died on the spot.

The male Gu Master pointed righteously at the clerks and shouted, "Gu Yue Fang Yuan defended the clan as a hero—how dare you slander him!"

Though he deeply hated Fang Yuan, having to praise him in public filled him with intense self-disgust.

"Lord, didn't you ask us... Urgh!" a clerk cried out in deep injustice.

A moonblade went flying, splitting him in half.

"Lowly slaves, not only slandering Fang Yuan but trying to implicate us!" a female Gu Master shouted fiercely with a cold expression as she struck.

The watching Gu Masters felt as if they were viewing a play.

# Chapter 121: Four Flavours Liquor worm

"Gu Yue clan..." Xiong Li stood on the mountainside, gazing at the distant Gu Yue village with a complicated expression.

Bai village's rise had broken Qing Mao mountain's balance. With the traditional hegemon Gu Yue clan weakened and Xiong village struggling, an upheaval loomed.

Xiong Li knew the pressure of the wolf tide kept this upheaval at bay, forcing the three villages to collaborate, set aside grievances, and maintain a tacit peace.

"Once the wolf tide ends, the mountain's structure will shatter. Bai Ning Bing reaching Rank three so quickly is terrifying..." Xiong Li felt deeply weighed down.

Xiong Li was Xiong village's top Rank two Gu Master. Renowned from countless victories, he held the Grand Bear Gu to unleash a bear's strength and was hailed as Qing Mao mountain's strongest person.

"We have two objectives: deliver our clan head's personal letter to Gu Yue's clan head, and investigate the River Swallowing Toad. Gu Yue village is not our territory, so keep your temper in check without diminishing Xiong village's prestige, understood?" Xiong Li shouted, his gaze sweeping over them.

Meanwhile, four wine jars sat before Fang Yuan in the second secret room.

Four flavours of wine stood ready: sweet golden honey wine, spicy white grains liquid, sour redberry wine, and bitter shell wine.

Sitting cross-legged on the ground, Fang Yuan willed the two Liquor worms out of his aperture.

Following his will, the two Liquor worms entered the redberry wine jar.

They began fusing within the redberry wine, forming a ball of white light whose brilliant radiance shone against the wall.

Fang Yuan threw primeval stones into the jar—one, ten, fifty...

At the hundredth piece, the light ball shrank to fist size and floated in the jar.

With the redberry wine exhausted, Fang Yuan picked up the golden honey wine and poured it in.

Sweat soaked Fang Yuan's brow as he maintained the mentally exhausting fusion of both Liquor worms' consciousness.

Fang Yuan followed the pattern and successively poured the bitter shell wine, then the white grains liquid.

When all four wines were completely consumed, the light flared brilliantly before vanishing entirely.

"Success." Fang Yuan knew he had succeeded without even checking the jar.

With a thought, a Gu flew unsteadily from the wine jar.

It was the Four Flavours Liquor worm.

Unlike the white Liquor worm, the Four Flavours Liquor worm flickered with four colors: red for spicy, blue for bitter, green for sour, and yellow for sweet.

Fang Yuan's newly refined Four Flavours Liquor worm could refine Rank two primeval essence, raising it by a small realm.

Having used a red steel Relic Gu to reach middle stage, the Four Flavours Liquor worm now granted him upper stage primeval essence.

His battle strength doubled, while the Four Flavours Liquor worm nourished his aperture to accelerate his cultivation.

Refining primeval essence with the Four Flavours Liquor worm increased his primeval stone consumption, which vitality leaf sales alone could no longer sustain.

"Next, I must fuse the Stealth Rock Gu into Stealth Scales Gu, adding another expense."

Refining Stealth Scales Gu required other common materials besides Stealth Rock Gu, which Fang Yuan had already asked Jiang Ya to gather.

"Refining Stealth Scales Gu will make entering the secret cave convenient, and allow me to advance or retreat freely during the wolf tide," Fang Yuan pondered.

As it grew late, he retrieved the Four Flavours Liquor worm into his aperture and headed back toward the village.

At the village gate, a strength contest had just concluded.

Xiong Li's group stood proudly, while Chi Shan's group and the gate guards wore grim expressions.

Though shorter than Chi Shan, Xiong Li looked down on him. "Chi Shan, with Brown Bear Innate Strength Gu, I possess a bear's strength. As our bout just showed, you are no match for me."

"Why boast about beating me? I am no longer the clan's strongest. If you have skill, speak after defeating Fang Yuan," Chi Shan retorted, forced to mention Fang Yuan.

"Fang Yuan? I heard an A grade genius named Fang Zheng emerged in Gu Yue clan, but who is Fang Yuan?" Xiong Li asked doubtfully.

Chi Shan sneered, "Fang Yuan is Fang Zheng's elder brother. He single-handedly pushed the Rank five River Swallowing Toad over a hundred meters to drive it away. Ask anyone in the village if you doubt it." The Xiong Li group's expressions changed. A Rank five River Swallowing Toad! Fang Yuan!

# Chapter 122: There are many blizzards in life

Three face-sized moonblades slashed through the air, carrying an eerie blue light.

Instantly, sixteen to seventeen jade eye stone monkeys were slain.

Inspecting his aperture, Fang Yuan found more than half his dark red primeval essence remaining.

Moonglow Gu required ten percent primeval essence per blade, allowing Fang Yuan sixteen casts with his refined upper stage essence.

The Four Flavours Liquor worm placed him at pseudo-upper stage, granting upper stage essence and sharply rising combat strength.

"Clearing three stone pillars in just two days is far faster than before. At this rate, I can reopen the path to the central area in half a month," Fang Yuan pondered.

"Following Flower Wine Monk's style, the central pillar's underground cave should be the next trial, likely housing another Earth Treasure Flower Gu. His strength inheritance is nearly finished. After all, he set up this inheritance hurriedly while gravely injured; at most, two obstacles remain."

"Flower Wine Monk's inheritance is a micro-scale legacy: limited in items, but advantageous because the initial trials adapt simply to local conditions. I hope that the Gu worms in them will be related to scouting or movement-type!"

Time passed quickly, autumn went and winter arrived.

Fang Yuan trekked alone through the snow, hurrying back to the village from the stone crack's secret cave.

"Over two months have passed, yet my progress clearing stone pillars has been poor," Fang Yuan thought with a scowl.

This was not for lack of effort, but because early signs of the wolf tide had already appeared.

Surrounding wild beasts were purged as a result, causing frequent small beast tides and packs of crippled wolves.

Gu Yue village mobilized numerous Gu Masters and scouts for cleanup operations, prompting Fang Yuan to sensibly reduce his visits to the secret cave.

A deep beastly howl suddenly echoed through the snowy wind.

Fang Yuan swiftly stopped and looked around, alert.

A small pack of over twenty lightning wolves quickly came into view.

"Wolves this close means more clan patrols. With the secret cave nearby, I must stay away for some time," Fang Yuan thought.

Howling, they charged at Fang Yuan one after another.

"Moonglow Gu," Fang Yuan willed; a moonblade flew out from his hand.

The eerie blue moonblade sliced through the snowy wind, beheading three sickly wolves before a fourth dodged.

Though handicapped, blind, or tailless, these crippled wolves possessed ample battle experience and cunning.

Moving nimbly under their coordinated assaults, he dodged calmly and attacked decisively.

Before long, the lightning wolf pack was cut down by half.

At a wolf's mournful howl, the pack immediately halted their assault and retreated.

Fang Yuan stood calmly, watching the crippled wolves disappear into the snow, seeing no need to reveal his hidden strength.

Once confirming the pack had fully fled, he promptly harvested the wolf corpses.

"In this world, all living things struggle to survive," Fang Yuan sighed, ruthlessly finishing off the dying wolves.

"Small crippled wolf packs are manageable, but large crippled packs or a healthy pack will be troublesome."

"Once the wolf tide breaks out and the clan mobilizes, I cannot remain aloof. To hunt lightning wolves alone, I must obtain a scouting or movement Gu, or I will fall in the wolf tide."

"If Flower Wine Monk's legacy lacks such Gu, the three clans' joint contribution board during the wolf tide will offer them. Then, I can exchange my contributions for Bai or Xiong village's Gu worms."

"Look, Fang Yuan is back." "He is carrying a bag—did he hunt crippled wolves again?" "Is he the one who saved our village?"

As Fang Yuan walked through the village, people parted ways with praise, curiosity, and envy.

"Fang Yuan!" Chi Shan suddenly appeared from a corner and shouted.

# Chapter 123: I travel alone through heaven and earth

"What's the matter?" Fang Yuan looked at Chi Shan.

Chi Shan stared at Fang Yuan gravely. "Do you know Xiong Li has been seeking you for a strength contest?"

"I know," Fang Yuan nodded.

Chi Shan sighed, "Xiong Li challenging you is no mere competition; it involves resource allocation for the three clans' alliance. Under the wolf tide, failing to ally means destruction. Yet distributing profits remains the biggest issue, keeping the three clans at a stalemate."

Chi Shan continued, "I competed with Xiong Li. Between Brown Bear Innate Strength Gu and Grand Bear Gu, he wields two bears' strength. I am far from his match, and the title of Qing Mao Mountain's number one strongest person undoubtedly belongs to him."

"Down to business: your strength matches mine, so you cannot beat him. But as the hero who drove off the River Swallowing Toad, you cannot lose. If you lose, Gu Yue clan's interests will suffer. For the clan, discard personal honor and avoid battle!"

Chi Shan looked down. "I know avoiding the contest disastrously damages your reputation, but if you lose, the clan must concede far more. But this matter started due to me, so in my personal name, I will compensate you for it, as part of my goodwill."

Saying so, Chi Shan handed Fang Yuan a large money bag.

Fang Yuan weighed it, and chuckled, "So my reputation is only worth two hundred primeval stones?"

Detecting sarcasm, Chi Shan said solemnly, "Do not be indignant, Fang Yuan. I am here on a mission. Avoiding the match is an order from the clan higher-ups. Regardless of what you think, understand your position."

Saying so, he turned around and left, leaving deep footprints in the snow.

"The clan used my driving off the toad as a bargaining chip; to eliminate it, Xiong clan sent Xiong Li to challenge me."

"To the clan, we are mere chess pieces. Sadly, they willingly serve as pawns, brainwashed by the illusion of glory."

"I never wanted to compete anyway; reputation is merely empty praise. I should thank Chi Shan for two hundred primeval stones."

Laughing, Fang Yuan recited a poem, "White snow blankets the land as I travel alone through heaven and earth. Unattached, my solitary shadow wanders freely."

Having already sold the bamboo buildings and tavern, he stayed in a simple rented flat, needing nothing more than shelter over his head.

As agreed, Jiang Ya arrived at Fang Yuan's residence by evening.

"Lord Fang Yuan, here are the primeval stones." Entering, he respectfully handed over five money bags.

Handing Jiang Ya nine vitality leaves, Fang Yuan asked, "Have you purchased what I requested?"

Looking disappointed, Jiang Ya shook his head, "Lord Fang Yuan, the timing is bad. With the wolf tide coming, the clan tightened resource control. The Fish Scale Gu is only slightly less valuable than Jade Skin Gu. I tried my best, but immediate results are difficult."

At night, unexpected guests arrived.

Gu Yue Qing Shu and Gu Yue Fang Zheng.

Qing Shu got straight to the point, reiterating his desire to buy the Liquor worm from Fang Yuan.

He also sought to buy the Black Boar Gu and even the Nine Leaf Vitality Grass.

Refusing the vitality grass was obvious, and having already fused the Four Flavours Liquor worm, Fang Yuan rejected both requests.

"Having gained a boar's strength, the Black Boar Gu has little value to me anymore. Its best advancement path is the Steel Mane Gu. With my White Jade Gu it is redundant. I might as well exchange it for a Fish Scale Gu."

Fang Yuan thought of this, and mentioned it.

"Fish Scale Gu?" Qing Shu frowned, then nodded. "I see, you lack a defensive Gu worm. The Fish Scale Gu fuses into the Rank two Scale Armor Gu, providing good defense."

Fang Yuan did not expose himself. "The Black Boar Gu is more valuable than the Fish Scale Gu, so you must compensate the price difference."

"Naturally." Qing Shu nodded. "Setting aside the grass, are you truly not selling the Liquor worm? It is useless to you now."

Fang Yuan shook his head, "Don't mention the Liquor worm, it is not for sale."

Qing Shu laughed bitterly. "Fang Yuan, this is complicated. Medicine hall elder Gu Yue Yao Ji wants it for her granddaughter, Gu Yue Yao Le. Gu Yue Yao Ji loves her granddaughter and once tried buying a Liquor worm in the treehouse, but failed."

"You've used the Liquor worm, so you know its benefits. Lord Yao Ji truly wants this Gu worm for her granddaughter, and an elder's affection is completely understandable. She offers a high price and special medicine hall care if you are injured in the wolf tide. Please consider it."

# Chapter 124: I don’t need your understanding

"So that's it," Fang Yuan rubbed his chin, then shook his head, "This Liquor worm is not for sale. Since Yao Ji is insistent, why isn't she finding that Gu Master who bought the Liquor worm?"

Qing Shu sighed, looking troubled. "We don't know who has it, and inspecting apertures is an absolute taboo among Gu Masters. That is the biggest taboo among Gu Masters. Causing public outrage over a Liquor worm isn't wise. Still, keeping a treasure hidden is human nature."

Beside them, Fang Zheng urged, "Brother, the Liquor worm is useless to you, why cling to it? Granny Yao Ji is very kind. Sister Yao Le is a good person who would cherish the Liquor worm. Brother, you saved the village and brought honor to our family. This brings honor to our family. Why be so stubborn now? Isn't this too petty?"

Fang Yuan's expression chilled. "Are you lecturing me? The Liquor worm is mine—you have no right to dictate what I do."

"Fang Zheng, go look around outside." Fang Zheng wanted to say more, but Qing Shu stopped him.

"Either way, I cannot understand you in this matter, brother," he said, opening the door.

"What I do, does not require your understanding, Fang Zheng." Fang Yuan was expressionless.

"If there is nothing else, brother Qing Shu can take your leave." Fang Yuan instantly evicted his guest.

Touching his nose, Qing Shu laughed awkwardly, "There is another matter, regarding the Nine Leaf Vitality Grass."

"Regarding vitality leaves, my group wants to buy your supply directly at appropriate compensation."

Seeing business approaching, Fang Yuan naturally did not reject, "Then, sixty-five primeval stones per leaf."

With the wolf tide nearing, the clan had raised vitality leaf prices to sixty primeval stones each as resources grew scarcer.

"Finding it expensive? You don't have to buy. But during the wolf tide, the clan will tightly control resources and vitality leaf demand will surge. Prices will soar with no stock left. What do you think?" Fang Yuan's tone was calm as he safely evaluated his pricing.

Qing Shu said helplessly, "Raising prices like this is overbearing. Aren't you afraid of breeding grudges instead of building valuable relationships?"

Fang Yuan chuckled. "With the wolf tide nearing, a small fry like me could die any day. How can I bother with such trivialities?"

Qing Shu shook his head. "Everyone makes their own choices. But be careful—Lord Yao Ji won't let this drop easily. Goodbye."

"Thanks for the advice, take care, I won't see you off," Fang Yuan said, watching Qing Shu leave, confident that he would return.

More and more crippled wolves appeared near the village surroundings.

In December, crippled wolves peaked and a large pack slaughtered an entire hamlet at the mountain foot.

Under such pressure, the stalemated negotiations progressed rapidly, and the three-clan alliance was formed.

A month later, at the end of winter.

Alliance Slope, Three Clan Alliance Meeting.

Thousands of Gu Masters gathered here, creating a grand spectacle.

The Gu Masters wore similar battle robes, their belts displaying cultivation ranks. Fang Yuan stood among them.

Gu Yue Gu Masters were slender, Xiong clan Masters burly, and Bai clan Gu Masters pale with an eerie Yin aura.

# Chapter 125: Oh, then I admit defeat

"The traditional overlord Gu Yue clan, the rising Bai clan, and the formidable Xiong clan..." Standing in the crowd, Fang Yuan observed the entire situation.

Amid the discussions, the three clan heads stood in the rock building, looking down at the thousands of Gu Masters.

Gu Yue's clan head stood in the center, flanked by the Bai and Xiong clan heads.

Gu Yue Bo gripped the railing. "Our alliance must pull together. For this sparring tournament, stop short of killing. Begin."

As the crowd hesitated, Xiong Li's group stepped out first from the Xiong clan's side.

Passing the Gu Yue clan under watchful eyes, Xiong Li's group stopped before the Bai clan.

Xiong Li held his ground, while Xiong Jiang stepped forward beside him.

Facing the Bai clan's Gu Masters, Xiong Jiang cupped his fists. "Xiong clan's Xiong Jiang challenges Brother Bai Bing Yi."

Bai Bing Yi stood up grim-faced. Small-framed and pale, he looked sickly, yet none dared underestimate him.

"You have courage to challenge me, Xiong Jiang. How do you want to compete? Name it, and I'll accept," he said.

Xiong Jiang laughed darkly, "I specialize in defense, and you specialize in attack. How about this, I defend and you attack. I'll give you three attacking opportunities, and we'll compare the results."

Bai Bing Yi raised his brows. "Do you truly think you can block my Water Drill Gu? Don't complain if injured."

With that, he activated his Gu; his body turned dry and green like a block of dead wood.

This was the Rank two Gu, Roaming Zombie Gu.

"Even so, I am not afraid," Bai Bing Yi laughed coldly, raising his right arm.

Within two breaths, a light-blue spiral drill spun continuously around Bai Bing Yi's fist.

Rank two Water drill Gu!

"Receive my blow," Bai Bing Yi coughed, throwing out his fist.

Xiong Jiang raised both arms, forming an arm shield.

The water drill hit his guard, spraying water before knocking Xiong Jiang back five or six steps.

The flesh on both his arms was torn by the drill, exposing pale white bone.

Standing in place, Bai Bing Yi's pale face suddenly warped, and he spat a mouthful of blood.

"Shadow Bond Gu!" Bai Bing Yi stared at the snow, where Xiong Jiang's shadow was distorted at an eerie angle, connecting the two together.

"It seems I win, Brother Bai. With Shadow Bond Gu, you suffer ten percent of my damage—which is deadly to someone frail."

Wiping blood from his lips, Bai Bing Yi stared deeply at Xiong Jiang. "Good. Very good tactics. I lost."

Not quitting while ahead, Xiong Li's group left the Bai clan and marched to the Gu Yue clan.

Xiong Li called out loudly, "Who is Fang Yuan? Step forward! I heard you chased off the River Swallowing Toad alone—how about a contest of strength?"

The Gu Yue Masters parted ways, leaving Fang Yuan exposed in their midst.

With an unobstructed view, Xiong Li and Fang Yuan locked eyes.

Xiong Li grinned with rugged grace. "I hope Brother Fang Yuan will generously teach me."

"Oh, is that so..." Fang Yuan shrugged flatly. "Then I admit defeat."

# Chapter 126: Demise of the wood charm

Instantly, nearly all Rank two Gu Masters looked at Fang Yuan in astonishment. Fang Yuan ignored them, standing with arms folded and an indifferent expression. "Admit defeat? He yielded immediately—did I hear wrong?" "He is Fang Yuan? By directly conceding, he shows he's a coward and disgraces the clan!"

Several Gu Yue Gu Masters urged Fang Yuan to accept the challenge, but he ignored them. Reputation and honor were mere illusions—shackles used by higher-ups to bind members. Gazing at Fang Yuan, Xiong Li laughed. "I am truly disappointed. Is this how a Gu Yue village's bravery looks like?"

In his memories, the three villages barely survived the wolf tide, only to be destroyed two years later. Fang Yuan needed to grow strong before leaving. Seeing the scene unfold, Gu Yue Qing Shu stood up.

"Xiong Li, how about sparring with me?"

"Hehe, what will we compete on?" Xiong Li smiled, though his expression was serious. Without looking at him, Qing Shu raised a palm and channeled primeval essence, watching a green vine slowly sprout from his hand.

He calmly said, "Let's test your strength. If you break free from my green vine, I concede. How about it?" "Hehe, sounds good!" Xiong Li grinned, eyes gleaming.

He thought, I already have the strength of a bear, and Grand Bear Gu gives me the strength of two bears. Tough as the green vine is, two bears' strength will surely break it. Xiong Jiang had defeated Bai Bing Yi; defeating Qing Shu would bring immense glory! Smiling gently, Qing Shu extended both hands, sending two green vines growing from his palms.

The green vines coiled a dozen times around Xiong Li's arms, tightly binding his hands behind his back. The watching Gu Masters couldn't take their eyes off the contest.

"Please," Qing Shu said. Xiong Li opened his eyes, clenched his fists, and exerted his strength, every muscle bulging like rugged stone.

Strength of a bear!Boom.

The vines began to snap under his enormous strength. "Hahaha, Brother Qing Shu, it seems victory is mine!" Xiong Li laughed, speaking effortlessly despite the strain.

"Not necessarily." Qing Shu smiled with confidence. As he spoke, the vines on his hands shifted from jade to dark green. At the same time, the vines grew thicker, sprouting broad leaves. Xiong Li's face changed as he felt their binding strength increase tenfold.

Sensing danger, he activated the Grand Bear Gu in his aperture. His hair stiffened like needles and his muscles swelled, turning him noticeably taller and bulkier. Strength of two bears!

The green vines creaked under the terrifying pressure, but held fast, firmly confining Xiong Li.

His face flushing red, Xiong Li strained with all his might, but could only snap a single vine before exhausting himself.

"I lost." He released the supporting strength of Grand Bear Gu and said while gasping."You let me win." Qing Shu cupped his hands and retrieved the green vines."Senior Qing Shu, you are the best." Gu Yue Clan's Gu Masters were jubilant."Senior Qing Shu really deserves his name. Brother can't compare at all." Fang Zheng stood beside Qing Shu, looking up at him with reverence.

Xiong Li looked at Qing Shu with a complicated gaze. He had advanced by obtaining the Brown Bear Innate Strength Gu, but Qing Shu had also grown stronger. This was his true rival. Fang Yuan was just a newcomer, not worth worrying about. "It really is the Wood Charm Gu," Fang Yuan thought, observing Qing Shu from afar.

Xiong Li had failed to notice two emerald-green leaves growing from Qing Shu's hair.

Wood Charm Gu allowed one to battle as a tree spirit, absorbing natural essence directly from the air—unlike Gu Masters, who could only use primeval essence in their apertures.

Furthermore, the tree spirit embodiment amplified Gu worms like the Green Vine Gu and Pine Needle Gu.

Fang Yuan instantly connected the dots, "Wood Charm Gu's fusion advancement can be said to be one of the most extravagant advancement routes. It is a Rank three Gu and needs to fuse with a hundred-years Lifespan Gu to advance to a Rank four hundred-years Wood Charm gu. When the Gu Masters finds Lifespan Gu, they will usually use it directly to increase their own lifespan."In this world, a human could live for at most a hundred years if they didn't encounter any accidents or illnesses.

"The Wood Charm Gu is powerful but cannot be used for long periods. Prolonged use causes the Gu's power to assimilate the body, eventually transforming the user into a wooden corpse. In my previous life, Gu Yue Qing Shu died this way." Fang Yuan's eyes flashed. Powerful Gu worms usually carried severe side effects, requiring complementary Gu to mitigate them. Xiong Li's group returned to their camp, having defeated Bai Bing Yi before losing to Qing Shu.

# Chapter 127: So-called morals and relationships (Long chapter)

Only until evening did the Gu tournament come to an end.

After Xiong Li's challenge, no one else approached Fang Yuan.

The alliance gathering concluded successfully as the Gu Yue, Bai, and Xiong clans reached an agreement.

First was the rules: the three clans would temporarily set aside grudges to resist the wolf tide together and assist each other in danger. Internal killing was prohibited, and an investigative battle group would be established. Any discovered murderer would be expelled from their clan and judged jointly by all three clans, paying with their life. If they escaped, their family's lives would be forfeited.

Gu worms retrieved from deceased Gu Masters had to be surrendered to the clan; using them secretly resulted in murder charges. Once submitted, they could be exchanged for merit points.

Second was the battle merit board. Using small groups as units, it constantly displayed group achievements and rankings from all three clans. A lightning wolf eyeball yielded ten merit points, redeemable for Gu worms, primeval stones, and other resources.

Very quickly, a month passed.

Stepping on the remaining snow, Fang Yuan regulated his breathing and observed the surrounding battlefield.

Wolf cries could now be heard from not far away.

Any other Gu Master low on primeval essence after an intense battle might consider retreating, but Fang Yuan disregarded this, squatting down to gather eyeballs from the wolf corpses.

Despite his fluid, efficient movements, by the time he finished extracting, the wolf pack had half-surrounded him.

Fang Yuan stowed the wolf eyes and stood up smiling. His body rippled like water, vanishing on the spot.

"This works only because the lightning wolf king observes with its eyes, not its nose. Lightning wolves have eagle-sharp vision, but their sense of smell is no better than a human's. My Stealth Scales Gu counters these lightning wolves perfectly, but cannot hide from a dog's nose," Fang Yuan knew.

When he returned to the village, it was still afternoon.

Fang Yuan entered the village with the crowd and headed to the square before the clan head pavilion.

In the center of the village square stood a large banner, its written characters constantly shifting.

This was the battle merit board, carrying over a dozen Swimword Gu.

Someone suddenly shouted, "It changed, it changed! Qing Shu group is now #1. They pushed Bai clan's Bai Bing Yi to #2!"

Gu Yue Qing Shu walked through the crowd with a faint smile, while Gu Yue Fang Zheng followed close behind, fists clenched in excitement.

"Brother..." He spotted Fang Yuan watching from the crowd with folded arms.

"Brother, you are still all alone at the bottom of the rankings. Only by casting aside your prejudice and joining the collective can you feel the warmth and happiness of the clan," Fang Zheng sighed.

Fang Yuan stared at the tall merit board, where the last position displayed "Fang Yuan Group" in large letters.

Having never recruited members after becoming a group leader, he remained a solo leader—the most unique group across all three clans of Qing Mao mountain.

Half a month soon passed, and the spring atmosphere intensified.

"The current situation exists because the three-clan alliance lowered mutual caution, letting Gu Masters focus on fighting the wolf tide. Furthermore, the wolves killed so far were only the crippled ones. Thirdly, spring is mating season. Once summer arrives, healthy, energetic wolves will attack from all directions, inflicting devastating casualties on the three clans."

This year's wolf tide reached an unprecedented level of danger, its severity greatly underestimated by the clan higher-ups.

In his memory, most Gu Masters perished and the three clans' trump cards were nearly exhausted.

I would rather let the world down, than be let down by the world!!!

Kinship, friendship, and love were mere ornaments in life, incomparable to a man's grand ambition.

"These fools, bound by emotions and morals, deserve to be manipulated by rules. Worse, when they see others unrestrained, they jump out to criticize and impose these morals, refusing to let anyone enjoy more freedom. In doing so, they even revel in ridiculous moral superiority and bliss."

Today was important: he was preparing to enter the rock crack secret cave again.

# Chapter 128: If not for the harshness of the winter, how could we look forward to spring!

After clearing the monkey groups, Fang Yuan advanced toward the center of the stone forest.

In the shadow of a huge stone pillar, an artificial cave entrance appeared before him.

Shrouding himself in faint jade light with the White Jade Gu, Fang Yuan carefully descended the stairs.

A crude artificial stone door was in front of him.

"Golden Centipede's cave is precarious, Earth Communication is the way to avoid the disaster." Raising his torch, Fang Yuan read the characters carved on the stone door.

"If this is what I think it is..." Crouching down, he felt the damp soil. "There's something." Pleased, he dug into the ground and indeed uncovered an Earth Treasury Flower.

He carefully parted the petals and retrieved a Gu worm from the flower's center.

It resembled a small, yellowish-brown human ear, shrivelled and dull. In his palm, it felt warm like pickled radish, with dozens of ginseng-like roots growing from its sides.

This was a Rank two grass Gu—Earth Communication Ear Grass.

Earth Communication Ear Grass possessed a scouting range of three hundred paces, placing it among the elite Rank two investigative Gu.

It was easy to feed as well, consuming only ginseng roots.

"Earth Communication Ear Grass is simple to feed, but using it is troublesome," Fang Yuan mused. Channeling primeval essence into it, he instantly refined the Gu.

"In any world, how could one gain without paying a price?" Smiling coldly, Fang Yuan stored the Earth Communication Ear Grass in his aperture.

He gazed at the stone door deeply then turned around and left.

Over the next few days, he exchanged battle merits for ginseng roots, feeding the Earth Communication Ear Grass to restore its vitality.

Tonight, the moon was bright and stars were few.

After securing the room, Fang Yuan stood naked before a table holding a basin of warm water, a white towel, and a sharp dagger.

Even the floor beneath him was covered with a layer of thick cloth.

With an indifferent expression, Fang Yuan picked up the dagger, its polished blade gleaming coldly like a mirror.

The opening sentence of the Sacred Sunflower Scroll read: "Castrate yourself to train this skill."

Caressing the cold blade, he softly hummed—

Moon like the frost-filled night, the dagger shines with a cold light.

If not for the harshness of the winter, how could we look forward to spring!

Fang Yuan had severed his entire right ear, blood spurting from the wound.

A fleeting coolness gave way to an intense wave of agony.

Gritting his teeth against the pain, he summoned the Earth Communication Ear Grass from his aperture.

Pressing the Earth Communication Flesh Ear Gu to his wound, Fang Yuan channeled red steel primeval essence into it.

Its roots sprang to life and grew rapidly, rooting themselves directly into Fang Yuan's wound.

Fang Yuan felt like dozens of earthworms were drilling towards his brain from his wound.

Gu Masters normally used numbing Gu worms for this process, but lacking any, Fang Yuan endured the agony through sheer iron willpower.

As roots burrowed into the flesh, the Earth Communication Ear Grass fused with the bloody wound, becoming Fang Yuan's new right ear.

Finally, the bleeding ceased completely, leaving behind no scar.

Picking up a mirror, he inspected himself under the faint moonlight.

In the mirror, his right ear was more than twice the size of his left, resembling a deformity.

Setting the mirror down, he dipped the towel into the warm water and wiped the blood from his body.

Once cleaned, Fang Yuan picked up his severed right ear from the table.

Snorting coldly, he activated the Moonglow Gu to slice the severed ear into paste, destroying it completely.

Fang Yuan placed the basin of bloody water under the bed and tossed a piece of coal into it.

Only after finishing everything did Fang Yuan lie down on the bed.

# Chapter 129: Chainsaw Golden Centipede

When Fang Yuan awoke, it was already noon the next day.

Last night, after planting the Earth Communication Ear Grass, his right ear had been swollen, but now appeared completely normal.

With a thought, Fang Yuan channeled red steel primeval essence from his aperture to his right ear.

Instantly, his hearing sharpened multifold, catching the sound of numerous footsteps.

"This gives me a recon-type Gu." Putting on his shirt, Fang Yuan retrieved the water basin from beneath his bed.

While the iron was still hot, Fang Yuan entered the rock crack secret cave again.

This time, he brought a captured, bound deerling, using the Stealth Scales Gu to reach the rock door unnoticed.

Instead of opening the door immediately, he activated the Earth Communication Ear Grass.

Listening intently, his right ear swelled as sentient ginseng roots grew nearly half a meter long, reaching out to pierce shallowly into the rock door.

Without its roots touching the ground, the Earth Communication Ear Grass was mediocre for a Rank two recon Gu, but once rooted, its range expanded drastically.

As the roots pierced the rock door, Fang Yuan instantly heard the commotion behind it.

The rock door opened to reveal a long, dark tunnel stretching straight into the unknown.

Fang Yuan untied the deerling and tossed it into the tunnel.

Fang Yuan snorted and threw a moonblade.

Blood gushed from its wound, and driven by pain and terror, the deerling bolted into the dark tunnel.

Fang Yuan reactivated the Earth Communication Ear Grass, letting its roots burrow into the walls.

Fang Yuan's gaze shone as he heard a special sound.

The buzzing roar sounded like a revving chainsaw, full of arrogance, dominance, and wildness.

Rank three wild Gu worm—Chainsaw Golden Centipede!

This was the king of centipedes, the true killer of the centipede cave.

Fang Yuan could vividly picture it: over a meter long and two fists wide, slithering through the cave.

Rows of saw-like teeth lined both sides of its body, whirring rapidly like an electric chainsaw as it moved.

"Even White Jade Gu cannot withstand the Chainsaw Golden Centipede's attack. Flower Wine Monk clearly intended for me to use the Earth Communication Ear Grass to avoid it. But I have the Spring Autumn Cicada, so to subdue this Chainsaw Golden Centipede, it is not impossible!" Fang Yuan had a thought in his head.

"I am only at Rank two middle stage, so subduing a Rank three Chainsaw Golden Centipede now is premature, even if possible," Fang Yuan assessed.

Higher-ranked Gu cost more to feed, were harder to control, and demanded far more essence. Forcing it would only crush oneself like a baby wielding a giant hammer.

"The deerling has alerted the colony; it will take ten to fifteen days for the centipedes to settle. My theory is confirmed, but with no chance to strike yet, I must be patient—this cannot be rushed."

Putting his thoughts aside, Fang Yuan closed the rock door and slipped away using the Stealth Scales Gu.

# Chapter 130: Clansmen are still more reliable

Dark clouds covered the sky, casting shadows over Qing Mao Mountain and heralding an impending storm.

On a remote mountainside, a dozen robust lightning wolves snarled with bared fangs, surrounding Fang Yuan.

Though surrounded, Fang Yuan took the offensive, kicking off the ground to pounce toward the lightning wolf on his left.

Laughing loudly without evading, Fang Yuan swung his right arm, sending an eerie-blue moonblade flying into the lightning wolf's open mouth.

With a wet tearing sound, the moonblade cleaved the unfortunate wolf in two from mouth to tail.

Shrouded in white jade light, Fang Yuan charged through the shower of blood and rampaged among the wolf pack.

With the strength of two boars, Fang Yuan punched and kicked, shattering skulls and seizing the upper hand.

Watching his essence dwindle with several wolves still standing, he was forced to activate the Stealth Scales Gu!

Fang Yuan watched the wolves under Stealth Scales concealment while absorbing primeval stones.

When his primeval essence recovered to thirty percent, the wolf pack finally retreated reluctantly.

Lacking a movement Gu, Fang Yuan could not chase them. He deactivated the Stealth Scales Gu and revealed himself.

With replenished primeval essence and formidable combat strength, he slew four more lightning wolves in moments.

Losing their will to fight, the remaining wolves whimpered in fear, tucked their tails, and fled.

The ground was littered with wolf corpses—his spoils of war—yet Fang Yuan cautiously refrained from harvesting their eyeballs right away.

Earth Communication Ear Grass!

He lowered his right ear to the ground as roots sprouted from it to pierce the soil.

Various sounds reached his ear, but none belonged to wolf packs or approaching Gu Masters.

"It is safe for now." Breathing a sigh of relief, Fang Yuan drew his dagger and began carving out the wolves' eyeballs.

After he killed two more packs, thick dark clouds covered the sky. Strong winds swept through the pine forests, rustling leaves and sending green waves across the mountain.

Faint howls of numerous lightning wolves echoed in the wind.

Fang Yuan's expression paled slightly; according to his past life's memories, today the wolf tide truly erupted.

"These lightning wolves are cunning, attacking in weather that masks their movements," Fang Yuan thought, sprinting toward the village.

Hundreds of meters later, he ran into a five-person Gu Master squad.

"Hey, you're Gu Yue Fang Yuan!" A young Gu Master in the group raised his brows upon seeing him, greeting him strangely.

This was Gu Yue Peng, an academy classmate. Fang Yuan gave him an indifferent glance and brushed past without stopping.

"Little Peng, don't slow down. We must hunt at least fifty lightning wolves today to preserve our rank on the battle merit board!" the leader urged.

"Our group ranks seventy-fifth on the battle merit board, while Fang Yuan is past two hundred—a difference like heaven and earth! I'm fortunate to have such a caring senior as group leader. Since the wolf tide began, I've already exchanged merits for a Gu worm. My future is bright—how could Fang Yuan compare to me?"

"What can anyone accomplish fighting alone? Many hands make light work, and clansmen are reliable! Relying on the clan's strength, newcomers like us can grow safely. He became a group leader without recruiting any members, so it serves him right to be ranked last! With his eccentric personality, who would join him anyway?"

Strangely, they encountered no wolf packs along the way.

Unafraid, Gu Yue Peng sneered inwardly, "Hahaha, was Fang Yuan rushing back just to avoid this rainstorm? What a coward—what is there to fear from a rainstorm?"

Just then, the group leader's face paled, and he sucked in a sharp breath.

"Run, quick!" he shouted in terror, turning and fleeing before the astonished members.

Continuous howls echoed behind them, signaling a pack of over a thousand lightning wolves!

"Wait for me!" Gu Yue Peng sprinted desperately, but falling behind, he panicked and screamed for help.

To his despair, the leader who normally showed him great consideration did not even turn back, while teammates who had sworn brotherhood ignored his cries.

The next moment, an enormous force slammed into his back, knocking him down.

Lightning flashed, revealing the silhouette of a bold lightning wolf in the darkness.

Gu Yue Peng's pupils shrank to needlepoints, a thought flashing in his mind—"Hundred beast king!"

The next second, the bold lightning wolf clamped down, biting off Gu Yue Peng's entire head with a sickening crack.

Countless lightning wolves came from behind it and rushed ahead.

Rumbling sounds continued incessantly.Pitter-patter, heavy rain also started pouring.

The true wolf tide had begun!

# Chapter 131: Loneliness is the deepest darkness

Heavy rain poured throughout the night as snake-like lightning danced across the sky.

Lying in bed, Fang Yuan heard Gu Masters shouting outside and footsteps splashing through the rain.

During the wolf tide of his previous life, he was a Rank one Gu Master in the support unit, hiding safely within the village to escape disaster.

Now, however, he was expected to risk his life fighting the wolf tide like the other Gu Masters.

Instead of sleeping in his rented apartment, he stayed at the inn.

"The clan higher-ups have severely underestimated this wolf tide. The correct move is to defend the village to the end, but they are blinded by past experience..." Fang Yuan tossed in bed, contemplating.

Life was a play, but Fang Yuan had no intention of joining the performance.

On the contrary, he felt a sense of unexplainable loneliness.

"This is loneliness. Savoring it only steels my resolve to pursue the demonic path!" Fang Yuan's eyes shone as he recalled the legend of Ren Zu.

Legends say Ren Zu obtained Attitude Gu, which was like a mask he could not wear without a heart.

Having given his heart to Hope Gu long ago, Ren Zu had never feared hardship.

Yet to use Attitude Gu, he needed a heart once more.

Troubled, Ren Zu asked Attitude Gu, "I lack a heart to wear you. What should I do?"

Attitude Gu replied, "Lacking a heart, you merely need to find a new one."

Ren Zu was overjoyed, "Quick, tell me, how?"

Attitude Gu warned him, "This heart is called loneliness. Human, are you sure you want it? Once obtained, you will face endless pain, loneliness, and even fear!"

Unable to defy Ren Zu, Attitude Gu said, "Simply gaze at the starry sky in silence. When daylight comes, you will obtain the heart of loneliness."

That night, it was a star-filled sky.

Following Attitude Gu's instructions, Ren Zu sat alone atop the peak, gazing into the night sky.

"Sigh, even with Hope Gu, Strength Gu, Rules and Regulation Gu, and Attitude Gu, survival remains difficult. If I die tomorrow, it would be no surprise. Will the world remember me? Will anyone rejoice over my existence or mourn my death?"

As loneliness overtook Ren Zu, a brand-new heart formed within his chest.

The sun rose and shone upon his face, yet Ren Zu felt no happiness—only endless pain, despair, confusion, and fear.

Weeping in agony, he reached up and gouged out his own eyes.

His left eye fell to the ground, transforming into a robust youth with golden hair. He knelt at Ren Zu's feet, saying, "Father Ren Zu, I am your eldest son, Verdant Great Sun."

Meanwhile, his right eye became a young girl who took Ren Zu's hand, saying, "Father Ren Zu, I am your second daughter, Desolate Ancient Moon."

Ren Zu laughed loudly, tears streaming from his empty sockets. "I have children now and can endure loneliness. Even if I die, you will remember me."

"However..." he sighed at last. "Having lost my eyes, I can no longer see the light. From now on, you two shall observe the world for me."

It rained all night, stopping only at dawn.

When Fang Yuan left the inn, passersby on the streets wore heavy, sorrowful expressions.

Overnight, twenty-five groups were wiped from the battle merit board in the wolf tide. Rare survivors were maimed or crippled.

Over the next dozen days, the situation worsened daily.

First came hundred beast king bold lightning wolves, followed quickly by sightings of thousand beast king frenzy lightning wolves!

The three clans were forced to dispatch Rank three elders to handle the crisis.

By late July, as the weather warmed, joint cooperation among the three clans brought the grim situation under control.

Somewhere near the mountainside.

Three Gu Masters, exhausted from an intense battle, confronted a newly arrived bold lightning wolf.

"Damn it, insufficient primeval essence!" Group leader Xiong Zhan spat bloody water, watching the bold lightning wolf slowly approach.

"The cliff is ahead—there's no path left! What do we do?" a group member asked, pale with terror.

"What can we do except hope for reinforcements? I heard Lord Bai Ning Bing left closed-door cultivation and is rushing to the battlefield."

"Rather than hoping for Bai Ning Bing, I'd rather fight for survival! The beast king is dangerous because of its Gu. My Plunder Gu can forcefully steal an enemy's Gu, but I must remain stationary while channeling it. You have to protect me!"

"Understood!" The two exchanged glances and stepped in front of Xiong Zhan to shield him from the bold lightning wolf.

None of them knew that high atop the cliff, a white-haired youth in a white robe was watching the scene.

"Life is so boring..." Bai Ning Bing murmured, pouring water from a bottle.

It held not wine, but sweet mountain spring water.

"Fight and die. A mundane life is too boring; only an intense battle like this brings excitement and color, giving your life meaning."

To him, loneliness was the deepest darkness, and kinship merely a facade.

# Chapter 132: Three Step Fragrant Grass

The bold lightning wolf took its move, slowly approaching.

Behind them, Xiong Zhan knelt on one knee, gripping his right wrist and clawing his hand toward the bold lightning wolf.

"Plunder Gu!" he shouted, channeling all his primeval essence toward the Gu.

"I must succeed or we're doomed!" He gritted his teeth, exerting all his power.

He poured every drop of essence into Plunder Gu, while the bold lightning wolf attacked in a frenzy.
