"Damn it, not enough primeval essence!" The failed attempt triggered severe backlash, making him cough up blood.

Eerie blue lightning discharged and shot from the wolf's mouth.

Xiong Zhan had no time to cry out before the lightning struck him, charring him black and killing him instantly.

"Run!" Stripped of all fighting spirit, the two remaining Gu Masters fled in opposite directions.

The bold lightning wolf overtook one, pouncing and tearing out his throat.

Cut off by the beast, the last Bai clan Gu Master could only retreat toward the cliff.

Suddenly his cries cut short—he spotted Bai Ning Bing atop the cliff.

"Lord Bai Ning Bing! Lord Bai Ning Bing, is that really you?" Stunned, he shouted in overjoyed relief.

"Oops, you found me," Bai Ning Bing laughed, raising his right hand.

Pointing his right index finger downward, he shot a newly formed icicle.

The icicle struck the pleading Gu Master, piercing straight through his skull to his chin.

The bold lightning wolf was shocked by the event.

"Ignorant beast." Bai Ning Bing stared impassively, leaping down lightly from the five-meter cliff.

Midair, he clapped his palms together, conjuring an icy mist.

As the bold lightning wolf discharged electricity upward, Bai Ning Bing laughed, venting two streams of white vapor from his nose.

The vapor swirled around his body, forming a protective water barrier.

"Die." The youth's eyes shifted from black to pure crystal blue, completely devoid of emotion.

Bai Ning Bing landed lightly as blood dripped from his translucent ice blade.

A breath later, blood gushed from the neck as the severed wolf head rolled across the ground and its carcass collapsed.

The Lightning Current Gu residing in its corpse darted like lightning toward Bai Ning Bing.

With a flick of Bai Ning Bing's wrist, blade light flashed.

Under his blade, the Lightning Current Gu exploded into blue electricity, perishing instantly.

Bai Ning Bing slowly walked towards Xiong Zhan's corpse.

"The other Gu worms are ordinary, but this Plunder Gu is rather interesting." Extracting the Plunder Gu from Xiong Zhan's corpse, he held it to his chest.

As the symbol of the Bai clan's rise, the clan would certainly protect him.

"Competition is more entertaining." Yawning, he smiled. "I wonder how Qing Shu and Xiong Li are doing after my secluded cultivation."

Days later.

In the crowded square, Fang Yuan stared at a giant banner.

Words formed by Swimword Gu on the banner detailed the resources co-supplied by the three clans.

A wolf's eye yielded ten merit points, while twenty-five exchanged for one primeval stone.

"Three Step Fragrant Grass Gu," he muttered, staring at the description on the banner.

The Three Step Fragrant Grass Gu was a movement-assisting Gu that could increase his speed.

However, it would not be easy to obtain—it was unique on the resource board, and the merit points required were exceptionally high.

"Using my true strength, I could easily gather enough merit points for the Three Step Fragrant Grass Gu. But doing so would invite undesirable suspicion from the clan."

"Producing Vitality Leaves for merit points is too slow; someone might exchange for the grass Gu before I finish."

"An alternate currency like merit points temporarily prevents economic collapse, much like wartime governments on Earth printing paper money—which inevitably causes inflation and rising prices. Hence, every wolf tide is followed by economic depression."

Fang Yuan was pondering deeply when conversations around him caught his ear.

"Do you guys know? Bai Ning Bing struck again!"

"What, what, which unlucky Gu Master is it this time?"

"It's Xiong clan's Xiong Li, that dejected-looking one."

"Sigh, what is Bai Ning Bing doing? First injuring Chi Shan, now attacking Xiong Li. With the wolf tide upon us, he's still fooling around!"

"He always does as he pleases. Already a Rank three Gu Master, he still troubles Rank two Gu Masters—even using a Gu to suppress his cultivation to Rank two for fairness! He's truly incomprehensible!"

"Bai Ning Bing... hmph, a man with one foot in the grave. But that reminds me." Hearing this, Fang Yuan felt a stir in his heart.

He knew how to amass large amounts of merit points quickly.

# Chapter 133: Thanks for letting me kill you

Roots swayed from his right ear as a deliberately enraged bold lightning wolf and several hundred ordinary wolves pursued him.

Just as the wolves were about to overtake him, ripples of light surrounded Fang Yuan, and he vanished.

Repeating the process, he finally reached his destination and entered complete stealth.

The pursuing wolf pack sniffed where he vanished, and the bold lightning wolf soon spotted a new target.

Nearby in the mountain valley, five Gu Masters were battling a pack of wolves.

Possessing limited intelligence, the bold lightning wolf promptly forgot Fang Yuan, growling as its wolves surged into the valley like a tidal wave.

"How can this be?" The battling Gu Masters despaired at the sight.

"Quickly send the distress signal to the clan!"

The investigative Gu Master launched a Gu worm from his hand.

"It's useless." Fang Yuan sneered inwardly from high atop the cliff, looking down.

He had already scouted this group; the nearest squad in the vicinity was also trapped deep within a wolf pack's encirclement.

Fang Yuan had lured that pack over as well to pin them down.

"Recovering Gu worms from the battlefield for the clan is worth thousands of merit points—enough to exchange for the Three Step Fragrant Grass Gu."

"Next, I'll wait for them to die, lure the wolf pack away, and return to collect the Gu." Fang Yuan walked to a nearby tree and sat down leisurely.

With the Monolith Gu's powerful defense, he alone could block attacks from many lightning wolves.

The more precious the recovered Gu, the more merit points awarded. Returning the Monolith Gu would yield Fang Yuan around 3,900 points, boosting his merit board ranking by at least twenty places.

Bai Ning Bing was sleeping when battle sounds reached his ears.

"Another boring scene." Resting on the cliff edge, a single turn gave him a clear view of the valley. He was about to resume sleeping when he spotted a figure.

"Oh?" His eyes shone with surprise upon seeing Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan leaned against a tree eating a wild fruit, watching the battlefield coldly with no intention to intervene.

Yet from Fang Yuan, he sensed a familiar loneliness and aloofness.

Bai Ning Bing had been resting motionlessly higher up the cliff, evading the Earth Communication Ear Grass, but moving exposed him immediately.

Fang Yuan turned to see Bai Ning Bing slide down the cliff and land nearby.

The foremost genius of Qing Mao Mountain had killed a Rank three elder as a Rank two, altering the mountain's power balance.

Yet Fang Yuan merely glanced at him for a second before looking back down at the valley, calm and indifferent. He already knew all of Bai Ning Bing's secrets and trump cards.

"Why does he feel so familiar? It's as though we were already friends long ago!" Bai Ning Bing's heart surged with turbulence.

As Bai Ning Bing drew too close, Fang Yuan turned to meet him with a calm, dark gaze.

Halting in surprise, Bai Ning Bing stared rudely and said, "Eh, you really are interesting."

Fang Yuan ignored him, biting into his fruit while watching the intense fight below.

After a moment of silence, Bai Ning Bing asked, "That battle isn't interesting at all, what's there to watch?"

His brows rose in realization. "I see. You lured the wolves to kill those Gu Masters without leaving evidence. You're too cautious. If I were you, I'd just do it myself!"

He chuckled like a child finding a wondrous new toy. His brows dancing, he grinned, "You're truly interesting, and so is everything you do. Hehehe, I'm starting to like you!"

Fang Yuan retracted his gaze, turning around and looking at Bai Ning Bing.

He was like Fang Yuan's shadow, though Fang Yuan was further along the demonic path.

In his past life's memory, after the three clans barely survived the wolf tide, this man slaughtered them all, turning lush Qing Mao Mountain into a frozen hell!

"Bai Ning Bing, Bai Ning Bing..." Fang Yuan sighed inwardly, saying slowly, "You're very lonely, aren't you?"

Bai Ning Bing's eyes widened. Squatting down and nodding repeatedly, he sighed, "Yes, life has been dreadfully boring. Killing a Xiong clan Gu Master recently was somewhat fun. Look, I got this Gu."

Looking at the Plunder Gu in his hand, Fang Yuan chuckled, "So, you want to kill me?"

Bai Ning Bing leaped to his feet, his eyes shifting from black to crystal blue.

"Hahaha!" He laughed excitedly. "You understand! Life-and-death battles are the ultimate thrill—meeting someone like you is the best thing ever!"

Ecstatic, he cried out, "My life has never felt so vibrant! Thank you for letting me kill you!"

# Chapter 134: Hunting down

"I'll restrict my cultivation to Rank two; come, let's have a fair fight!" Bai Ning Bing laughed savagely.

Facing the crazed youth, Fang Yuan met his glare calmly.

"Wanna kill me, then come."

With nothing more to say, Fang Yuan took off, drifting away like a falling petal.

"Don't you run from me!"

Bai Ning Bing pursued immediately, giving relentless chase.

Fang Yuan scoffed inwardly, sprinting toward the mountain valley.

Disturbed by their intrusion into the valley's inner battlefield, the furious wolf pack immediately attacked Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing.

"Scumbag, stop right there!" Bai Ning Bing continued shouting furiously behind him.

Fang Yuan paid no heed, weaving through the wolf pack.

Nearby, water vapor blew from Bai Ning Bing's nostrils, forming a spherical shield of condensed moisture that enveloped him.

Whenever the lightning wolves struck this water shield, their charging force was dispersed by the flowing current, sending them flying back amidst rippling waves.

Even restricted to Rank two, Bai Ning Bing was faster, but the wolves' interference widened the distance.

Possessing the strength of two boars, Fang Yuan barreled forward, while Bai Ning Bing's water shield merely absorbed blows without clearing a path.

A bold lightning wolf took notice and was drawn toward the pair.

Without hesitation, the bold lightning wolf sprang forward, charging at Fang Yuan.

Yet the next instant, Fang Yuan vanished before Bai Ning Bing's eyes like a fading ripple.

Losing its quarry, the bold lightning wolf howled in anger and redirected its charge at full speed toward Bai Ning Bing.

"Hehehe, you truly are interesting! Hahaha, then I'll start my meal with this appetizer!"

Extending his ice blade, he began to spin rapidly, carving dazzling waves into a frosty tornado.

The radiant sword waves formed a raging blizzard that swiftly engulfed the wolf pack.

The bold lightning wolf howled fiercely against the storm, activating its internal Gu to coat its fur in crackling golden sparks.

Bounding forward, it shot into the ice blade storm like a golden meteor.

The bold lightning wolf collapsed, its heart pierced through by an ice blade, dead almost instantly.

The ice blade had snapped, but Bai Ning Bing wiped it with icy mist, instantly restoring the blade.

The bold lightning wolf's death scattered the pack, but Fang Yuan was nowhere to be seen.

Bai Ning Bing snorted, his arm flicking as the ice blade traced gleaming arcs through the air.

Caught off guard, the three Gu Masters never expected their savior to strike; they collapsed lifeless with looks of utter disbelief.

"Bai Ning Bing, what are you doing?!" Gu Yue Man Shi yelled in shock and fury.

"Murdering, of course. Are you stupid? Isn't it obvious?" Bai Ning Bing shrugged with a cold laugh.

With a shout, Gu Yue Man Shi charged toward Bai Ning Bing.

He activated the Monolith Gu at full power; his muscles bulked up beneath thick rock skin, transforming him into a stone man.

"Courting death." Bai Ning Bing laughed coldly, raising his blade.

The ice blade came down relentlessly, bisecting Gu Yue Man Shi in an instant.

Shaking his head, he turned toward the wide valley to shout:

"Come out! I've killed the Gu Masters for you. Come, let's have a life-and-death match!"

Just as he finished speaking, Fang Yuan appeared nearby.

His gaze flashing with excitement, the white-haired youth raised his ice blade and slashed toward Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan chuckled silently, turning on his heel to leave.

Infuriated that he was fleeing again, Bai Ning Bing chased Fang Yuan relentlessly.

Fang Yuan led him toward the nearest battle, where Xiong Li's group was fighting another pack led by a bold lightning wolf.

"Hold on, everyone!" Xiong Li urged his group, but choked on his words upon seeing Fang Yuan with Bai Ning Bing pursuing him.

"Bai Ning Bing!" Xiong Li's eyes spewed fire upon seeing the white-robed youth, uncontrollable rage surging within him.

"Wait, Bai Ning Bing seems to be chasing Fang Yuan," Xiong Lin noticed.

Fang Yuan quickly approached Xiong Li's group, shouting in feigned panic:

"Protect me! I saw the Plunder Gu on him—Bai Ning Bing is eliminating witnesses!"

# Chapter 135: Northern Dark Ice Soul, Ten Extreme Talents

"What?!" Xiong Lin was stunned.

"Plunder Gu? That belongs to my cousin Xiong Zhan!" Xiong Jiang's face darkened.

"Bai Ning Bing, do you have the Plunder Gu?" Xiong Jiang stepped forward to confront him as Fang Yuan slipped past.

Bai Ning Bing laughed coldly, "So what if it is?"

With a grim face, Xiong Jiang demanded, "How did my cousin die? Why is his Gu worm in your hands?"

Xiong Lin chimed in, "Bai Ning Bing, I respect you as a senior, but seizing our clan's Gu without returning it openly violates the alliance treaty!"

He raised a Signal Gu, firing bright flares that burst loudly across the sky.

Bai Ning Bing laughed. "I saw someone using the Plunder Gu, found it interesting, and took it."

Unable to contain his rage, Xiong Jiang roared and charged at Bai Ning Bing. "You watched my cousin die without lifting a finger, you bastard!"

Battle was unavoidable; the scene immediately turned chaotic.

Bai Ning Bing clashed with Xiong Li's group while both sides remained under assault from the surrounding wolf pack.

Fang Yuan slipped away to watch calmly from a distance.

With Xiong Jiang's eerie green eyes from the Roaming Zombie Gu, Xiong Li's blood-red glare, and Bai Ning Bing's crystal azure pupils, their three-way clash was the fiercest on the battlefield.

In zombie form, Xiong Jiang resisted Bai Ning Bing's frost, while Xiong Li's two-bear strength smashed against Bai Ning Bing's water shield.

Bai Ning Bing had suppressed his cultivation to Rank two throughout the battle, mostly using Rank two Gu.

"Hahaha, how can nobodies like you qualify to make me go all out?" Bai Ning Bing laughed coldly. His attacks grew fiercer, yet he still held back, avoiding Rank three Gu.

Spectating from afar with arms crossed, Fang Yuan understood the reason clearly.

"It's not that he won't unleash his true strength, but that he can't. Bai Ning Bing possesses the Northern Dark Ice Soul physique..."

"Ren Zu bore ten children—Verdant Great Sun, Desolate Ancient Moon, Northern Dark Ice Soul... alluding to profound cultivation secrets. Ren Zu's ten children each represented an ultimate aptitude," Fang Yuan recalled.

"Every Ten Extreme Physique surpasses A-grade aptitude. While the finest A-grade aperture holds only ninety-nine percent primeval essence, the Ten Extreme Physiques hold a full hundred percent!"

"Yet a hundred-percent aperture is too perfect for heaven to tolerate; none of Ren Zu's ten children lived long. In reality, Gu Masters with Ten Extreme Physiques rarely mature, almost all dying prematurely. Yet if they reach Rank six, they can sweep through same-stage peers and even fight above their rank!"

"Bai Ning Bing is no different. With hundred-percent primeval essence, his aperture is under extreme strain, risking collapse at any moment. To reduce this risk, he must constantly cultivate and use primeval essence to reinforce his aperture walls, resulting in his astonishing cultivation speed."

"Yet higher cultivation yields denser primeval essence, increasing aperture pressure and heightening the risk. Bai Ning Bing is like a castaway drinking seawater to quench his thirst—the salty water only absorbs his body's moisture, leaving him even thirstier."

"The higher his cultivation, the closer to destruction. Yet facing assassination threats and clan expectations, he cannot stop cultivating. Forced to grow stronger despite knowing his certain, imminent death, the Northern Dark Ice Soul possessor developed his wild temperament."

In Fang Yuan's past life, three years post-wolf tide, Bai Ning Bing reached Rank four; unable to support the primeval essence's burden, his aperture exploded.

The detonation of the Ten Extreme Physique annihilated all three villages, transforming Qing Mao Mountain into a frozen domain of death.

To stall his demise, Bai Ning Bing deliberately used Gu to dilute his Rank three silver primeval essence into Rank two red steel essence, rarely activating Rank three Gu.

Bai Ning Bing was truly a pitiful youth, and Fang Yuan had had no desire to provoke him. But since Bai Ning Bing sought his life, Fang Yuan would gladly exploit him before eliminating the threat early.

The battle was still continuing.

Bai Ning Bing had slain the bold lightning wolf, sending the pack fleeing in defeat. Xiong Li's healing Gu Master had fallen to Bai Ning Bing's blade, but at a heavy cost: a vicious punch from Xiong Li had fractured Bai Ning Bing's right arm, leaving it dangling limply.

Xiong Li, Xiong Jiang, and the prodigy Xiong Lin were among the strongest Rank two Gu Masters of their clan. Even combining their strength, the four Gu Masters were still suppressed by Bai Ning Bing.

"It is the Northern Dark Ice Soul physique after all. Though he used a Gu worm to dilute his primeval essence and seal its true advantage, his recovery speed remains. His advantage will only grow the longer this lasts," Fang Yuan sighed inwardly.

"My current strength cannot defeat him," Fang Yuan calmly admitted.

With only C-grade aptitude, Fang Yuan knew that facing Bai Ning Bing alone would be far worse than this three-man struggle.

"Yet failing to defeat you doesn't mean I can't kill you," Fang Yuan smiled coldly.

Though similar in nature, compared to this centuries-old devil, Bai Ning Bing was merely a junior shaped by cruel fate.

# Chapter 136: Clash of brawn and brain

Xiong Li and the other's fight against Bai Ning Bing had reached its climax.

Even Xiong Li had to suppress his anger and acknowledge Bai Ning Bing's strength. Despite suppressing his Rank three cultivation to use only Rank two Gu, Bai Ning Bing still overpowered their entire group.

Yet Bai Ning Bing was still not giving his all—not to prove a point, but as a precaution against Fang Yuan.

"Did he leave? Impossible! Though it's our first meeting, he's not the type to retreat. He will definitely act—he's just biding his time." Bai Ning Bing brandished his ice blade against Xiong Li, thoughts racing.

"Bai Ning Bing is truly meticulous. First he decisively killed the bold lightning wolf to eliminate my pawn, then risked injury to kill Xiong Li's healing Gu Master. Every move was the most sensible choice. Even while fighting Xiong Li's group, he is holding back to guard against my ambush. In fact..."

In a tree's shadow, Fang Yuan watched with crossed arms, eyes glinting uncertainly.

The longer this dragged on, the more Bai Ning Bing's strength drained. Though the Ten Extreme physiques had recovery rates far exceeding A grade, primeval essence was still expended over time.

Without primeval essence, even the Northern Dark Ice Soul Physique could be beheaded by a single moonblade!

"I cannot go on like this!" With flashing blue eyes, Bai Ning Bing leaped back to create distance from Xiong Li's group.

A small ice-blue bird emerged from his mouth, raised its head past his teeth, and locked onto its target.

Leaping from Bai Ning Bing's mouth, the icy bird flapped vigorously toward Xiong Li.

"Rank three Blue Bird Ice Coffin Gu?"

"Boom!"

It slammed into Xiong Li's support Gu Master and promptly exploded.

As the blue light cleared, a translucent block of icy, watery-blue ice appeared.

Sealed inside the ice, the Gu Master's face still held his dying terror, but all signs of life were gone.

Though Bai Ning Bing was a Rank three Gu Master, activating a Rank three Gu rapidly depleted the suppressed Rank two primeval essence in his aperture, nor could it unleash the Gu's true power.

Bai Ning Bing did not follow up, turning around to retreat from the battlefield.

The four had barely maintained an encirclement; losing one man allowed Bai Ning Bing to easily break their blockade.

Just as Bai Ning Bing moved to escape, a moonblade flew from the bushes, and a figure leapt out right behind it.

"Bam!"

Bai Ning Bing deflected the moonblade with his ice blade, but a blow to the abdomen knocked him back three steps.

Bai Ning Bing ignored the three, staring at the newcomer instead, chuckling.

"So you finally appeared ..."

Fang Yuan gave a faint, triumphant smile, though his heart sank. Bai Ning Bing's retreat had been a ploy to force him out. Even seeing through it, Fang Yuan had no choice but to show himself.

"Rest assured, everyone," Fang Yuan called out. "I've informed the clan. Hold on a bit longer, reinforcements are coming!"

Xiong Li was relieved at once.

Xiong Lin and Xiong Jiang's spirits soared, while Bai Ning Bing laughed wildly. "Haha, so you're Fang Yuan! What an interesting fellow... now I want to kill you even more!"

"I'm afraid you lack the ability," Fang Yuan said with cold eyes, stepping forward.

"Fang Yuan, stay back to support us and keep him from escaping!" Xiong Li said.

Fang Yuan maintained a calm expression and attacked relentlessly, completely ignoring Xiong Li's words.

Xiong Li's group frowned, fearing an outsider would disrupt their coordination. Yet Fang Yuan's attacks were ruthless, decisive, and flawlessly timed, proving to be of immense help.

With Fang Yuan joining the fray alongside Xiong Li's remaining three, they drove Bai Ning Bing into a corner.

"Bai Ning Bing, just go and die!"

Xiong Li roared, leaping up and bringing down a clasped two-handed punch toward Bai Ning Bing.

Bai Ning Bing moved to dodge, but Fang Yuan intercepted him with a moonblade, cutting off his escape route.

"Water Shield Gu!"

With a loud pop, Xiong Li's heavy fist slammed into the barrier, instantly shattering it.

However, the impact robbed the blow of its momentum, allowing Bai Ning Bing to escape death.

The Water Shield Gu had saved Bai Ning Bing again; its defensive strength exceeded even the White Jade Gu.

"If I can counter the Water Shield Gu, Bai Ning Bing is finished. But how?" While Fang Yuan pondered, Bai Ning Bing opened his mouth, releasing another blue bird.

He snorted coldly, shooting a moonblade.

With a crash, the moonblade struck the ice bird, detonating it.

Bai Ning Bing grunted in pain, caught at close range in the blast.

The impact threw him to the ground, freezing half of his left arm and rendering it useless.

"A divine opportunity!" Xiong Li's eyes lit up. Shouting "Die!", he brought both fists down toward Bai Ning Bing once more.

The blow was heavy, and pinned to the ground, Bai Ning Bing's Water Shield Gu had less than half its usual defense; a direct strike to the head would kill him instantly.

# Chapter 137: Shadow of death

Seeing Xiong Li's fist about to land on Bai Ning Bing, Xiong Lin and Xiong Jiang looked overjoyed.

Only Fang Yuan stood far away, his eyes shining with solemnness.

Knowing Bai Ning Bing, how could the genius make such a rookie mistake?

Bai Ning Bing suddenly struck, his right arm turning into translucent frosty ice, exposing the finger bones beneath.

Straightening his fingers into a palm blade, he struck like lightning, piercing Xiong Li's heart!

"Urgh!" Xiong Li's descending body came to a halt.

"You think my arm was broken by your punch? Naive! I forged ice muscles with the Rank three Ice Muscle Gu—the ultimate defense! I only feigned weakness to surprise a certain someone." Bai Ning Bing rose slowly with disdain in his eyes, glancing toward Fang Yuan as he finished.

"Bai... Ning Bing..." Xiong Li gasped his final words. Frost radiating from Bai Ning Bing's ice hand froze his heart, snuffing out his life force.

"Scheming scumbag, I'll fight you to the death!" Overcome with grief and rage at Xiong Li's death, Xiong Lin recklessly charged Bai Ning Bing.

"The leader is dead—we are no match for him! Run quickly, I'll block him!" Xiong Jiang said, suppressing his grief.

Shadow Bond Gu!

The shadow beneath his feet came alive, distorting eerily and linking to Bai Ning Bing's shadow.

"With the Shadow Bond Gu, any injury I suffer is shared with Bai Ning Bing! He won't dare kill me easily. Xiong Lin, you have greater talent than us—run!" Xiong Jiang shouted, eyes locked on Bai Ning Bing.

Xiong Lin remained rooted to the spot. Turning to Fang Yuan, he demanded, "Fang Yuan, why aren't the reinforcements here yet?!"

Bai Ning Bing flexed his right arm as it reverted to flesh, sneering, "Reinforcements? Hehehe, they would have arrived long ago. It's all a lie. Tsk tsk, you're even more naive than the last one. You truly think a cheap trick like the Shadow Bond Gu can restrain me?"

"Fang Yuan, is this true?!" Xiong Lin clenched his fists, eyes blazing with fury.

Ignoring him, Fang Yuan stared at Bai Ning Bing with a smirk. "That move cost you heavily, didn't it? How much primeval essence do you have left in your aperture?"

Judging from earlier, Fang Yuan deduced that Bai Ning Bing had used the Rank three Frost Demon Gu.

Even so, forcefully activating a Rank three Gu with Rank two primeval essence incurred massive consumption, but it was necessary to break the stalemate.

Bai Ning Bing's expression shifted; the move had consumed massive primeval essence, and Fang Yuan had seen through it.

He laughed heartily and admitted, "Correct. My primeval essence is nearly spent, and I feigned helplessness with my right arm to bait you. But you didn't bite. Hehehe, the real fight is just beginning!"

Had Bai Ning Bing denied it, Fang Yuan would have struck immediately. Instead, he openly confessed with complete fearlessness...

Either Bai Ning Bing knew his death was inevitable and welcomed it, or he possessed formidable trump cards to ensure survival.

Fang Yuan himself possessed seven Gu: Moonglow Gu, White Jade Gu, Stealth Scales Gu, Four Flavours Liquor worm, Spring Autumn Cicada, Earth Communication Ear Grass, and Nine Leaf Vitality Grass.

As the Bai clan's favored genius with the Northern Dark Ice Soul physique, Bai Ning Bing enjoyed the full backing of his clan; his assets easily surpassed Fang Yuan's.

He had revealed over six Gu worms so far, and Fang Yuan was certain he possessed more.

Such Gu allowed him to remain calm even with nearly depleted primeval essence.

The Rank three Frost Demon Gu could pierce even the White Jade Gu's defense, which was why Fang Yuan had refrained from fighting him head-on.

Fang Yuan had used Gu Yue Man Shi and Xiong Li's group to bait out Bai Ning Bing's secrets; victory required knowing one's enemy.

Bai Ning Bing was strong while Fang Yuan was weak; this was an undeniable truth he fully acknowledged.

Yet in battle, the strong did not always triumph, nor did the weak always perish.

For Fang Yuan to overcome the odds, he had to utilize every scheme and marshal every scrap of strength available.

"Gu Yue Man Shi was just the beginning, and Xiong Li group is not the end." Thinking so, Fang Yuan struck.

With a moonblade, a head flew away, and blood spewed!

Bai Ning Bing's pupils shrank to pinpricks, his expression shifting drastically.

Fang Yuan's moonblade had targeted not Bai Ning Bing, but Xiong Jiang's head.

Bam!

Bai Ning Bing turned pale and spat a mouthful of blood as blood seeped from his eyes, ears, and nose.

The Shadow Bond Gu transmitted ten percent of any damage suffered by its master to the bonded target. With Xiong Jiang's death, that fatal trauma was instantly transferred to Bai Ning Bing.

Shrouded in the glow of the White Jade Gu, Fang Yuan charged at Bai Ning Bing.

Reeling with dizziness and severely weakened by the backlash, Bai Ning Bing immediately retreated as Fang Yuan charged.

"Bai Ning Bing, didn't you want a life-and-death battle?" Fang Yuan pursued relentlessly.

Bai Ning Bing gritted his teeth and fled in silence, forcing himself to focus and ignore Fang Yuan's taunts.

Over time, Bai Ning Bing's bleeding stopped and his wounds began to mend, his footing growing steadier.

Rather than merely fleeing, he was actively healing his injuries with a Gu worm.

"His primeval essence was nearly dry, yet he has already recovered enough to heal his wounds. The Ten Extreme Northern Dark Ice Soul physique—what terrifying recovery speed!"

The more he knew, the more determined he was to kill Bai Ning Bing.

Moonglow Gu!

Fang Yuan unleashed a face-sized moonblade, buzzing as it arced through the air.

Hearing the sound, Bai Ning Bing tried to dodge, but the moonblade still sliced his arm.

Blood welled from the cut, but a layer of frost quickly formed over it, stopping the bleeding.

Yet Bai Ning Bing's heart sank. New wounds were inflicted before the old could heal—Fang Yuan clearly meant to wear him down to death!

# Chapter 138: Someone is actually chasing and trying to kill Bai Ning Bing?

"Hehehe, no one has ever pushed me this far! Fang Yuan, you're interesting... Once I recover, I will definitely kill you!" Bai Ning Bing roared inwardly as he ran.

The heavy aura of death made him shudder with twisted excitement.

Behind him, Fang Yuan was chasing and getting closer.

"Take this!" He suddenly flicked his wrist, and threw a black coloured Gu worm.

Fang Yuan paused warily, but upon closer inspection, recognized it as Xiong Zhan's Plunder Gu.

With a light sound, Fang Yuan caught the Gu in his hand.

Unleashing the Spring Autumn Cicada's aura, Fang Yuan instantly refined the Plunder Gu and stored it in his aperture.

He continued chasing.

"Unfortunately, I lack a movement Gu. Otherwise, I would have caught Bai Ning Bing already," Fang Yuan lamented inwardly.

"Fang Yuan refined the Plunder Gu instantly?!" Bai Ning Bing was inwardly shocked.

He grew increasingly wary of Fang Yuan, who now appeared far more dangerous and mysterious.

However, as time passed, Fang Yuan's heart was the one that sank.

The longer the pursuit lasted, the more primeval essence Bai Ning Bing regained and the further his wounds healed, gradually eroding Fang Yuan's advantage.

"I won't be able to kill him this time," Fang Yuan sighed inwardly.

Thoughts swirling through his mind, Fang Yuan began considering a retreat.

"Against the wolf tide, we must stay united and avoid isolation. Once isolated, you're in danger," Qing Shu told Fang Zheng as they rushed along the road.

"Against a bold lightning wolf pack, we can use terrain to defeat them head-on. But against a frenzy lightning wolf pack, we must retreat, use the Signal Gu, and wait for surrounding Gu Masters to regroup. We need at least three groups cooperating to have a good chance of defeating a frenzy lightning wolf. Of course, opponents in the wolf tide include Gu Masters as well."

Lately, Bai Ning Bing had challenged people everywhere, suppressing his cultivation to Rank two red steel primeval essence and picking fights with Xiong Li, Chi Shan, and the rest.

He had declared Gu Yue Qing Shu his next target.

"If we encounter Bai Ning Bing..." Qing Shu continued, "the best course is to avoid battle."

"Everyone is discussing Bai Ning Bing, what kind of person is he?" Fang Zheng frowned and asked.

Gu Yue Qing Shu smiled kindly. "He is Qing Mao Mountain's number one genius! Listen well, Fang Zheng: you are young with A grade talent, and may surpass him in the future. So until you mature, avoid direct conflict with him. Remember the story I told you? Sometimes, people have to lower their heads."

Fang Zheng met Qing Shu's gaze and nodded. "I understand, Brother Qing Shu. Please rest assured."

"Good..." Qing Shu began, but the recon Gu Master interrupted, "A Gu Master is moving rapidly just ahead."

The healing Gu Master Gu Yue Yao Hong looked solemn. "A small group scattered by wolves—let's go save them."

"Whether it's him or not, any lost Gu Master is an ally we must save," Gu Yue Qing Shu said, turning to charge toward them.

Soon, the recon Gu Master corrected, "It's not one Gu Master—there's another right behind him."

From above, one could see Qing Shu's group, Fang Yuan, and Bai Ning Bing closing in along the mountain road.

"Oh?" Bai Ning Bing's expression shifted as his recon Gu detected five Gu Masters approaching.

He quickly changed his direction.

Yet Qing Shu's group was packed with elites, and their recon Gu Master was highly skilled.

"Someone's there!" Soon after, Fang Yuan detected Qing Shu's group.

But Qing Shu group followed closely, changing their direction to match.

At this, both Bai Ning Bing and Fang Yuan grew solemn.

Both were sharp and realized immediately: this unknown group was a major uncertainty that could decide the battle.

"If it's Bai clan, I'll use the Stealth Scales Gu and retreat immediately. But if it's the other two clans, hehe." Cold light gleamed in Fang Yuan's eyes.

Yet if he lost this opportunity, he might never have another shot at killing Bai Ning Bing.

Besides, Fang Yuan held a sixty-six percent chance in his favor while Bai Ning Bing had only a third—reason enough to gamble.

"They're right around that bend," the recon Gu Master said, pointing at a curve in the path.

A white figure rounded the corner, appearing before them.

"Bai Ning Bing!" Fang Zheng cried in dismay, recognizing him from portraits.

The other four, even Qing Shu's expression sank.

"Bai Ning Bing is in such a sorry state, he must've encountered a frenzy lightning wolf pack."

Just then, Fang Yuan shouted from around the corner, "Bai Ning Bing, why are you running? I'll kill you today!"

If they were Bai clan, his proclamation would provoke an immediate rebuttal.

Hearing this around the corner, Qing Shu and the others stared in wide-eyed shock.

"There's actually someone chasing and trying to kill Bai Ning Bing?"

Shocked and suspicious, Fang Zheng thought, "Why is that voice so familiar?"

Before he could place the voice, Fang Yuan rounded the corner.

"What?" Gu Yue Qing Shu's serious expression turned into dumbfoundedness.

"Brother!" Fang Zheng was tongue tied.

# Chapter 139: Broken Arm

"I won the gamble. Bai Ning Bing, you die today," Fang Yuan thought, before shouting, "Little brother, you're here! Great! Stop Bai Ning Bing—he slaughtered Xiong Li's group and our clan's Gu Yue Man Shi group!"

"Will I really die here? No, I have just enough primeval essence to detonate the Frost Demon Gu—there's still hope!" Bai Ning Bing thought. Trapped between Qing Shu's group and Fang Yuan, his situation was dire.

Raising his right arm, he channeled all his recovered primeval essence into the Frost Demon Gu in his right palm.

Bang!

With a blast, Bai Ning Bing's right arm suddenly self-detonated.

"Retreat, quick!" Gu Yue Qing Shu grabbed Fang Zheng and hurried back.

Bai Ning Bing was encased in ice like an insect in amber, his expression frozen in ruthless, malevolent resolve.

"No!" Qing Shu's face darkened. "Bai Ning Bing has formed ice muscles. The ice won't kill him—it's armor giving him time to recover."

"Fang Yuan, is what you said true?" Qing Shu turned his gaze toward him.

"Of course. Only Xiong Lin survived from Xiong Li's group, and he can testify. We can't linger—the details can wait. We must leave immediately," Fang Yuan answered, nodding.

"Leave? Why should we leave?" Fang Zheng asked loudly. "Bai Ning Bing lost his right arm and is utterly exhausted physically and mentally. We can shatter this ice and use the Signal Gu for reinforcements to eliminate him! This is a rare opportunity."

"Truly stupid—how could you fathom the Northern Dark Ice Soul Physique's power?" Fang Yuan sneered inwardly, refusing to seek death with them. Gu Yue Qing Shu also hesitated.

"Bai village's rise is solely due to Bai Ning Bing. If we kill him, our Gu Yue clan will remain number one on Qing Mao Mountain! Though he is a Rank three Gu Master, I have the Wood Charm Gu to fight him. Moreover, having just lost his right arm, he'll need time to adjust to the missing limb. In a life-and-death battle, that could be his greatest disadvantage!" Qing Shu thought.

"If Gu Yue Qing Shu uses the Wood Charm Gu recklessly, he'll be far stronger than ordinary Rank three Gu Masters and able to fight above his rank. Inside the ice, he has ample time to remove the restriction. Once he breaks out, he will likely fight as a Rank three Gu Master."

Ignoring the others' urging to stay, Fang Yuan promptly left the battlefield.

"Fang Zheng, you leave too," Gu Yue Qing Shu suddenly spoke.

"As the clan's only A grade talent, you cannot be lost. Bai Ning Bing may have lost an arm, but this battle will be perilous. We can die for the clan, but you must live."

"Well said!" Hearty laughter echoed as an old Gu Master appeared. "Lord Elder," Fang Zheng greeted him respectfully, recognizing a senior clan elder.

"Fang Zheng, you don't need to fight, but don't leave—observe from a distance. Isn't it just Bai Ning Bing? I've heard how outstanding he is, supposedly possessing an elder's strength. Hmph, he's just a kid with little battle experience. Mutilating himself is utterly amateurish!" the elder snorted in disdain.

# Chapter 140: Gu Yue Qing Shu vs Bai Ning Bing (Part 1)

The clan elder, Gu Yue Qing Shu, Gu Yue Yao Hong, and the others—five in all—surrounded him. Farther up the slope, Fang Zheng watched the battle vigilantly from higher ground.

"Bai Ning Bing, you dared kill our clan's Gu Masters and openly break the alliance! A life for a life—what do you have to say?" the elder snorted haughtily.

Bai Ning Bing ignored him, quietly staring at his severed right arm.

His body radiated the oppressive aura of a Rank three Gu Master, having unsealed his cultivation inside the ice.

"Forcing me to use white silver primeval essence and sacrifice the Frost Demon Gu! Where is Fang Yuan?" Bai Ning Bing's killing intent flared. His blue eyes scanned the surroundings, finding no trace of him.

"Arrogant junior, take my moonblade!" the elder shouted, leaping forward.

"Hmph!" Bai Ning Bing swept his left arm, condensing the gushing frost into a long ice blade.

Golden moonlight reflected on Bai Ning Bing's pale face. Laughing, he raised his left-handed ice blade to attack head-on.

As moonblade and ice blade collided, the golden moon vanished while the ice blade shattered into fragments.

"Hmph, you know nothing, old man." Holding his ice blade horizontally, Bai Ning Bing began to spin.

"Dodge, quick!" Even the clan elder dared not face it, quickly leaping aside.

Caught in the storm, the two male Gu Masters were minced to paste within seconds, their cries cut short.

"Save me!" Gu Yue Yao Hong shrieked in terror as the tornado threatened to engulf her.

Gu Yue Qing Shu shot out green vines that coiled around Yao Hong's waist like snakes.

Just as he moved to drag her back, the ice blade storm engulfed and devoured her instantly.

"Yao Hong!" Seeing this scene, Qing Shu grieved with sorrowful eyes.

"Damn it, Steel Shirt Gu!" The elder inhaled deeply as black light cloaked him like steel armor.

A moment later, the storm ended.

Though snapped in two, the ice blade in Bai Ning Bing's left hand still pierced straight through the elder's heart.

Pale blue frost spread across the elder's face before he collapsed dead onto the ground.

"Where is Fang Yuan? Tell me, and I'll let you die peacefully," Bai Ning Bing said, walking toward Qing Shu.

"Bai Ning Bing..." Qing Shu sighed deeply, gazing fearlessly. "We've fought over ten times; you've grown stronger and surpassed me, but that will never make me betray my clansman. Come, fight me!"

Wood Charm Gu!

# Chapter 141: Gu Yue Qing Shu vs Bai Ning Bing (Part 2)

Icicle Gu.

Two vines sprouted from Qing Shu's palms, weaving like agile snakes to easily deflect the icicles.

With so many icicles, one pierced clean through his right shoulder.

Pin Needle Gu.

Enduring the pain, Qing Shu swept his hair, unleashing countless jade pin needles from the tips.

The needles engulfed Bai Ning Bing, but his Water Shield Gu activated in time to block them.

Yet this also forced Bai Ning Bing to halt his icicles.

Seeing the opening, Qing Shu ignored the pain and ripped the icicle from his shoulder.

Through his torn shirt, Qing Shu's wound revealed tree rings as his flesh healed visibly.

As the Wood Charm Gu transformed him into a tree spirit, Gu Yue Qing Shu felt the rich natural essence in the air.

Staring at Bai Ning Bing, Qing Shu said, "Let's fight."

Blue bird ice coffin Gu!

Seizing an opportunity, Bai Ning Bing opened his mouth, and an ice-blue bird flew out.

The bird swelled to the size of an eagle and dove toward Gu Yue Qing Shu.

With an explosion, Gu Yue Qing Shu was sealed inside a room-sized ice crystal.

The ice crystal shattered, and Gu Yue Qing Shu stood up, completely transformed; his handsome face had become the sharp-nosed, huge-eyed countenance of a tree spirit.

Standing three meters tall and powerfully built, he wore tree-bark armor covered in thick leaves and vines.

"That look... you refined the Wood Charm Gu?" Bai Ning Bing realized.

Inwardly, he thought, "Qing Shu, using the Wood Charm Gu like this, aren't you afraid of turning into a dead treeman? Even if you beat me, you're doomed to die!"

"Bai Ning Bing..." Qing Shu's voice turned low. "I understand you like myself. We are both orphans adopted by clan leaders, yet walk entirely different paths. A dying tree in a forest is nothing; as long as the forest remains, new trees will always rise. Let us have our final battle, my nemesis!"

Hundreds of green vines surged toward Bai Ning Bing like giant pythons.

Unable to dodge in time, Bai Ning Bing raised his shield.

Knowing the Gu's weakness, Qing Shu didn't attack forcefully, instead wrapping the vines around the shield.

Under the vines' constriction, the water shield shrank toward collapse.

In the next moment, the water shield exploded.

An ice blade storm erupted from within, rapidly expanding.

The ice blade storm surged toward Qing Shu, shredding the obstructing vines.

"Good timing!" Fearless, Gu Yue Qing Shu thrust his massive body straight into the ice blade storm.

Pain tore into him and Qing Shu growled, but he refused to let go, gripping the tornado with all his strength.

The ice blade storm was actually shrinking under his strength.

"Damn it! This suicidal lunatic..." Bai Ning Bing cursed, spinning faster to compete directly with him.

# Chapter 142: Gu Yue Qing Shu vs Bai Ning Bing (Part 3)

The storm finally ceased, but Gu Yue Qing Shu had paid a heavy price.

As his palms came together, fresh branches sprouted rapidly from his hands, interweaving tightly.

His palms formed a wooden cage, trapping Bai Ning Bing inside.

"Damn it!" Bai Ning Bing gritted his teeth, his primeval essence dry, leaving him at Qing Shu's mercy.

Yet the palms slowed, freezing motionless in mid-air.

Qing Shu felt deep helplessness as his hands lost sensation, turning completely into wood.

Refusing to yield, he focused and activated the Green Vine Gu.

Thick vines surged forward, striking Bai Ning Bing through the cage's gaps.

The cramped cage restricted Bai Ning Bing's movements, and a vine entangled his leg, tripping him.

The sharp blade severed the vine on his leg. Bai Ning Bing rolled away awkwardly, narrowly evading dozens of vines.

Worse, the Wood Charm Gu's power had completely corroded Gu Yue Qing Shu's body and began seizing his consciousness.

He was blind as the Wood Charm Gu claimed his eyes, and deaf with his ears reduced to mere ornaments.

His efforts succeeded—a vine wrapped around Bai Ning Bing's neck, lifting him into the air and tightening.

After pulling away, he concealed himself with the Stealth Scales Gu, circling the battlefield toward where Man Shi and Xiong Li had died.

He looted the Gu worms from Man Shi and his teammates' corpses, but Xiong Li's group and their Gu were already gone.

"Xiong Lin must have collected their corpses. What a waste—I wanted Xiong Li's Brown Bear Innate Strength Gu," Fang Yuan sighed.

"The noise has stopped; the victor must be decided." Listening through the cliff wall, Fang Yuan heard reinforcements from both clans approaching.

Having the shorter distance, Fang Yuan entered the mountain path first.

Through the cage's opening, he saw Bai Ning Bing hanging from the vines, barely clinging to his last breath.

"The Ice Muscles are keeping him alive, but too bad for you, you met me." Fang Yuan dashed forward.

Suddenly, a shower of pin needles rained around Fang Yuan.

Gazing at the giant tree, he realized, "Gu Yue Qing Shu's mind is too blurred to tell friend from foe. Driven purely by willpower, he's only trying to kill Bai Ning Bing. He treats anyone intruding on the battlefield as an interferer."

Just then, Bai clan Gu Masters appeared on the mountain path.

"Lad over there, don't move!" a Rank three Gu Master shouted threateningly at Fang Yuan.

"Bai Ning Bing really is a cockroach." Fang Yuan laughed coldly, realizing he had lost his chance to deliver the killing blow.

Fang Yuan sighed internally, "He's at least twenty paces away, while my Moonglow Gu only reaches ten. Furthermore.., risking my life and disrupting my rebirth plans just to kill Bai Ning Bing, who is doomed anyway, is not worth it."

Thinking so, Fang Yuan took a few steps back.

# Chapter 143: Answer

Soon after, Fang Zheng rushed over as well.

"Brother, what's wrong with Lord Qing Shu?" Fang Zheng turned to Fang Yuan in shocked confusion.

Ignoring him, Fang Yuan squatted and reached a claw-like right hand toward Bai Ning Bing.

Plunder Gu!

He pulled back sharply, drawing a Gu worm out of Bai Ning Bing's body.

It was a red steel Relic Gu!

Fang Yuan instantly refined the red steel Relic Gu with the Spring Autumn Cicada, hiding it in his robes.

He clawed again, and a grey beetle flew out.

"Stone Aperture Gu..." Recognizing it instantly, he refined it and tucked it away as well.

The Bai clan Gu Masters growled, running furiously from the mountain path.

"Qing Shu won't last long—only time for one more attempt." Fang Yuan activated the Plunder Gu again.

Eventually, Fang Yuan caught Bai Ning Bing's Water Shield Gu.

Paired with the White Jade Gu, the Water Shield Gu would bolster his defense significantly.

The Bai clan Gu Masters finally felled the treant Gu Yue Qing Shu had become.

Just as they prepared to slaughter Fang Yuan and Fang Zheng, Gu Yue clan reinforcements arrived.

After a brief standoff, both sides retreated by mutual understanding.

Gu Yue clan Gu Masters recovered Gu Yue Qing Shu's corpse and Gu worms.

Before a newly erected gravestone carved with 'Gu Yue Qing Shu', the crowd bowed in grief.

"Why did this happen?"

"Why do good people in this world die so easily, while the bad live long?"

The crowd gradually dispersed until only Fang Zheng remained.

"Lord Qing Shu!" He fell to his knees, tears streaming onto the ground.

Rain began falling as Bai Ning Bing lay on a soft bed, staring blankly outside.

His severed right arm had been treated and bandaged in white. His eyes had returned to black, and he no longer suppressed his Rank three cultivation to Rank two.

"What is the meaning of living?" This question plagued him alongside the threat of his impending self-detonation.

He recalled Fang Yuan calmly eating wild fruit by a tree while watching the battle below.

Looking back, Fang Yuan's gaze held something he lacked.

"What is the meaning behind living?" In the dark room, Gu Yue Fang Zheng asked the same question.

Gu Yue clan leader Gu Yue Bo sighed, looking pitifully at the despondent youth before gazing out the window at the rain.

After a long silence, Gu Yue Bo spoke, "Over ten years ago, another youth in your situation asked me the same question."

Stunned, Fang Zheng raised his swollen, red eyes, brimming with an intense thirst for answers.

# Chapter 144: Sacrifice is common, and faith is undying

"I told him that a person has countless reasons to live. Why you live is something only you can answer. Go find it yourself," Gu Yue Bo said.

"Then Clan Leader, what is your own answer?" Fang Zheng asked in confusion.

The clan leader pondered the past before repeating his old answer: "Sacrifice is inevitable in an organization. From the moment we are born, death is imminent. Between life and death, humans are fragile, but one thing warms the heart and illuminates our souls. That is love—this is my answer."

The clan leader chuckled, taking a letter from his desk drawer and handing it to Fang Zheng.

"This letter from Gu Yue Qing Shu records the answers he pondered for years. Take a look—this is his answer."

"The clan is like a forest, and its members are the trees. The old trees spread their branches to shelter the young from wind and rain. When saplings grow tall, old trees become nutrients in the soil to nourish new growth. Yet new trees prove the old trees once existed. Through this unbroken legacy, the clan forest expands toward prosperity and flourishment."

"Death is inevitable; even Rank seven and eight Gu Masters merely live longer. I fear death too, but I deeply understand that one day, I, Gu Yue Qing Shu, will also die. Whether by old age, illness, or the battlefield, I can leave peacefully with no regrets."

Gu Yue Bo took back the letter and said, "When you find your answer, write to me as well. Now go rest. The wolf tide isn't over, and we need your strength."

"I've found my answer," Fang Zheng declared with fierce resolve. "I want strength! To protect my family from harm, to protect the clan, and to strengthen it! I will stop the wolf tide from tormenting us and restore joy to my companions! I won't let this pain repeat. With my hands, body, and soul, I will protect everyone around me!"

Unable to endure loneliness, Ren Zu dug out both eyes, turning them into a son and daughter to ease his solitude.

Attitude Gu told him, "That is the eternal light given out by Faith Gu."

"Faith?" Bai Ning Bing scoffed, tossing aside the book of ancient legends.

The clan leader looked grim. "The situation is dire. Scouts report three frenzy lightning wolf packs near the village, and your defeat has dealt a heavy blow to clan morale. I need you to show yourself tonight. Just seeing you standing will greatly motivate our clansmen. Do you understand?"

Left alone in the room, Bai Ning Bing opened his eyes, filled with loneliness and confusion.

He had told no one about his bodily condition or his premonition of death.

The Northern Dark Ice Soul physique was an absolute death talent—its aperture walls reaching their limit would trigger self-destruction.

Though raised leniently, Bai Ning Bing knew the clan leader would execute him immediately if the extreme physique were exposed.

"What's the point of a person being alive?"

Though still lost on the meaning of living, he knew where the answer lay.

Moreover, the Stone Aperture Gu was in Fang Yuan's hands.

"Fang Yuan... we will meet again," he muttered softly, his eyes glittering like diamonds.

"Stone Aperture Gu..." Inside his rented house, Fang Yuan held the Gu worm, lost in thought.

This single-use expendable Gu permanently turned a Gu Master's aperture wall into a sturdy rock wall.

The cost was losing all natural essence regeneration and any hope of reaching Rank three.

"Refining this Stone Aperture Gu comes at great cost. Bai Ning Bing likely intended to solidify his aperture to ward off death, but this can only delay his demise, not prevent his destruction. If the Northern Dark Ice Soul physique were easily broken, how could it be called a ten extreme physique?"

While the Stone Aperture Gu was useless to Fang Yuan, the red steel Relic Gu and Water Shield Gu taken from Bai Ning Bing were very useful.

He exchanged the ordinary Gu from Man Shi's group for substantial clan merits.

With the wolf tide raging, all three clans suppressed tensions over Qing Shu and Bai Ning Bing's clash, needing each other's strength to survive the ordeal first.

When questioned about his White Jade Gu, Fang Yuan brushed it off as a caravan purchase.

# Chapter 145: Sacrifices are encouraged

As a person involved, Fang Yuan underwent clan investigation.

The investigation was superficial as the worsening wolf tide consumed the elders' attention, leaving his hidden Gu undiscovered.

By the time any full investigation concluded, Fang Yuan would already be Rank three and gone from Qing Mao Mountain.

The wolf tide worsened as battles grew increasingly fierce.

Bold lightning wolf packs now played supporting roles as thousand beast king frenzy lightning wolf packs roamed near the village.

Mortals and Gu Masters alike struggled between life and death. Even high-ranking clan elders were forced to don armor and enter the battlefield.

Fang Yuan sat cross-legged on the bed, eyes closed as he inspected his aperture.

Within his aperture surged a 44% red steel sea of dark red primeval essence, characteristic of Rank two peak stage.

Having advanced to upper stage days ago, his crimson essence was refined by the Four Flavors Liquor worm and replenished with primeval stones, fully transforming into dark red essence.

The aperture wall was no longer a flowing light membrane, but a thick stone membrane formed by stacked layers of white light.

The White Jade Gu and Stealth Scales Gu lay deep within the red steel sea.

Its two wings had fully recovered like tender leaves, though its main body remained withered.

Besides the Spring Autumn Cicada, two new members resided in Fang Yuan's aperture.

Both taken from Bai Ning Bing, one was the jellyfish-like Water Shield Gu floating in the sea, and the other was the red steel Relic Gu.

Fang Yuan had reached upper stage naturally through the Four Flavors Liquor worm's purification and constant nourishment of his aperture walls, without yet using the red steel Relic Gu.

In cultivation alone, Fang Yuan ranked third among the Gu Yue clan's Rank two Gu Masters. With Xiong Li and Qing Shu dead, he was in the top five across all of Qing Mao Mountain.

Chi Shan and Mo Yan, previously at Rank two upper stage, had recently advanced to peak stage, lifting clan morale after Qing Shu's sacrifice.

"Yet in terms of battle strength, I am number one among all Rank two Gu Masters on Qing Mao Mountain. Once I use this red steel Relic Gu to reach peak stage, combined with my vast experience, I can even contend with Rank three Gu Masters," Fang Yuan calculated.

Reaching this stage with mere C grade aptitude would shock the entire clan, especially since A grade Fang Zheng was still only at Rank two middle stage.

The red steel Relic Gu only worked for Rank two Gu Masters, making it pointless to keep.

Just as Fang Yuan was about to use this Gu, a knock sounded at the door.

"Lord Fang Yuan, it's me, Gu Yue Jiang Ya," a voice called after the knock.

Fang Yuan frowned. Jiang Ya had grown increasingly pushy, repeatedly coming to ask for vitality leaves.

Mounting casualties sent vitality leaf prices soaring, resulting in massive demand with zero supply.

"I've told you repeatedly, I have no more vitality leaves. Get lost," Fang Yuan snorted coldly. Why sacrifice cultivation time for petty primeval stones?

Jiang Ya offered a flattering smile outside. "Lord Fang Yuan, please appease your anger. In this situation, what choice do I have? The Gu Masters all know I sell vitality leaves and seek me out. Life isn't easy for me. How about this: I'll pay ten percent more. Lord Fang Yuan, please be kind and give me a dozen vitality leaves."

Fang Yuan remained indifferent. "That's your affair, what does it have to do with me? Hmph, you've grown bold lately, breaking our agreement to bring someone else."

"Ah..." Jiang Ya smiled bitterly, glancing at the old Gu Master beside him. Powerless to resist, he had been stubbornly trailed here by the formidable elder.

"Little friend Fang Yuan," the old Gu Master said. "I am Gu Yue Ye; surely you've heard of me. I've come to buy vitality leaves and hope you'll show me some face by sparing time to produce some."

"Give you face? What reputation do you have?" Fang Yuan sneered. Gu Yue Ye had some fame as a retired Gu Master recalled by the clan for the wolf tide.

At his peak he had reached Rank three, but injuries dropped him to Rank two peak stage, and old age had further reduced him to Rank two upper stage.

Gu Yue Ye looked ashen; despite preparing for Fang Yuan's arrogance, he had still underestimated him.

Any organization needs sacrifices.

Every organization encouraged self-sacrifice, though higher-ups never said so directly—preaching 'protection, honor, family, dreams, and passion' while offering material rewards.

# Chapter 146: Oppressive

The old man had forced Jiang Ya to lead him here. As a Rank one junior, Jiang Ya had no choice but to comply.

Gu Yue Ye longed to slap and lecture this arrogant junior, but for the vitality leaves, he restrained himself. "Junior Fang Yuan, you cannot say that. You are young and do not understand the value of relationships; a time will come when you need help. If you come asking me for a favor in the future and I treat you like this, how will you feel?"

"Bullcrap human relationships!" Fang Yuan sneered inwardly.

Relationships were merely a byproduct of strength; the truly powerful could simply seize whatever they desired.

Since he had been disturbed anyway, Fang Yuan decided to do business.

Thus, he said, "I can sell them. Sixty-eight primeval stones per vitality leaf. How many do you want?"

"Seventy primeval stones now—every complaint wastes my time. Buy or leave," Fang Yuan replied.

Fang Yuan sneered, "It is seventy-two primeval stones now. Every sentence wastes my time, and when annoyed, I raise the price. Choose wisely."

Finally, he gritted his teeth. "Fine, I'll buy five vitality leaves."

"Give the primeval stones to Jiang Ya and collect the vitality leaves from him in three days," Fang Yuan said.

Still, Gu Yue Ye paid, handing multiple money bags to Jiang Ya on the spot.

Outside, Jiang Ya said cautiously, "Lord Fang Yuan, that was satisfying, but you've offended Gu Yue Ye. He was notoriously vengeful in his youth and won't let this go."

"Let him be. An old man relying on seniority will soon be purged by the clan." Fang Yuan opened the door, taking the bags from Jiang Ya.

Resisting the wolf tide demanded immense resources. Facing shortages, the clan had to cull members to sustain its structure.

"In every wolf tide, few mobilized veterans survive. Gu Yue Ye likely won't make it out alive either."

Fang Yuan fixed his dark gaze on Jiang Ya. "From now on, do not disturb me outside scheduled times, or face the consequences. I allow you to sell vitality leaves only for your brother's sake. Respect is earned from others and ruined by oneself. Consider your actions."

"Also, raise the vitality leaf price to seventy primeval stones from now on," Fang Yuan added.

Fearful, Jiang Ya asked meekly, "Lord Fang Yuan, won't profiting like this during a crisis make people think we're exploiting the clan's misfortune and provoke public outrage?"

"Public outrage? What can they do? Follow my orders and cut the nonsense. If anyone causes trouble, tell them the vitality leaves are mine," Fang Yuan snorted.

"Yes, yes, yes." Jiang Ya nodded vigorously; Fang Yuan's backing was exactly what he wanted.

Using the red steel Relic Gu would bring him to Rank two peak stage, near Rank three. Having passed the initial hurdles, his newfound strength would reshape his future.

In peacetime, idle higher-ups might look for trouble. But during a wolf tide, who had the energy to nitpick minor details?

Half a month later.

Lightning wolves surged like tides, forcing the Gu Masters into a hasty retreat.

"Xiong Yuan Zhen, hang in there! Gu Yue village isn't far!" Xiong Jiao Man shouted with reddened eyes at her fallen clansman.

"Big sis, bad news—another bold lightning wolf pack is here!" the recon Gu Master shouted in panic.

Xiong Jiao Man set Xiong Yuan Zhen down; as group leader, her surviving members needed her.

The report distressed her. She could defeat one bold lightning wolf pack or break through two, but against four, the only wise choice was retreating into the valley to fire a Signal Gu for reinforcements.

"Retreat to the valley!" Xiong Jiao Man shouted, using both giant bears to cover them.

By the time they reached the valley, only one bear remained; exhausted after killing dozens of wolves, the black bear had been overwhelmed and devoured.

A Gu worm shot from the bear's corpse, returning instantly to Xiong Jiao Man's hand.

This was the Bear Enslavement Gu.

Planted in bears, it allowed a Gu Master to control them.

"Eh?" A voice suddenly drifted down from the cliff above.

Everyone looked up and saw a young man standing above.

"It's him..."

"Gu Yue Fang Yuan!"

# Chapter 147: This Fang Yuan is too arrogant

Looking down while waving a tree vine, Fang Yuan said slowly, "You can wait for reinforcements, or climb up this vine to escape."

Xiong Jiao Man's group naturally chose the vine, but Fang Yuan demanded payment first.

"Fang Yuan, we are alliance mates. How can you do this!" Someone shouted furiously.

"Asking for the Bear Enslavement Gu, how shameless can you be!" Someone sneered.

Fang Yuan retorted calmly, "One Bear Enslavement Gu and seven hundred primeval stones for seven lives is a bargain. Refuse, and I will simply report your situation to the clan. Whether reinforcements arrive in time without being ambushed by wolves, I cannot guarantee."

"Fine, I agree. Fang Yuan, you truly live up to your reputation," Xiong Jiao Man conceded bitterly for her group's survival.

Fang Yuan shrugged nonchalantly, the result was as expected.

Xiong Jiao Man's group climbed the vines to safety. Soon after, three Gu Master groups arrived, including Chi Shan's unit.

This caused the seven Gu Masters to become even more upset.

Xiong Jiao Man was filled with regret. Having already relinquished the Bear Enslavement Gu and assisted Fang Yuan in refining it, backing out was impossible.

"Fang Yuan, I will remember you." Xiong Jiao Man stared at Fang Yuan fiercely in the eye.

"That is your business." Fang Yuan chuckled faintly, turning to leave.

Cupping her fists to Chi Shan, Xiong Jiao Man pleaded, "Brother Chi Shan, we need Gu Yue reinforcements—Xiong clan has been besieged by wolf packs for six days and nights."

"Reinforcements won't be an issue after our previous successes, but I must report to the clan leader first. Follow me," Chi Shan replied.

Xiong Jiao Man's group followed Chi Shan back to Gu Yue village.

Gu Yue village was heavily fortified: reinforced outer walls boasted dozens of towers, with spiked bamboo trenches dug below.

Having suffered injuries while fighting through wolf packs from Xiong clan, the seven Gu Masters were led into a bamboo building for medical treatment.

Over ten healing Gu Masters—mostly Rank one with a few Rank twos—tended the wounded. Surprisingly, the person in charge was not a Rank two elder, but a young girl.

The girl had bright crystal eyes, a delicate face, and a charming voice, yet she treated patients with astonishing skill and experience.

Xiong Jiao Man suddenly thought of one person, asking, "This little sister, are you Gu Yue Yao Le?"

After dispatching her healers, Gu Yue Yao Le walked over. "Greetings, Sister Jiao Man. I'm surprised you know my name."

"Sister Yao Le, you are famous. With Lord Yao Ji's benevolence and your medical skill, you are one of Qing Mao Mountain's top ten rising stars despite being Rank one. Even in Xiong clan, many admire you." Knowing Yao Le's background while seeking aid, Xiong Jiao Man flattered her politely.

A Xiong Gu Master added, "Though both top ten rising stars, Gu Yue Fang Yuan cannot compare to you at all."

"Don't mention Fang Yuan, my blood boils thinking of him!"

"He took my Bear Enslavement Gu and seven hundred primeval stones just to rescue us! No one is more shamelessly greedy!" Xiong Jiao Man fumed.

"Though he is my senior, Fang Yuan's extreme actions are unforgivable. Extorting Sister Jiao Man for the Bear Enslavement Gu completely violates our clans' alliance treaty. I must tell Grandma to teach him a lesson before he brings shame upon our Gu Yue clan."

She lacked the ability herself, but believed her grandmother, Medicine Hall's Gu Yue Yao Ji, could.

"You want me to deal with Gu Yue Fang Yuan?" Yao Ji stared blankly at her granddaughter before growing serious.

"He extorted Sister Jiao Man for a Bear Enslavement Gu and seven hundred primeval stones. If this continues, he will ruin our clan's reputation!" Gu Yue Yao Le argued.

"Grandma understands. Run along now; I won't disappoint you," Yao Ji said after brief contemplation.

She had long wanted to move against Fang Yuan—not out of naive indignation, but because he held assets worth seizing.

First was the Liquor worm.

Though she had bought a Liquor worm from the caravan for Yao Le, Yao Ji still needed another as fusion material for a Rank three Gu.

Next was the Nine Leaf Vitality Grass.

Controlling most of the clan's Nine Leaf Vitality Grass, Yao Ji used vitality leaf sales to consolidate her authority.

"Fang Yuan is far too arrogant! First he refused to sell his Liquor worm, and now he profits off price-gouging vitality leaves. Does he take this Medicine Hall elder for air? Hmph!"

# Chapter 148: Circumstance

Two giant banners fluttered in the wind with a ringing noise.

One banner displayed the battle merit rankings, where Fang Yuan sat in the top ten; the other listed exchangeable goods.

Yet most Gu Masters in the crowded plaza focused on a newly posted decree.

The announcement declared that with the worsening wolf tide, clan casualties were climbing daily. To bolster medical supplies, the Medicine Hall ordered all Gu Masters holding Nine Leaf Vitality Grass to temporarily surrender them for centralized production.

Naturally, Fang Yuan was also listed.

Standing in a distant corner reading the notice, Fang Yuan's expression darkened.

He immediately recognized this as Gu Yue Yao Ji's strike against him.

"Hmph, Yao Ji has held a grudge since failing to buy my Liquor worm. She covertly blocked my Three Steps Fragrant Grass Gu exchange, and now covets my Nine Leaf Vitality Grass..."

Monopolizing vitality leaves allowed Yao Ji to consolidate authority and connections. By selling them at marked-up prices, Fang Yuan had inadvertently infringed on her profits and undermined her influence.

"The Nine Leaf Vitality Grass is my economic lifeline; losing it would checkmate me. Yao Ji struck viciously," Fang Yuan frowned.

The discussions of the surrounding Gu Masters entered his ears.

"Elder Yao Ji is truly benevolent. Look at the list—it includes Gu Masters from both Chi and Mo factions. She risked offending powerful elders for the clan."

"Look, the notice changed! Gu Masters from the Chi and Mo factions have handed over their Nine Leaf Vitality Grass!"

Centralizing Nine Leaf Vitality Grass production promised an abundance of vitality leaves, bringing great relief to the crowd.

Watching closely, Fang Yuan saw the words 'handed over' appear beside the other names, leaving his own glaringly conspicuous.

His heart felt increasing pressure.

As a crafty veteran, Yao Ji leveraged public sentiment to corner Fang Yuan. Resisting would only invite greater trouble and hand his enemies leverage.

Collecting the Nine Leaf Vitality Grass forced Gu Masters to sacrifice personal interests under public welfare, enriching Yao Ji while branding disobedience treason.

"Delaying tactics or petty schemes won't work against Yao Ji's righteous public momentum—they will only deepen my disadvantage. I can only counter her openly with an even greater momentum."

Fang Yuan already had a solution.

This plan was simple but grand; advance to Rank three!

Rank three carried entirely different status from Rank two. Advancing made a Gu Master an elder and decision-maker whose interests even the clan leader could not casually violate.

"Advancing to Rank three is essential. The clan readily sacrifices Rank two Gu Masters, but Rank three elders are vital pillars. Furthermore, my liabilities are growing—Qing Shu died without deep scrutiny, the clan overlooked my possession of the Water Shield and Plunder Gu, and the higher-ups ignored my rapid cultivation without probing my secrets."

Fang Yuan was very clear on his current situation.

"If I advance to Rank three, the suppression will ease, though it won't vanish completely. Rank three is a qualitative threshold—crossing it opens an entirely new world. Similarly, with Rank three cultivation, I can freely move about. At worst, I will simply leave the clan." Fang Yuan was clear on this.

However, advancing to Rank three was daunting—given his C grade aptitude, it was an almost impassable barrier.

In his past life, he had stagnated at Rank two peak stage for over a century before paying a painful price for a Gu to raise his aptitude and break through.

Fortunately, across his five hundred years of memories, there were two or three alternate methods—or plainly put, demonic practices.

One option required refining the Man-beast Life Burial Gu, an ancient demonic recipe devised to boost cultivation.

The Man-beast Life Burial Gu was specialized for Rank two Gu Masters to breakthrough to Rank three realm.

In his past life, Fang Yuan had stumbled upon this recipe during his explorations, committing it firmly to memory.

Crucially, the fusion materials were not rare.

Normally, it might take him two to three years to collect them, but the wolf tide provided a great opportunity.

He had already amassed sufficient primeval stones and Gu worms, only needing the right opportunity.

"Little sister Yao Le," Xiong Jiao Man walked in, "I came to say goodbye. That medicine hall notice helped me vent my anger."

"Big sister, you are leaving?" Gu Yue Yao Le asked with concern.

Xiong Jiao Man rested a hand on her hip and thumbed toward the Gu Masters outside: "I have gathered reinforcements and must hurry back. Let's meet again next time."

# Chapter 149: Wolf pack attacks the village

Yao Le watched from the doorway as Xiong Jiao Man and her group departed.

Yet barely fifteen minutes later, Xiong Jiao Man and the others returned to the bamboo building.

"We are unlucky, there's a wolf tide," Xiong Jiao Man laughed bitterly, explaining.

"Everyone get ready, the lightning wolf pack is about to assault the village," Xiong Jiao Man said.

Sirens immediately wailed throughout the entire village.

"This alarm... a large-scale wolf pack is attacking the village!"

Gu Masters cursed and hardened their expressions, swiftly gathering from buildings and streets toward the village's four gates and outer walls.

Along the inner side of the village walls, wooden ladders stood at regular intervals for Gu Masters to climb.

Defensive towers rose higher than the outer walls like courageous protectors of the village.

Reaching the base of the wall with the crowd, Fang Yuan pulled down a climbing Gu Master and ascended in his place.

"Which bastard..." The Gu Master began to curse, but seeing Fang Yuan, he shut up immediately.

Climbing the ladder, Fang Yuan activated the White Jade Gu and peered toward the front lines.

Outside the village roamed thousands of lightning wolves!

An endless sea of glimmering green wolf eyes stretched out, chilling all who saw it.

Alongside ordinary lightning wolves were bold lightning wolves.

"These bold lightning wolves are not the real threat." Fang Yuan scanned the distance and soon spotted three massive shadows lurking under the trees behind the pack.

Thousand beast king, frenzy lightning wolf!

Each frenzy lightning wolf was as large as an elephant.

Fang Yuan did not see the lightning crown wolf.

Fang Yuan secretly breathed a sigh of relief; without a lightning crown wolf, the village was relatively safe.

Howl!

The three frenzy lightning wolves raised their heads, howling long and loud.

At their command, numerous lightning wolves surged forward to attack the village.

On the village's side, Gu Masters were already in defensive formation.

"They're here." Fang Yuan's eyes glimmered from atop the ladder.

Lightning wolves poured across the mountainside like a tidal wave, assaulting the village.

They plunged into pitfalls outside the walls, skewered on bamboo spears like dumplings in a pot.

Stimulated by the blood, the frenzied wolf packs only attacked with greater fury.

Lightning wolves filled the traps with their corpses, paving the way for others to surge forward and slam into the walls.

Suppressing their terror, Rank one Gu Masters desperately poured primeval essence into Steel Vine Gu and Poison Flower Gu.

Wall thorns impaled the wolves, while Poison Flower Gu imbued the steel vines with lethal toxins.

Pewpewpew.

Rank two Gu Masters on the ladders and towers unleashed a barrage of moonblades.

Moonblades sliced into the packs, sending blood and wolf limbs flying.

Bold lightning wolves joined the fray, immediately inflicting casualties among the Gu Masters.

A bolt of blue lightning pierced the wall, blowing a hole through it and electrocuting a Rank one Gu Master inside.

The bold lightning wolf responsible instantly drew heavy retaliation.

Howl!

Opening its sharp fangs, it spat an electric current straight toward Fang Yuan.

Water Shield Gu.

Fang Yuan snorted, exhaling two streams of light-blue water vapor from his nose.

The water vapor expanded into a spherical shield, protecting him.

The lightning current hit the water shield and caused it to shake, almost breaking.

Moonglow Gu!

Dispelling the shield, Fang Yuan shot three moonblades from his wrist.

At tricky angles, the second blade crippled the wolf's front leg while the third smashed into its head, obliterating its left eye.

Bathed in blood from deep wounds, the bold lightning wolf collapsed, sending ripples of panic through the surrounding pack.

However, anchored by the three frenzy lightning wolves behind them, the pack quickly resumed clawing at the village walls.

Fang Yuan fired a few more moonblades before descending the ladder, his dark red primeval essence already below half and needing replenishment.

Fang Yuan squeezed into a reinforced bamboo building where many Gu Masters sat holding primeval stones to recover their essence.

Healing Gu Masters tended to the wounded with first-aid kits placed nearby.

Through this rotation, Fang Yuan deployed five times by nightfall. Yet the wolves showed no sign of thinning; more packs arrived continuously, answering the frenzy lightning wolves' calls.

Gu Yue Yao Ji's face was sullen.

Watching the crimson sunset, Yao Ji sighed faintly, "How is the Nine Leaf Vitality Grass acquisition going?"

Her subordinate reported that three people had yet to hand theirs over, including Fang Yuan.

Yao Ji snorted. "Where is Yao Le?"

"Miss Yao Le is at the east gate leading the healing team. Despite repeated warnings, she insists on staying at the front lines," the subordinate reported, kneeling in fear.

With a clan elder nearby and Xiong Jiao Man's squad fighting at the wall, the east gate was practically the safest place.

# Chapter 150: I am only helping myself

Wolf corpses piled in the traps halfway up the wall, creating a ramp for charging lightning wolves.

Astride a tower alongside other Gu Masters, Fang Yuan endlessly hurled moonblades at the wolf packs.

Suddenly!

A frenzy lightning wolf stepped forward, opening its maw to spit a thunderball.

Rank three thunderclap Gu.

Though only millstone-sized, the orb crackled with dense blue electricity and flew with blistering speed.

It collided violently with the tower Fang Yuan was on.

Water Shield Gu, White Jade Gu!

Fang Yuan barely raised his defenses before blinding white light engulfed his vision.

BOOM!

The thunder shattered his water shield within two breaths; residual lightning struck Fang Yuan, leaving him numb despite White Jade Gu.

He fell four meters to the ground. White Jade Gu offered no impact cushioning like Raiment Gu, leaving his back throbbing in pain.

The stone tower lay half-destroyed amid charred corpses. The blast tore a massive breach in the outer wall, through which wolves poured like a tide.

Every Gu Master beside him on the tower was killed; only Fang Yuan survived.

The Thunderclap Gu was devastating and swift, yet had a weakness: it required a cooldown before firing again.

BOOM!

Just as Fang Yuan stood, another thunderball hurtled toward him.

"Two Thunderclap Gu!" Fang Yuan blanched and hurriedly dodged.

"Beast, cease your insolence!" An elder leapt into mid-air, radiating golden light to block the thunderball.

The elders had made their moves!

Accelerating into a sprint, the beast charged headfirst toward the village wall.

The intense battle between the three elders and this frenzy lightning wolf unfolded.

Soon, the other two frenzy lightning wolves assaulted the village, opening two new battlefronts.

Nine elders clashed with three frenzy lightning wolves across three battlefronts. Lightning shattered bamboo buildings, destroying any wolves or Gu Masters caught in the crossfire.

As wolves continued storming the village, Fang Yuan disengaged to a secluded corner, watching the three major clashes from afar.

"Three frenzy wolves: one wields twin Thunderclap Gu for high offense, another has Thunder Wings Gu for brief flight and mobility, and the third possesses Thunder Roar Gu to kill with soundwaves."

"The outcome is set." Just when Yao Ji puffed out a sigh, a roar sounded out from the eastern gate.

An ordinary lightning wolf ballooned rapidly, swelling to the size of an elephant.

"Another frenzy lightning wolf!" The elders watched in alarm.

With the clan's forces concentrated entirely on the three battlegrounds, the eastern gate's defenses were left dangerously thin.

"Not good, Yao Le is still there!" Yao Ji blanched and bolted toward the eastern gate.

"An unforeseen twist. That frenzy lightning wolf must possess a Rank three Aura Restraint Gu, disguising itself among the pack to deceive the defenders."

Fang Yuan observed closely; the eastern gate was in dire straits.

The fourth frenzy lightning wolf smashed through the eastern gate, tearing open a massive breach.

Behind the bold wolves, an endless swarm howled savagely and charged into the village.

Fortunately, she had veterans like Xiong Jiao Man beside her. At this critical timing, Xiong Jiao Man took it upon herself to control the scene and ordered, "Quick, close the doors and the windows. We need to defend this place till the reinforcements arrive, and only then will we have a fighting chance!"

Before she finished speaking, the bamboo building's roof collapsed under the frenzy lightning wolf's attack.

Gu Yue Yao Le shrieked, losing her composure in the sudden crisis.

"Brute beast, stop!" Gu Yue Yao Ji shouted as she sprinted desperately.

In this dire moment, Xiong Jiao Man stepped forward once more.

With a thought, she sent her remaining brown bear charging at the frenzy lightning wolf with a roar.
