While the wolf was occupied with the bear, Xiong Jiao Man rushed out with Yao Le in tow, opening a small gap.

Unwilling to lose its prey, the frenzy lightning wolf roared and leaped a dozen meters, cutting off Xiong Jiao Man.

Dragging Yao Le, Xiong Jiao Man dared not charge head-on and fled outside the village instead.

As the wolf lunged, Yao Ji arrived and struck its back with a pale-purple moonblade, which dissolved into miasma and drilled into its nostrils.

Enraged, the frenzy lightning wolf roared, unleashing an invisible concussive shockwave into its surroundings.

The shockwave hurled them in opposite directions. Xiong Jiao Man slammed into a bamboo building and lay motionless, while Yao Le was blasted through the breached gate and out of the village.

Dazed from the fall, she tried to stand, only to see a lightning wolf lunging with open jaws.

Aah—!

She shrieked as a moonblade struck at the last second, slaying the wolf.

Surrounded by snapping fangs, a terrified Yao Le suddenly heard a calm voice: "Move a step back!"

She moved instinctively, narrowly dodging a wolf's bite.

"Roll to the right." That voice came again.

Her mind was blank, but her body reacted instantly; a wolf's jaws snapped shut, missing her by a hair.

Following the relentless voice, she dodged repeatedly; whenever she slipped up, a moonblade would shoot out from nowhere to save her.

Only when no lightning wolves remained around her did Yao Le realize she was safe.

She had drifted far from the village into a remote forest.

A figure appeared before her. Her eyes widened. "Gu Yue Fang Yuan, you helped me?" "No, I am only helping myself," Fang Yuan grinned malevolently.

# Chapter 151: Demonic nature

"What?" Gu Yue Yao Le was shocked when she heard this.

Fang Yuan struck like lightning, chopping her neck and knocking her out instantly.

As the girl fell, Fang Yuan caught her by the waist and activated the Stealth Scale Gu, vanishing on the spot.

Waking groggily, Gu Yue Yao Le found herself in a dark mountain cave.

Her arms were bound behind her back and a rope looped around her neck, tying her firmly to a large rock.

All the Gu worms on her body had been plundered and refined by Fang Yuan.

"Fang Yuan, what do you want? Let me go, or my grandma will deal with you!" Yao Le struggled frantically.

"You sure are lively," Fang Yuan coldly snorted.

Gu Yue Yao Le was about to rage further when she saw a large bear moving behind Fang Yuan.

"B-bear..." Her widened eyes showed shock as she stuttered.

Caressing the bear's black fur, Fang Yuan said coldly, "Because of the wolf tide, finding this wild bear took much effort."

Gu Yue Yao Le immediately remembered that Fang Yuan had taken a Bear Enslavement Gu from Xiong Jiao Man.

"So that's it..." She sneered, but before she could speak, Fang Yuan squatted before her.

"What are you doing?!" She tried to pull away, but Fang Yuan easily gripped her cheeks firmly.

"Such a cute appearance, lovable indeed," Fang Yuan plainly commented.

His right hand moved quickly, grabbing Yao Le's shirt, and pulled.

The shirt was instantly torn, showing her pink undergarment.

"Ah—!" Stunned for a moment, the girl shrieked and struggled frantically, ignoring the ropes chafing her tender skin bloody.

Yet Fang Yuan did not continue as she expected, stepping back instead.

At that moment, the black bear stepped forward.

Whoosh!

The bear's paw struck with a whistling rush of air.

With a crisp crack, the paw struck her skull, snapping her slender neck.

Driven by hunger, the bear needed no command from the Bear Enslavement Gu as it lowered its head to feast.

It began gnawing at her throat, fresh blood bubbling out and splashing onto its black fur.

Unhindered by bone, the bear dug deeper into her body, biting out her still-beating heart and swallowing it in one gulp.

Folding his arms, Fang Yuan watched Gu Yue Yao Le, recalling an Earth Buddhist saying: 'Without self, without others; detached from all beings and time. Void is the red skull, white bones, skin, and flesh!'

Beautiful men and women eventually become skeletons. Skin, flesh, and bones are one, yet people cherish flesh while fearing bone—fixated on appearances, blind to the equality of all things.

Witnessing the girl's violent death, his heart remained unmoved.

Death is death; none can escape it. Girl, bear, ant, fox, tree, crone, murderer—all are lowly, humble mongrels!

Only by seeing past the superficial to this truth does one gain divinity.

Stepping toward the light, this divinity becomes Buddha; stepping into darkness, it becomes a demon.

Demonic nature!

# Chapter 152: Advance to Rank three

An hour later, Gu Yue Yao Le was nearly devoured.

The bear was reluctant to eat her intestines and brain, but Fang Yuan would not permit that.

According to the Man-beast Life Burial Gu's recipe, a beast had to consume every scrap of the person to maximize the refinement's success rate.

The human had to be a virgin girl with Gu Master aptitude of at least C grade, though A and B grade yielded far better chances.

Once the beast finished eating, refinement had to begin immediately before the human remains digested, ruining the process.

Displeased that the bear refused Yao Le's intestines and brain, Fang Yuan snorted and activated the Bear Enslavement Gu within it.

Growling under Fang Yuan's coercion, the bear swallowed the intestines and brain, then crushed her bones to bits and gulped them down.

Seeing the bear swallow the spine, Fang Yuan knew the time was ripe.

He flicked his finger, and a Single Aperture Charcoal Gu flew out.

The black bear roared in agony as the Charcoal Gu burned its internal organs.

After fifteen minutes of burning, Fang Yuan flicked out three Kerosene Gu, bursting into kerosene and intensifying the flames inside the bear's body.

As the hoarse bear shrieked, Fang Yuan carefully tossed several more Gu worms into its body.

Sparks erupted from its orifices, swelling into a giant fire pillar that enveloped its body.

Yet the fire burned an eerie blood-red instead of normal orange.

Fang Yuan waited patiently until the fire began dying down, then tossed in the final Gu.

A fist-sized lump of white light emerged from the flames.

Fang Yuan quickly drew primeval stones from his pouch and threw them into the lump of light.

After consuming up to a thousand primeval stones, the light reached door-size and suddenly shrank.

The flames on the corpse went out, returning the cave to darkness as a Gu worm wobbled toward Fang Yuan.

It resembled a black spider with long furry legs and a bear's head. On its back was a blood-colored tattoo outlining Gu Yue Yao Le's beautiful face.

Without hesitation, he swallowed the Man-beast Life Burial Gu.

Sliding down his throat, the Man-beast Life Burial Gu dissolved into a stream of black and red water.

Immediately, his primeval essence turned an eerie black-red, thick with the stench of blood.

Fang Yuan mobilized the black-red primeval essence, surging it against the aperture walls.

At 10%, 20%, 30%—when 38% was expended, the aperture boomed and the white crystal walls shattered.

A brand-new spherical light membrane wall took their place.

Fang Yuan had advanced to Rank three! Yet without relaxing, he forced the remaining black-red primeval essence out of his body.

This unusable primeval essence would kill Gu worms if injected, and lingering in the aperture would pollute the light membrane walls, lowering aptitude.

After expelling every trace of black-red essence, he held a primeval stone to recover his primeval essence.

A white silver fog condensed into droplets, forming his white silver Primeval Sea.

Yet this white silver Primeval Sea retained a trace of black-red—the side effect of the Man-beast Life Burial Gu.

After several attempts, the black-red impurity diminished to a faint trace that further purging could not remove.

Cleansing Water Gu could wash away aperture impurities; one had appeared in the treehouse shop, but the Chi faction bought it.

"You useless idiots, how could you fail to find a living person?!" Inside the medicine hall, Gu Yue Yao Ji raged, worry and fear gnawing at her.

Three days had passed; the four frenzy lightning wolves were slain and Gu Yue village was safe, yet Yao Le remained missing.

Despite mobilizing all her contacts and scouring the battlefield and surrounding areas with search parties, not a single trace emerged.

A Rank two Gu Master entered. "Reporting to Medicine Hall Elder, this subordinate has news."

"What! Is it news about Yao Le?" Gu Yue Yao Ji's eyes shone, asking quickly.

"Someone reached Rank three. The clan leader is convening an elder meeting at the pavilion to confirm it," the Gu Master reported.

Gu Yue Yao Ji waved her hand dismissively. "Not news of Yao Le? Wait... someone reached Rank three to become a clan elder?" She frowned, suddenly realizing.

Frowning, Gu Yue Yao Ji asked, "Who reached Rank three?"

"Reporting to Lord Yao ji, it is Gu Yue Fang Yuan," the Gu Master answered.

"What? Him!" Gu Yue Yao Ji's pupils shrank; this was the worst possible news.

Yao Ji's ears twitched as intuition sparked an intense suspicion toward Fang Yuan.

Changing her mind, she headed for the clan leader's pavilion.

# Chapter 153: Really aged

"He has truly advanced to Rank three!"

Clan Head Gu Yue Bo sat at the head of the hall, flanked by rows of elders.

Amid hushed whispers, dozens of elders focused on the young man standing upright in the center of the hall with his head held high.

"Medicine Hall Elder, Lord Gu Yue Yao Ji has arrived!" the doorguard loudly announced.

An old woman with a pale, wrinkled face walked through the doors, her sharp eyes instantly locking onto Fang Yuan beneath weary eyelids.

Striding forward, Gu Yue Yao Ji demanded, "Fang Yuan, you reached Rank three? I don't believe it! Let me inspect your aperture myself!"

Fang Yuan turned toward the approaching Yao Ji and sneered, "Gu Yue Yao Ji, on what basis do you demand to inspect my aperture?"

"Insolent junior, you dare address me by name?" Yao Ji's eyes widened. "As Medicine Hall Elder and your senior, I have every right to inspect you!"

"Old hag, calling you by name is showing respect. We still have scores to settle. You bore a grudge when I refused to sell my Liquor worm and tried to plunder my Nine Leaf Vitality Grass. You secretly blocked me from exchanging merits for the Three Step Fragrant Grass Gu. Now that I am a Rank three Gu Master, stop putting on airs!"

She demanded, "Fang Yuan, where did you disappear for three days? Had you not shown up, your name would be listed among the battle casualties in the medicine hall. You vanish for three days and nights, only to return as a Rank three Gu Master. What happened during that time? I am sure everyone here desires an explanation."

Under their scrutiny, Fang Yuan laughed aloud. "Old woman, am I obligated to answer you? Still, in light of your granddaughter's tragic demise, I will generously enlighten you. During the past few days, I have been in closed door cultivation in Elder Chi Lian's manor, trying to breakthrough to Rank three. This point can be verified by Elder Gu Yue Chi Lian!"

All eyes immediately turned to the gloomy Gu Yue Chi Lian.

Under everyone's eyes, Chi Lian reluctantly replied, "It is true. I testify that Fang Yuan was in closed-door cultivation within a secret room of my manor these past few days."

At his words, the entire hall erupted into an uproar.

Gu Yue Yao Ji turned deathly pale, staggering back in trembling shock. Opposing an isolated elder versus one backed by the Chi faction were two completely different matters!

# Chapter 154: Vomit blood out of anger

"This Fang Yuan has monumental guts, daring to threaten me!" Gu Yue Chi Lian was fuming and bewildered.

Last night, Fang Yuan had secretly approached him, blackmailing him over Gu Yue Chi Cheng's faked aptitude.

Chi Cheng was only C grade, but for the faction's sake, Chi Lian had risked faking his aptitude as B grade.

Fang Yuan held the Chi faction's fatal weakness. When threatened, Chi Lian had nearly struck out to eliminate him on the spot.

Yet he held back, unsure if Fang Yuan had shared the secret or hidden evidence. "Pacify him first, understand the situation, then eliminate him!" he had resolved.

Soon after, Fang Yuan revealed his Rank three cultivation.

Shell-shocked, Chi Lian could only shift to cooperation.

Fang Yuan spoke again, "Gu Yue Yao Ji, I know you are distraught. Your granddaughter died without a corpse, leaving the medicine faction heirless. Seeing a C grade reach Rank three fills you with envy, directing your rage at me. I understand that."

"However," he shifted sharply, "you wasted vital clan resources and manpower forming search parties for your granddaughter, concerning every elder here and the clan leader himself. We entrusted you with the medicine hall, yet you dispatched healing Gu Masters to search for your granddaughter, abandoning our injured and crippled clansmen. You are utterly selfish! Yao Le's death is a loss, but your actions worsened it. You are unfit to remain Medicine Hall Elder!"

Turning to Gu Yue Bo, he said, "Clan Leader, I question Yao Ji's ability to lead the medicine hall. I propose temporarily revoking her elder title pending a thorough assessment. Each one of the clan's healing Gu Masters are precious to us. We cannot let her waste them like this. Otherwise, the ones that suffer the most are the brave Gu Masters that fight using their lives for our precious clan!"

Pausing briefly, he added, "Elder Chi Lian fully supports this proposal."

"WHAT?!" Gu Yue Chi Lian nearly jumped out of his seat.

"Elder Chi Lian, you support this?" Gu Yue Bo asked, eyes gleaming.

Gritting his teeth, Gu Yue Chi Lian stood up slowly, resisting the urge to look at Fang Yuan lest he lose his temper.

"Revoking Yao Ji's title was Fang Yuan's idea alone. With the wolf tide looming, suddenly changing leadership is inappropriate. However, I agree Elder Yao Ji let personal feelings compromise her role as a healing Gu Master. Had those efforts been directed properly, our clan's losses would be greatly reduced."

Even so, as he finished speaking, a thump sounded.

Turning, he saw Gu Yue Yao Ji had coughed up blood in fury and collapsed onto her back.

"Lord Yao Ji worked hard; resting is for her own good. Since the hall cannot remain leaderless, I advise letting Fang Yuan take over," Gu Yue Chi Lian suddenly said.

Sure enough, Gu Yue Bo declared, "Elder Chi Zhong shall temporarily oversee the medicine hall. Fang Yuan has indeed reached Rank three, so by clan rules, he becomes a clan elder. Spread the news and let the village rejoice."

With that, Gu Yue Bo stood and left. "Congratulations, congratulations..." The clan elders approached Fang Yuan with beaming smiles, cupping their fists.

Fang Yuan cupped his fists in return, smiling warmly.

# Chapter 155: Borrow

Fang Yuan tactfully declined several invitations and smiled as he left the clan head pavilion alongside Gu Yue Chi Lian.

"Hmph, satisfied now? After dragging down Gu Yue Yao Ji, you pulled my Chi faction into hot water too!" Upon reaching the study, Chi Lian dropped his facade, his smile replaced by fury.

Seated opposite him, Fang Yuan smiled leisurely. "You should thank me. Yao Ji's downfall brings great benefit to your Chi faction."

Gu Yue Chi Lian's gaze flickered. "Hmph, naive boy. Chi Zhong belongs to my Chi faction, but his wife is from the Yao faction. The clan leader appointed him acting medicine hall elder precisely to balance the strife between our factions. But how did you discover Chi Cheng's secret?"

Fang Yuan shrugged nonchalantly. "Old man, I'm short on primeval stones. Give me three thousand."

Gu Yue Chi Lian slammed the table, roaring in a low voice, "Fang Yuan! Don't think knowing that secret lets you extort my Chi faction. I'm old and have little time left—at worst, I'll forfeit my life! I accept cooperating, but I will never tolerate threats!"

Once he finished, Fang Yuan smiled leisurely. "Don't be so angry, old man. I am genuinely short on funds, and I'm merely borrowing those three thousand primeval stones. I'll even sign a promissory note."

Gu Yue Chi Lian snorted, "You won't lack stones. Every elder receives a weekly stipend of one hundred primeval stones. That is during peacetime, but during the wolf tide, you receive three hundred primeval stones as allowance."

"Additionally, you may claim a free Rank three Gu worm. The clan's Rank one through three recipes are also opened for you to choose and refine Rank three Gu. Among other privileges: ordinary Gu Masters can only marry one wife, while elders may take one wife and two concubines."

"Even so, I still need three thousand primeval stones. Refining Rank three Gu consumes vast funds," Fang Yuan said.

With that in mind, Gu Yue Chi Lian relented and promptly fetched brush and paper.

Fang Yuan drafted the promissory note and pressed his fingerprint.

Gu Yue Chi Lian instructed his butler, who soon brought over several bags filled with primeval stones.

Having secured the stones, Fang Yuan did not leave, smiling instead. "I want to borrow something else."

"Don't push your luck." Chi Lian looked grim, but relented. "Speak, what is it?"

"Cleansing Water Gu," Fang Yuan said candidly, eyes narrowing.

"That is impossible!" Gu Yue Chi Lian firmly refused.

He had indeed bought it, but prepared it for his grandson Gu Yue Chi Cheng. Finding another would depend entirely on luck.

"Don't refuse so quickly," Fang Yuan chuckled. "Surely the head of the Chi faction knows what matters more: a Cleansing Water Gu or your faction's reputation."

Chi Lian's face darkened, glaring at Fang Yuan. "Fang Yuan, understand what you are doing. You are blackmailing the head of the Chi faction!"

"This isn't blackmail, merely a discussion. I'm only borrowing the Cleansing Water Gu and will return a replacement later," Fang Yuan chuckled.

An hour later, Fang Yuan left the Chi manor with three thousand primeval stones and a Cleansing Water Gu.

Three days later.

The ball of light dissipated, and a brand-new Gu worm flew into Fang Yuan's palm.

Resembling a large ladybug, its semicircular milky-white carapace was decorated with dark spots.

Rank three Sky Canopy Gu!

Sky Canopy Gu was refined by fusing the Rank two White Jade Gu with an aqua defense Gu.

Yet Sky Canopy Gu was not his first Rank three Gu; he had already obtained one directly from the clan.

Fang Yuan used this chance to pick a Thunderwings Gu.

Obtained by the elders after slaying a frenzy lightning wolf, this Gu materialized thunder wings granting temporary flight.

With Thunderwings Gu aiding his movement, Fang Yuan's final combat shortcoming was resolved.

# Chapter 156: Digital Shade Recipe, Flower Wine mystery

"Lord Fang Yuan, this way to the secret room, please." In the dark, eerie tunnel, an elderly Rank two Gu Master led the way.

The secret room holding the clan's combination recipes lay ahead. Advancing to Rank three granted Fang Yuan authority to browse all clan recipes below Rank four.

Moments later, the guide brought Fang Yuan before a stone door.

A Gu flew from his aperture and struck the stone door. The surface rippled like water and faded, revealing the secret room.

The secret room was rather small, spanning only 666 2⁄3 m². Every few steps stood a half-height stone table bearing a white jade plate, upon which a Gu worm rested silently.

"Lord Fang Yuan, these Rank two Digital Shade Gu record recipes. The green ones hold Rank one recipes. Red-black holds Rank two, white holds Rank three, orange has Rank four, and purple records Rank five."

"These Digital Shade Gu belong to the Secret Hall elder, but you may browse them with primeval essence. However, your current status restricts you from viewing Rank four and five recipes. Furthermore, this room is a restricted zone where every action is secretly monitored, and you may only stay fifteen minutes daily. Once time is up, you must leave," the Gu Master added.

"Mm, I understand." Fang Yuan nodded.

With limited time, Fang Yuan quickly set down the first Digital Shade Gu and picked up a white one to observe.

The white Digital Shade Gu contained Rank three fusion recipes. Most did not suit Fang Yuan, requiring base Gu like Moonwhirl or Moonscar Gu.

Having fused Moonlight Gu with Little Light Gu into Moonglow Gu, only three subsequent refinement recipes were worth using.

First was the Rank three Golden Moon Gu, maintaining a ten-step range with increased power; fired, the golden crescent spanned half a man's height with an imposing aura.

Second was the Frost Moon Gu. Its icy-white moonblade carried frost power, invading wounded targets with cold to slow their movement.

Third was the Illusory Moon Gu. Non-offensive and unique, activating it allowed a Gu Master to create shadow clones that drew attacks and confused enemies.

Furthermore, Illusory Moon Gu served as the basis for Rank four Moonshadow Gu, which could be implanted into an aperture to suppress primeval essence.

"Moonshadow Gu... wasn't that used by the fourth generation clan leader to ambush the Flower Wine Monk?"

Moonshadow Gu had a unique effect, suppressing 60% of primeval essence in a Rank three aperture, 30% in Rank four, and 15% in Rank five.

What puzzled Fang Yuan, was not the Moonshadow Gu's effect, but the Flower Wine Monk.

"On the shadow wall, the Flower Wine Monk was drenched in blood and heavily wounded. Yet his battle with the fourth generation leader and elders caused only mild injuries. I assumed those severe injuries came from this Moonshadow Gu, but it only suppresses primeval essence. Where did the Flower Wine Monk's grave wounds actually come from?"

Meticulous by nature, Fang Yuan wondered what had truly happened when the Flower Wine Monk fled.

"Lord Fang Yuan, fifteen minutes are up. Please return tomorrow." The door turned ethereal as the old Gu Master called from outside.

"Alright." Eyes shining, Fang Yuan set down the Digital Shade Gu and left the room.

Whether Golden Moon, Frost Moon, or Illusory Moon, none of these recipes suited him, as they all required large quantities of moon orchid petals.

Moon orchid petals spoiled within days. Since Fang Yuan planned to roam the world, these Gu would starve to death without food.

However, some Rank three recipes remained that he had not yet seen.

# Chapter 157: Blood Moon Gu

Second day, secret room.

Blood Moon Gu.

Fusing Rank two Moonglow Gu with Blood Essence Gu formed this Rank three Gu worm.

When activated, it unleashed a face-sized, blood-red moonblade that caused continuous bleeding on wounded targets.

"That's it." Fang Yuan scanned the recipe, memorizing it perfectly without leaving any margin for error.

Its food was no longer moon orchid petals, but fresh blood.

Any type of blood sufficed, and the forested mountains of the Southern Border teemed with wild beasts.

"Next, I must refine this Blood Moon Gu," Fang Yuan resolved internally.

He already held the Moonglow Gu, though Blood Essence Gu was harder to acquire.

Though possessing white silver primeval essence, not all his Gu were Rank three yet.

"With Thunderwings Gu and Sky Canopy Gu, refining Blood Moon Gu gives me three Rank three Gu—still nowhere near enough."

Planning to leave his homeland and roam the Southern Border, Fang Yuan needed at least six Gu worms to handle every situation.

By his experience, six distinct roles were essential: attack, defense, healing, storage, reconnaissance, and movement.

Blood Moon Gu provided attack, Sky Canopy Gu defense, and Thunderwings Gu temporary flight.

For healing, the Nine Leaf Vitality Grass was underwhelming; as a Rank two Gu, its healing power was modest and its advancement path unsatisfactory.

For reconnaissance, Earth Communication Ear Grass had immense range, remaining viable despite being Rank two.

Fang Yuan lacked a storage Gu, the most critical type of all; when traveling alone, supplies formed the bedrock sustaining all other five categories.

"For storage Gu, the requirement is holding food and primeval stones while preserving goods. Nothing on the clan boards suits me; I must squeeze the Chi faction for their reserves."

When fifteen minutes expired, Fang Yuan walked out of the tunnel, still pondering.

"Greetings, Lord Fang Yuan." A middle-aged Gu Master stood waiting outside specifically for him.

"You are?"

The man smiled. "I am Gu Yue Chi Zhong, the current acting medicine hall elder."

"Here is your elder stipend of three hundred primeval stones. Knowing you were here, I brought it personally," Gu Yue Chi Zhong said, handing over a money bag.

"This man..." Fang Yuan squinted, taking the bag.

"Truthfully, I took the initiative to seek Lord Fang Yuan out because I have a request."

"Oh, you want me to turn in the Nine Leaf Vitality Grass?" Fang Yuan showed a deep profound expression.

# Chapter 158: Times have changed

Seeking to establish his new authority, Gu Yue Chi Zhong took the initiative to find Fang Yuan and persuade him to surrender the Nine Leaf Vitality Grass.

"Handing over the Nine Leaf Vitality Grass is not impossible," Fang Yuan murmured after a moment's contemplation.

Gu Yue Chi Zhong smiled knowingly. "If you are willing to hand over the Nine Leaf Vitality Grass, I will compensate you with this command token."

"As a newly promoted elder, you may not know: this Prize Token is awarded to Gu Masters with great contributions; few elders possess one. With it, you may enter the clan's underground Gu cave to choose any Gu worm; the cave houses many rare, precious Gu. Four of the top ten Gu on the exchange board were chosen from that underground cave," Gu Yue Chi Zhong explained.

Fang Yuan was instantly moved.

"I need a storage Gu to leave the clan. This token is a good opportunity, but..." Fang Yuan still harbored concerns.

Observing Fang Yuan's expression, Gu Yue Chi Zhong misunderstood his hesitation: "The underground cave holds not only Rank three Gu worms but also Rank four. Elder Fang Yuan, you won't be at a disadvantage. If you regret it later, we can secretly re-exchange them."

With that thought, Fang Yuan accepted the token from Gu Yue Chi Zhong and handed over the Nine Leaf Vitality Grass. Since he transferred it willingly, Chi Zhong quickly refined it.

"Lord Chi Zhong, it is delightful chatting with you. Do you have some time right now?" Fang Yuan asked as he stored the token.

Gu Yue Chi Zhong's eyes flashed; he was puzzled.

"We pay respects to the two elders!" Fang Yuan's uncle and aunt bowed, greeting them respectfully with visible apprehension.

Fang Yuan chuckled, "Uncle, Aunt, no need for courtesy. Though I am an elder, I remain your nephew. Come, please take a seat."

Gu Yue Chi Zhong remained silent and did not drink. Fang Yuan calmly took a sip and asked, "How have Uncle and Aunt been lately?"

"Sigh, the wolf tide has thrown life into chaos. The tavern was forced to close and rental income plummeted. We are so poor we are nearing starvation," Uncle said, tears unexpectedly welling in his eyes.

Fang Yuan sneered inwardly. He harbored no respect or affection for his aunt and uncle, but that did not make them useless.

"Uncle, Aunt, you cared for me for so long; my achievements are thanks to your nurturing. Having just received the elder stipend, please accept these three hundred primeval stones." Fang Yuan handed the money bag to his uncle.

"What? This..." Gu Yue Dong Tu's expression was truly marvelous.

"It is Elder Fang Yuan's filial piety, do accept it," Gu Yue Chi Zhong expressionlessly spoke from his seat.

"Yes, yes, yes." Uncle nodded repeatedly and took the bag, powerless before the medicine hall elder.

He held the pouch of three hundred primeval stones in both hands. Usually avaricious, he now felt it was scalding hot and wanted to fling it away immediately!

"Since uncle has received it, I will take my leave," Fang Yuan said and started leaving.

# Chapter 159: This Fang Yuan......

"Elder Fang Yuan, here is the medicine hall's latest casualty report, please take a look!" A courier Gu Master arrived, greeting Fang Yuan and presenting the report with both hands.

Now a Rank three elder, Fang Yuan was assigned by the clan to lead Gu Masters in suppressing the western sector.

Fang Yuan looked away from the battlefield, took the report, and told the Gu Master, "You may leave."

"This wolf tide is among the most severe in clan history. Such casualties are unavoidable."

Moonlight gathered in his palm as he thought inwardly, reducing the report to ashes.

Only clan elders were authorized to read this report; releasing it publicly would cause social unrest.

The moonlight in Fang Yuan's palm was no longer eerie blue, but blood-red.

This is the Rank three Blood Moon Gu.

Fang Yuan closed his eyes, his mind entering his aperture.

Inside, the aperture walls pulsed rhythmically in pure white, completely free of impurities.

His primeval essence was pure white with a silver metallic lustre—the white silver primeval essence of a Rank three Gu Master.

The Cleansing Water Gu extorted from Gu Yue Chi Lian had long been used, completely clearing the Man-beast Life Burial Gu's side effects.

But this was not without a price.

Fang Yuan's C grade aptitude had dropped by 2% due to the Man-beast Life Burial Gu, from 44% to a maximum capacity of 42%.

A white-shelled, black-spotted ladybug danced above the sea—the Sky Canopy Gu.

The Rank three Thunderwings Gu resided on Fang Yuan's back as two lightning bolt tattoos.

To be worth mentioning is the Spring Autumn Cicada.

Its condition was improving and recovering faster, causing Fang Yuan both joy and worry.

The Spring Autumn Cicada was Rank six while he was only Rank three; his aperture could not endure a fully recovered cicada.

As its aura strengthened, his aperture would eventually fail to withstand the load, like a paper tube containing stones.

"Lord Elder, a frenzy lightning wolf has entered the fray! Gu Yue Jiang Jian and two other elders holding the eastern gate request your support," a Gu Master reported frantically.

With a crack of lightning, two wings sprouted from Fang Yuan's back.

Formed of abstract blue lightning, the wings flapped and carried Fang Yuan high into the air, speeding toward the eastern gate.

Flying in a straight line, Fang Yuan reached the battlefield within a few breaths.

The frenzy lightning wolf was attempting to breach the eastern gate, fiercely contested by several clan elders.

The frenzy lightning wolf leaped out of the fray, sweeping its tail toward a Rank one female Gu Master in the corner.

But at this crucial moment, a shadow descended from the sky, hugging her.

"The battlefield is dangerous, retreat immediately." Fang Yuan flapped his wings, landing to set her down gently.

He vaguely recognized the young girl as a former classmate, though he could not recall her name.

With that, he turned and sprinted into the battle instead of flying.

Thunderwings Gu was swift, but its essence consumption was even faster. With only C grade talent and the lowest essence capacity among Rank three Gu Masters, Fang Yuan had to conserve energy carefully.

He threw a moonblade out.

The blood-red moonblade struck the frenzy lightning wolf, inflicting a continuously bleeding wound.

"What do you think of this Fang Yuan?" Gu Yue Bo asked a close aide while watching from afar.

The aide replied, "Elder Fang Yuan answered the medicine hall's call by surrendering his Nine Leaf Vitality Grass. After receiving the payout, he gave it entirely to his aunt and uncle as a filial gesture. Lately, he has fought aggressively to slay lightning wolves, accumulating significant battle merits. Having saved numerous clansmen, his reputation is improving. The clan already has rumors—him turning over a new leaf, the Fang faction's new clan elder etc."

Fang Yuan's surrender of the Nine Leaf Vitality Grass was clearly a deal with Gu Yue Chi Zhong, while giving his payout to his relatives was merely political theater.

Regardless, Fang Yuan no longer kept to himself, and his conduct was commendable.

Advancing to elder and taking these initiatives showed a willingness to integrate and contribute, which the clan's prosperity required.

Gu Yue Bo instructed, "The Hidden Hall found nothing during their recent investigations either. Forget it, withdraw the investigation into Fang Yuan for now."

"Yes, this subordinate shall inform them now." Saying so, he took his leave.

He thought inwardly, "Regardless of the reason, his cultivation rose too fast. This Fang Yuan harbors a great secret! However in this period, with the severity of the wolf tide, every bit of strength is needed, and wasting it on Fang Yuan is too wasteful. Investigation is still necessary, but this can only wait after the wolf tide."

# Chapter 160: Astounding applause

With a bam, the frenzy lightning wolf fell on the ground like a giant elephant.

"Fortunately, Elder Fang Yuan's Blood Moon Gu inflicted continuous damage, or killing it would have been even harder."

Though initially disapproving of Fang Yuan refining the Blood Moon Gu, the elders now saw its immense value against frenzy lightning wolves.

"You flatter me, esteemed elders. Without your protection, I could not have attacked so freely," Fang Yuan replied politely, wiping rainwater from his face. "No, it is our duty."

Fang Yuan's sharp judgment and ruthless combat in the recent clash had surpassed even veteran elders, earning their newfound respect.

His polite, modest demeanor completely defied rumors of an aloof, unruly youth, leaving the elders with a favorable impression.

"I still have much to learn from all of you," Fang Yuan said respectfully, though his eyes glinted darkly.

The Blood Moon Gu excelled in protracted battles by causing unhealable wounds that worsened over time.

"The Blood Moon Gu's main drawback is losing strength while oozing blood for several days each month. As my primary attack, it is too unstable, but obtaining the Chainsaw Golden Centipede from the Flower Wine inheritance will cover this weakness."

With the village on high alert from constant wolf attacks, Fang Yuan drew too much attention as an elder to visit the rock crack's secret cave.

"Lord Fang Yuan, do you recognize me?" Along the road, a person greeted Fang Yuan respectfully.

"You are..." Fang Yuan squinted, recognizing a classmate whose name escaped him. During past extortions, this youth had always yielded primeval stones submissively, revealing clever tact beneath a meek exterior.

"Lord Fang Yuan, I am Gu Yue Ding Zong. It is an honor to have been your classmate. After news of your promotion spread, our classmates have longed to discuss cultivation with you. Would Lord be free tonight?"

Fang Yuan shook his head, "No, before that, I must visit the underground cavern."

"Ah, I see." Gu Yue Ding Zong's expression shifted; the underground cavern was a forbidden ground accessible only to clan elders.

The secret chamber housing precious Gu worms was vast, nearly the size of the village square.

Tusita flower.

This Rank three grass Gu resembled a red lantern with three thick, round green leaves arranged in symmetrical directions.

Like the tusita grass, the tusita flower could store food and primeval stones, making it an ideal Gu in Fang Yuan's eyes.

"To think the Gu Yue village had a tusita flower. This covers my storage weakness," Fang Yuan rejoiced, selecting it immediately.

Changing into a clean shirt, Fang Yuan walked toward the gathering point.

"Elder Fang Yuan, you are here! It is our greatest honor!" Gu Yue Ding Zong called out from the door with wide eyes. Spotting Fang Yuan from across the street, he eagerly welcomed him into the bamboo building, where a banquet was already laid out before seated guests.

Fang Yuan scanned the table, seeing familiar faces of classmates he had once extorted.

"My pleasure." He lightly smiled, sitting at the highest position.

After toasting everyone and drinking moderately, Fang Yuan raised his cup and laughed, "Youth is foolish; I was immature and did terrible things in the past. I hope everyone can forgive and forget."

Everyone knew he was referring to the primeval stone extortion.

Fang Yuan drained his cup in one gulp amid cheers and applause.

# Chapter 161: Willingly exploited

Only then did the others take their seats. Classmates with strong backgrounds, such as Mo Bei and Chi Cheng, were absent.

"It is getting late; I must depart. This was a well-organized meetup," Fang Yuan said, preparing to leave.

Overjoyed by the praise, Gu Yue Ding Zong quickly stood and pulled a money bag from his pocket.

Bowing with a smile, he said, "Hearing Lord's wisdom today was truly enlightening. Please accept this humble token of my gratitude."

Fang Yuan did not refuse, smiling faintly as he accepted the money bag.

Next, a second and third classmate came forward to offer gifts of primeval stones.

"Alright, alright." Fang Yuan smiled as he accepted them all.

Seeing dozens of heavy pouches, Gu Yue Ding Zong quickly ordered several servants to carry the load for Fang Yuan.

In moments, Fang Yuan gained nearly ten thousand primeval stones!

Lastly, Fang Yuan stood and raised his cup once more, "Our encounter is fated, and our bond as classmates is cherished. Let us drink to this."

Lacking strong backgrounds, they feared retribution from the new elder while yearning to enter his social circle.

"This is the allure of the system," Fang Yuan mused, eyes glinting as he gazed at the wine in his cup.

Back then, extorting a few primeval stones had provoked public outrage.

Now, without a single word, people eagerly competed to present him pouches containing over a hundred primeval stones each!

This contrast seemed like the result of his elder status, but fundamentally stemmed from moving from outside the system into the clan's upper management.

Within the system, members offered themselves willingly to exploitation—bribing unprompted, leveraging connections to enter his faction, or using charms to draw closer.

"Everyone, please drink up!" Fang Yuan downed the wine in a single gulp.

Everyone toasted him hastily, not daring to leave a single drop.

"Farewell." Fang Yuan cupped his fists and took his leave, followed by servants carrying the primeval stones.

"Place the items on the table and you may leave," Fang Yuan said.

The servants quietly set down the pouches, bowed to Fang Yuan, and withdrew.

After advancing to elder, the clan allocated him a brand new bamboo building.

"Tusita flower, come out."

With a mental command and a surge of white silver primeval essence, the tusita flower tattoo on his tongue awakened.

He opened his mouth, and with a red glow, the tusita flower spun like a lantern and floated before him.

Under the red light, a formless attraction drew every primeval stone from its pouch into the tusita flower.

Once the light faded, Fang Yuan swallowed the tusita flower, which settled onto his tongue as a red flower lantern tattoo.

"This tusita flower is a Rank three Gu capable of storing primeval stones and items. Among Rank three storage Gu, it is exceptional, holding up to thirty thousand stones. Considering other supplies I must store, the practical limit is around fifteen thousand primeval stones."

"First I borrowed three thousand primeval stones from Chi Lian, and with today's earnings, I do not have to fret over primeval stones for a while. In the six classifications, I have the Blood Moon Gu and Sky Canopy Gu for attack and defense, Thunderwings for movement, tusita flower for storage, and Earth Communication Ear Grass for recon. I only lack a healing Gu," Fang Yuan calculated.

"Among Rank three healing Gu, Endless Vitality Gu sustains healing with little primeval essence, making it ideal for low-aptitude Gu Masters like me. The greater the strength, the faster injuries recover." But how could Fang Yuan obtain any of these three Gu?

The only hope lies in the Flower Wine Monk's inheritance.

Yet chances were slim; Fang Yuan sensed the inheritance was nearly exhausted. How likely was it to contain the exact Gu he needed right at the end?

"Even so, I must finish exploring. At the very least, I must subdue that Chainsaw Golden Centipede," Fang Yuan thought.

# Chapter 162: Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus

Although Fang Yuan intended to investigate the inheritance ground, his elder status drew watchful eyes, and the endless wolf tide battles left him no chance to slip away.

Over ten days passed before he could return to the rock crack's secret cave.

"After my rebirth, the wolf tide's timeline shifted. In my memory, the thunder crown wolf arrived three days ago, yet now it is nowhere to be seen."

The grey ash covering the cave entrance showed no footprints, confirming the place remained undiscovered.

He traversed the tunnel into the second secret room, then pushed open the rock door into the rock forest.

In the rock forest, jade eye stone monkeys had reoccupied the path he previously cleared.

Spending six hours, Fang Yuan slaughtered nearly ten monkey groups to reopen the path.

Reaching the third secret room, a rock door blocked his path, carved with: "Golden Centipede's cave is precarious, Earth Communication is the way to avoid the disaster."

This time, he unhesitatingly opened the door and strode into the darkness.

The cave was three meters high and two meters wide, with numerous narrow branches sprawling in all directions.

At Rank three, Sky Canopy Gu easily withstood the common centipedes; his sole concern was their king, the Chainsaw Golden Centipede.

Fang Yuan deliberately leaked white silver primeval essence into the air, radiating a Rank three aura.

Sensing a territorial threat, the Chainsaw Golden Centipede viewed Fang Yuan as an encroaching beast to be eliminated immediately.

In the firelight, the Chainsaw Golden Centipede's exoskeleton gleamed eerily. Silver sawteeth lined its flanks, buzzing into motion like a chainsaw as it drew near.

Unfazed, Fang Yuan activated Sky Canopy Gu, coating himself in thick white crystal armor.

Three steps away, Fang Yuan cut off the primeval essence leakage, instantly weakening his aura.

Sensing this, the centipede surged forward like a golden line.

In an instant, it closed the gap and coiled around Fang Yuan's stomach.

Fang Yuan swiftly grabbed the golden centipede's head with both hands.

As the centipede thrashed, even Fang Yuan's two-boar strength felt strained.

A powerful grinding and slashing force bore into the Sky Canopy Gu's white light.

Sparks flew as the saws ground against the armor, rapidly depleting Fang Yuan's white silver primeval essence.

With only 42% initial-stage light silver primeval essence, he could not sustain such heavy expenditure.

Yet Fang Yuan remained unfazed; though pinned, he held a trump card.

With a thought, the shadow of the Spring Autumn Cicada emerged from his aperture.

The moment its aura leaked, the violently thrashing centipede surrendered immediately.

As a mere Rank three wild Gu, it dared not stir before the aura of the Rank six Spring Autumn Cicada.

Fang Yuan smiled faintly and poured white silver primeval essence into the docile centipede, effortlessly erasing its wild consciousness.

Within breaths, the Chainsaw Golden Centipede was fully refined.

Releasing his grip, Fang Yuan watched the centipede slither rhythmically over his armor and coil obediently around his arm.

The surrounding centipedes ebbed away like a receding tide.

Draping the centipede across his shoulder, he ventured deeper into the cave.

Using Earth Communication Ear Grass, Fang Yuan bypassed dead ends and took the left path.

Along the left path, retreating centipedes revealed a cavern containing fresh clues.

"Signs of human construction!" Fang Yuan's heart stirred.

Evidently, Flower Wine Monk had carved this tunnel using the Thousand Li Earthwolf Spider.

The tunnel stretched longer than expected; he walked over three li across twelve hours.

"Water sounds... could it be?" An idea struck him.

At the end of the tunnel, he saw a crystal wall.

Within the water, an ash-gray stream swirled in a perpetual vortex like a miniature tornado.

"As expected, a natural essence spring!" Fang Yuan's attention was piqued.

A flower bud alternating in blue and white hues drifted leisurely in the spring water.

"This... is actually the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus!" Fang Yuan was shocked!

# Chapter 163: Thunder Crown Wolf

Gazing at the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus, Fang Yuan pieced together what had happened.

Years ago, the demonic Gu Master Flower Wine Monk arrived at the village alone.

Having obtained the recipe for Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus, he lacked only a natural spirit spring to refine it.

Though he slew the fourth-generation clan leader and felled most elders with formidable strength, he was infected by the Moonshadow Gu.

He used the Thousand Li Earthwolf Spider to tunnel here, using his prepared materials to refine the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus in the spirit spring.

Though currently Rank three, it could advance into the Rank six Heavenly Essence Treasure Imperial Lotus—ranked sixth among the Top Ten Great Immortal Gu.

"Carrying this Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus would be like having a miniature spirit spring. Though its Rank three stone production cannot match a true spring, it is plenty to sustain my cultivation!"

"However, its fusion process shifts between ethereal and physical forms, invisible to normal sight and visible only through crystal. It must be nourished in the spirit spring for nine days and nights until nine complete leaves grow before it can enter the aperture's Primeval Sea. If one picks it up before the time is ripe, all their efforts would be completely wasted."

Peering through the crystal wall, Fang Yuan noticed it had only eight full leaves and a damaged half-leaf—half a leaf short of completion.

In response, the lotus began replenishing the damaged spring at the expense of its own foliage, damaging its ninth leaf.

"It requires nine full leaves to be plucked. With only eight and a half, I must throw primeval stones into the spring!"

"This crystal wall must be formed by a Moat Gu..." Fang Yuan touched the wall; like an illusion of light, his hand passed straight through.

The stone passed through into the spring, striking the lotus's phantom form as blue and white ripples spread across it.

Fang Yuan threw stones while silently counting; after five hundred, the damaged leaf finally grew slightly.

"Over fifty thousand primeval stones... I only have ten thousand, leaving me forty thousand short."

The real issue lay elsewhere: harvesting the lotus would ruin the spirit spring, triggering a frantic, furious investigation by the whole clan.

"I cannot give up on this lotus, even without the recipe. But taking it now would poke a hornet's nest and bring calamity."

"Without new evidence, speculation is pointless. I should return to the village." Shaking his head, Fang Yuan retraced his steps.

As he exited the rock crack, piercing wolf howls echoed continuously.

Fang Yuan concealed his figure and climbed a hill.

Endless wolf packs surged toward Gu Yue village like a flood.

At the rear of the pack loomed a lightning wolf as large as a small hill.

Myriad beast king: Thunder Crown Wolf!

Tall and lean with powerful limbs, its body was covered in eerie blue scales, with golden fur tufting its claws and tail.

"The myriad beast king has arrived; the village's survival hangs in the balance." Fang Yuan watched countless Gu Masters battling desperately against the charging wolf tide.

Suddenly, a dozen figures shot out from the village, charging through the wolf tide toward the Thunder Crown Wolf.

They were all clan elders, led by Clan Leader Gu Yue Bo!

# Chapter 164: Intense Battle

Fighting the thunder crown wolf required striking first; letting such monstrous power enter the village would cause devastating losses.

Drenched in blood, the elders pressed forward without losing anyone, though all were injured.

Clearing all obstacles, they charged straight at the thunder crown wolf.

"Continue fighting!" Gu Yue Bo roared.

On signal, five elders swung their arms, launching moonblades skyward.

Another elder roared as his muscles tripled, transforming him into a white-furred giant ape.

Leaping to the front, he clasped his hands together.

Gu Yue Bo stepped onto his palms, and with a heave, the ape hurled him high into the sky.

Moon-invite Gu.

Gu Yue Bo spread his left hand, radiating a whirlpool of hazy purple moonlight.

The rising moonblades were instantly absorbed into this purple moonlight.

With flashing eyes, Gu Yue Bo thundered and cleaved downward with both palms.

Amid wind and thunder, a purple moonblade larger than a carriage flew toward the thunder crown wolf.

Deceptively swift, the moonblade struck its target in a split second.

The wolf howled as lightning armor enveloped its body at the last second.

A tremendous explosion erupted as eerie blue lightning clashed with alluring purple moonlight.

As the glare faded, the Gu Masters were already engaging the thunder crown wolf.

Blood poured from a massive gash on the wolf's right front leg, torn open by the purple moonblade.

Yet the advantage was fleeting; the wolf adapted as its wounds continuously healed.

Fifteen minutes in, the wolf howled skyward, cloaking itself in lightning as its speed doubled.

Unable to react in time, the white ape elder was bitten, his skull ripped in two.

Breaching the line, the wolf swept its tail, sending arcs of blue lightning that forced the Gu Masters into retreat.

At this perilous moment, Clan Leader Gu Yue Bo stepped forward.

As a Rank four expert with formidable offense and defense, he anchored the line to salvage the situation.

The battlefield grew more desperate. Deep gashes exposed the wolf's bone, yet it fought on through relentless bleeding. At the same time, the Gu Masters suffered heavy losses, already losing six clan elders. Only immediate reinforcements from the village kept the battle from collapsing.

"Hold strong, endurance is key!" Gu Yue Bo bellowed hoarsely, fighting with bloodshot eyes to rally morale.

Suddenly, the thunder crown wolf went berserk, enshrouded in bright red light.

Rank four Berserk Gu!

The wolf's strength, speed, and agility instantly doubled.

With a loud crash, its right claw pulverized a clan elder into meat mush.

Gritting his teeth nearly to breaking, Gu Yue Bo yelled, "Trap it, use the Iron Chain Snake!"

"Wind Snare Gu!" an elder cried, blowing green wind from his nostrils to bind the wolf's claws.

"Swamp Gu!" another roared, slamming both hands onto the ground to turn the earth beneath the beast into mud.

These simultaneous attacks momentarily slowed the thunder crown wolf.

Flung outward, the snakes wriggled across the ground and swiftly climbed the wolf's body.

Interlocking head to tail, they formed a rooted metal net, trapping the thunder crown wolf in place.

But this situation was only temporary, for as the thunder crown wolf struggled continuously, one by one, the iron chains would break off. In just five to six minutes, these metallic snakes would completely disintegrate away, no longer trapping the thunder crown wolf.

"Chi Guang, Suo Ping, defend the chains from the wolf pack! Everyone else, fall back to the village with me!" Gu Yue Bo shouted, ordering a retreat.

They hurried back to the village, where a clan elder immediately met them: "Lord clan leader, everything has been prepared!"

Gu Yue Bo nodded, ordering the group toward the clan pavilion.

Nearly a hundred Gu Masters were already seated in the plaza before the clan pavilion.

Gu Yue Bo and the elders entered the clan pavilion's ancestral hall.

Kneeling before the ancestral tablets, Gu Yue Bo spoke, "Great ancestor, we descendants are deeply ashamed! The wolf tide rages, and the village faces life and death. We beseech our great ancestor to aid us!" As his low voice faded, the ancestral hall fell silent.

When the first generation leader felt death approaching, he departed the village, leaving a testament: if the clan ever faced destruction, they could pray before his memorial tablet.

# Chapter 165: Blood Guillotine

"Why did they retreat?" Watching from the slope, Fang Yuan grew suspicious.

In his previous life, his cultivation had been too low to learn the clan's secrets.

Soon he froze, spotting a swarm of flying worms descending from the sky.

Nearly a hundred Gu worms clustered into a red cloud, descending upon the village square.

Hearing the swarm's buzz reach the ancestral hall, the elders looked up in wild joy.

Stepping onto the stairway outside, they saw a red tornado rampaging across the village square.

These fingertip-sized Gu were shaped like locusts, crimson all over with hideous faces.

They burrowed into the seated Gu Masters, draining blood and primeval essence. Within breaths, each insect multiplied.

Once sated, the flying Gu soared back up with an imposing presence, multiplying tenfold in the blink of an eye!

Leaving piles of white bones on the square, the Gu swept out like a tornado, rampaging through the wolf packs outside.

"The Blood Guillotine indeed..." Fang Yuan thought, watching from the slope.

The Blood Guillotine was an unusual Rank five Gu whose use and feeding were fused into one.

They specialized in consuming a Gu Master's vital origin blood, multiplying from one to two, two to four after feasting...

Tens of thousands of Blood Guillotines swept the battlefield like a tornado; everywhere they went, wolf packs wailed in agony.

Darting back and forth, they burrowed into the lightning wolves and completely drained their blood within breaths.

Yet beast blood could not make them multiply; only a Gu Master's origin blood containing primeval essence could.

The steel web shattered, and the freed thunder crown wolf howled in fury.

Detecting it, the Blood Guillotines quickly formed a large red cloud, completely engulfing the thunder crown wolf.

The wolf swung its tail, unleashing blue lightning that burned thousands of Blood Guillotines to a crisp.

Though a Rank five Gu, without a master the Blood Guillotine could only attack head-on. The thunder crown wolf housed several Rank four Gu, including wide-area attacks that countered them.

During the fierce clash, many Blood Guillotines fell, but they breached the wolf's armor and burrowed in to drain its blood wantonly.

Left with no choice, the thunder crown wolf discharged lightning plasma over its own body to electrocute the insects.

This fried its surface muscles, emitting the aroma of cooked meat.

The thunder crown wolf broke free, howling as it fled at top speed.

The leader's flight broke the pack's morale, sending the other lightning wolves fleeing in all directions.

The scattered Blood Guillotines circled high above the village once before vanishing into the clouds.

Yet wolves bore grudges. Unless this myriad beast king died, it would return during the next wolf tide, even shrewder and stronger after years of recovery.

It was currently at its weakest. Slaying it and harvesting its Gu worms could recoup some of the clan's losses.

"Yao Zhong, Ge Yan, stabilize the village. Other elders, pursue the thunder crown wolf with me!" Gu Yue Bo ordered, leading seven elders in pursuit.

Fang Yuan's eyes shone as he weighed his options: "The clan is weakened but not chaotic. The Blood Guillotine's origin is suspicious—I should follow the leader and see."

With that, he entered stealth and descended the slope.

Severely injured, the thunder crown wolf moved slowly. After half an hour, Fang Yuan heard fighting and shouting ahead.

Stealthily scaling a boulder, he watched the Gu Masters fight.

The thunder crown wolf lay gasping on the ground, bleeding heavily from fresh wounds, its dimming eyes close to death.

"Old geezer Bai, you have some nerve trying to take advantage of our situation!"

"Hehehe, Gu Yue Bo, we stopped this thunder crown wolf. Get a clue and fuck off!"

Gu Yue Bo and the Bai clan leader stood deadlocked, watching each other warily.

Having already repelled their own wolf tide, the Bai clan had rushed over to Gu Yue territory to seize the dying beast.

"Hehehe, Fang Yuan you really came. I've waited for so long!"

Fang Yuan looked up to see a youth in white descending, resolutely slashing an iceblade toward his face.

# Chapter 166: Second Battle versus Bai Ning Bing

Snowy white hair, blue eyes, a one-armed figure in white.

Unflinching, Fang Yuan leaped backward and poured primeval essence into the Sky Canopy Gu, forming protective white armor.

Bam! The iceblade slammed down, erupting a cluster of white icicles from the ground.

"Hehehe, Fang Yuan, why do I feel such an urge to kill you every time we meet? Tell me!" Bai Ning Bing shivered in manic excitement.

"Lord Bai Ning Bing, let me assist you!" A nearby Bai clan Gu Master shouted, striking at Fang Yuan.

"Clan leader, it's Fang Yuan!" the Gu Yue Gu Masters shouted.

"I know." Dodging the Bai clan leader's strike, Gu Yue Bo hesitated before shouting, "Secure the materials first!"

Fang Yuan's disappearance had raised deep suspicions, and under the Bai clan's pressure, no elders came to his aid.

Enraged by the intrusion, Bai Ning Bing roared, "Scram, this is my battle, don't interfere!"

"Lord Bai Ning Bing, with your lost arm you are vulnerable. You are our clan's hope—let me clear this obstacle for you!"

As the mud arm grabbed for him, a dark golden ribbon of light shot from Fang Yuan's aperture, wrapping around his body.

"What Gu worm is this?" Bai Ning Bing looked on in surprise.

Over a meter long and two fists wide, the centipede bore a dark golden exoskeleton and razor-sharp side blades shining with cold light.

Chainsaw Golden Centipede!

Fang Yuan appeared to wield a giant, hiltless dark-golden sword lined with silver blades.

"Have a taste," Fang Yuan smirked, injecting primeval essence into the Chainsaw Golden Centipede.

The centipede's razor blades spun violently, emitting a terrifying roar that chilled onlookers to the bone.

With a crisp sound, a pair of eerie blue thunderwings formed on Fang Yuan's back.

Sensing death, the man shrieked and triggered his strongest Gu, cloaking himself in thick yellow light.

Before the yellow light armor could fully materialize, Fang Yuan closed the distance.

Exerting his muscles to the limit, Fang Yuan unleashed the full strength of two boars.

The centipede blade swept like a golden shadow, its spinning razors slashing toward the Bai Gu Master's waist.

The unfinished yellow light armor broke under the chainsaw's onslaught, instantly scattering.

The spinning razors shattered the armor and tore through his waist, severing his spine and pulpifying his entrails in spraying blood.

This gruesome scene paused the clash between both clans, drawing shocked and angry stares toward Fang Yuan.

"What a great Gu worm!" Bai Ning Bing shouted, eyes ablaze with manic excitement.

"Noisy fucker." Cold as ice, Fang Yuan turned to counterattack.

The Chainsaw Golden Centipede struck the iceblade, its spinning razors shattering it to pieces.

Breaking through the iceblade, the Chainsaw Golden Centipede swept across Bai Ning Bing's face.

Bai Ning Bing's expression changed; letting go of the blade, he leaped back nearly two meters.

Contracted, the centipede was only 1.5 meters, falling just short of Bai Ning Bing. But with a thought from Fang Yuan, it suddenly extended.

The centipede's tail struck Bai Ning Bing's chest, tearing a deep gash despite his ice muscles.

The massive force sent Bai Ning Bing flying backward into a huge boulder.

Since his battle with Gu Yue Qing Shu, Bai Ning Bing had stopped suppressing his cultivation.

Yet despite his full cultivation, the one-armed youth was suppressed by Rank three initial stage Fang Yuan!

"KILL!" he growled, channeling primeval essence into the Iceblade Gu to forge a new weapon.

Empowered by the Boar Gu, Fang Yuan wielded the heavy centipede as lightly as a tree branch.

Crucially, the golden centipede extended, shrank, straightened, and coiled at Fang Yuan's will, leaving Bai Ning Bing struggling to adapt and defend.

"Good on you, Fang Yuan!" Bai Ning Bing shouted after several blades shattered, realizing he could not face him head-on.

Several ice birds flapped their wings toward Fang Yuan.

Unflustered, Fang Yuan swung his left arm; blood-colored moonblades intercepted the ice birds, detonating them in mid-air.

Just as Bai Ning Bing sought to press his slight advantage, Fang Yuan sneered and released the Chainsaw Golden Centipede to burrow into the earth.

Stunned, Bai Ning Bing cursed loudly and scrambled backward.

# Chapter 167: A fourth one?!

"You forced my hand!" Bai Ning Bing gritted his teeth, forming an iceblade.

Simultaneously, he activated the Swirl Gu and Fierce Wind Gu.

The white iceblade storm appeared once again!

The gust rapidly swelled into a roaring blizzard. Hard ice coated the ground, preventing the Chainsaw Golden Centipede from emerging to attack.

Fang Yuan's thunderwings flapped, pulling him far away from the iceblade storm.

Eyes gleaming, Fang Yuan banked in mid-air and flew toward the valley.

The iceblade storm chased him relentlessly.

Fang Yuan crooked his fingers, and a long golden shape leaped from the earth onto his right arm like a greatsword.

Centipede blade in hand and thunderwings crackling, Fang Yuan charged toward the valley with the mountain-sized blizzard in relentless pursuit.

Ignoring him, Fang Yuan rushed straight toward the dying thunder crown wolf.

The thunder crown wolf turned to Fang Yuan, lightning gathering on its fangs; the pride of a myriad beast king refused to perish without retaliating!

Just before crashing into the wolf, Fang Yuan pulled up sharply, rocketing out of the valley.

Just as he cleared the valley, a violent explosion erupted behind him.

The thunder crown wolf's howls merged into the roar of the iceblade storm.

Already near death, the beast could resist no longer. Its armor and flesh were shredded, exposing bones that were quickly pulverized.

Beams of light shot out as wild Gu worms fled the thunder crown wolf's body.

"Catch them!"

Chaos erupted as elders hindered each other, with only a few able to pursue the fleeing Gu.

As wild Gu scattered, Fang Yuan focused intently on identifying them.

"What a pity Earth Communication Ear Grass doesn't enhance vision. There's definitely a healing Gu here, but catching it depends on luck!"

Spreading his thunderwings, he darted toward the nearest Gu worm.

Drawing near, he spotted the turtle-shelled, light-blue Thunder Shield Gu.

Just then, a white figure holding an iceblade charged from the valley, shouting Fang Yuan's name.

Sighing at his poor luck, Fang Yuan took the next best option and grabbed the nearby Thunder Shield Gu.

The Thunder Shield Gu manifested an eerie blue lightning shield to resist his grasp.

Fang Yuan sneered, releasing the Spring Autumn Cicada's aura. Subdued instantly, the Thunder Shield Gu dispersed its shield and fell toward the forest.

Snatching it, Fang Yuan channeled primeval essence and refined it instantly.

Stealth Scales Gu!

Ripples washed over his body as he vanished on the spot.

"Fang Yuan!" Bai Ning Bing shouted, eyes crackling with lightning.

This was the Rank three Lightning Eye Gu, using lightning to pierce stealth.

Lacking X-ray vision, it could not pierce physical cover. Fang Yuan used the trees to hide his stealthed form, leaving Bai Ning Bing growling in frustration after scanning the area.

Finding a secluded spot, Fang Yuan sat down and made the tusita flower spit out primeval stones.

With his primeval essence down to three percent, Fang Yuan used primeval stones to rapidly replenish his aperture.

Battle sounds echoed outside. After eight or nine minutes, Fang Yuan fully replenished his aperture's primeval essence.

Emerging from hiding, he saw that five clan elders had died.

Elsewhere, three groups fought around the wild Gu worms.

The clan elders surrounded the three Gu worms, attacking and blocking them while facing interference from the opposing clan, locked in a stalemate.

Just as he prepared to attack, a rhythmic wolf howl sounded like a war horn, rising and falling in succession.

Soon after, countless lightning wolves rushed down like a tide.

Amid the cries of bold and frenzy lightning wolves, a mountain-like giant wolf made everyone's expression change.

Thunder crown wolf!

"There's a fourth thunder crown wolf?!" the clan elders shouted, losing all composure.

# Chapter 168: Lightning lycan

Wolf packs were on the move, rushing in like the tide.

The situation worsened, and the clan leaders and elders paled.

At once, both clans subconsciously stopped battling.

This thunder crown wolf had well-developed hind legs and shrunken forelegs, causing it to bound forward on its hind legs like a kangaroo.

"Wait, this doesn't seem like a thunder crown wolf..." the Bai clan leader shuddered, realizing something.

"Lightning lycan!" Fang Yuan had an answer already.

The lightning lycan was a myriad beast king with human intelligence, far more dangerous than ordinary wolves.

"Go!" Fang Yuan spread his wings and flew into the sky.

Just then, the lightning lycan growled and opened its maw.

Between its dagger-sharp fangs, a black mass materialized and condensed into a sphere.

The black sphere shot out in an arc and slammed into the ground.

The sphere exploded on impact, sending thick black mist spreading everywhere.

Rank four Wolf Smoke Gu! The thick smoke spread blindingly fast, completely engulfing Fang Yuan.

Plunged into pitch-black darkness, he was choked by suffocating smoke that made breathing agonizingly uncomfortable.

Fortunately, with Thunderwings Gu, he could escape the wolf smoke simply by flying upward.

Next moment, a lightning bolt ripped through the smoke like a raging dragon, tearing across a hundred li to strike Fang Yuan head-on.

At this crucial moment, Fang Yuan's battle instincts reacted faster than thought.

Blinding lightning roared like a heavenly dragon, slamming into the lightning shield.

The shield shattered in less than a second under the lightning's immense force.

Lightning struck Fang Yuan; even with shut eyes, piercing light stabbed through his eyelids.

Thunder Shield Gu was destroyed, Thunderwings Gu crippled, and Sky Canopy Gu heavily damaged as he crashed down.

Surrounded by thick dark smoke, Fang Yuan lost his sense of direction.

"Careful! That thunder crown wolf shrank to normal size and is hiding in the pack!" an elder shouted through the smoke.

The lightning lycan was crafty and sinister, aiming to slaughter all the Gu Masters without letting a single one escape.

The surroundings quickly shone with countless wolf eyes.

While darkness blinded the Gu Masters, the wolves were unaffected, possessing keen vision at the expense of smell.

"I must escape quickly. Whether I face the lightning lycan or get besieged by wolves, my remaining 40% primeval essence cannot sustain this expenditure!"

Weighing his options, Fang Yuan activated Earth Communication Ear Grass.

A cacophony reached him: wolf howls, combat noises, frantic shouts from elders, and dying whines of wolves.

Fang Yuan frowned, only listening to a direction with less noises.

He soon encountered a pack of over a hundred wolves.

Fang Yuan summoned the Chainsaw Golden Centipede and activated the Sky Canopy Gu.

The centipede's razors whirred like a great sword, cleaving through the smoke and shredding wolves into a rain of blood and bone.

Encountering endless wolf packs in the dark, Fang Yuan realized the lycan had surrounded them.

Enveloped in pitch darkness, he was blind as wolf packs surged from all directions, overwhelming him alone.

"Gu Yue Bo, what do you say?" the Bai clan leader called through the dark smoke.

"Alright, let's cooperate and break out first!" Gu Yue Bo yelled back.

A wolf howl and a violent explosion cut through; the two leaders shouted in disarray, unable to rally the elders.

Evidently, the lightning lycan had launched an ambush.

With human cunning, its strike disrupted the leaders' coordination and shattered the elders' organized resistance.

"I can't continue like this—my primeval essence is draining too fast, I need allies!" Fang Yuan felt exhausted from the slaughter.

Running low on primeval essence and covered in wounds, he had to deactivate Sky Canopy Gu.

The Chainsaw Golden Centipede grew dim, its razors battered after slaying nearly a thousand wolves, including bold lightning wolves.

"I can't hold on much longer!" Fang Yuan sensed death looming, yet the greater the danger, the calmer he became.

"Fighting ahead?" Hearing the clash, Fang Yuan altered course. At his limit, any Gu Master from either clan was useful.

"Kill! Kill! Kill!" a distant Gu Master screamed, fighting fiercely.

Fang Yuan sprinted over, and suddenly came to a stop.

Bai Ning Bing!

# Chapter 169: Finding a path

Bai Ning Bing was battered and bloodied, breathing raggedly in disarray.

Fang Yuan's eyes darkened, assessing: "Though crazy and resigned to his fate, Bai Ning Bing hasn't given up on living."

There were no eternal enemies. Teaming up with Bai Ning Bing was best, but how to convince him?

"Hehehe, Fang Yuan, it's actually you!" Bai Ning Bing laughed aggressively. "Die with me then! Dying alongside you makes for an interesting end."

"Interesting?" Fang Yuan smiled faintly and stepped closer.

"Amidst these wolves, isn't an intense life-and-death battle even more fun?" Fang Yuan closed in, smiling cruelly.

"You truly want to die? Then I'll grant your wish!" Bai Ning Bing narrowed his eyes dangerously.

Fang Yuan laughed, speaking leisurely: "Humans live barely a century, fleeting as a transient dream. What is the purpose of life? Merely walking a journey and witnessing brilliance. I do not seek death, nor do I fear it. Walking my own path, I have no regrets even in death."

Bai Ning Bing heard this, and his entire body shuddered.

"Witnessing interesting things... walking your path... no regrets in death?" Bai Ning Bing muttered, then asked, "Path? What is a path?"

Fang Yuan sneered, stepping closer. "Everyone has their own path; I won't share mine, nor do I know yours."

Bai Ning Bing's blue eyes suddenly shone with a blinding light.

"Path... that's right, I must find my path!"

Looking at Fang Yuan, he said meaningfully, "I finally see our difference. You walk your path, while I'm still searching for mine."

"Hehehe!" He laughed wildly. "Fang Yuan, I'll fight you, but not now! Let's cooperate. My Lightning Eye Gu can only see thirty paces through this smoke. Once we escape, we can fight another day. Teaming up with a mortal enemy—isn't that far more interesting?"

"Oh, how can I believe you?"

"I'm not asking for trust. Cover my back or backstab me—it's entirely up to your mood!" Bai Ning Bing shrugged with nonchalance.

"Fine." Fang Yuan stroked the Chainsaw Golden Centipede's carapace and looked up. "Stay on guard for my strike at any moment!"

"This is south. Going this way leads to Gu Yue village," Bai Ning Bing said, eyes crackling with lightning.

"No, too many wolves there. We must detour," Fang Yuan replied, roots twitching at his right ear.

"Hehe... then how about southeast?" Bai Ning Bing licked his lips.

After listening, Fang Yuan stood up. "There's a gap southeast, but hurry—it's closing!"

They fought side by side, maintaining a wary distance of five paces.

Breaking through the blockade, they emerged into blinding sunlight, squinting at the clear path ahead.

Explosions and shouts still echoed from the smoke where the clan leaders battled the lycan.

"Working with you was surprisingly pleasant," Bai Ning Bing remarked, turning with a faint smile.

"I feel the same," Fang Yuan smiled faintly.

In the next moment, their eyes flashed with sharp intent.

They clashed: the iceblade nicked the golden centipede's carapace before instantly shattering.

Stepping back, their killing intent slowly dispersed.

Fang Yuan's eyes narrowed. "He is doomed to die regardless. My priority is the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus before the village falls."

"Hahaha! I've found my path: to witness this world's brilliance! Fang Yuan, we will fight another day. May your death illuminate my life!"

Leaping backward to open some distance, he turned and departed.

# Chapter 170: Can the blood of Incorruptible Justice be cold?

Fang Yuan watched until Bai Ning Bing was far away.

"Witness this world's brilliance..." Fang Yuan's dark eyes flashed. A clear-minded Bai Ning Bing was more dangerous than ever.

"Fight me later? Heh," Fang Yuan chuckled, "you are bound to be disappointed..."

The moment he escaped the smoke, Fang Yuan resolved to leave Qing Mao Mountain.

"Time to leave. Neither clan can withstand the cunning lycan alone. Unless a Rank five expert intervenes or they ally, destruction is certain."

"This battle decides the mountain's fate. If both leaders fall, wolves will rule. If only one survives, the other clan will be reduced to cannon fodder. Even if the lycan is killed, severe casualties will force the clan to reorganize internally, leading to investigations into me."

"I must return, seize the Heavenly Essence Lotus and a healing Gu from the vault, and depart immediately!"

Turning, he hurried toward Gu Yue village.

Soon, Gu Yue village appeared on the horizon.

"Who are you?" Fang Yuan halted as two strangers blocked his path.

One was a tall older man, steady as a mountain and wearing a distinctive copper mask.

Beside him stood a youth with sword-straight brows and sharp, bright eyes.

"Tie Xue Leng..." Fang Yuan's mind jolted as he recognized the man.

Beneath the copper mask, the amethyst plate on the man's belt was carved with 'five'.

He was the Rank five enforcer and Southern Border's number one detective: Divine Investigator Tie Xue Leng!

"Little brother, please wait," Tie Xue Leng said, cupping his fists.

"Seeing your injuries and haste, have your clan leader and elders run into trouble?" he asked.

Fang Yuan feigned panic. "Who are you? We ran into a lightning lycan, and I'm rushing back for reinforcements!"

"I am Tie Xue Leng. No need to fetch reinforcements—I shall do all I can," Tie Xue Leng volunteered immediately.

Heavily wounded and near primeval essence exhaustion, the two clan leaders sensed death closing in.

The smoke cleared, revealing that every elder had perished; only the two leaders remained.

The lightning lycan was also badly hurt, slyly retreating behind the wolf pack to avoid frontline combat.

Backing away, the lycan howled, commanding ordinary lightning wolves to swarm the two Rank four Gu Masters.

Just as despair gripped them, a distant voice called, "Clan leaders, hold on for a moment!"

The figure flickered effortlessly through the pack, landing beside the two leaders moments later.

"You are?" the two leaders asked together.

"My name is Tie Xue Leng."

Recognizing him, Gu Yue Bo exclaimed joyfully, "So it is Divine Investigator Tie!"

Just as he said this, the wolf packs charged in.

"Foul beasts!" Tie Xue Leng's voice boomed like thunder as he activated a Gu in his aperture.

The wolves halted, limbs trembling under the aura's oppressive weight, not daring to advance.

From the rear, the lycan howled, trying to rally its frozen pack.

"Impudent!" Tie Xue Leng glared, extending his right hand to grasp at the lycan.

Sky rippling, a colossal black iron hand tore through the air, clutching toward the lightning lycan.

Rank five Ironfist Grappling Gu!

The giant hand shattered, but the lycan was gravely injured, its bones nearly pulverized.

Whimpering miserably, the lycan dared not fight again, tucking its tail and fleeing.

Fang Yuan and the young girl arrived just in time to see the iron hand drive the lightning lycan away.

"Dad! Why did you do that? You're injured!" The young girl rushed over, stamping her foot in reproachful concern.

"Ruo Nan, driving it off was best," Tie Xue Leng said, his body suddenly trembling.

Puff! He spurted out blood from his mouth.

His blood held an eerie greenish tint, corroding the grass where it fell with foul smoke.

"Dad, are you okay?!" Tie Ruo Nan quickly raised her hands to heal him.

"No need to fuss. Spitting blood actually makes me feel better," Tie Xue Leng chuckled, waving her off.

The two clan leaders cupped their hands, expressing their gratitude.

"Lord Divine Investigator, please honor Bai village with your presence. I will do my utmost to repay your life-saving grace!" the Bai clan leader said.

Gu Yue Bo smiled proudly, "Lord Divine Investigator comes to investigate Jia Jin Sheng's murder. Our Gu Yue clan will fully cooperate!"

Tie Xue Leng replied, "Indeed. However, my daughter Tie Ruo Nan will lead the investigation, not me."

"Eh?" Everyone present turned to look at the girl.

# Chapter 171: Inbred demonic nature

"What, Father, you want me to solve the case?" She pointed at herself in utter shock.

"Why, are you unwilling?" Tie Xue Leng smiled gently.

"No, no, of course I am!" Tie Ruo Nan beamed, jumping for joy.

Tie Xue Leng nodded. "You aspire to walk my path, and you have learned much traveling with me. I cannot accompany you forever. As the new generation replaces the old, the rest is up to you."

Tie Xue Leng turned to Gu Yue Bo. "Rest assured, Clan Leader Gu Yue, my daughter has mastered eighty percent of my abilities. With my guidance, she will ensure your clan suffers no injustice."

"Of course," Gu Yue Bo cupped his fists. "Please enter our village so we may hold a welcoming feast!"

Gu Yue Bo sat at the head of the table, flanked by Tie Xue Leng and Tie Ruo Nan.

The surviving elders were also present: Gu Yue Chi Zhong, Ge Yan, Mo Chen, Yao Ji, Fang Yuan, and a few others.

"Without Brother Tie's help today, I would have perished. To your life-saving grace, I toast you!" Gu Yue Bo raised his cup.

"I merely did what I could," Tie Xue Leng replied, draining his cup in one gulp.

Accustomed to such scenes, Tie Xue Leng consoled him, "My condolences, Clan Leader Gu Yue. So long as your clansmen endure, the village will thrive once more. That lightning lycan is heavily wounded and the wolf pack decimated; they will not attack for years. The wolf tide is safely weathered."

"Why don't you counterattack and destroy their den?" Tie Ruo Nan asked in confusion.

"The wolf den holds swarms of thunder gas worms and wild Gu. Counterattacking is impossible unless all three clans unite," Gu Yue Yao Ji explained.

She snorted, glaring at Fang Yuan. "Yet that's unlikely when even our own elders cower in fear, hiding god-knows-where during the wolf tide's assault!"

With that, the other clan elders turned their eyes toward Fang Yuan.

Having been exploring the Flower Wine Monk's secret cave, in everyone's eyes Fang Yuan had committed cowardly evasion.

Fang Yuan sipped his wine calmly, completely unfazed by their stares.

"Things are getting troublesome," he sighed inwardly, concerned but unfrightened.

Tie Xue Leng's arrival wrecked his escape plans; taking the Heavenly Essence Lotus would provoke the Ties to arrest him.

Even vanishing mysteriously would trigger an investigation into Jia Jin Sheng's death, drawing in the Ties.

Left with no alternative, Fang Yuan had to stay and observe the situation.

"Is the wine nice?" Gu Yue Yao Ji laughed sinisterly, staring at Fang Yuan, "Aren't you going to explain?"

Fang Yuan set down his cup and looked up at Yao Ji's severed arm.
