"Explain what? How you snapped your own arm just to save your miserable life?" Fang Yuan sneered.

"WHAT?!" Gu Yue Yao Ji leapt up, furious and shocked.

"Gu Yue Yao Ji, sit down!" Gu Yue Bo barked. "We have an honored guest!"

Turning to Fang Yuan, Gu Yue Bo asked, "Elder Fang Yuan, where were you during the wolf tide's assault?"

"Fang Yuan... he's Fang Yuan?" Tie Xue Leng's eyes glinted. According to Jia Fu, Fang Yuan was a key suspect in Jia Jin Sheng's disappearance.

Looking around calmly with a mocking smile, Fang Yuan said, "There isn't much to explain. Since you ask, I will confess: I was terrified. The prolonged stress and overwhelming fear of death broke my courage. Weak and cowardly, I hid in a corner, too terrified to take the front lines."

"This young man, he has an inbred demonic nature!"

# Chapter 172: No one more suited than Fang Yuan!

Clan Leader Gu Yue Bo sighed, "Since you have admitted to desertion, as clan leader, I must punish you. Ancestral rules dictate stripping a deserter's elder status, but the final punishment will be decided after consulting the remaining elders. Whatever the outcome, I expect you to accept it."

Fang Yuan nodded silently, appearing resigned to fate.

"Elder status was only a tool for cultivation. Walking the demonic path toward eternal life, I would discard life itself—what is an elder's title?"

With few elders remaining, Fang Yuan's Rank three strength was indispensable; Gu Yue Bo had to punish him nominally while keeping him appeased.

"What is a system? It is merely a tool to protect the interests of those in power. It divides the benefits of the lower ranks while establishing rules for the rulers' internal competition," Fang Yuan sneered, seeing through it completely.

"Everyone, I have something to say," the pale Gu Yue Mo Chen spoke up.

His next words stunned everyone: "In truth, Elder Fang Yuan failed to reach the battlefield because of me."

"Oh, what do you mean?" Gu Yue Bo asked.

Gu Yue Mo Chen sighed, "My granddaughter Mo Yan begged me in tears not to let Elder Fang Yuan die on the battlefield. Dotingly, I secretly knocked Fang Yuan out and detained him in my estate, only releasing him after the lightning crown wolf fled. The fault is entirely mine. It has nothing to do with Fang Yuan."

"Young love is natural," Gu Yue Bo nodded, searching Fang Yuan's face for clues.

By fabricating this lie to protect Fang Yuan, Mo Chen had sacrificed his own reputation and political prospects!

Gu Yue Mo Chen continued, "Elder Fang Yuan endured the blame to protect my name. I implore the clan leader to consider this when judging. I accept my fault, and will not object if stripped of my elder status."

The clan leader waved his hand, "Elder Mo Chen, your actions are understandable. Weakened as we are, the clan needs elders like you. Even so, punishment must be settled later. Today we have an honored guest, so let us set this aside. Come, Brother Tie, let us toast you!"

"You are too kind. I will need your cooperation later," Tie Xue Leng said without pretension, standing to drink his wine.

In the pavilion, Fang Yuan set down his teacup, expressionless.

Gu Yue Mo Chen sat opposite him, having invited him over immediately after the banquet.

With Gu Yue Mo Bei dead, the Mo faction lacked a successor.

Though Mo Bei had a sister, Mo Yan, ancient clan rules dictated that only males could inherit factional assets.

However, a son-in-law marrying into the family could legitimately inherit the faction!

Seeing Fang Yuan remain silent, Mo Chen asked, "What does Elder Fang Yuan think of my Mo faction?"

His injuries had permanently dropped his cultivation to Rank two, with no hope of regaining Rank three.

He relied on a Gu to conceal his aura, but paper could not hold fire—exposure was inevitable.

With his heir dead and cultivation regressed, Mo Chen urgently needed someone strong to anchor the faction.

After weighing his options, Fang Yuan was indisputably the best candidate!

# Chapter 173: White form immortal snake

Bai Ning Bing had just revealed his Northern Dark Ice Soul physique.

"My aperture can no longer bear the strain; my end approaches," Bai Ning Bing sighed.

"How could this be? Your test showed an A grade, not the Northern Dark Ice Soul," the Bai clan leader protested.

"My aptitude advanced during cultivation. Clan records confirm a ninety-nine percent A grade can advance into a ten extreme aptitude," Bai Ning Bing explained.

"The ten extreme aptitudes defy heaven—even Ren Zu's children met tragic ends. Is there truly no remedy?" the clan leader asked with sorrow.

"I cannot avoid doom, only delay it. But those methods require years of preparation that I lack."

"Do not worry. Who does not die? True immortality does not exist—living brilliantly is enough," he consoled the clan leader.

"I refuse to accept this! Our clan finally found hope—there is still a way!"

"Follow me, I will take you somewhere..." he said, rising.

The bright moonlight shone upon a deep pool.

"This is the spirit spring?" Bai Ning Bing asked after following the clan leader through a long secret passage.

"Indeed. This restricted area houses our spirit spring. A clan's foundation rests on a natural spring, using its primeval stones for cultivation. Bai clan is only mid-sized; only by annexing Gu Yue and Xiong clan to control three springs can we become a large clan," the leader sighed.

"Then clan leader, what did you bring me here for?"

With a conflicted and hesitant look, the clan leader turned to Bai Ning Bing. "I never intended to bring you here, but circumstances are unpredictable. To think you have the Northern Dark Ice Soul! Your sole chance of survival lies in this spring."

"What is in this spring?" Bai Ning Bing asked.

"A Great Immortal!" the clan leader said gravely.

"Great Immortal is a snake Gu left by our first ancestor, dwelling here in secret," the clan leader explained.

"Our first ancestor established a secret inheritance with this snake Gu as the key. If it approves of you, it will open the inheritance. But if not..."

His expression turned grim: "It will kill you!"

At the same time, Gu Yue village.

Inside their room, Tie Ruo Nan retrieved her recon Gu and nodded. "The environment is clean enough."

"Good. What should your next step be, then?" Tie Xue Leng tested her.

Tie Ruo Nan looked outside. "Father, may I show you around the village?"

Having barely survived the fiercest wave of the wolf tide, Gu Yue village lay in ruins.

"Fang Yuan, you bastard, get lost!" an angry roar erupted from a courtyard.

The courtyard gate opened and Fang Yuan emerged with a faint smile.

"Greetings, Little Brother Fang Yuan," Tie Xue Leng greeted him warmly.

"Junior greets Lord Divine Investigator," Fang Yuan said, restraining his smile and cupping his hands.

"We are newcomers unfamiliar with the area. Could you show us around?" Tie Xue Leng asked.

Fang Yuan's eyes narrowed; he had planned to approach them, yet the opportunity fell right into his lap.

"The village naturally owes you full support. Please follow me," Fang Yuan said, leading the way.

The spirit spring was raging like the boiling water.

Surging spring water splashed outward, revealing a slender snake covered in snow-white scales.

Rank five white form immortal snake Gu!

"Greetings to the Great Immortal!" the Bai clan leader knelt reverently, urging, "Bai Ning Bing, kneel down!"

"I will never kneel to a Gu!" Bai Ning Bing snorted coldly, standing tall and proud.

Though the snake radiated chilling grandeur and murderous aura, Bai Ning Bing met its gaze fearlessly with icy blue eyes.

The white snake hissed and shot like a rainbow directly into Bai Ning Bing.

Bai Ning Bing jolted as the light entered his aperture, reforming into the snake.

The white snake took center stage in his primeval sea, arrogantly shoving all other Gu aside.

"That sums up Gu Yue village. The underground cave is a restricted clan area forbidden to outsiders," Fang Yuan said.

Looking at the moon, he added, "It is late. Please rest so you have energy for tomorrow's investigation."

"Hahaha, Elder Fang Yuan is right. Thank you for your guidance, please rest as well," Tie Xue Leng said.

"I will take my leave." Fang Yuan cupped his hands and left.

"Ruo Nan, what do you think of Fang Yuan?" he suddenly asked.

"I dislike him; intuition tells me he is shrouded in shadows." The girl frowned.

Tie Xue Leng nodded. "Solving cases requires solid evidence. Do not rely on Intuition Gu for this case."

"Yes, father."

# Chapter 174: Junior divine investigator

Tie Xue Leng said again, "Now, analyze this person Fang Yuan."

"Father, you suspect Fang Yuan?" Tie Ruo Nan frowned. "When introducing the village, every word was measured and orderly. Gasp!..."

"Fang Yuan speaks too well. Everything was objectively true, devoid of personal feelings—calmly from a bystander's perspective. He left no flaws and gave away no clues. His words were extremely clean."

Tie Xue Leng said, "He didn't abandon his feelings, merely concealed them and controlled his emotions completely. This lad has a demonic nature."

"Demonic nature?"
"At the banquet, he admitted he was too frightened to fight. Would a normal person do that?"

"Right, but who were they?" Tie Xue Leng's gaze deepened.

"Throughout history, those who sacrificed reputation had only two goals: pursuing an ambition where reputation was worthless, or self-preservation to avoid suspicion."

Tie Ruo Nan's eyes shone, "Father, you are saying?"

"You overthink. I simply find him interesting, though pity he is only C grade..." Tie Xue Leng said.

Fang Yuan walked the empty street, his footsteps slightly heavy yet resolute.

Clearing his suspicion before such people was extremely difficult. Given enough time, they would surely discover the truth!

"It is a race against time, but Mo faction's recruitment can be put to good use."

Earlier, Fang Yuan had made absurd demands to marry Mo Yan: a hundred thousand primeval stones and ten rare Rank three Gu.

Thus, the discussion faltered, and he chased Fang Yuan out.

Knowing Mo Chen's desperation, Fang Yuan was sure he would compromise; his absurd demands were merely bargaining tactics.

"More primeval stones can feed the Heavenly Essence Lotus, but Mo Chen sacrificing his standing to protect mine ruins my plan to escape clan politics."

"Riding winds and breaking waves across thirty thousand li—that is my true demonic path!" Fang Yuan looked at the moon and sighed.

The next day.

"What? Jia Jin Sheng's murderer was already found and killed?" Tie Ruo Nan felt extremely perturbed.

"Right, a demonic Gu Master. He attacked our clan's rising genius and was killed on the spot," a clan elder informed them.

"Did he confess to killing Jia Jin Sheng?" Tie Ruo Nan frowned deeply.

"He didn't. But who else could it be?" The clan elder shrugged.

Tie Ruo Nan raised her head. "Where is he buried? I want to inspect the corpse!"

Inside a crude, damaged coffin lay the corpse.

"Anything?" Tie Xue Leng asked lightly, testing her secretly.

"Gu Yue clan preserved the corpse believing he was the killer, but there are major inconsistencies with it," Tie Ruo Nan answered.

"He was a middle-aged man, right arm thicker than the left, with thick calluses on both hands. He also has old injuries, particularly his left foot, which lost three toes years ago."

She deduced, "He was likely a hunter. His uneven arms and hand calluses show he was an experienced archer. Old claw marks and bites show frequent battles with beasts. His clothes aren't typical for Gu Masters, and his shoes are woven from bamboo hemp grass. This grass only grows near Qing Mao bamboo, which is unique to Qing Mao Mountain; none of the surrounding mountains have it."

"So what are you getting at?" Tie Xue Leng pursued.

"Before becoming a demonic Gu Master, he was a hunter. Judging by his clothes, he lived on Qing Mao Mountain," Tie Ruo Nan said, eyes shining.

"Why assume he was a resident? He could have killed villagers and stolen their grass shoes," Tie Xue Leng countered.

"No. Stolen shoes wouldn't fit so perfectly. His feet are disproportionate, yet the shoes fit precisely; they were made just for him. With three missing toes on his left foot, that shoe is shorter. The cleanly cut stump suggests an old hunting trap injury," Tie Ruo Nan said.

Tie Ruo Nan added, "We can investigate the surrounding hamlets for clues... wait!"

She remembered: Qing Mao Mountain had just suffered a wolf tide. If villages were devastated, what became of the hamlets?

"Even if chances are slim, I must try!" Full of drive, this was the girl's first solo case.

After half a day, she returned empty-handed; the wolf tide had left many hamlets with no survivors.

"The lead is gone. What will you do next?" Tie Xue Leng asked.

Gritting her teeth with stubborn tenacity, the girl replied, "No, it's not over. Father, you said real leads are hidden deeper, and digging further will uncover them."

"Why attack Fang Zheng on a suicide mission? And if he was a local, why did no one recognize him?"

Hearing her words, Tie Xue Leng was a little surprised.

"Child, you have truly grown," the divine investigator sighed with gratification.

# Chapter 175: Increasing pressure

"Why did that Gu Master try to kill me? How would I know!" Facing Tie Ruo Nan's questioning, Fang Zheng blinked innocently.

Fang Zheng shook his head. "I'm perplexed too. I was cultivating non-stop back then—how could I provoke an assassination? Others realized he might have been hired by the Bai or Xiong clan to eliminate a rising star like me. You know our rivalry with them. Xiong clan is the prime suspect, having recruited demonic Gu Masters before."

"Xiong clan..." Tie Ruo Nan felt discouraged. Since Xiong clan was wiped out by the wolf tide, this lead seemed dead too.

Soon, news came from the clan leader pavilion.

"Xiong clan still has a large number of survivors."

"They retreated using an ancestral Gu that concealed many people at once, cheating death!"

The Xiong clan envoy's arrival completely shifted Qing Mao Mountain's political landscape.

The supposed two-way rivalry between Bai clan and Gu Yue clan remained a three-way contest.

Once the Xiong envoy departed, Gu Yue Bo immediately convened the clan elders.

The clan elders discussed heatedly until reaching a conclusion.

Gu Yue clan would send an envoy to assess Xiong clan's remaining strength.

If Xiong clan was strong, they would ally with Bai clan; if weak, they would eliminate them and seize their spirit spring.

"Who is suitable to go?" Gu Yue Bo looked around. "Which clan elder will take this responsibility?"

"Someone senior and experienced must go. Among us, only Elder Mo Chen fits this role!" Gu Yue Yao Ji said.

Gu Yue Mo Chen snorted. "Lord Yao Ji has far more experience and fame. We must trouble you instead."

Gu Yue Bo watched coldly from the head seat in silence.

Just then, Fang Yuan stood up.

His words shocked everyone: "Serving as envoy concerns our clan's survival. I am willing to take this responsibility and investigate the Xiong clan!"

Fang Yuan had his plans: going to Xiong clan offered a chance to instigate conflict among the three clans, or at least an opportunity to leave.

"Wait! Anyone can be the envoy except Fang Yuan!" Tie Ruo Nan barged into the room.

Fang Yuan turned. The Tie pair entered followed by his twin brother, Fang Zheng, and Gu Yue Jiang He.

"What brings the Divine Investigator today?" Gu Yue Bo stood to greet him, displeased by their intrusion into an internal meeting.

"Clan Leader and elders, my daughter has identified the demonic Gu Master who assaulted Gu Yue Fang Zheng," Tie Xue Leng said.

"His true identity was Wang Er, a mountain hamlet hunter who became a demonic Gu Master by chance. He assaulted Gu Yue Fang Zheng because of his brother, Fang Yuan!" Tie Ruo Nan stared intently at Fang Yuan.

"Brother, to think you were such a person!" Fang Zheng clenched his fists, eyes blazing with fury.

"What do you mean by this, young lady?" Gu Yue Mo Chen demanded solemnly.

"Did Fang Yuan hire that demonic Gu Master to assassinate his own brother?" Gu Yue Yao Ji asked excitedly. Even Gu Yue Bo shifted in surprise.

"You are mistaken," Tie Ruo Nan said. "Fang Yuan murdered old man Wang's family. Seeking revenge, Wang Er mistook Fang Zheng for his twin."

"Young lady, everything needs proof," a clan elder spoke.

"Of course I have evidence. Gu Yue Jiang He, tell them everything," Tie Ruo Nan said calmly.

Gu Yue Jiang He stepped forward in fear, kneeling on the ground. "It was my dereliction of duty—I beg Clan Leader's punishment!"

Gu Yue Bo's face darkened. "Speak clearly and hide nothing!"

Old man Wang's family had died in Jiang He's jurisdiction. To protect his clan evaluation, Jiang He had covered up the incident upon discovering it.

"The truth is..." Jiang He stammered, recounting the facts truthfully without daring to lie before the elders.

Fang Zheng clenched his fists, growling in fury, "Brother, how could you kill innocent mortals so easily? How could you cut down an old man and a young girl?!"

Fang Yuan remained indifferent to his brother's accusations.

Fang Yuan watched the Tie duo. Uncovering the truth so quickly proved their reputation as divine investigators.

Their skill convinced Fang Yuan that they would eventually uncover Jia Jin Sheng's murder; in this world, Gu could both commit and solve crimes.

# Chapter 176: The resolve to sacrifice

"Elder Fang Yuan, did you massacre old Wang's family?" Gu Yue Bo asked heavily from the high seat.

Under everyone's gaze, the young elder smiled coldly. "It is indeed true."

"Fang Yuan, do you feel no guilt slaughtering innocents?" Tie Ruo Nan frowned, detesting people like him.

"So what if I did? I need not explain myself. I didn't know Wang Er was a demonic Gu Master back then, so Fang Zheng was merely caught in the crossfire," Fang Yuan said frankly.

"Brother, do you have nothing to say to me?" Fang Zheng asked, eyes reddening.

"Comfort you or apologize? Hmph, little brother, you are too soft," Fang Yuan sneered.

"Don't think you're extraordinary just because you're an elder. I already have the qualifications to reach Rank three!" Fang Zheng gritted his teeth.

"Enough!" Gu Yue Bo barked. "Fang Zheng, return! What decorum is there in causing a scene here?"

Sensing the clan leader's veiled dissatisfaction, Tie Xue Leng stepped forward and cupped his hands. "Clan Leader and elders, my daughter was improper to barge into your council. I apologize for the offense."

Tie Xue Leng added, "Since young Fang Yuan is a suspect in Jia Jin Sheng's case, I request he remain in the village and not leave for another."

Gu Yue Bo massaged his temples. "We wish to give Lord Jia Fu an explanation. At the Divine Investigator's request, Elder Fang Yuan must remain until cleared of suspicion. I hope you understand."

"Yes." Fang Yuan looked at Gu Yue Bo expressionlessly.

Tie Xue Leng sighed. "Child, your hatred for evil mirrors my youth. It brings me both joy and worry."

He handed a letter to Tie Ruo Nan.

"This is...?!" Tie Ruo Nan opened it, overjoyed.

Jia Fu's letter detailed Fang Yuan winning the Liquor worm through gambling, passing interrogation under Bamboo Gentleman, and Jia Fu's recruitment offer.

"Fang Yuan is ruthless and calculating. If he truly is the killer, he should show fear. Yet the Bamboo Gentleman detected no lies. How did he bypass it?" Tie Ruo Nan muttered.

"What are you preparing to do next?" Tie Xue Leng asked.

"Much time has passed since Jia Jin Sheng's death, leaving everything shrouded in mystery. His corpse remains unfound and the death site unknown. However, because there are no other clues, I can only rely on my intuition to investigate this Fang Yuan!" Tie Ruo Nan enthusiastically replied.

"You feel that Fang Yuan is suspicious?" Tie Xue Leng asked.

"Extremely suspicious! Fang Yuan has only C-grade aptitude, yet cultivates faster than Fang Zheng even with the Liquor worm and Relic Gu. The Gu Yue clan may not have noticed, but onlookers see more clearly. This is the first suspicious point."

"Then there is his luck. Rock gambling for the first time, he bought six purple gold stones and obtained two live Gu. Isn't that far too lucky?"

Tie Xue Leng nodded, "Yes, continue."

"Fang Yuan appears ordinary, but under scrutiny, he is shrouded in mysteries like mountain fog. Several clues he inadvertently revealed give pause. For instance, he dissected rocks with the Moonlight Gu without damaging the core. Such delicate control is unimaginable for an academy student... wait!"

After a long while, she looked up excitedly. "I found it. Fang Yuan has a huge problem!" ...

Seeing Fang Yuan's leisurely attitude, Gu Yue Mo Chen endured with veins bulging on his forehead.

Circumstances forced his hand. The Yao faction was pressuring him relentlessly, and his drop to Rank two would soon be exposed. In imminent danger, the Mo faction urgently needed an elder son-in-law to stabilize the situation.

"This bastard makes sky-high demands," Gu Yue Mo Chen cursed inwardly, but put on a warm smile. "Elder Fang Yuan, your price greatly exceeds what my Mo faction can support. Can you lower it?"

Fang Yuan glanced at Gu Yue Mo Chen. This old man who knew when to yield deserved respect.

In fact, his current situation was also becoming messed up.

If his murder of Jia Jin Sheng came to light, the clan would surely surrender him to appease the Jia family and preserve trade.

Fang Yuan softened his tone. "Thirty percent off, on one condition: pay forty thousand primeval stones in advance, along with a Pig Iron Gu and an Afterlife Grass Gu."

Gu Yue Mo Chen replied, "I can give the Pig Iron Gu, but we have no Afterlife Grass Gu. The forty thousand primeval stones must be paid in installments."

Fang Yuan knew the old fox was lying, but understood that being too overbearing could backfire.

"Very well. Send them first, then we can discuss marriage. Until then, I guarantee no allegiance." Leaving these words, Fang Yuan walked away.

A long while later, Gu Yue Mo Chen suddenly said, "You can come out."

A hidden door opened, and a young girl stepped out, tear stains on her face and eyes red from crying.

"Grandpa." She paid her respects; she was Gu Yue Mo Yan.

Gu Yue Mo Chen sighed. "You understand our family's situation, Mo Yan. We need your sacrifice—can you understand?"

"Yes." The young girl sobbed and lowered her head.

Gu Yue Mo Yan had already made this resolve. She had no feelings for Fang Yuan, only hatred and disgust, but knew she had to marry him for her family's sake!

# Chapter 177: Brother, how can you do this to me?!

Living Steel Gu was a Rank two Gu resembling a fist-sized, charcoal-black briquette with numerous holes on its surface.

As Fang Yuan injected primeval essence, the Living Steel Gu floated and rotated, spewing black smoke from its holes.

The Chainsaw Golden Centipede coiled around his leg, its dark-gold armor battered and side razor rows tattered.

As the steel smoke enveloped these wounds, they gradually healed.

Now mostly healed, the Chainsaw Golden Centipede's razor rows shone with a sharp, cold gleam, leaving only a few scars.

"This way, its battle strength is completely recovered..." Fang Yuan touched the centipede's cold exoskeleton, his face slightly pale.

Cold sweat formed on his pale face. "Damn it, why now..." Fang Yuan gritted his teeth, his left hand pressing his stomach.

Looking into his aperture, he saw the white silver primeval sea still and the entire space filled with an oppressive weight.

All other Gu were suppressed to one side, while above the sea, the Spring Autumn Cicada glowed with yellow-green luster.

The Spring Autumn Cicada had restored both wings, regaining much of its energy.

Its recovery speed accelerated over time after the initial difficult period.

The Spring Autumn Cicada was Rank six, while Fang Yuan was only a Rank three Gu Master; his aperture was gradually unable to hold it.

When the cicada was weak, the burden was manageable, but as it regained Rank six power, Fang Yuan's aperture could no longer contain it.

"If this continues, the Spring Autumn Cicada will kill me before the Ties uncover the truth! Truly, misfortunes never come singly..."

The best solution was raising his cultivation level; reaching Rank six would allow his aperture to store the Spring Autumn Cicada again.

Raising it outside was dangerous: unable to protect itself, a Rank six Gu manifested outside would also disrupt natural laws and create a mirage.

Fang Yuan was in a crowded village with the Ties watching him. Once the Spring Autumn Cicada left his body, everyone would discover it.

"The cicada's recovery is accelerating. Once I get the forty thousand primeval stones from Mo Chen, I'll take the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus and leave. As for the Ties, I can deal with them later."

He could delay dealing with the Ties, but the Spring Autumn Cicada gave him no time.

"Six fist-sized purple gold stones. At his cultivation level, he opened five on the spot. Where did he get so much primeval essence?" Tie Ruo Nan stared at the letter, laughing smugly.

Tie Xue Leng nodded. "Meticulousness reveals what others miss. What do you deduce?" Tie Ruo Nan closed her eyes and activated the Intuition Gu.

In the darkness, she felt a sudden enlightenment and snapped her eyes open. "Instinct tells me Fang Yuan had the Liquor worm long ago!"

"Finding evidence is simple: a Liquor worm must be fed, leaving traces." Tie Ruo Nan smiled. "Let's go question Fang Yuan's brother, Gu Yue Fang Zheng, again. As his younger brother, he must know Fang Yuan best."

"You're asking about brother's actions back then?" Fang Zheng showed a complicated expression.

He sighed, recalling, "Back then, my brother was outstanding. He displayed talent from young and wrote many poems, drawing the entire village's attention. I held him in awe. When the awakening ceremony tested him at only C-grade talent, he became discouraged—sleeping in class, staying out at night, and constantly buying wine to get drunk. From that moment on, I realized, brother was also human..."

"Wait, you said buy wine?" Tie Ruo Nan caught onto the crucial phrase, squinting.

"Yes, from then on he drank constantly, spending heavily on wine. For a time, he loved bamboo wine, our clan's expensive specialty. He extorted primeval stones from classmates to buy wine. Because of this overbearing behavior, no student liked him. Why, is there a problem?" Fang Zheng asked in confusion.

"A huge problem. I suspect he didn't obtain the Liquor worm from gambling rocks, but had it all along. His drunkenness was merely an act to conceal feeding the Liquor worm," Tie Ruo Nan replied solemnly.

"That makes me even more suspicious. Where did he usually buy wine? I must investigate." Tie Ruo Nan stood up, racing against time to solve the case.

"In our entire village, only one inn sells bamboo wine."

"The innkeeper confirmed everything," Tie Ruo Nan concluded. "Fang Yuan bought so much wine solely to feed the Liquor worm. Afterwards, he gambled on rocks intentionally to expose the Liquor worm plausibly before everyone."

Beside her, Fang Zheng appeared distraught, his face looking lifeless.

"Brother, how can you treat me like this?!"

"Without Lady Tie, I would still be in the dark. How long do you intend to lie to me and the clan? Killing innocents at will, full of deception and indifference—is that the real you?"

# Chapter 178: Black White Grey

"Lady Tie, thank you for revealing the truth. Ask away—I will tell you everything I know," Fang Zheng said.

Tie Ruo Nan nodded, then quickly shook her head. "I understand what I needed to. The question now is: where did Fang Yuan obtain that Liquor worm?"

"You are right. Back then, my brother had not inherited our parents' assets and was only a C-grade novice. Where did he get a Liquor worm?" Fang Zheng frowned.

"There are only two possibilities: outside help or an inheritance. Who would invest in C-grade talent? It must be an inheritance," Tie Ruo Nan said.

"An inheritance legacy explains everything! First, the Liquor worm's origin makes sense. Second, the motive for killing Jia Jin Sheng is established!" Tie Ruo Nan thought excitedly.

"Clan history is kept in forbidden grounds; only clan elders may enter," Fang Zheng replied.

"Is that so... then what can we do now?" Tie Ruo Nan frowned.

"Lady Tie, maybe I can help." From the shadows, a crippled old woman walked out.

It was none other than Gu Yue Yao Ji.

"Come with me into the forbidden grounds. Coincidentally, I am in charge of guarding it right now," Gu Yue Yao Ji laughed sinisterly.

She hoped to harm Fang Yuan, and was ready to frame him if needed.

Inside a hidden room in the underground tunnel, Tie Ruo Nan examined the Gu Yue clan's historical records.

The records detailed the clan's history from its founding over several centuries.

"The first generation clan leader had mysterious origins and founded Gu Yue village singlehandedly. He was likely a demonic cultivator!" Tie Ruo Nan said, flipping through the opening pages.

"That is common," Tie Xue Leng said. "Many weary demonic cultivators settle down and establish clans. Centuries later, their descendants become righteous cultivators."

"Are his crimes simply forgiven? Demonic cultivators commit atrocities, then settle down in old age. Isn't that too easy on them?"

Tie Xue Leng sighed. "When I was young, I also thought the world was black and white. But the world is actually grey. Often black turns to white, and white to black. Sometimes 'white' bears even greater sins."

As the girl continued to flip, her hand suddenly stopped.

"Fourth generation clan leader... Flower Wine Monk? Li... Liquor worm?" Her eyes shone!

Electric currents flowed along sharp, blade-like teeth that shone with cold light.

A frenzy lightning wolf pounced at Bai Ning Bing, its claws slicing shadows through the air.

Bai Ning Bing did not move, showing no intention to dodge as the wolf's claws loomed larger.

Suddenly, a white rainbow shot out from his aperture.

The rainbow burst into a rain of white light, revealing an elegant white snake whose long whiskers exuded an ethereal grace.

Before the Rank five snake Gu, the frenzy lightning wolf shrank back, but the white form immortal snake spewed smoke.

It suddenly opened its mouth, shooting a blue thunderbolt.

The thunderbolt hit Bai Ning Bing on the chest, penetrating it.

Bai Ning Bing looked down at the gaping hole through his chest, seeing right through to behind him.

Quickly, the hole froze over with layers of white ice, which melted and reformed into flesh.

"This is the Northern Dark Ice Soul physique's true power. Fatal injuries heal in seconds," Bai Ning Bing thought, raising his right arm.

His broken right arm had already fully recovered through the Northern Dark Ice Soul physique.

"The white form immortal snake entered my aperture, naturally attracted to the Northern Dark Ice Soul physique. Such strength, yet so brittle!"

Bai Ning Bing sighed at the heavens.

These days, without suppressing his cultivation, the Northern Dark Ice Soul physique grew stronger, making him feel death approaching even faster.

"The great immortal didn't act?" Bai clan leader approached, asking in concern.

Bai Ning Bing shook his head. "The snake hasn't acknowledged me. It moved from the spirit spring into my aperture and emerges only in mortal peril."

"The three clans' negotiations concluded. We agreed on a tournament for Gu Masters aged thirty and below to decide compensation."

"I understand." Bai Ning Bing nodded. "Sensing my life is ending, having a competition at the end makes me glad. Thank you, Lord Clan Leader."

Having found his path, Bai Ning Bing felt no fear—only one lingering battle remained on his mind.

"Fang Yuan, will you attend the three-clan tournament? Don't disappoint me, because right now, I am truly strong..."

# Chapter 179: History is written by men

"Flower Wine Monk begged for mercy. The fourth clan head intended to spare him, but Flower Wine Monk ambushed him and was killed on the spot. Due to heavy injuries, the clan head passed away shortly after. Demonic path members are truly treacherous..."

As Tie Ruo Nan read this, her eyes gradually dimmed.

"What a pity. Flower Wine Monk died on the spot without leaving an inheritance, which would have explained everything," the girl sighed inwardly.

Refusing to give up, she continued flipping through the clan records until time expired and Gu Yue Yao Ji rushed them out.

Fang Zheng greeted the Ties outside the clan pavilion. "Were there any discoveries?"

Tie Xue Leng remained silent, while Tie Ruo Nan smiled bitterly and shook her head.

"My brother worked closely with the Chi faction, and Elder Chi Lian defended him publicly. Could Chi Lian have secretly given him the Liquor worm?" Fang Zheng asked.

"Chi faction?" Tie Ruo Nan frowned.

"If Fang Yuan didn't obtain it from an inheritance, the Chi faction is the prime suspect. But why invest in an ordinary C-grade student?"

"What made Fang Yuan worth their investment? Perhaps Chi faction didn't invest willingly—Fang Yuan blackmailed them!"

"If so, what did Fang Yuan hold over them before he was even a Gu Master?"

"Father," the girl looked up at Tie Xue Leng, "I would like to borrow the Cactus Pointer."

"The Cactus Pointer only deduces from information you possess. Are you sure?" Tie Xue Leng asked.

The young girl nodded.

There were only one word written on the white paper —'Aptitude'.

Meaningless or even ridiculous to others, to Tie Ruo Nan it was a vital clue.

"Right, it is aptitude!" Tie Ruo Nan shouted.

She immediately retrieved the letter recording the intelligence gathered by Jia Fu.

It detailed when and at what price Gu Yue Chi Lian had purchased the Cleansing Water Gu.

"The Cleansing Water Gu has only one use: cleansing foreign primeval essence auras from the aperture."

"Why did Gu Yue Chi Lian need it? Hehe, this is common across many clans. He must have bought it for the Chi faction's inheritor. Chi faction's inheritor must have poor aptitude, so Gu Yue Chi Lian infused his own primeval essence to raise his cultivation, needing the Cleansing Water Gu to purge the aftereffects. Fang Yuan discovered this and blackmailed the Chi faction for the Liquor worm!"

"Lady Tie, you are amazing to deduce so much from a single word! But Gu Yue Chi Cheng clearly has B grade aptitude," Fang Zheng said.

"B grade aptitude? Hehe, can't that be faked? We only need to examine Chi Cheng's aperture to reveal the truth." Tie Ruo Nan's gaze burned as her mouth curved slightly upward.

"That's inappropriate." Tie Xue Leng, however, shook his head disapprovingly.

Tie Xue Leng said, "We came to solve a case, not wreak havoc. As outsiders, we cannot meddle in another clan's politics unless absolutely necessary."

Tie Ruo Nan nodded. "Clans record important rituals with Photo-audio Gu. I want to see the Awakening Ceremony's recording!"

"Divine Investigator Tie, you have toiled for days investigating this case. Please, have some tea. As for the image, it is kept in a secret room within our restricted underground cave. While inconvenient to let you enter, I have already sent someone to retrieve it. It will arrive shortly," Gu Yue Bo smiled.

"I have troubled the clan leader," Tie Xue Leng politely said.

"Divine investigator Tie, if I may be so bold to ask a favor...." Gu Yue Bo suddenly said.

Tie Xue Leng. "Oh? Please speak, clan leader."

"This concerns Qing Mao Mountain's internal affairs. The deadlock between our clan, the Bai clan, and the Xiong clan shifted due to the wolf tide. When compensation negotiations failed, our three clans decided to hold a tournament. Open only to Gu Masters aged thirty and below, it will decide each clan's future strength."

"If Fang Yuan truly is the killer, my clan will not shield him. Yet I hope you can delay action until after the three-clan competition the day after tomorrow."

As a Rank three Gu Master, Fang Yuan was vital fighting strength Gu Yue Bo wanted to extract.

# Chapter 180: Watching the Awakening Ceremony again

"It's about time, I can't wait any longer."

As the night fell, Fang Yuan opened his eyes in a dark room.

Dressed in a long black gown with his hair loose, he looked like a specter in the dark.

From his deal with Mo Chen, he had received the Pig Iron Gu and forty thousand primeval stones.

Fang Yuan sighed, stood up, and slipped out the door, activating the Stealth Scales Gu to vanish into the night.

"The situation is dire; I can only gamble on this method!" Fang Yuan thought, pinning his hopes on the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus.

Plucking the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus would ruin the underground spirit spring. The clan would frantically investigate, but beyond that, what could they do?

There was only one recourse: seize a new spirit spring!

With only three spirit springs on the mountain, destroying theirs would force the clan to seize another.

That meant war—against either Bai village or Xiong village.

It was a perilous gamble, but the Spring Autumn Cicada's rapid recovery was already straining his aperture. Running out of time, he had to fight for a sliver of survival, seeking life in death.

The hall was bright with all the lights.

A Gu Master had delivered the Photo-audio Gu, which Gu Yue Bo now held. "Divine Investigator Tie, what do you think of my request?" Gu Yue Bo asked with a smile.

Tie Ruo Nan lightly snorted.

Tie Xue Leng agreed, "Alright, if Fang Yuan proves to be the killer, I will leave him time to participate in the three-clan competition." "Father..." Tie Ruo Nan's eyes flashed.

"The Divine Investigator's word is gold. I thank you for your understanding." Gu Yue Bo smiled, though he sneered inwardly.

"Clan Leader Gu Yue, my father has agreed. Now show us the image." Tie Ruo Nan's tone showed her displeasure.

"Even without his agreement, I would cooperate fully," Gu Yue Bo replied gently, then crushed the Photo-audio Gu between his fingers.

Like an ink drop in water, a colorful imprint spread across the spotless wall.

The imprint expanded into an image of the past Awakening Ceremony.

Within the image, Fang Zheng quickly spotted himself and many familiar faces.

"That is Fang Yuan..." Tie Ruo Nan likewise soon found him.

Fang Yuan walked with the group, looking around like the others, yet his gaze was tranquil and detached—a crane among chickens. Without paying close attention, ordinary people would notice nothing unusual.

With everyone in the hall focused on him now, this peculiarity stood exposed.

The scene shifted to when the youths reached the sea of flowers.

The Awakening Ceremony began as the youths stepped into the underground river, wading toward the floral sea.

First, Gu Yue Mo Bei tested with B grade aptitude to great uproar, followed by Gu Yue Chi Cheng also testing B grade.

"Gu Yue Chi Cheng looks far too nervous. His movements are stiff and his complexion abnormal, masked only by the dim underground light. Looking closely reveals something applied to his body attracting the Hope Gu. Hehe, a clear cheat." Tie Ruo Nan observed confidently, but her brow quickly furrowed.

When Fang Yuan's turn came, he waded into the river toward the opposite bank.

The Hope Gu shone dimly. The greater the expectation, the deeper the disappointment; sighs from youths and elders echoed through the crowd.

Everything seemed normal, but Tie Ruo Nan's frown deepened—Fang Yuan's expression had remained calm throughout.

It was as if... he already knew what would happen!

"Impossible! A fifteen-year-old bearing everyone's expectations who tests as a mere C grade—how could he not feel the slightest frustration or discouragement? How can this be?!" Tie Ruo Nan's brow knotted.

"Wait, aptitude... Could it be?!" Tie Ruo Nan looked up abruptly, an extremely bold conjecture forming in her mind.

Watching his turn, Fang Zheng recalled the inferiority and weakness he had felt back then.

He saw himself tumble into the river, struggle, and get pulled up by Fang Yuan, cutting a pathetic figure.

Then he saw himself walk dazed toward the opposite bank, enveloped in Hope Gu light amid gasps of shock and admiration.

An A grade aptitude—his life had changed forever from that moment!

"Fang Zheng, I have a question," Tie Ruo Nan spoke, breaking his recollection.

"Please ask. I will answer everything I know," Fang Zheng replied with a smile.

"When your brother pulled you up, his mouth moved, but the noise drowned it out. What exact words did he say?" Tie Ruo Nan asked intently.

"His words..." Fang Zheng tried to recall. "He seemed to say... road?"

"Right, road!" Fang Zheng's eyes brightened. "He said, 'The road to the future will be interesting." Looking back, his words held profound meaning... ...as if he already knew I had A grade aptitude!" "No, he meant something else." Tie Ruo Nan trembled, exhaling with a complicated expression."

# Chapter 181: By an odd twist (Long chapter)

"Lady Tie, what do you mean?" Fang Zheng was confused.

Tie Ruo Nan pointed at the wall. "Look at Gu Yue Fang Yuan! Don't you think he looks far too calm? Learning he had C grade while his brother had A grade, anyone would react. Yet he showed no emotion, unmoved by praise or mockery. Throughout the process, he is too calm."

"Earlier, I assumed the Cactus Pointer's 'aptitude' clue referred to Gu Yue Chi Cheng. Now I realize it likely refers not to Chi Cheng, but to Fang Yuan!" Tie Ruo Nan said.

Tie Ruo Nan raised a finger. "Only an unknown trump card could make him so calm. He feels no jealousy only because he possesses something far greater. If two people walk together and one finds a primeval stone, the other won't envy him if he finds a hundred. Fang Yuan is just like that."

"You mean Fang Yuan concealed his true aptitude and isn't C grade?" Gu Yue Bo asked. "If he is A or B grade, why hide it?"

"Because he knew, he didn't dare reveal it," Tie Ruo Nan answered confidently.

"What do you mean?" Gu Yue Bo was lost.

Tie Ruo Nan turned to Gu Yue Bo solemnly. "Clan Leader Gu Yue, do you know of Ren Zu's ten children?"

Gu Yue Bo was stunned before instantly grasping her meaning.

Ren Zu's ten children possessed heaven-defying aptitudes and short lifespans, representing the ten extreme physiques!

"According to legend, we all carry the bloodlines of Ren Zu and his ten children!" Tie Ruo Nan continued. "In most people, dilution and intermarriage keep them dormant. Yet some are born with stronger bloodlines that dominate through growth and cultivation, giving rise to the ten extreme physiques!"

"Don't tell me, Fang Yuan has one of the ten extreme aptitudes?!" Gu Yue Bo was stunned.

"Very likely! Only a ten extreme physique explains his calm demeanor and indifference to resources. Only an extreme physique explains his lack of envy, and fear of being killed early would force him to conceal the truth!"

Even Tie Xue Leng would never imagine rebirth, let alone his daughter. Through sheer coincidence, she had deduced a conclusion entirely contrary to the truth.

"Yes, the ten extreme physiques... It must be the Ancient Moon Desolate Yin physique!" Gu Yue Bo's heart surged with overwhelming excitement.

His will predicted that the Gu Yue clan would one day produce a Gu Master with the Ancient Moon Desolate Yin physique. The ancestor had instructed that if such a physique appeared, they must be brought to the blood pond graveyard.

That method lay with him in his coffin in the graveyard.

"How could my brother cheat under everyone's eyes?" Fang Zheng shook his head. Tie Ruo Nan countered, "If Chi Cheng could cheat, why couldn't Fang Yuan? Perhaps Fang Yuan awakened early and deceived the Bamboo Gentleman, or killed Jia Jin Sheng to protect this secret."

"Young Lady Tie, speak with caution," Gu Yue Bo warned darkly. "It is not yet confirmed whether Fang Yuan killed Jia Jin Sheng. Fang Yuan belongs to my clan. Even if guilty, how we answer to the Jia family is our affair."

"Damn it. This shameless clan leader went back on his word so quickly! I shouldn't have revealed the ten extreme physiques so soon. A ten extreme physique surpasses A grade talent, so he clearly wants to protect Fang Yuan now. This will severely obstruct the case. What do I do now, Father?" Her heart sank as she looked around for Tie Xue Leng.

Divine investigator Tie was still around earlier, but in the blink of an eye he was gone.

Gu Yue Bo tensed. Where could Tie Xue Leng have gone? The clan leader immediately thought of Fang Yuan.

"Fang Zheng, alert the elders and assemble to find me!" Leaving his instructions, Gu Yue Bo activated a Gu and flew out the window.

Tie Ruo Nan snorted and ran toward Fang Yuan's residence.

In the entire hall, only Fang Zheng was left.

"How... how can this be?!"

"Brother has a ten extreme physique? My A grade talent is nothing compared to him—a total failure!"

He screamed in his heart, "From start to finish, you were just watching me perform like a clown!"

"So what if he has an extreme physique? Brother, you committed murder! You must pay for your sins—you killed Jia Jin Sheng, didn't you? You took the wrong path! What does superior aptitude matter if you did wrong?!"

A flame began to burn in Fang Zheng's heart.

The cold night wind made him shudder. "HEHEHEHE..." Slumped on the ground, Fang Zheng let out a shrill, eerie laugh.

# Chapter 182: Blood pond graveyard

"No one here!" Ransacking the house without finding Fang Yuan, Gu Yue Bo felt a heavy dread. The sudden disappearance of both Fang Yuan and Tie Xue Leng made him link them together.

"Speak, where is Fang Yuan?" Gu Yue Bo demanded with a grim face, shouting at the newly arrived Tie Ruo Nan.

"Hmph, why did Tie Xue Leng go missing? Tie family young lady, explain yourself," Gu Yue Bo demanded, approaching Tie Ruo Nan.

As he approached, the girl raised her head to stare at him. "Explanation? You want one, but why must I give it?"

Gu Yue Bo's face darkened. "Little wretch, talking tough—it seems I must capture you to trade for our Fang Yuan!"

Tie Ruo Nan chuckled. "Gu Yue clan leader, would you dare? My uncle is the current Tie clan leader—do you want to cross swords with my Tie family?"

Her tone softened. "Gu Yue clan leader, I am not here as an enemy. Believe my sincerity. I don't know where father went, but I won't run away. The Tie family only dies in battle; we have no cowards. I will stay, and I will arrest Jia Jin Sheng's murderer!"

The spirit spring swirled violently as a lotus phantom flickered above the water.

"I threw in fifty thousand primeval stones. Why hasn't this Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus revealed its true form yet?"

The Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus was extremely precious; once advanced to Rank six, its value would equal the Spring Autumn Cicada.

"Fifty thousand primeval stones is plenty. Why fret?" Hesitating no longer, he jumped toward the crystal wall.

Fang Yuan knew this well. Having estimated the distance and accounted for water refraction, he reached out according to memory.

The blue-and-white lotus had closed petals and resembled a lamp filled with a holy aura. Possessing its own consciousness, it resisted Fang Yuan's grasp.

Releasing a trace of Spring Autumn Cicada's aura, the Rank three flower Gu was instantly refined.

With a thought, the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus transformed into blue-and-white light, shooting into his aperture.

Without the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus, the whirlpools vanished. The once-vibrant spring turned into a lifeless puddle of stagnant water.

"The spirit spring is gone. It's no longer safe here; I must leave immediately." Fang Yuan's expression turned grave, but just as he moved, an abrupt change occurred!

Deep in the spirit spring, blinding blood-colored light shot out.

A powerful suction force erupted, dragging the caught-off-guard Fang Yuan deep into the water.

The spring water turned bloody, enveloping Fang Yuan completely and dragging him down. Sky Canopy Gu! Thunderwings Gu!

In the crisis, Fang Yuan commanded white armor onto his body while large lightning wings unfurled from his back.

Amid the roar of water, Fang Yuan was swept down the tunnel by rushing currents.

Just when his breath was used up, the surrounding pressure vanished.

Fang Yuan inhaled deeply. Though spared from suffocation, he found himself plunging from a great height.

He reflexively flapped his wings, but the Thunderwings Gu was malfunctioning.

This underground area was filled with red light. Fang Yuan was falling from fifteen meters toward a pond below.

A piercing stench of blood assaulted Fang Yuan as he plunged.

The golden centipede fully extended, plunging its tail into the mountain wall.

Relying on the Chainsaw Golden Centipede, Fang Yuan secured footing on an uneven ledge.

"Where the hell is this?" Stabilizing himself, Fang Yuan immediately surveyed his surroundings.

By his estimation, this lay deeper underground, beneath the cave.

In truth, this was the blood pond graveyard—the forbidden grounds housing the first generation's coffin, known only to one or two clan elders.

Around a hundred holes in the cave ceiling gushed river water, washing down shells, turtles, snakes, and fish.

River creatures swarmed the turbulent blood water, but within breaths, their blood was drained completely. Desiccated corpses bobbed up and down on the rolling waves.

Fang Yuan's pupils shrank. Landing in this pond would be disastrous even with Sky Canopy Gu's protection.

Waves from the blood pond battered the cavern walls, their glowing red water seeping in and dyeing the surrounding earth into bright red soil.

# Chapter 183: Blood Sea Ancestor

"Wait a second, red soil?"

He reached out and scooped a lump of red soil from the wall.

The red soil was very soft and faintly luminous; it crumbled under Fang Yuan's light press.

When he first entered the rock crack's secret cave, it was filled with this glowing red soil, rendering torches unnecessary.

"This place is dangerous. I must find an exit." Fang Yuan looked at the holes above, wondering which connected to the spirit spring.

However, the thunderwings were sluggish, their blue lightning tinged with scarlet.

Thunderwings Gu had become unreliable and could easily fail mid-flight, dropping him.

An undercurrent surged within the blood lake, exuding the immense aura of a Rank five Gu.

Its length surpassed forty meters and its width over six meters.

An enormous python dwelling in the lake depths caught Fang Yuan's scent and emerged to hunt!

Hundreds of black specks surfaced from the blood lake like a schooling shoal of fish.

These were not fish, but bats.

Clawless, their two pairs of wings had edges as sharp as steel blades.

"Rank three—bladewing blood bat Gu?" A name immediately appeared in Fang Yuan's mind.

In the Photo-audio Gu's image, Flower Wine Monk had been drenched in blood and critically injured.

Though a Rank three Gu, it was easily fed solely on blood.

Centuries into his previous life, he had obtained Blood Sea Ancestor's inheritance, using bladewing blood bats to become a regional overlord.

Blood Sea Ancestor was a Rank seven demonic Gu Master who slaughtered effortlessly, his evil reputation echoing throughout history.

He succeeded in creating fusion recipes for bladewing blood bat, blood guillotine, and blood frenzy Gu.

The Rank three bladewing blood bat Gu was easy to feed, and a swarm could be commanded simply by controlling the male bat.

His second success was the Rank four blood frenzy Gu—a formless mass of blood essence that attached to other beings and possessed an unusual ability. Gu infected by its aura could intermittently absorb natural primeval essence. However, infected Gu gradually slipped from control and eventually melted into blood.

Knowing his death was certain, Blood Sea Ancestor used his Rank seven strength to establish hundreds of thousands of inheritances across the Central Continent, Southern Border, and beyond.

"Subduing these blood bats now would bypass centuries of effort."

With Spring Autumn Cicada's Rank six aura, the bats would submit instantly. The only hurdle was the Rank five Blood Python lurking in the lake.

"Blood Sea inheritances usually hold only two or three Gu. Why are there hundreds of bats here?"

"Could this be a true Blood Sea inheritance ground?" Fang Yuan's heart pounded.

As bladewing blood bats swarmed him, Fang Yuan stayed calm and prepared to summon Spring Autumn Cicada.

"As expected, a blood calamity lurks here!" A deep, resonant voice, clashing like iron against stone, echoed down from above the cave.

Divine Investigator Tie Xue Leng!

# Chapter 184: Rank five intense battle

"Tie Xue Leng, why is he here?" Fang Yuan recognized the voice in shock.

"Heaven Earth Magnificent Sound Gu?" Fang Yuan gritted his teeth as the booming voice made his brain buzz.

A loud explosion occurred as blood and water flew everywhere.

The giant python's upper body appeared in Fang Yuan's sight.

"A blood river python... Legend says the first ancestor killed it, but could it be..."

Glancing at Fang Yuan on the wall, the blood river python raised its head toward the cave ceiling.

At the cave top, another tiny silhouette floated down from a ceiling hole.

Wearing a bronze mask with hands behind his back, his imposing aura radiated crushing pressure in all directions.

Righteous Gu!

Oppressed by the aura, the bladewing blood bats shrieked and fluttered erratically under the crushing pressure.

Even the ferocious blood river python lowered its head before Tie Xue Leng's presence.

"What a Righteous Gu!" Fang Yuan sneered from the wall.

"Eh?" Fang Yuan's immunity startled Tie Xue Leng.

Aware of Fang Yuan's crimes, Tie Xue Leng had planted a tracking Gu on him during their first meeting, allowing him to trace Fang Yuan's movements.

"Only those possessing true demonic nature remain unaffected by Righteous Gu despite committing sins. To think Fang Yuan possesses such intense demonic nature! Hmph, I'll slay this greater demon first. If Fang Yuan survives, I will execute him later!"

Despising evil, Tie Xue Leng snorted coldly and turned toward the blood river python.

He had recently received an anonymous letter providing concrete evidence of a blood calamity in Gu Yue village.

"First gen Gu Yue, I know you live. Your thousand-year scheme ends today," Tie Xue Leng spoke, trembling the air.

The blood pond remained still, but the blood river python opened its maw, hissing furiously.

Surging with the blood tides, the massive serpent lunged at mid-air Tie Xue Leng with sky-rending killing intent.

Carried by its immense length and momentum, the python crashed straight into the cave ceiling.

With a loud crash, part of the ceiling collapsed, sending heavy debris tumbling into the turbulent blood lake.

"Do you think a blood python can protect you? Reveal yourself!" Tie Xue Leng scoffed, evading the python's attacks.

"Damn it!" Caught in the crossfire as the red soil crumbled, Fang Yuan lost his foothold.

Curiously, Thunderwings Gu began intermittently absorbing primeval essence directly from the surrounding air.

"Blood Frenzy Gu!" Fang Yuan realized in a flash of inspiration.

Despite his best efforts, Fang Yuan tumbled into the blood lake.

Fang Yuan activated Sky Canopy Gu. Having lived in the Eastern Sea during his past life, he swam with ease.

The white armor transmitted painful impacts from falling rocks, steadily draining his primeval essence.

Reviewing what happened, only one explanation remained: Blood Frenzy Gu resided within this blood water.

Fortunately, his armor held; had the Blood Frenzy Gu entered his body, his Gu would have been corrupted.

"I must escape immediately!" Gritting his teeth, Fang Yuan swam about, searching for an exit.

Mid-air, Tie Xue Leng battled the blood river python—a Rank five clash Fang Yuan could not interfere in.

"Wait, a cave entrance?"

# Chapter 185: Gu Yue first gen, Five great flying zombies

The small tunnel opening had been buried by red soil, but surging lake waves had washed the sediment away, exposing it once more.

The air rippled as an iron hand descended onto the blood river python's tail.

The blood river python thrashed; its slick scales caused the iron hand to slip and erupt with golden-red light.

Pitch-black oil cascaded like a waterfall, forming a dragon in mid-air before plunging toward the Blood River Python.

Though matching the python in size, the Rank four oil dragon was quickly shredded by the beast.

With a shout, Tie Xue Leng released a Fire Dragon Gu from his aperture.

The fire dragon inflicted great pain on the Blood River Python by igniting the black oil clinging to its scales.

Black oil had also coated the water, igniting a raging inferno across the crimson lake that sent temperatures soaring.

Submerging into the lake, the python healed rapidly. With the blood water sustaining it, the beast was virtually unkillable.

Fang Yuan's situation was even more perilous.

The strain on Sky Canopy Gu mounted, but worse still was Fang Yuan's growing difficulty in breathing.

Fang Yuan realized Tie Xue Leng had intentionally driven the python back to buy time for his next killer move.

Sure enough, Tie Xue Leng extended both arms mid-air and let out a deep roar.

His bronze mask brightened with dazzling green radiance.

Boulders and earth from the ceiling and walls tore free, magnetically flying toward the bronze mask.

From one meter, the rocky figure rapidly swelled to three, five, eight, and finally eighteen meters!

The colossal, indomitable figure descended toward the blood lake.

The fire dragon flew up and coiled around the giant puppet's right arm, resting its head on the shoulder.

Next, the oil dragon coiled around the puppet's left arm, resting its head on the other shoulder.

Fang Yuan swam toward the tunnel entrance, which had been cleared when the debris formed the puppet.

The Blood River Python attacked from the lake, coiling up the giant puppet and ramming its massive head toward the puppet's face.

"Iron fist grappling!" the puppet shouted as an iron hand flew out to grab the snake's head.

The giant puppet grabbed the Blood River Python with both hands, while the dragons merged into blazing flames, roasting the beast.

Retaining the softness of red soil, the puppet's waist quickly shrank under the python's constriction.

As Fang Yuan finally reached the tunnel entrance, the giant puppet suddenly coughed. Tie Xue Leng's old illness had relapsed!

Right at this moment, dense bloody fog rose from the center of the blood lake.

"Blood Frenzy Gu!" With a hint of dread in his eyes, Fang Yuan immediately scrambled into the tunnel entrance.

A giant blood wave ten meters high gushed out from the center of the whirlpool.

From within the blood wave, a vertical crimson coffin gradually rose.

"Young junior, thou dare spoil my plan, thou court death!" Before the voice faded, the coffin lid shot off, revealing a shriveled corpse.

He had a scarlet face, fangs, and blood-red hair, his sunken eyes harboring a dying flame.

"Rank five Blood Wight Gu! Is he really the first head?" Fang Yuan was flabbergasted, but wasn't surprised.

Prolonged use of this Gu turned a Gu Master completely into a zombie; as long as they drank blood periodically, they could live forever.

# Chapter 186: Spring and autumn does not appear

"Even as a Rank five Blood Wight, you are only a zombie full of death essence. Your aperture is dead as well. Though you can store primeval essence, you cannot recover it on your own; whatever you use is gone," the giant puppet said coldly.

"Hehehe... so what if I cannot recover primeval essence? I can still use primeval stones. Injured as you are, you dare disturb me... die!" First gen Gu Yue shouted.

Blood mist spewed out amid surging waves as a large swarm of insects flew from the blood lake.

It was the Rank five Blood Guillotine!

At the same time, swarms of bladewing blood bats flew from the cave ceiling and emerged from the blood lake, converging to attack the giant puppet.

"First gen Gu Yue has the terrain and an army against an injured opponent—Tie Xue Leng is being suppressed."

Fang Yuan hid inside a wall crevice, using the shadows to observe the battle.

"Yet Tie Xue Leng has roamed the Southern Border for years. To enter this trap while injured, he must have a trump card. Regardless, this intense battle might cause the entire cavern to collapse. Should I stay and watch? This is the deepest part of the underground cavern; I risk being buried alive. What are the chances I can profit from their conflict?"

Just as Fang Yuan was about to leave, a small group of blood bats broke away from the swarm toward him.

"Clan junior, do not worry. Traps and underground beasts lurk in that cave. These blood bats will ensure your safety," First gen Gu Yue's voice resounded.

Hearing this, Fang Yuan sprinted away. Seeing through his malice, First gen Gu Yue directed hundreds more blood bats to pursue him.

This distraction allowed Tie Xue Leng to exploit an opening.

Heaven Earth Magnificent Sound Gu!

Howl! The giant puppet roared, unleashing a heaven-shaking howl as thunderous soundwaves surged in all directions.

The nearby bladewing blood bats were instantly slaughtered, falling one by one.

Caught in the crossfire, Fang Yuan's white armor flickered, draining ten percent of his primeval essence against the soundwave.

Of the two bat swarms pursuing him, the second was decimated, while the first was thrown into chaos, scattering aimlessly through the cavern.

Seizing the chance, Fang Yuan sprinted ahead, but the surviving bats quickly recovered and resumed their pursuit.

Though his thunderwings were unreliable, they still provided some speed, so Fang Yuan pushed them with all his strength.

Even so, the vast speed difference allowed the bats to steadily close the distance.

"They're almost on me; it's time to use the Spring Autumn Cicada!" Fang Yuan gritted his teeth.

Releasing the Spring Autumn Cicada would produce a massive phantom and a disturbance bound to draw their attention.

Fang Yuan exhaled in anticipation, willing internally, "Spring Autumn Cicada, come out!"

Fang Yuan froze in shock. In the center of his aperture, the Spring Autumn Cicada remained motionless, radiating brilliant yellow-green light.

On the slope, Gu Yue Bo, Bai clan and Xiong clan leader stood side by side.

"Though the main competition is tomorrow, we must hold the preliminaries. The time is ripe; shall we begin?" the Xiong clan leader said with a smile.

"Let us begin, then," Gu Yue Bo replied distractedly, searching the gathered Gu Masters in vain for Fang Yuan.

The Xiong clan leader announced, "This competition spans the surrounding hundred li until sunset. Each of you holds a token; collecting thirty qualifies you for the three-clan tournament. Ready... begin!"

Cold sweat beaded on Fang Yuan's forehead; why couldn't the Spring Autumn Cicada emerge?

Checking his aperture with lightning speed, he found nothing wrong with the Spring Autumn Cicada—it was still recovering rapidly.

"I get it!" Fang Yuan realized. "Once the Spring Autumn Cicada becomes a vital Gu, it remains fixed in the aperture and cannot be moved."

# Chapter 187: There exists one river of time, and the Spring Autumn Cicada acts as the crossing boat

"Rebirth conceals disaster. The Spring Autumn Cicada's recovery is accelerating, and the aperture will inevitably burst under its immense pressure!"

Rebirth was wondrous, but bearing the Spring Autumn Cicada was like possessing half a Ten Extreme Physique—a sword of execution hanging overhead at all times.

"If I cannot release the Spring Autumn Cicada, can I only use it for rebirth?" Fang Yuan's frown deepened.

Restricted by the world's Great Dao, his physical body could not travel back in time and had to self-detonate. The detonation's energy served as propellant, while the Cicada acted as a lone boat carrying his consciousness into the past.

Still incompletely recovered, the Spring Autumn Cicada was currently like a leaky, battered boat.

Moreover, Fang Yuan was only at Rank three initial stage. The propulsive force of his self-detonation would pale drastically compared to his Rank six detonation in his previous life. Such feeble force could hardly propel the Spring Autumn Cicada against the River of Time. "Attempting rebirth through self-detonation now would likely fail. Who is to say this battered boat won't run aground in the long river? To maximize my chances, I must push the aperture to its limit, stalling to let the Spring Autumn Cicada recover further and patch its leaks. Meanwhile, raising my cultivation will provide stronger propulsion to travel against the stream upon self-detonation," Fang Yuan sighed.

This development was unexpected, but ever cautious, Fang Yuan already had a backup plan. As the blood bats drew near, he focused entirely on his aperture.

Yellow-green light flooded the aperture, where perilous cracks spread across the light membrane under the Cicada's aura.

With a thought, Fang Yuan summoned a Gu to the sea's surface against the immense pressure.

Resembling a solid grey die, this was one of the Gu Fang Yuan had plundered from Bai Ning Bing.

A consumable Gu, it permanently drained all latent potential in an aperture to instantly elevate a Gu Master's cultivation to that rank's peak stage.

"Stone Aperture Gu, explode."

As grey powder coated the membrane, the walls thickened rapidly, transforming into solid stone.

The yellow-green radiance of Spring Autumn Cicada still fluctuated continuously, but the aperture could now support its pressure.

From Rank three initial stage with light silver primeval essence, Fang Yuan's cultivation soared directly to Rank three peak stage, possessing snow silver primeval essence!

"Using the Stone Aperture Gu exhausts all potential and severs future advancement, making it nearly impossible to reach Rank four. Their potential is limitless; even if petrified, the aperture walls rapidly recover."

At that moment, the bladewing blood bats pounced in for the kill.

Fang Yuan snorted coldly, brandishing the Chainsaw Golden Centipede to fight as he retreated.

Fortunately, the narrow tunnel, Sky Canopy Gu's defense, and the Chainsaw Golden Centipede's broad body prevented the bats from surrounding him.

The primeval essence in his aperture depleted rapidly.

Despite reaching Rank three peak stage, producing snow silver essence with his C-grade aptitude was far too slow. Nor did the intense battle allow him to absorb primeval stones.

Fortunately, Fang Yuan had recently obtained the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus.

Entering his aperture, this natural essence was automatically refined into snow silver primeval essence.

Fighting on the retreat with the Lotus's support, his essence recovery matched that of a B-grade aptitude Gu Master.

"Kill!"

The Chainsaw Golden Centipede struck, its spinning silver saws screeching as they cleaved through a bladewing blood bat lurking in the rear.

Slightly more imposing than the rest, this was the group's sole male bat, refined by First gen Gu Yue to command the entire swarm.

The male bat was instantly pulped, and the leaderless female bats scattered immediately!

# Chapter 188: Senior paves the way for juniors to run smoothly

Without the male bat, the entire group fell into chaos.

"Time to go!" Fang Yuan retreated at full speed.

"Gasp..." Fang Yuan panted as he sprinted through the narrow tunnel, activating the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus.

After the intense clash, his depleted snow silver primeval essence gradually replenished.

Suddenly, his thunderwings flapped erratically, nearly hurling him into the cavern wall.

Infected by the Blood Frenzy Gu, the Thunderwings Gu had reached its limit and was becoming uncontrollable.

Knowing it would soon dissolve and infect others, Fang Yuan forced the rebellious Thunderwings Gu from his back.

"Go." Fang Yuan decisively threw the Thunderwings Gu behind him.

The pursuing blood bats swarmed the discarded Gu, tearing it to shreds.

By the time the bats caught up, Fang Yuan's primeval essence had recovered past half.

He laughed heartily, waving the Chainsaw Golden Centipede to fight once again.

After he struck down eight or nine, the remainder fled.

Stowing the Chainsaw Golden Centipede, Fang Yuan headed deeper into the cavern.

"I must leave Qing Mao Mountain as soon as possible!" Unable to turn back, he pushed deeper along the tunnel in search of an exit.

Though clearly artificial, the long-abandoned tunnel had collapsed in many sections.

At dead ends, the Chainsaw Golden Centipede proved invaluable for burrowing through blockages.

This slowed his progress considerably, taking three to four hours to reach the tunnel's end.

A large sturdy rock wall blocked his path completely.

Even the Chainsaw Golden Centipede couldn't drill its way through.

"Did First gen Gu Yue seal this cave completely?" Fang Yuan's heart sank.

Three Gu Masters led by Fang Zheng leaped down from the branches.

"Gu Yue clan, this is my prize," Bai Zhong Shui said, her eyes narrowing dangerously.

His eyes strangely bloodshot, Fang Zheng glared at Bai Zhong Shui. "So what? Now you are our prey!"

Several figures stepped from the shadows, led by the Xiong clan's young genius Xiong Lin.

Attacked from both sides, Bai Zhong Shui was caught off guard and surrounded.

Coughing up blood, Bai Zhong Shui's expression darkened. "What? The Gu Yue clan is colluding with the despicable Xiong clan?"

Xiong Lin laughed. "The Gu Yue and Xiong clans have formed an alliance—Bai Zhong Shui, today you die!"

"Bah!" Spitting bloody saliva, Bai Zhong Shui sneered disdainfully. "So what if I die? Ganging up like this... you're simply terrified of Bai Ning Bing. It's futile—after this preliminary stage comes the duels. Does either clan have anyone who can match Bai Ning Bing? Victory is destined to be ours!"

"Hehehe, which is precisely why we agreed to eliminate Bai Ning Bing first!" Xiong Lin laughed loudly.

Drenched in sweat, Fang Yuan alternated between his Gu for hours against the resilient rock, advancing barely fifty centimeters.

"Is this truly my end... eh?" Fang Yuan's heart stirred as he sensed a living presence within the rock wall.

Pressing the Earth Communication Ear Grass to the wall, he detected a mysterious lifeform on the other side, though its aura was extremely faint.

Half an hour later, Fang Yuan dug through, revealing the mysterious creature.

Pitch-black with a metallic sheen, its heavy carapace radiated a commanding aura. It had three pairs of legs tipped with spiral drill-like feelers.

"The Thousand Li Earthwolf Spider!" Fang Yuan realized, instantly recalling the Flower Wine Monk.

"These suspicions are minor details. What matters is leaving!" Fang Yuan channeled primeval essence, quickly refining the Thousand Li Earthwolf Spider.

The rock wall was merely its hibernation cocoon, and in its weakened state, the Rank five spider was easily refined.

As Fang Yuan injected snow silver primeval essence, the spider gradually awakened, its aura strengthening.

Once it partially recovered, an impatient Fang Yuan climbed onto its back and commanded it forward.

The only worry was that steed-type Gu consumed vast amounts of primeval essence.

Rank three essence could not sustain a Rank five spider. Periodically, Fang Yuan halted to activate the Lotus and absorb primeval stones.

Commanding the spider forward, resting to recover essence, and drilling through cave-ins, Fang Yuan steadily moved from the blood lake graveyard toward the surface.

# Chapter 189: Shocking change

Clad in white with snowy hair, Bai Ning Bing stood on a slope as the sunset reflected in his waning eyes. "Such a beautiful sunset... ...I have barely seen nature's brilliance, what a waste. Especially with these annoying fellows arguing around me."

The remaining hundred Gu Masters of the Gu Yue-Xiong alliance surrounded Bai Ning Bing.

Led by Fang Zheng and Xiong Lin, the surrounding Gu Masters spoke in turn, trying to break his spirit.

"Rats screaming before an elephant," Bai Ning Bing sneered. "Among you, only Gu Yue Fang Yuan is interesting. Come at me together."

Forming an iceblade, Bai Ning Bing wiped the frigid edge, not even looking at his surroundings.

"Then who attacks first?" Chi Cheng asked.

"I'll take him first. Honestly, I've wanted to fight you for a long time." Xiong Jiao Man stepped forward.

She blew a whistle, and stampeding echoed from afar as over two hundred black bears emerged from the forest shadows!

"Lord Qing Shu, I will avenge you personally!" Fang Zheng stared intently at Bai Ning Bing, thoughts racing.

"Merely a hundred beast king, what a boring trick."

"Northern Dark Ice Soul physique... I'm at my limit." Bai Ning Bing sensed his impending death as his flesh and blood turned to ice.

"Since you all seek death, I shall grant your wish. Perhaps that will bring some excitement to my life," he sighed softly.

Nearby, the three clan leaders and their Gu Masters stood completely silent.

Gu Yue Bo showed a worried expression.

Neither Fang Yuan nor Tie Xue Leng had appeared yet.

"Desolate Yin Ancient Moon physique... If Fang Yuan truly has such aptitude, we must protect him at all costs and bring him into the blood lake graveyard!"

"Fuck!" Fang Yuan cursed, clinging to the Thousand Li Earthwolf Spider's back to keep from falling off.

Before long, however, the Thousand Li Earthwolf Spider began to go mad.

Absorbing natural essence from the air, its six legs dug new paths, scurrying erratically through the underground cave.

Though losing control, Fang Yuan maintained the general heading toward the surface, unsure where they would emerge.

Bai Ning Bing stood arrogantly on the battlefield, thick ice spreading outward beneath his feet.

The bear group was annihilated. Xiong Jiao Man had been beheaded by his iceblade, her head flying as blood fountain from her neck.

Icicles impaled Xiong Lin, while Gu Yue Chi Cheng was frozen vividly into an ice sculpture.

Fang Zheng was riddled with wounds, but ice and snow froze them before blood could flow.

Bai Ning Bing was merely Rank three, yet his strength neared Rank four!

"Hmph, you look alike, but you aren't him. Boring..." Bai Ning Bing approached Fang Zheng, having deliberately spared him until now.

"Die." Bai Ning Bing jumped, raising his iceblade high while in mid-air.

The iceblade swelled to five or six times its size, slashing downward with an unstoppable aura.

Gu Yue Bo moved to intervene, but the Bai clan leader blocked him. "Will you break our agreement?"

Gu Yue Bo narrowed his eyes. "Bai Ning Bing is only Rank three—you are cheating blatantly!"

"Cheating? Hehe, truth be told, our Bai Ning Bing possesses the Northern Dark Ice Soul physique!" the Bai clan leader yelled.

"What? Ten extreme aptitude!" At once, Gu Yue Bo and Xiong clan leader were shocked.

# Chapter 190: Third battle against Bai Ning Bing

"Moon Raiment!" Facing the blade, Fang Zheng activated his Gu. Misty moonlight-blue radiance gushed from him, shielding the surrounding Gu Masters.

Yet despair still gripped them. "Struggle is useless," Bai Ning Bing sneered, his blue eyes cold and cruel.

Bam! The ground suddenly bulged and exploded, sending soil flying everywhere.

A black-clothed, black-haired youth stood tall atop the steel-like spider.

"I've finally reached the surface!" Fang Yuan clenched his fist, eyes glowing brightly.

"Eh?" He immediately saw Bai Ning Bing hacking toward him in mid-air.

"Fang Yuan, you're finally here!" Bai Ning Bing's stunned expression transformed into fierce excitement as he slashed downward.

Bam! The iceblade collided with the Chainsaw Golden Centipede; its razors spun furiously, showering ice shards everywhere.

Cracks spread across the iceblade. "Break!" Fang Yuan roared, his two-boar strength completely overpowering Bai Ning Bing.

The Thousand Li Earthwolf Spider went berserk, lunging at Bai Ning Bing with all six legs.

Bai Ning Bing launched a volley of icicles that shattered against the spider. Growling furiously, the spider pounced, carrying Fang Yuan atop it.

Sensing danger, snowy light burst from Bai Ning Bing's aperture.

Bai Ning Bing leaped onto the Rank five white form immortal snake Gu. "Interesting! Fang Yuan, you didn't disappoint me!"

"Fang Yuan, you finally appeared! Rank five Gu... isn't that the Thousand Li Earthwolf Spider?" Gu Yue Bo's attention was attracted.

"Hmph." Fang Yuan stared at Bai Ning Bing while scanning his surroundings.

He had no time to waste fighting Bai Ning Bing with two Rank five monsters looming, yet he could not escape without breaking through.

The white form immortal snake and Thousand Li Earthwolf Spider clashed, coiling and stabbing in close-quarters combat.

Astride their Gu, iceblade and chainsaw clashed amid continuous explosions.

Icicles deflected off Fang Yuan's Sky Canopy Gu armor. When a blood moonblade opened a deep wound on Bai Ning Bing, frost instantly sealed the injury. The frost became flesh and blood, healing him completely. "It really is the Northern Dark Ice Soul physique!" the Xiong clan leader stared wide-eyed.

"It must be a ten extreme physique! In such a fight, C-grade essence would have run out long ago. Fang Yuan, you truly possess the Desolate Yin Ancient Moon physique!" Gu Yue Bo clenched his fists in agitation.

Both Gu took heavy damage. The white form immortal snake was not refined by Bai Ning Bing, merely attracted by his Northern Dark Ice Soul physique.

Spitting a cloud of white fog across the battlefield, the snake shook Bai Ning Bing off its back and fled.

Roots sprouted from Fang Yuan's ears, allowing him to perceive everything within three hundred paces.

"I'm not playing with you." Fang Yuan abandoned the spider and slipped away into the mist.

"Iceblade storm!" Behind him, Bai Ning Bing's voice came.

"That moving mist must be Fang Yuan! Quickly stop him!" Tie Ruo Nan shouted from the side.

"Fang Yuan, don't go, Gu Yue village is your home!" Anxious, Gu Yue Bo tried to give chase, but the other two clan leaders blocked him.

"Our clan concedes! Whoever blocks me now, I will murder without hesitation!" Gu Yue Bo raged.

"We can't let him escape; I'm going ahead!" Seizing the chance, Tie Ruo Nan unfurled black steel wings and took to the air after Fang Yuan.

Just then, a green shadow shot over—the Rank five Giant Mountain Puppet Gu!

Resembling a bloodstained bronze mask with eye and mouth slits, it flew straight toward the startled girl's face.

"Father!" Seeing the blood traces, Tie Ruo Nan screamed subconsciously.

A giant steel hand flew over, grabbing Tie Ruo Nan and carrying her away without pause.

"It seems Tie Xue Leng is defeated. Hehe, Senior Brother... you brought your junior brother quite a surprise." High above, an elderly Gu Master watched with cold indifference.

Astride a giant crane, he held a cicada-shaped Kinship Blood Worm Gu that pulsed with light toward Gu Yue village.

"Senior Brother, no matter where you hide, I will find you. You stole my opportunity back then—I will repay you a thousandfold!"

The white-browed elder gritted his teeth, his expression dripping with deep hatred.

# Chapter 191: Crane disaster

Beneath the azure sky, the elder rode his white crane. Dagger-sharp white eyebrows framed eyes filled with intense killing intent.

"Hehehe, with this grudge, I shall begin my vengeance on your descendants," he chuckled, pointing a withered finger down at the battlefield.
