The white crane raised its elegant neck, letting out a loud, piercing cry.

The cry echoed across the sky, answered by distant calls.

"What sound is this?" Listening through the Earth Communication Ear grass, Fang Yuan heard the endless, multi-pitched crane cries first, shocked by their magnitude. This was no flock of hundreds or thousands, but the unified cries of over ten thousand flying cranes.

Fresh off the devastating wolf tide, Qing Mao Mountain's three clans could hardly withstand a crane disaster. Folding their wings, the cranes dived like a rain of arrows.

After brief resistance, over half the Gu Masters were slaughtered.

Assailed in the blinding fog, Fang Yuan relied on the Earth Communication Ear grass to evade the attacks.

"Fang Yuan, hang in there!" Gu Yue Bo shouted from behind.

As a Rank four expert, Gu Yue Bo easily repelled and slew the ordinary cranes attacking Fang Yuan.

"Fang Yuan, is that you?" Gu Yue Bo reached the fog.

Gu Yue Bo breathed a sigh of relief. "Good! Forget the past; the clan will ensure your safety. Let us retreat to the village—I'll cover you!"

Without hesitation, Fang Yuan replied, "Lead the way, Clan Leader, I will keep up!"

Just then, a giant crane descended. The white-browed elder astride it said coldly, "None of you can escape; you shall all die here."

Unable to see, Fang Yuan heard Gu Yue Bo cry out, "A Rank five Gu Master!"

Fang Yuan was inwardly stunned—why were so many Rank five experts gathering at unremarkable, resource-poor Qing Mao Mountain?

"Could this be related to First gen Gu Yue?" An inspiration struck Fang Yuan.

"Clan Leader, the First gen ancestor has fully awakened! We'll be safe if we return to the village!" Fang Yuan said.

"What?" Gu Yue Bo shouted in shock.

Resolute as ever, Gu Yue Bo brought Fang Yuan and sped toward the village.

Yet the flying cranes blocked their path, with hundred and thousand beast kings appearing relentlessly.

Gu Yue Bo fought desperately to protect Fang Yuan, but was ultimately encircled by the flock, keeping Fang Yuan safe for now.

Once time was up, the maze fog vanished.

"Isn't this an Iron Beak Flying Crane?" Fang Yuan wondered.

"A myriad beast king and a Rank five expert!" Fang Yuan spotted the giant crane hovering in mid-air, carrying a cold-faced, white-haired, white-browed elder on its back.

Covered in blood and severely injured, the Gu Yue clan leader fought desperately, repeatedly taking hits to protect Fang Yuan when he could have dodged.

"Clan leader! Our Gu Masters are divided by the cranes and will be overwhelmed. We must unite our strength. Only then can we break out and return to the village!" Fang Yuan urged Gu Yue Bo.

"You are right." Gu Yue Bo shouted across the battlefield, "Everyone, the Gu Yue clan has a method to deal with the enemy—assemble with me and fight our way out!"

Under the threat of death, the former enemies quickly united in one spot.

"As his descendants, they can be hostages in the coming battle. But these three Rank four Gu Masters might interfere—I must eliminate them first!"

Thinking so, the white-browed elder screeched eerily, flicking three balls of light forward.

"What Gu is this?" The Xiong clan leader was struck first by the white ball; engulfed in its light, his speed slowed to a crawl.

The other two clan leaders met the same fate. "Fang Yuan, go! Gu Yue clansmen, protect Fang Yuan at all costs—he alone knows the method!" Unable to remove the ring of light, Gu Yue Bo roared and turned back to face the white-browed elder.

Clashing sounds echoed behind them as the three rival clan leaders fought together against the elder, outmatched from the start.

With a flick of the white-browed elder's sleeves from atop his giant crane, the three clan leaders fell one by one. The crane flock filled the sky, forcing their way into Gu Yue village.

Gu Yue village fell into chaos amid widespread wailing.

The clash between Tie Xue Leng and First gen Gu Yue had heavily damaged the village, leaving Gu Yue Yao Ji to face the three clans' survivors.

"What is going on?" She shouted loudly.

"Senior brother, your junior has traveled far to see you. Why won't you come out to meet me?" The white-browed elder stood atop the crane, his voice dripping with icy killing intent.

Before he finished speaking, bloody water erupted like a ten-meter fountain in the square, and the scarlet coffin emerged upright.

Standing within the coffin as a blood wight, First gen Gu Yue fixed his blood-red eyes on the white-browed elder.

"To think you survived... how did you find this place? As expected, that Gu Master earlier was your doing!" First gen Gu Yue spat hatefully.

# Chapter 192: Raising Eyebrows & Exhale

"How did I find you? Hahaha!" The white-browed elder laughed gleefully with ruthless eyes, opening his palm to reveal a Gu.

It was the Kinship Bloodworm.

Crystal clear like red agate, the cicada-like Kinship Bloodworm pulsed with light, pointing toward First gen Gu Yue.

"Though you backstabbed me and stole my Gu, I still mastered the secret art and refined this Kinship Bloodworm. Do you know how? Hehehe, using the heart blood of your two biological sons. They were only five or six and lacked enough heart blood, so I kept them as living sources, drawing their blood monthly. After decades, I finally gathered enough to refine this Gu."

"Both died shortly after. HAHAHA! After refining it, I searched for years, but the Kinship Bloodworm's range was limited. Near death, I used the life-retaining Jade Burial Gu to seal myself into hibernation. Having unsealed recently, I made one final attempt—and found you by pure coincidence! Senior brother, do you know how thrilled I was at that moment?!"

"As for Tie Xue Leng, I lured him here with a letter. How nostalgic—we were once sect brothers known as the Righteous Twin Cranes," the elder mused.

This white-browed elder from the Middle Continent was known as Lord Sky Crane.

Lord Sky Crane and First gen Gu Yue both hailed from the Middle Continent's Immortal Crane Sect. Centuries ago, they were closer than blood brothers, slaying demonic Gu Masters in perfect synergy.

Lord Sky Crane claimed the inheritance first, but First gen Gu Yue ambushed him, stole the Gu, and attempted murder.

With his betrayal exposed, First gen Gu Yue went into hiding under a false name to evade his former sect. Failing to find a Lifespan Gu, he settled on Qing Mao Mountain, disguised as Gu Yue, and founded the village.

"Despicable traitor! When you backstabbed me, did you ever expect retribution? I finally found you. Today, not only will I exterminate your bloodline and ruin your thousand-year scheme, I will also reclaim my opportunity! I have waited far too long for this day!!"

But before he struck, First gen Gu Yue acted even faster!

Bloody water erupted from the ground like a fountain, exuding a terrifying aura.

Amid a pungent stench of blood, tens of thousands of densely packed blood guillotines surged from the concentrated blood water.

Wings flapped as countless bladewing blood bats took to the air, blotting out the sky and charging toward the crane flock.

Furious, Lord Sky Crane bellowed and commanded his flying crane king to dive toward First gen Gu Yue.

Suddenly, a blood wave surged as the hidden blood river python struck under its cover, snapping at the crane king's neck and coiling to drag it into the blood pond below.

"Hand over your life!" Lord Sky Crane roared, transforming into a white flash and lunging at First gen Gu Yue in the red coffin.

Stepping from the coffin, First gen Gu Yue sprouted a pair of large, powerful jet-black bat wings from his back.

Eyes blazing, First gen Gu Yue unfurled his bat wings, bared wicked claws, and soared upward, colliding mid-air with Lord Sky Crane with a resounding boom.

As a frail elder on the brink of death, Lord Sky Crane gradually lost strength and fell into a disadvantage against First gen Gu Yue.

"After hundreds of years, you did not improve one bit!" First gen Gu Yue laughed loudly and mocked.

"Hmph, it's time to use my trump card!" Lord Sky Crane snorted secretly, his white eyebrows raising lightly, transforming.

His eyebrows grew wildly into two thick, tens-of-meters-long tendrils that shot out like serpents, rapidly coiling around First gen Gu Yue.

First gen Gu Yue struggled in vain as the eyebrows bound him like steel roots.

"The Raise Eyebrows & Exhale Gu!" First gen Gu Yue's voice shifted in shock and fury.

The Raise Eyebrows & Exhale Gu converted primeval essence into primeval energy rather than ordinary qi—a rare Gu with a mutual-destruction mechanic.

Though both were Rank five peak stage, First gen Gu Yue's zombie aperture was dead and incapable of naturally recovering primeval essence. Though near death, Lord Sky Crane's living aperture recovered primeval essence, striking First gen Gu Yue's greatest weakness.

First gen Gu Yue directed the blood guillotines and bats to sever the brows, but severed hairs instantly reconnected.

With his primeval essence steadily draining toward certain defeat, First gen Gu Yue gritted his teeth and redirected the blood guillotines and bladewing blood bats to attack Lord Sky Crane directly.

Forced to sustain the Raise Eyebrows & Exhale Gu, Lord Sky Crane could only rely on his light barrier to defend passively against the assault.

His barrier would likely shatter and First gen Gu Yue would kill him before the zombie's primeval essence ran out!

# Chapter 193: Divine investigator’s last move

With a malevolent sneer and sinister gleam in his eyes, Lord Sky Crane suddenly unleashed a rain of feather arrows below.

When an arrow flew at Fang Yuan, he tossed a nearby man into its path to take the fatal headshot, then rolled aside to dodge an Ironbeak Flying Crane's talons.

"Stop!" First gen Gu Yue bellowed, his heart bleeding as he watched the Gu Masters being slaughtered.

The clansmen were the fruits of centuries of planning; Lord Sky Crane's slaughter was destroying his life's work.

Left with no choice, First gen Gu Yue broke off his assault on Lord Sky Crane and redirected his bladewing blood bats and blood guillotines to defend the village.

Midway through the deadlock, First gen Gu Yue cried out as illusory iron chains suddenly manifested around his body.

A paper talisman also materialized, sticking firmly between his eyebrows.

Lord Sky Crane burst into mad laughter. "So it's the Demon Suppression Iron Chain Gu and Problem Nipped in the Bud Gu! Hahaha, I guess that so-called Divine Investigator wasn't a good-for-nothing after all. My good senior brother, you are sure to die today!"

The Demon Suppression Iron Chain Gu immobilized Gu Masters, while the Problem Nipped in the Bud Gu extracted and sealed their Gu worms.

"When did this happen?!" Startled and furious, First gen Gu Yue recalled the moment he had struck Tie Xue Leng dead.

Facing certain death without fear, the investigator's resolute eyes had shone as his bloody palm struck lightly against First gen Gu Yue's chest.

"So it was then! Damn it—a living body would have sensed it, but my zombie form lacks sensitivity despite its strength." First gen Gu Yue was filled with regret.

"Hahaha! The Sky Crane Lord's aura grew with his laughter, then he charged towards the First gen Gu Yue."

Battered and bloody, First gen Gu Yue had his ribs exposed and both arms severed from the shoulders by Lord Sky Crane.

Piercing, concentrated sonic waves coalesced and slammed into Lord Sky Crane.

Blasted back dozens of paces with his eyebrow tendrils pulled taut, Lord Sky Crane crashed down.

Bleeding from his seven orifices, the reverberating sound threw his qi and blood into chaos, temporarily paralyzing him.

With the cranes ineffective, the crane king battling the python in the distance, and himself paralyzed by chaotic qi and blood, Lord Sky Crane could only move his eyes. He focused on the Raise Eyebrows & Exhale Gu, drastically accelerating the primeval essence consumption.

With his primeval sea emptying, First gen Gu Yue could not prevent the Problem Nipped in the Bud Gu from sealing his Gu worms.

First gen Gu Yue shouted in panic, "Quick, lend me a hand! Grab the eyebrows and activate your primeval essence."

How could Rank two essence withstand a Rank five's drain? In moments, their primeval seas emptied and their apertures cracked and collapsed.

Seeing this, many rushing Gu Masters hesitated and slowed down.

"What are you afraid of? If the first ancestor falls, we all die! Do you think you'll be spared?" Fang Yuan shouted, rushing forward to grab the white eyebrows.

With the Thousand Li Earthwolf spider dead and enemy cranes patrolling the skies, escaping alone was impossible.

Conversely, aiding First gen Gu Yue so both Rank fives fought to mutual destruction might yield opportunities to profit.

The white eyebrows bound Fang Yuan's wrists, draining his snow-silver primeval essence as he gritted his teeth and held on.

Spurred by his example, other Gu Masters rushed forward in succession.

"Hahaha, fighting against a Rank five—how thrilling!" Bai Ning Bing stepped forward as well.

"Life or death will be decided by this." Xiong Jiao Man similarly stood up.

The Problem Nipped in the Bud Gu slowly extracted a ball of yellow light from his aperture.

It revealed a Gu resembling a palm-sized child's crystal skull, veined with reddish blood streaks.

"Blood Skull Gu! After hundreds of years, I finally see you again!" Lord Sky Crane wept tears of joy from afar, overcome with emotion.

# Chapter 194: Blood Curtain Skyflower

Soon, another orb was extracted by the yellow talisman and dropped to the ground, shifting into a black-and-white Taiji light sphere.

"Yin Yang Rotation Gu!" Lord Sky Crane laughed. "Senior brother, I must thank you for finding such a wonderful Gu! Hahaha, you actually wanted to turn back into a human, but what a pity, for your plan has been destroyed by me!"

First gen Gu Yue snarled in panic. Both vital Gu of his millennium plot were now sealed, his efforts going up in smoke.

Another extracted orb fell to the ground, revealing a translucent scarlet Gu resembling a sphere of water.

Seeing this Gu, First gen Gu Yue stopped struggling, exultant. Baring his fangs, he shouted, "Quick, destroy this Gu worm!"

Several Gu Masters immediately stepped forward and unleashed their attacks.

A soft cry resounded as blood light bloomed, rapidly expanding into a spherical shield.

Spanning six mu, the blood shield enclosed Bai Ning Bing and Fang Yuan, leaving Xiong Jiao Man and others outside.

The shield instantly severed the white eyebrows in two; as they disintegrated, Fang Yuan and the others regained control of their bodies.

"This shield's aura reaches Rank five, but why does it resemble Water Curtain Skyflower Gu?" Fang Yuan wondered, bewildered.

"Just as I intended!" Killing intent flashing in his eyes, Sky Crane Lord unleashed white beams, flying cranes, and feather arrows at the shield.

Yet the blood shield remained unshakeable and firm.

Sky Crane Lord's face darkened as he halted his attacks. "What Gu is this?"

First gen Gu Yue laughed, "This is my fused Blood Curtain Skyflower Gu. If the Rank four version blocks Rank five attacks, this has even greater defense. And Blood Curtain Skyflower Gu lets me exit! Once I recover, I will come out and chop off your head, hahaha!"

Sky Crane Lord attacked furiously, slaughtering all Gu Masters outside the shield, including Xiong Jiao Man.

After attacking fruitlessly for a long time, Sky Crane Lord abruptly stopped, coming to his senses.

Realizing this, Sky Crane Lord sat cross-legged, crushing two primeval stones to recover his primeval essence.

"Help me if you want to live! I need primeval stones, in large quantities!" First gen Gu Yue shouted, and the Gu Masters immediately piled stones around him.

Sky Crane Lord shouted, "Don't give them to him, fools! Once he can move, he'll slaughter you all to upgrade his aptitude!"

While an ordinary body could not withstand such an influx of primeval essence, First gen Gu Yue's blood wight zombie body held firm, rapidly filling his primeval sea.

The Demon Suppression Iron Chain Gu weakened and eventually dissolved into a pool of blood, completely destroyed.

Freed, First gen Gu Yue roared, absorbing blood to regrow powerful arms with razor-sharp claws.

Simultaneously, the Problem Nipped in the Bud Gu on his forehead melted into blood and completely dissipated.

"It seems heaven does not want me dead!" First gen Gu Yue threw back his head and laughed.

His laughter subsided, fire burning in his eyes as he spoke calmly, "I will certainly slay my enemy. But first, there is something I must do."

"Not good." Hearing his tone, Fang Yuan's heart jumped, and he discreetly slipped toward the group's outer edge.

First gen Gu Yue cackled wildly, "Haha, what I must do is take your lives!"

With a squelch, his claw impaled a nearby Rank three Gu Master, pulling back a still-beating heart.

The Gu Master, a Gu Yue clansman, stared in utter disbelief; screaming as blood fountained from his chest, he collapsed onto his back.

"Good blood must not be wasted," First gen Gu Yue exclaimed, sending the Blood Skull Gu circling the corpse.

The corpse twitched as the Blood Skull Gu extracted all its blood into its two hollow eye sockets.

Having drunk its fill, the blood stripes on the crystal skull glowed brighter.

A massacre unfolded. First gen Gu Yue was a peak Rank five with fully restored primeval essence; how could Rank one, two, or three Gu Masters match him?

Sparing only the Bai and Xiong clansmen, he slaughtered every Gu Yue clansman, using the Blood Skull Gu to harvest their blood to capacity.

Laughing loudly, First gen Gu Yue summoned the Blood Skull Gu to float above his head.

"Centuries of planning have finally borne fruit!" He watched as the Blood Skull Gu spewed a fountain of blood.

Mysteriously, not a single drop touched the ground; all the blood adhered to First gen Gu Yue, slowly seeping inward to his aperture.

First gen Gu Yue stood silently, then burst into manic laughter, "Kakakaka, my aptitude has advanced—it really advanced!"

Outside the shield, Sky Crane Lord pointed furiously at First gen Gu Yue, "Return my Blood Skull Gu!"

# Chapter 195: Then let me become a demon

First gen Gu Yue laughed with dark glee, then abruptly turned toward Lord Sky Crane, his voice dripping with hatred, "Junior brother, you never thought this day would come, did you? We were both orphans raised by Master. Yet since childhood, Master always favored you. Why? Simply because you were A grade, while I was only C grade!"

"With your A grade aptitude, you progressed rapidly while I sweated blood just to keep pace. Although we were called the Twin Cranes of Righteous Paths—you were always the main and I the second. You were like the brightest star, just because you were an A grade genius!"

"Even your luck was astounding—you gained the Blood Sea inheritance. The Blood Skull Gu purifies slain kin's blood to raise aperture aptitude. I plotted day and night. The Blood Skull Gu was my sole hope to alter my life and change my fate!"

"But it is mine, MINE!" Lord Sky Crane stamped his feet in fury.

"Yes, it was yours," First gen Gu Yue nodded. "You had supreme talent and luck. We started together and fought side by side, yet heaven favored only you with that true inheritance. Watching your smug face talk of grand ambitions while I forced a smile, I suddenly realized something."

"Heaven blessed you with A grade aptitude and the Blood Sea inheritance. How could an insignificant C grade like me succeed on my own? I could only seize and rob! Make what wasn't mine my own! Fate decreed I remain a shadow subservient to others, but I refused. On what basis were you placed above and I below?"

"Since heaven, Master, and others disfavored me, I had to rely solely on myself. I had to strive harder, take greater risks, and push myself further to succeed! Yet the righteous path preaches morals, ethics, camaraderie, seniority, and hierarchy. A nobody with no backing, talent, or resources—tell me, how was I to succeed?"

"On the righteous path, I was exploited and bullied, bowing to young masters and geniuses. Even grinding seniority into old age yielded meager success. The righteous path is utter bullshit!"

"Only as a demon could I carve my own path! So I schemed against you, seized your inheritance, and became a demon!"

First gen Gu Yue resumed killing. The Blood Skull Gu gathered the slain Gu Masters' blood into a fountain, raising his aptitude.

As he butchered more clansmen, his aptitude climbed; devouring primeval stones, his primeval essence replenished and his battle prowess surged.

Soon, only a few people remained alive inside the blood shield.

"Brat, you stole the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus. Surrender it obediently, and your life will be spared!" First gen Gu Yue advanced, his killing intent locking onto Fang Yuan.

Knowing his chances were slim, Fang Yuan sneered, "Step any closer, and I will destroy the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus. I've refined it and can detonate it with a single thought. The choice is yours."

First gen Gu Yue slowed briefly, then pressed resolutely toward Fang Yuan.

"Brat, you have guts and brains—truly my descendant. I'll use your blood to elevate my aptitude; I was never letting you go. I originally purified my descendants' bloodline with Gu, hoping to produce the Ancient Moon Desolate Yin physique. Absorbing the blood of such a genius would have raised my aptitude straight to ninety-nine percent A grade!"

"I despise being coerced; even if the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus is destroyed, you will still die!"

Before his words finished, his wings flapped, propelling him before Fang Yuan in an instant.

As First gen Gu Yue lunged to grab him, Fang Yuan's pupils shrank and hairs stood on end as he retreated hastily.

Yet dodging a Rank five expert was impossible. Just as the blood wight claws reached him, an ice hand struck from the side.

With a sharp collision, the ice hand shattered into frosty mist, forcing First gen Gu Yue back a step.

"Bai Ning Bing?!" Fang Yuan turned in shock toward the person who saved him.

Bai Ning Bing withdrew his broken arm; frost billowed from the stump, reforming an ice hand as his lifespan neared its end.

"To think I could have such a splendid death. What a pity I won't see this battle's conclusion!" Bai Ning Bing smiled at Fang Yuan: "Fang Yuan, you and I are alike. My death is certain, but it would be a pity if you died too. So I will protect you, but I have a condition."

Though only a Rank three Gu Master, Bai Ning Bing's battle strength surged exponentially as death neared. He was the final hope against First gen Gu Yue!

"What condition?" Fang Yuan's gaze flashed.

Clad in white robes with snowy hair and sharp brows, Bai Ning Bing spread his arms as if embracing the world: "Live on for me, and witness this world's myriad wonders!"

In that moment, Fang Yuan was emotionally moved!

Looking at Bai Ning Bing, Fang Yuan felt he was seeing his past life's younger self.

"The Northern Dark Ice Soul physique!" First gen Gu Yue sneered. "Yet you think this physique can stop me? Naive!"

With an explosive crash, the two traded blows.

First gen Gu Yue was knocked back two steps, while Bai Ning Bing's skull and upper body were blasted away.

Frigid mist condensed, immediately reforming his skull and body—Bai Ning Bing was resurrected!

"What!" First gen Gu Yue was shocked; Ten Extreme physiques were rare, and this was his first encounter with one.

First gen Gu Yue attacked frantically. Bai Ning Bing lost all color of life, turning completely into an iceman.

"Infinite brilliance in the mortal realm, the sky and sea reach the world's end. As winds scatter my ashes to earth, none compares to the moon reflected in water!" Bai Ning Bing recited with a faint smile.

Ice spread rapidly from beneath his feet as a glacier surged upward like a newborn mountain!

Like an avalanche, tsunami, and rolling dragon, the majestic ice surged to crush First gen Gu Yue.

First gen Gu Yue gasped, summoning all his strength to resist. Bristling with bloody mist, First gen Gu Yue hurled swarms of Blood Guillotine and Bladewing Blood Bats to slow the glacier.

Ultimately, he was suppressed, engulfed, and trapped by the glacier.

The glacier filled the blood shield, bypassing Fang Yuan within a small space Bai Ning Bing had deliberately spared.

The Blood Curtain Skyflower burst softly; unhindered, frigid air surged outward as the glacier expanded relentlessly.

"It broke the Blood Curtain Skyflower! Such power..." Not daring to face the glacier, Lord Sky Crane took flight.

Under his astonished gaze, the glacier engulfed Qing Mao Mountain, transforming the lush mountain into desolate ice and snow.

"Not good, Bai Ning Bing is losing control of the glacier!" Fang Yuan realized his space was shrinking as ice advanced.

# Chapter 196: Rebirth once more

Fang Yuan unleashed the Chainsaw Golden Centipede and Blood Moon Gu to shatter the ice.

But the ice grew faster than he could carve it. Trapped within, he had no escape.

Meanwhile, blood-red light emerged from beneath the ice.

"Hahaha!" First gen Gu Yue broke through the ice amid soaring blood light. Battered yet arrogant, he laughed, "At Rank four, that Northern Dark Ice Soul might have killed me. Sadly for him, he was only peak Rank three..."

"If he couldn't kill you, then I will." Lord Sky Crane glided over atop the Ironbeak Flying Crane King.

Only three living people now remained on Qing Mao Mountain.

Bai Ning Bing had dissolved into the ice mountain, mind extinguished, while Fang Yuan was trapped like an insect in amber.

First gen Gu Yue held abundant primeval essence from his raised aptitude, while Lord Sky Crane had recovered most of his strength.

First gen Gu Yue wanted Fang Yuan's blood, while Lord Sky Crane resolved to eliminate any potential bystander.

Given their nature, neither would tolerate a bystander who might profit if both were injured.

Fang Yuan sighed. Seeing their gazes, he knew he was finished.

Having lost his movement Gu worms, escaping two Rank five Gu Masters was impossible.

Unable to fight or flee, Fang Yuan still possessed one final move.

That is—Spring Autumn Cicada!

Focusing on his aperture, Fang Yuan saw its light walls had turned to rough stone.

Half his primeval essence remained, maintained only by the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus.

His vital Gu, the Rank six Spring Autumn Cicada!

Only this Gu could travel against time's flow, creating a miracle and impossibly changing fate!

"To use the Spring Autumn Cicada, I must self-detonate, sacrificing my cultivation, flesh, and Gu worms to propel it. Compared to my previous life, this force is too small even with self-detonation, likely killing me directly. But with no other options, I'm forced to use this Gu!"

There was less than a ten percent chance of success.

"Little rascal, hand over your life and contribute your blood to your ancestor!"

"Lad, you're unlucky; blame your despicable ancestor. I'll set you free from your misery!"

"Spring Autumn Cicada, do it!" His eyes shone brilliantly as green and yellow light burst from his body.

"How can this be? A Rank six Gu!"

Shock struck the two Rank five Gu Masters, quickly followed by greed.

But at this time, a loud boom occurred.

Fang Yuan self detonated!

Beneath the turbulent water, a tiny Gu worm swam upstream.

Spreading its wings, the cicada struggled forward against the crashing temporal currents.

Shielded by faint light, it carried Fang Yuan's consciousness upstream through the river.

Reaching its limit as the light dispersed, the Spring Autumn Cicada burrowed into a temporal ripple.

Fang Yuan shuddered, a tint of yellow-green light deep in his eyes.

Consciousness and memories assaulted his brain, merging instantly.

He succeeded!

He was extremely happy, he had rebirthed once more!

After sensing this, he immediately looked around, observing his surroundings.

He found white eyebrows coiled around his body and arms, expending his primeval essence.

First gen Gu Yue was immobilized by Tie Xue Leng's Demon Suppression Iron Chains, a Problem Nipped in the Bud talisman on his forehead.

Lord Sky Crane had landed within a white barrier, activating the Raise Eyebrows & Exhale Gu to exhaust First gen Gu Yue's primeval essence.

Bound by the white eyebrows, Fang Yuan could only expend primeval essence and bide his time.

Under the Problem Nipped in the Bud Gu's effect, a yellow light flew from First gen Gu Yue's body.

This fist-sized yellow light landed on the ground, revealing the Blood Skull Gu within it.

Another ball of light was captured by the yellow talisman, falling to the ground as an alternating black-and-white Taiji sphere.

Two peculiar Gu worms chased each other within this light sphere—the Yin Yang Rotation Gu.

"Wait a bit longer, my opportunity is coming." Fang Yuan's eyes shone, choosing to bide his time.

The third captured light dropped to the ground, a scarlet Gu resembling a translucent sphere of water.

Fang Yuan's heart jumped: "Blood Curtain Skyflower!"

Indeed, afterwards, First gen Gu Yue shouted, "Quick someone, destroy this Gu worm!"

Several Gu Masters rushed forward at once and unleashed their attacks.

With a soft cry, the Blood Curtain Skyflower activated, its spherical blood shield separating those inside from those outside.

The blood shield severed the white eyebrows, freeing Fang Yuan and the others from the essence drain.

Lord Sky Crane attacked furiously in vain. Calming down, he replenished his primeval essence, waiting for the blood shield to expire.

First gen Gu Yue demanded more primeval stones, and everyone quickly contributed theirs.

Though chained and immobile, First gen Gu Yue continuously crushed primeval stones with his mouth, recovering quickly.

Lord Sky Crane shouted, "You fools! Once he recovers, he will slaughter you all for your blood!"

No one believed him.

# Chapter 197: Changing Fate!

First gen Gu Yue continuously chewed and crushed large numbers of primeval stones, absorbing them.

Bai Ning Bing watched coldly.

Fang Yuan moved, choosing a different path than before instead of merely watching.

"First gen Ancestor, the situation is dire; I offer the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus!" He walked up quickly with sincere, determined helplessness.

Gu Yue laughed heartily, "Very good, truly my descendant! Such filial piety pleases your ancestor!"

Having long coveted the Treasure Lotus, First gen Gu Yue held no suspicion of Fang Yuan's approach.

As Fang Yuan approached, First gen Gu Yue laughed, "With the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus, my chances rise by twenty percent. Step aside and let him in!"

Outside the blood barrier, Lord Sky Crane was stunned. "Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus? You mustn't do it!"

Under everyone's gaze, Fang Yuan reached First gen Gu Yue's side.

"First gen ancestor, you founded our clan and led us toward brilliance. The Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus should be used by you, great ancestor." He was full of admiration, his tone excited and nervous.

First gen Gu Yue nodded repeatedly, thinking: This lad speaks well. Too bad I still have to kill him later for his blood.

But at this time!

Fang Yuan lowered his back, striking.

He grabbed First gen Gu Yue, twisting his waist and swinging both arms.

Growling, he unleashed the strength of two boars to throw with all his might!

First gen Gu Yue was thrown right out of the blood barrier.

Dead silence!

First gen Gu Yue slammed into the ground outside, staring blankly in shock.

Lord Sky Crane was less than ten steps ahead of him.

Lord Sky Crane was equally stunned by this sudden turn of events.

Even Bai Ning Bing lost his composure, staring at Fang Yuan in astonishment.

"First gen ancestor!" Someone tried to pull him back, but the Blood Curtain Skyflower blocked them.

"Despicable thief! Deceiving your ancestor, I'll tear you to shreds!" First gen Gu Yue screamed from the ground.

"Hahaha..." Amidst the frightened individuals, someone laughed.

It was none other than Bai Ning Bing.

Laughing until tears flowed, he gave Fang Yuan a thumbs up: "Interesting, truly spectacular!"

As the crowd pointed in rage, Fang Yuan chuckled, "I am Rank three peak stage. Who dares kill me? Who can kill me?"

Within the blood barrier, few Rank three Gu Masters remained. Drained from the battle and having surrendered their primeval stones, they had no replenishment. The rest were mortals or Rank one and two Gu Masters.

Without First gen Gu Yue, Fang Yuan was truly unstoppable inside the blood barrier.

Fang Yuan chuckled, bending down to pick up the two Gu worms.

In his hands lay the Blood Skull Gu and Yin Yang Rotation Gu, sealed by the yellow talisman light.

He knew the Demon Suppression Iron Chain Gu and Problem Nipped in the Bud Gu would soon be corrupted by Blood Frenzy Gu, melting into blood water.

"Hahaha, my good senior brother, to think you have this day! Hand over your life!" Lord Sky Crane howled, soaring into the sky to attack First gen Gu Yue.

Immobilized by the demon suppressing chains, First gen Gu Yue was a sitting target, forced to take every hit.

The iron chains suddenly dissolved into blood water. Free once more, he flew up in a hurry and crashed against the blood barrier.

With a loud bang, the blood barrier firmly blocked him.

The Blood Curtain Skyflower possessed impenetrable defense, but once deployed, one could only exit, never enter.

"Little rascal, you will die a painful death!" First gen Gu Yue's fury toward Fang Yuan surged like a tsunami.

Lord Sky Crane naturally chased him, laughing non-stop.

Giving up on re-entering, First gen Gu Yue fought back against Lord Sky Crane using his replenished primeval essence.

The battle between two Rank five attracted everyone's attention. Fang Yuan however, retracted his gaze and looked at Bai Ning Bing.

Fang Yuan already had a plan, and one person played a crucial role in it: Bai Ning Bing.

A Northern Dark Ice Soul physique's self-detonation would drastically impact the battlefield, especially with the two Rank fives fighting outside.

But how was he going to convince Bai Ning Bing?

Fang Yuan looked at Bai Ning Bing, who sensed it and turned to meet his gaze.

With black robes, hair, and eyes, Fang Yuan curled his lips into a devilish smile.

"I have a way to resolve the ten extreme aptitudes and bring you back to life," he said.

Bai Ning Bing shuddered, desperate to live. "What method?"

Fang Yuan added, "However, this method has little chance of success and will likely fail."

Yet this made Bai Ning Bing even more convinced.

Fang Yuan delivered his third sentence: "Even if we fail, your final moments will be as spectacular as ever."

Thinking quickly, Bai Ning Bing asked directly, "What do I need to do?"

Fang Yuan's smile vanished as the Problem Nipped in the Bud Gu melted into blood water.

Their seals gone, the Blood Skull Gu and Yin Yang Rotation Gu tried to fly away, but Fang Yuan seized them.

Under the Spring Autumn Cicada's aura, the two Rank four Gu were instantly subdued and refined!

"What? Fuck!" Outside the blood barrier, First gen Gu Yue's head ached as his connection to the Blood Skull Gu and Yin Yang Rotation Gu severed.

Losing all composure in shock, he rushed toward the blood barrier!

"How did you refine my Gu worms so quickly!" First gen Gu Yue screamed in anguish.

Seizing the Blood Skull Gu and Yin Yang Rotation Gu dealt First gen Gu Yue a fatal blow.

But quickly, Lord Sky Crane's attack came.

"I want you to die, I want you to die!" First gen Gu Yue went frantic, attacking without defending, exerting all his anger on Lord Sky Crane.

Fang Yuan laughed loudly, telling Bai Ning Bing, "Come, kill them all with me!"

His Chainsaw Golden Centipede whirred, instantly slicing a nearby Gu Master in half.

Blood Skull Gu!

Fang Yuan activated the Blood Skull Gu, absorbing the blood.

# Chapter 198: Ruthless in murdering people, cruelty exercised to profit from disaster

Everyone was shocked. Fang Yuan was ruthless, killing another clansmen.

"I'm only killing Gu Yue clansmen—outsiders fuck off, or I'll kill you too!" Fang Yuan shouted.

Stunned, everyone stepped back, not expecting Fang Yuan to be so insane.

Like a disturbed beehive, the crowd stirred, but no one dared attack; most merely yelled for others to fight.

"Hahaha, interesting!" Bai Ning Bing laughed, suddenly striking down someone beside him.

"Lord Bai Ning Bing, you!" The dying person was a Bai clansmen.

Fang Yuan focused on Bai Ning Bing, surprised by his drastic change.

Bai Ning Bing laughed wildly, "Since you can slaughter your kin, I won't lose to you. The Bai clan is finished anyway—let's enjoy the fun!"

"Hehehe, wonderful," Fang Yuan laughed, moving quickly to begin his slaughter.

Since these clansmen were doomed, their blood was better spent raising Fang Yuan's aptitude than First gen's.

Facing imminent death, Bai Ning Bing was unstoppable, slaughtering the trapped crowd alongside Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan took the last hit and extracted their blood.

After a hundred kills, the Blood Skull Gu reached its limit, its crystal skull turning blood-red.

Fang Yuan laughed, raising the activated Blood Skull Gu above his head.

The teeth opened, shooting out a fragrant blood stream, drenching Fang Yuan from head to toe.

Fang Yuan breathed in deeply, relishing the moment despite the discomfort.

Bathed in blood in his black robes, Fang Yuan resembled a demon from hell.

As the blood gushed into his aperture, his aptitude rose by ten percent to fifty-three percent!

"Truly a great treasure—no wonder First gen Gu Yue valued it like his life!" Fang Yuan nodded in satisfaction and resumed slaughtering.

"Fang Yuan, you mad monster!" Gu Yue Mo Yan charged forward.

Fang Yuan dodged her attack and brought down his Chainsaw Golden Centipede.

The young girl was split in half.

"Fang Yuan, spare us, we're your uncle and aunt!" Dong Tu and his wife begged.

Fang Yuan sneered, waving a blood moonblade that beheaded both.

The Blood Skull Gu absorbed the Gu Yue blood, pouring a thick stream into Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan closed his eyes as warm bloodline power spread through his body.

Aptitude rose again, becoming 63%!

"My cultivation dropped..." Fang Yuan opened his eyes, staring intently.

Though his aptitude reached B grade, his cultivation dropped from Rank three to Rank two.

Being Rank four, the Blood Skull Gu overpowered the Rank three Stone Aperture Gu, turning his aperture back to light. However, the cultivation boost from the stone aperture vanished, reducing his realm in the process.

With Bai Ning Bing slaughtering everyone, Fang Yuan conserved his strength and focused solely on collecting the bloodline.

The slaughter continued, until six hours later.

Fang Yuan's cultivation fell to Rank one initial stage, but his aperture regained its light walls, recovering primeval essence naturally.

Bathed in blood over ten times, the aptitude gains gradually diminished with each successive attempt.

His aptitude rose from 43% C grade to over 90%.

90%, A grade aptitude!

In his past life, rising from C to B grade took two centuries. To reach A grade now was an incredible windfall!

Bai Ning Bing stopped his killing, walking over, "Next, what do we do?"

Looking outside, Fang Yuan said casually, "Next, we wait for First gen Gu Yue's defeat and the Blood Curtain Skyflower to vanish."

"And then?" Bai Ning Bing pursued.

"Then?" Fang Yuan turned to Bai Ning Bing with a smile. "Then you can go and die."

Bai Ning Bing was stunned.

"Oh? What do you mean?" His eyes narrowed, blue irises flashing with dangerous killing intent.

"The Northern Dark Ice Soul's self-detonation can threaten Rank five experts. Can you direct the frost after detonating?" Fang Yuan asked.

"How would I know?" Bai Ning Bing's lips twitched. "I've never self detonated before!"

"I know you can," Fang Yuan chuckled, remembering Bai Ning Bing's self-detonation from before.

And that line—"Live for me, and witness the brilliance of this world!"

"I will stay by your side. When the time is right after you detonate, I will revive you," Fang Yuan continued.

"What if you can't?"

"Then you must gamble. If you doubt me, let your ice storm kill me too—at Rank one, I have no way to survive."

"Even if you believe me, I might fail. You must wager everything on the moment before you die."

Bai Ning Bing was solemn.

Fang Yuan was gambling too, wagering his life on Bai Ning Bing's final decision.

"Hehe. So exciting! Then I shall bet on it, haha!" Bai Ning Bing raised his head, laughing loudly.

Ignoring him, Fang Yuan focused on the battle outside the blood barrier.

As expected, First gen Gu Yue had the lower hand, suppressed by Lord Sky Crane.

"Little thief, you ruined my thousand-year plan! Someday I will slaughter you to appease my wrath!" First gen roared.

With that furious parting roar, First gen Gu Yue flapped his wings and fled into the sky.

"Don't think of escaping!" Lord Sky Crane flashed into white light, giving relentless chase.

Four hours later, the blood barrier thinned, about to vanish.

Just then, a white light flew over, resolving into Lord Sky Crane.

Covered in grievous wounds dripping purple blood, Lord Sky Crane's face was radiant with the joy of revenge.

In his right hand, he held a bloody severed head—First gen Gu Yue!

"He actually killed First gen Gu Yue?" Bai Ning Bing was astonished.

Fang Yuan laughed instead, "It seems this old fellow hates First gen Gu Yue to the core. Hehe, to kill the First gen, he definitely paid a huge price."

As they spoke, the blood barrier vanished, leaving no obstacle between them.

"Hand over the Gu worms and I might spare your lives!" Lord Sky Crane hovered above, laughing triumphantly.

Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing looked at each other before laughing lightly, not speaking.

Lord Sky Crane flew into a rage. "Good lad, refusing a toast only to drink a forfeit, you deserve to get killed!"

Before his words finished, he transformed into white light and charged.

Bai Ning Bing laughed grandly, stepping forward. "I was waiting for you!"

Saying so, his body lost all color, turning into an ice statue.

Frigid winds raged into a hurricane; frost blanketed the sky as a boundless river of ice emerged.

"This is—!" Caught completely unprepared by Bai Ning Bing's attack, Lord Sky Crane was instantly sealed within the ice.

# Chapter 199: Walking towards their own destiny

The glacier shook violently under Lord Sky Crane's fierce attacks; he was seconds away from breaking free.

"Don't let this old geezer break free!" Fang Yuan shouted.

Unable to speak, Bai Ning Bing detonated an arm into howling frost winds, expanding the glacier by a hundred feet.

Detonating his other arm, the condensing glacier bore down, suppressing Lord Sky Crane's attempts to break through.

"The Northern Dark Ice Soul Physique! You won't kill me with this!" Lord Sky Crane activated Life-retaining Jade Burial Gu.

Green jade light solidified into a transparent coffin, encasing Lord Sky Crane within.

Standing beside Bai Ning Bing, Fang Yuan watched him turn into an armless ice sculpture as the frost blurred his features.

"It's time." He immediately took a pair of Gu worms from his aperture.

"Go." At Fang Yuan's will, the black Gu flew forth, merging into Bai Ning Bing's ice sculpture.

With silver hair, a white robe, restored arms, and a blushing, picturesque countenance, Bai Ning Bing broke free from the ice!

"Hahaha," Fang Yuan laughed. "The Yin Gu of the Yin Yang Rotation Gu converts yang to yin—a Rank four healing Gu capable of reviving the dead. However, it has a drawback—the Gu Master's aptitude is lowered by 10% once used."

"This feels great! My aptitude dropped, breaking the Northern Dark Ice Soul Physique. Haha, what does ninety percent matter?" Bai Ning Bing laughed loudly.

Fang Yuan shook his head. "Changing extreme physiques is not so simple. Your cultivation will inevitably recover until the Northern Dark Ice Soul Physique returns. When that day comes, you will need this Yang Gu to reverse yourself and drop your aptitude by another 10%."

"Yin Yang Rotation Gu come in pairs. Only my Yang Gu will affect you now. Don't try stealing it—I can destroy it with a single thought," Fang Yuan explained.

"I see, Fang Yuan, you really planned it all!" Bai Ning Bing sighed. "What do you want for that Yang Gu?"

Fang Yuan's laughter faded into solemnity. "Qing Mao Mountain is frozen solid. The three clans are doomed within days. This disaster will draw the attention of many forces. Furthermore, that old man is sealed in his jade coffin, waiting to break out. We cannot stay on Qing Mao Mountain; we must leave."

"With only Rank one cultivation, I cannot escape alone. I must rely on your strength. The world is vast and wondrous; Qing Mao Mountain is merely a tiny speck. Roam the world with me, and witness its splendor!"

"You truly planned everything!" Bai Ning Bing gritted his teeth, moved by the proposal. "I agree, on one condition."

"What?" Fang Yuan asked.

"You are following me as I roam the lands!" Bai Ning Bing smiled arrogantly.

Fang Yuan laughed to tears. "Have you still not noticed anything wrong?"

"What's wrong?" Bai Ning Bing raised an eyebrow, before his expression turned utterly priceless.

Looking down at her full chest, she cried out in shock, "How did I become a woman?!"

"Naturally! The Yin Gu turns a man into a woman, while the Yang Gu turns a woman into a man. Rotating Yin and Yang... what else did you think it meant?" Fang Yuan spoke matter-of-factly.

"I... what the fuck!" Bai Ning Bing glared at Fang Yuan, cursing furiously. Now, she had to get hold of that sole Yang Gu.

Two days later.

The jade coffin shattered, and Lord Sky Crane broke free into daylight.

Diving into the ice, he quickly extracted a frozen block.

Inside the ice, Fang Zheng lay near death under the Moon Raiment Gu.

Disappointed, Lord Sky Crane muttered, "This isn't him, they merely look alike..."

"Wait, twins—a close relative of that little thief!" His eyes flashed with sharp brilliance.

With a close relative on him, he could refine the Kinship Blood worm!

"Who are you? Where are you taking me?" Fang Zheng cried in alarm.

Awakening atop the soaring crane, Fang Zheng heard the elder declare: "I am Lord Sky Crane, and we are heading to the Middle Continent."

"Middle Continent?!" Fang Zheng shouted in shock.
